 ‘‘āyāmaina  catubbīsa, yaujanaṃ āsi yaujanāsiṃsu (syā॰ ka॰) tāvadai|

vimānaṃ sukataṃ mayhaṃ, vitthāraina catuddasa||

5.

 ‘‘sataṃ satta (syā॰) kañ‍ñāsahassāni, parivārainti maṃ sadā|

sauṇṇamayañ‍ca pal‍laṅkaṃ, byamhai āsi sunimmitaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ, dibbayānaṃ upaṭṭhitaṃ|

pāsādā sivikā caiva, nibbattanti yadicchakaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘maṇimayā ca pal‍laṅkā, añ‍ñai sāramayā bahū|

nibbattanti mamaṃ sabbai, sīhāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘sauṇṇamayā rūpimayā, phalikāvaiḷuriyāmayā|

pādukā abhirūhāmi, pādapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau catunavutai itau (sī॰ syā॰) kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘taisattatimhitau kappai, indanāmā tayau janā|

dvaisattatimhitau kappai, tayau sumananāmakā||

11.

 ‘‘samasattatitau kappai, tayau varuṇanāmakā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, catudīpamhi issarā||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sīhāsanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sīhāsanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. aikatthambhikatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, mahāpūgagaṇau ahu|

saraṇaṃ gatā ca tai buddhaṃ, saddahanti tathāgataṃ||

14.

 ‘‘sabbai saṅgamma mantaitvā, māḷaṃ kubbanti satthunau|

aikatthambhaṃ alabhantā, vicinanti brahāvanai||

15.

 ‘‘taihaṃ arañ‍ñai disvāna, upagamma gaṇaṃ tadā|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, paripucchiṃ gaṇaṃ ahaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘tai mai puṭṭhā viyākaṃsu, sīlavantau upāsakā|

māḷaṃ mayaṃ kattukāmā, aikatthambhau na labbhati||

17.

 ‘‘aikatthambhaṃ mamaṃ daitha, ahaṃ dassāmi satthunau|

āharissāmahaṃ thambhaṃ, appaussuk‍kā bhavantu tai bhavantu vau (sī॰), bhavātha vau (?)||

18.

 ‘‘tai mai thambhaṃ pavaicchiṃsu, pasan‍nā tuṭṭhamānasā|

tatau paṭinivattitvā, agamaṃsu sakaṃ gharaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘aciraṃ gatai pūgagaṇai, thambhaṃ ahāsahaṃ tadā|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, paṭhamaṃ ussapaisahaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, vimānaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ|

ubbiddhaṃ bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ, sattabhūmaṃ satabhūmaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) samuggataṃ||

21.

 ‘‘vaj‍jamānāsu bhairīsu, paricāraimahaṃ sadā|

pañ‍capañ‍ñāsakappamhi, rājā āsiṃ yasaudharau||

22.

 ‘‘tatthāpi  bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ, sattabhūmaṃ samuggataṃ|

kūṭāgāravarūpaitaṃ, aikatthambhaṃ manauramaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘aikavīsatikappamhi, udainau nāma khattiyau|

tatrāpi bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ, sattabhūmaṃ samuggataṃ||

24.

 ‘‘yaṃ  yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

anubhaumi sukhaṃ sabbaṃ sabbamaitaṃ (syā॰), aikatthambhassidaṃ phalaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ thambhamadadaṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, aikatthambhassidaṃ phalaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikatthambhikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikatthambhikatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. nandatthairaapadānaṃ

27.

 ‘‘padumuttarassa bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

vatthaṃ khaumaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, sayambhussa mahaisinau||

28.

 ‘‘taṃ mai buddhau viyākāsi, jalajuttaranāmakau|

‘iminā vatthadānaina, haimavaṇṇau bhavissasi||

29.

 ‘‘‘dvai  sampattī anubhautvā, kusalamūlaihi cauditau|

gautamassa bhagavatau, kaniṭṭhau tvaṃ bhavissasi||

30.

 ‘‘‘rāgarattau sukhasīlau, kāmaisu gaidhamāyutau|

buddhaina cauditau santau, tadā tatau (syā॰) tvaṃ pabbajissasi||

31.

 ‘‘‘pabbajitvāna tvaṃ tattha, kusalamūlaina cauditau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissasināsavau’||

32.

 ‘‘satta  sata (syā॰) kappasahassamhi, caturau caiḷanāmakā|

saṭṭhi kappasahassamhi, upacailā catuj‍janā||

33.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca kappasahassamhi, caiḷāva caturau janā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, catudīpamhi issarā||

34.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nandau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

nandatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. cūḷapanthakatthairaapadānaṃ

35.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

gaṇamhā vūpakaṭṭhau sau, himavantai vasī tadā||

36.

 ‘‘ahampi  himavantamhi, vasāmi assamai tadā|

acirāgataṃ mahāvīraṃ, upaisiṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘pupphacchattaṃ gahaitvāna, upagacchiṃ narāsabhaṃ|

samādhiṃ samāpaj‍jantaṃ, antarāyamakāsahaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, pupphacchattaṃ adāsahaṃ|

paṭiggahaisi bhagavā, padumuttarau mahāmuni||

39.

 ‘‘sabbai daivā attamanā, himavantaṃ upainti tai|

sādhukāraṃ pavattaisuṃ, anumaudissati cakkhumā||

40.

 ‘‘idaṃ vatvāna tai daivā, upagacchuṃ naruttamaṃ|

ākāsai dhārayantassa dhārayantaṃ mai (ka), dhārayatau mai (?), padumacchattamuttamaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘satapattachattaṃ paggayha, adāsi tāpasau mama|

‘tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

42.

 ‘‘‘pañ‍cavīsatikappāni, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

catuttiṃsatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

43.

 ‘‘‘yaṃ  yaṃ yauniṃ saṃsarati, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

abbhaukāsai patiṭṭhantaṃ, padumaṃ dhārayissati’||

44.

 ‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina nāmaina (sī॰ ka॰), satthā laukai bhavissati||

45.

 ‘‘‘pakāsitai pāvacanai, manussattaṃ labhissati|

manaumayamhi kāyamhi, uttamau sau bhavissati||

46.

 ‘‘‘dvai  bhātarau bhavissanti, ubhaupi panthakavhayā|

anubhautvā uttamatthaṃ, jautayissanti sāsanaṃ’||

47.

 ‘‘sauhaṃ  aṭṭhārasavassau sau aṭṭhārasavassauhaṃ (syā॰), pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

visaisāhaṃ na vindāmi, sakyaputtassa sāsanai||

48.

 ‘‘dandhā mayhaṃ gatī āsi, paribhūtau purai ahuṃ ahaṃ (syā॰)|

bhātā ca maṃ paṇāmaisi, gaccha dāni sakaṃ gharaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘sauhaṃ paṇāmitau santau, saṅghārāmassa kauṭṭhakai|

dummanau tattha aṭṭhāsiṃ, sāmañ‍ñasmiṃ apaikkhavā||

50.

 ‘‘bhagavā  tattha athaittha satthā (sī॰ syā॰) āgacchi, sīsaṃ mayhaṃ parāmasi|

bāhāya maṃ gahaitvāna, saṅghārāmaṃ pavaisayi||

51.

 ‘‘anukampāya mai satthā, adāsi pādapuñchaniṃ|

aivaṃ suddhaṃ adhiṭṭhaihi, aikamantamadhiṭṭhahaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘hatthaihi tamahaṃ gayha, sariṃ kaukanadaṃ ahaṃ|

tattha cittaṃ vimuc‍ci mai, arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ||

53.

 ‘‘manaumayaisu kāyaisu, sabbattha pāramiṃ gatau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

54.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā cūḷapanthakau cul‍lapanthakau (sī॰ syā॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

cūḷapanthakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. pilindavacchatthairaapadānaṃ

55.

 ‘‘nibbutai  laukanāthamhi, sumaidhai aggapuggalai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, thūpapūjaṃ akāsahaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘yai ca khīṇāsavā tattha, chaḷabhiñ‍ñā mahiddhikā|

taihaṃ tattha samānaitvā, saṅghabhattaṃ akāsahaṃ||

57.

 ‘‘sumaidhassa bhagavatau, upaṭṭhākau tadā ahu|

sumaidhau nāma nāmaina, anumaudittha sau tadā||

58.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, vimānaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ|

chaḷāsītisahassāni, accharāyau ramiṃsu mai||

59.

 ‘‘mamaiva  anuvattanti, sabbakāmaihi tā sadā|

añ‍ñai daivai abhibhaumi, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘pañ‍cavīsatikappamhi, varuṇau nāma khattiyau|

visuddhabhaujanau susuddhabhaujanau (sī॰) āsiṃ, cak‍kavattī ahaṃ tadā||

61.

 ‘‘na tai bījaṃ pavapanti, napi nīyanti naṅgalā|

akaṭṭhapākimaṃ sāliṃ, paribhuñ‍janti mānusā||

62.

 ‘‘tattha  raj‍jaṃ karitvāna, daivattaṃ puna gacchahaṃ|

tadāpi aidisā mayhaṃ, nibbattā bhaugasampadā||

63.

 ‘‘na maṃ mittā amittā vā, hiṃsanti sabbapāṇinau|

sabbaisampi piyau haumi, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

64.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, gandhālaipassidaṃ phalaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘imasmiṃ bhaddakai kappai, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

mahānubhāvau rājāhaṃ rājīsi (syā॰ ka॰), cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

66.

 ‘‘sauhaṃ pañ‍casu sīlaisu, ṭhapaitvā janataṃ bahuṃ|

pāpaitvā sugatiṃyaiva, daivatānaṃ piyau ahuṃ||

67.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pilindavacchau pilindivacchau (sī॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pilindavacchatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. rāhulatthairaapadānaṃ

68.

 ‘‘padumuttarassa bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

sattabhūmamhi pāsādai, ādāsaṃ santhariṃ ahaṃ||

69.

 ‘‘khīṇāsavasahassaihi, parikiṇṇau mahāmuni|

upāgami gandhakuṭiṃ, dvipadindau dipadindau (sī॰ syā॰) narāsabhau||

70.

 ‘‘viraucaintau viraucayaṃ (syā॰) gandhakuṭiṃ, daivadaivau narāsabhau|

bhikkhusaṅghai ṭhitau satthā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

71.

 ‘‘‘yaināyaṃ  jautitā saiyyā, ādāsauva susanthatau|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

72.

 ‘‘‘sauṇṇamayā rūpimayā, athau vaiḷuriyāmayā|

nibbattissanti pāsādā, yai kaici manasau piyā||

73.

 ‘‘‘catusaṭṭhikkhattuṃ  daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

sahassakkhattuṃ cak‍kavattī, bhavissati anantarā||

74.

 ‘‘‘aikavīsatikappamhi, vimalau nāma khattiyau|

cāturantau vijitāvī, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

75.

 ‘‘‘nagaraṃ raiṇuvatī nāma, iṭṭhakāhi sumāpitaṃ|

āyāmatau tīṇi sataṃ, caturassasamāyutaṃ||

76.

 ‘‘‘sudassanau nāma pāsādau, vissakammaina māpitau visukammaina§māpitau (ka॰), vissakammaina nimmitau (sī॰)|

kūṭāgāravarūpaitau, sattaratanabhūsitau||

77.

 ‘‘‘dasasaddāvivittaṃ  taṃ avivittaṃ (sī॰), vij‍jādharasamākulaṃ|

sudassanaṃva nagaraṃ, daivatānaṃ bhavissati||

78.

 ‘‘‘pabhā niggacchatai tassa, uggacchantaiva sūriyai|

viraucaissati taṃ nic‍caṃ, samantā aṭṭhayaujanaṃ||

79.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

80.

 ‘‘‘tusitā sau cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

gautamassa bhagavatau, atrajau sau bhavissati||

81.

 ‘‘‘sacai  vasaiyya sacā’vasaiyya (?) agāraṃ, cak‍kavattī bhavaiyya sau|

aṭṭhānamaitaṃ yaṃ tādī, agārai ratimajjhagā||

82.

 ‘‘‘nikkhamitvā agāramhā, pabbajissati subbatau|

rāhulau nāma nāmaina, arahā sau bhavissati’||

83.

 ‘‘kikīva aṇḍaṃ rakkhaiyya, cāmarī viya vāladhiṃ|

nipakau sīlasampan‍nau, mamaṃ rakkhi mahāmuni aivaṃ rakkhiṃ mahāmuni (sī॰ ka॰), mamaṃ dakkhi mahāmuni (syā॰)||

84.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ dhammamañ‍ñāya, vihāsiṃ sāsanai ratau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

85.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā rāhulau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

rāhulatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. upasainavaṅgantaputtatthairaapadānaṃ

86.

 ‘‘padumuttaraṃ  bhagavantaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

pabbhāramhi nisīdantaṃ, upagacchiṃ naruttamaṃ||

87.

 ‘‘kaṇikārapupphaṃ  kaṇikāraṃ pupphitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) disvā, vaṇṭai chaitvānahaṃ tadā|

alaṅkaritvā chattamhi, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

88.

 ‘‘piṇḍapātañ‍ca  pādāsiṃ, paraman‍naṃ subhaujanaṃ|

buddhaina navamai tattha, samaṇai aṭṭha bhaujayiṃ||

89.

 ‘‘anumaudi mahāvīrau, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

iminā chattadānaina, paraman‍napavaicchanā||

90.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, sampattimanubhaussasi|

chattiṃsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati||

91.

 ‘‘aikavīsatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

92.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati yaṃ vadanti sumaidhauti, bhūripañ‍ñaṃ sumaidhasaṃ| kappaitau satasahassai, aisa buddhau bhavissati| (ka॰)||

93.

 ‘‘sāsanai dibbamānamhi, manussattaṃ gamissati|

tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau||

94.

 ‘‘upasainauti nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau|

idaṃ pādadvayaṃ thairagāthāaṭṭhakathāyamaiva dissati samantapāsādikattā, aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaissati

idaṃ pādadvayaṃ thairagāthāaṭṭhakathāyamaiva dissati||

95.

 ‘‘carimaṃ vattatai mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, jaitvā māraṃ savāhanaṃ savāhiniṃ (?)||

96.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā upasainau vaṅgantaputtau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

upasainavaṅgantaputtatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

tatiyabhāṇavāraṃ|

8. raṭṭhapālatthairaapadānaṃ

97.

 ‘‘padumuttarassa  bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

varanāgau mayā din‍nau, īsādantau urūḷhavā||

98.

 ‘‘saitacchattau pasaubhitau, sakappanau sahatthipau|

agghāpaitvāna taṃ sabbaṃ, saṅghārāmaṃ akārayiṃ||

99.

 ‘‘catupañ‍ñāsasahassāni, pāsādai kārayiṃ ahaṃ|

mahaughadānaṃ mahābhattaṃ (sī॰), mahaughañ‍ca (ka॰), mahādānaṃ (?) karitvāna, niyyādaisiṃ mahaisinau||

100.

 ‘‘anumaudi mahāvīrau, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

sabbai janai hāsayantau, daisaisi amataṃ padaṃ||

101.

 ‘‘taṃ mai buddhau viyākāsi, jalajuttaranāmakau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

102.

 ‘‘‘catupañ‍ñāsasahassāni, pāsādai kārayī ayaṃ|

kathayissāmi vipākaṃ, suṇautha mama bhāsatau||

103.

 ‘‘‘aṭṭhārasasahassāni, kūṭāgārā bhavissarai|

byamhuttamamhi nibbattā, sabbasauṇṇamayā ca tai||

104.

 ‘‘‘pañ‍ñāsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

aṭṭhapañ‍ñāsakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

105.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

106.

 ‘‘‘daivalaukā  cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

aḍḍhai kulai mahābhaugai, nibbattissati tāvadai||

107.

 ‘‘‘sau pacchā pabbajitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

raṭṭhapālauti nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

108.

 ‘‘‘padhānapahitattau  sau, upasantau nirūpadhi|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau’||

109.

 ‘‘uṭṭhāya abhinikkhamma, jahitā bhaugasampadā|

khaiḷapiṇḍaiva bhaugamhi, paimaṃ mayhaṃ na vij‍jati||

110.

 ‘‘vīriyaṃ  mai dhuradhaurayhaṃ, yaugakkhaimādhivāhanaṃ|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

111.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā raṭṭhapālau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

raṭṭhapālatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. saupākatthairaapadānaṃ

112.

 ‘‘pabbhāraṃ saudhayantassa saivayantassa (sī॰ ka॰), vipinai pabbatuttamai|

siddhatthau nāma bhagavā, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

113.

 ‘‘buddhaṃ  upagataṃ disvā, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

santharaṃ santharitvāna pañ‍ñapaitvāna (syā॰ aṭṭha), pupphāsanamadāsahaṃ||

114.

 ‘‘pupphāsanai nisīditvā, siddhatthau laukanāyakau|

mamañ‍ca gatimañ‍ñāya, anic‍catamudāhari||

115.

 ‘‘‘anic‍cā vata saṅkhārā, uppādavayadhamminau|

uppaj‍jitvā nirujjhanti, taisaṃ vūpasamau sukhau’||

116.

 ‘‘idaṃ vatvāna sabbañ‍ñū, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

nabhaṃ abbhuggami vīrau, haṃsarājāva ambarai||

117.

 ‘‘sakaṃ diṭṭhiṃ jahitvāna, bhāvayānic‍casañ‍ñahaṃ|

aikāhaṃ bhāvayitvāna, tattha kālaṃ katau ahaṃ||

118.

 ‘‘dvai sampattī anubhautvā, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

pacchimai bhavai sampattai, sapākayaunupāgamiṃ||

119.

 ‘‘agārā abhinikkhamma, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

jātiyā sattavassauhaṃ, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

120.

 ‘‘āraddhavīriyau  pahitattau, sīlaisu susamāhitau|

tausaitvāna mahānāgaṃ, alatthaṃ upasampadaṃ||

121.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

122.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ bhāvayiṃ tadā|

taṃ sañ‍ñaṃ bhāvayantassa, pattau mai āsavakkhayau||

123.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saupākau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saupākatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. sumaṅgalatthairaapadānaṃ

124.

 ‘‘āhutiṃ yiṭṭhukāmauhaṃ, paṭiyādaitvāna bhaujanaṃ|

brāhmaṇai paṭimānaintau, visālai māḷakai ṭhitau||

125.

 ‘‘athaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, piyadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ|

sabbalaukavinaitāraṃ, sayambhuṃ aggapuggalaṃ||

126.

 ‘‘bhagavantaṃ jutimantaṃ, sāvakaihi purakkhataṃ|

ādic‍camiva raucantaṃ, rathiyaṃ paṭipan‍nakaṃ||

127.

 ‘‘añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

manasāva nimantaisiṃ, ‘āgacchatu mahāmuni’||

128.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukai anuttarau|

khīṇāsavasahassaihi, mama dvāraṃ upāgami||

129.

 ‘‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

pāsādaṃ abhirūhitvā, sīhāsanai nisīdataṃ nisīda tvaṃ (sī॰)||

130.

 ‘‘dantau  dantaparivārau, tiṇṇau tārayataṃ varau|

pāsādaṃ abhirūhitvā, nisīdi pavarāsanai||

131.

 ‘‘yaṃ mai atthi sakai gaihai, āmisaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ|

tāhaṃ buddhassa pādāsiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

132.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau  sumanau, vaidajātau katañ‍jalī|

buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ namassāmi, ahau buddhassuḷāratā||

133.

 ‘‘aṭṭhan‍naṃ  payirūpāsataṃ, bhuñ‍jaṃ khīṇāsavā bahū|

tuyhaivaisau ānubhāvau, saraṇaṃ taṃ upaimahaṃ||

134.

 ‘‘piyadassī ca bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

135.

 ‘‘‘yau sau saṅghaṃ abhaujaisi, ujubhūtaṃ samāhitaṃ|

tathāgatañ‍ca sambuddhaṃ, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

136.

 ‘‘‘sattavīsatikkhattuṃ sau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

sakakammābhiraddhau sau, daivalaukai ramissati||

137.

 ‘‘‘dasa aṭṭha cakkhattuṃ dasañ‍caṭṭhakkhatthuṃ (sī॰), dasa caṭṭhakkhattuṃ (syā॰) sau, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

pathabyā raj‍jaṃ pañ‍casataṃ, vasudhaṃ āvasissati’||

138.

 ‘‘arañ‍ñavanamauggayha, kānanaṃ byagghasaivitaṃ|

padhānaṃ padahitvāna, kilaisā jhāpitā mayā||

139.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

140.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumaṅgalau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sumaṅgalatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

sīhāsanī  aikathambhī, nandau ca cūḷapanthakau|

pilindarāhulau caiva, vaṅgantau raṭṭhapālakau||

saupākau maṅgalau caiva, dasaiva dutiyai vaggai|

satañ‍ca aṭṭhatiṃsa ca, gāthā caittha pakāsitā||

sīhāsaniyavaggau dutiyau|



3. subhūtivaggau

1. subhūtitthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, nisabhau nāma pabbatau|

assamau sukatau mayhaṃ, paṇṇasālā sumāpitā||

2.

 ‘‘kausiyau nāma nāmaina, jaṭilau uggatāpanau|

aikākiyau aikākikau (ka॰) adutiyau, vasāmi nisabhai tadā||

3.

 ‘‘phalaṃ mūlañ‍ca paṇṇañ‍ca, na bhuñ‍jāmi ahaṃ tadā|

pavattaṃva supātāhaṃ pavattapaṇḍupattāni (sī॰), upajīvāmi tāvadai||

4.

 ‘‘nāhaṃ  kaupaimi ājīvaṃ, cajamānaupi jīvitaṃ|

ārādhaimi sakaṃ cittaṃ, vivaj‍jaimi anaisanaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘rāgūpasaṃhitaṃ cittaṃ, yadā uppaj‍jatai mama|

sayaṃva pac‍cavaikkhāmi, aikaggau taṃ damaimahaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘‘raj‍jasai raj‍janīyai ca, dussanīyai ca dussasai|

muyhasai mauhanīyai ca, nikkhamassu vanā tuvaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘‘visuddhānaṃ ayaṃ vāsau, nimmalānaṃ tapassinaṃ|

mā khau visuddhaṃ dūsaisi, nikkhamassu vanā tuvaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘‘agārikau bhavitvāna, yadā puttaṃ sadāyuttaṃ (sī॰), yadāyuttaṃ (syā॰), labhissasi|

ubhaupi mā virādhaisi, nikkhamassu vanā tuvaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘‘chavālātaṃ yathā kaṭṭhaṃ, na k‍vaci kic‍cakārakaṃ|

naiva gāmai arañ‍ñai vā, na hi taṃ kaṭṭhasammataṃ||

10.

 ‘‘‘chavālātūpamau tvaṃsi, na gihī nāpi sañ‍ñatau|

ubhatau muttakau aj‍ja, nikkhamassu vanā tuvaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘‘siyā nu khau tava aitaṃ, kau pajānāti tai idaṃ|

saddhādhuraṃ vahisi saddhādhuraṃ jahasi (sī॰), sīghaṃ dhuraṃ vahisi (syā॰) mai, kausaj‍jabahulāya ca||

12.

 ‘‘‘jigucchissanti  taṃ viñ‍ñū, asuciṃ nāgarikau yathā|

ākaḍḍhitvāna isayau, caudayissanti taṃ sadā||

13.

 ‘‘‘taṃ viñ‍ñū pavadissanti, samatik‍kantasāsanaṃ|

saṃvāsaṃ alabhantau hi, kathaṃ jīvihisi jīvissasi (sī॰) tuvaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘‘tidhāpabhin‍naṃ  mātaṅgaṃ, kuñ‍jaraṃ saṭṭhihāyanaṃ|

balī nāgau upagantvā, yūthā nīharatai gajaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘‘yūthā vinissaṭau santau, sukhaṃ sātaṃ na vindati|

dukkhitau vimanau hauti, pajjhāyantau pavaidhati||

16.

 ‘‘‘tathaiva jaṭilā tampi, nīharissanti dummatiṃ|

taihi tvaṃ nissaṭau santau, sukhaṃ sātaṃ na lacchasi||

17.

 ‘‘‘divā vā yadi vā rattiṃ, saukasal‍lasamappitau|

ḍayhasi pariḷāhaina, gajau yūthāva nissaṭau||

18.

 ‘‘‘jātarūpaṃ yathā kūṭaṃ, naiva jhāyati yāyati (syā॰) katthaci|

tathā sīlavihīnau tvaṃ, na jhāyissasi yārissati (syā॰) katthaci||

19.

 ‘‘‘agāraṃ vasamānaupi, kathaṃ jīvihisi tuvaṃ|

mattikaṃ paittikañ‍cāpi, natthi tai nihitaṃ dhanaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘‘sayaṃ kammaṃ karitvāna, gattai saidaṃ pamaucayaṃ|

aivaṃ jīvihisi gaihai, sādhu tai taṃ na ruc‍cati||

21.

 ‘‘‘aivāhaṃ tattha vāraimi, saṃkilaisagataṃ manaṃ|

nānādhammakathaṃ katvā, pāpā cittaṃ nivārayiṃ’||

22.

 ‘‘aivaṃ mai viharantassa, appamādavihārinau|

tiṃsavassasahassāni, vipinai mai atik‍kamuṃ||

23.

 ‘‘appamādarataṃ disvā, uttamatthaṃ gavaisakaṃ|

padumuttarasambuddhau, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘timbarūsakavaṇṇābhau, appamaiyyau anūpamau|

rūpaināsadisau buddhau, ākāsai caṅkamī tadā||

25.

 ‘‘suphul‍lau  sālarājāva, vij‍jūvabbhaghanantarai|

ñāṇaināsadisau buddhau, ākāsai caṅkamī tadā||

26.

 ‘‘sīharājā  vasambhītau chambhitau (ka॰),

gajarājāva dappitau dammitau (ka॰)|

lāsitau abhītau (syā॰) byaggharājāva, ākāsai caṅkamī tadā||

27.

 ‘‘siṅgīnikkhasavaṇṇābhau, khadiraṅgārasan‍nibhau|

maṇi yathā jautirasau, ākāsai caṅkamī tadā||

28.

 ‘‘visuddhakailāsanibhau, puṇṇamāyaiva candimā|

majjhanhikaiva majjhantikaiva (sabbattha) sūriyau, ākāsai caṅkamī tadā||

29.

 ‘‘disvā nabhai caṅkamantaṃ, aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā|

‘daivau nu khau ayaṃ sattau, udāhu manujau ayaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘‘na mai sutau vā diṭṭhau vā, mahiyā aidisau narau|

api mantapadaṃ atthi, ayaṃ satthā bhavissati’||

31.

 ‘‘aivāhaṃ cintayitvāna, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

nānāpupphañ‍ca gandhañ‍ca, san‍nipātaisahaṃ san‍nipātaitvāhaṃ (sī॰) tadā||

32.

 ‘‘pupphāsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā, sādhucittaṃ manauramaṃ|

narasārathinaṃ aggaṃ, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

33.

 ‘‘‘idaṃ mai āsanaṃ vīra, pañ‍ñattaṃ tavanucchavaṃ|

hāsayantau mamaṃ cittaṃ, nisīda kusumāsanai’||

34.

 ‘‘nisīdi  tattha bhagavā, asambhītauva achambhitauva (ka॰) kaisarī|

sattarattindivaṃ buddhau, pavarai kusumāsanai||

35.

 ‘‘namassamānau aṭṭhāsiṃ, sattarattindivaṃ ahaṃ|

vuṭṭhahitvā samādhimhā, satthā laukai anuttarau|

mama kammaṃ pakittaintau, idaṃ vacanamabravi||

36.

 ‘‘‘bhāvaihi buddhānussatiṃ, bhāvanānamanuttaraṃ|

imaṃ satiṃ bhāvayitvā, pūrayissasi mānasaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissasi|

asītikkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissasi|

sahassakkhattuṃ cak‍kavattī, rājā raṭṭhai bhavissasi||

38.

 ‘‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ  vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ|

anubhaussasi taṃ sabbaṃ, buddhānussatiyā phalaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘‘bhavābhavai  saṃsarantau, mahābhaugaṃ labhissasi|

bhaugai tai ūnatā natthi, buddhānussatiyā phalaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

41.

 ‘‘‘asītikauṭiṃ chaḍḍaitvā, dāsai kammakarai bahū|

gautamassa bhagavatau, sāsanai pabbajissasi||

42.

 ‘‘‘ārādhayitvā sambuddhaṃ, gautamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ|

subhūti nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

43.

 ‘‘‘bhikkhusaṅghai  nisīditvā, dakkhiṇaiyyaguṇamhi taṃ|

tathāraṇavihārai ca, dvīsu aggai ṭhapaissati’||

44.

 ‘‘idaṃ vatvāna sambuddhau, jalajuttamanāmakau|

nabhaṃ abbhuggamī vīrau, haṃsarājāva ambarai||

45.

 ‘‘sāsitau laukanāthaina, namassitvā tathāgataṃ|

sadā bhāvaimi muditau, buddhānussatimuttamaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsaṃ agacchahaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘asītikkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ|

anubhaumi susampattiṃ, buddhānussatiyā phalaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘bhavābhavai saṃsarantau, mahābhaugaṃ labhāmahaṃ|

bhaugai mai ūnatā natthi, buddhānussatiyā phalaṃ||

50.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhānussatiyā phalaṃ||

51.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā subhūti thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

subhūtitthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. upavānatthairaapadānaṃ

52.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

jalitvā aggikkhandhauva, sambuddhau parinibbutau||

53.

 ‘‘mahājanā samāgamma, pūjayitvā tathāgataṃ|

citaṃ katvāna sukataṃ, sarīraṃ abhiraupayuṃ||

54.

 ‘‘sarīrakic‍caṃ katvāna, dhātuṃ tattha samānayuṃ|

sadaivamānusā sabbai, buddhathūpaṃ akaṃsu tai||

55.

 ‘‘paṭhamā  kañ‍canamayā, dutiyāsi maṇīmayā|

tatiyā rūpiyamayā, catutthī phalikāmayā||

56.

 ‘‘tathā tattha (syā॰ ka॰) pañ‍camiyā bhūmi naimi (sī॰), lauhitaṅgamayā ahu|

chaṭṭhā masāragal‍lassa, sabbaratanamayūpari||

57.

 ‘‘jaṅghā maṇimayā āsi, vaidikā ratanamayā|

sabbasauṇṇamayau thūpau, uddhaṃ yaujanamuggatau||

58.

 ‘‘daivā tattha samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

mayampi thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau||

59.

 ‘‘dhātu āvaiṇikā natthi, sarīraṃ aikapiṇḍitaṃ|

imamhi buddhathūpamhi, kassāma kañ‍cukaṃ mayaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘daivā sattahi ratnaihi sattaratanaihi (sī॰), añ‍ñaṃ vaḍḍhaisu yaujanaṃ|

thūpau dviyaujanubbaidhau, timiraṃ byapahanti sau||

61.

 ‘‘nāgā  tattha samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

manussā caiva daivā ca, buddhathūpaṃ akaṃsu tai||

62.

 ‘‘mā  nau pamattā assumha assumhā (sī॰ syā॰), āsimhā (?), appamattā sadaivakā|

mayampi thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau||

63.

 ‘‘indanīlaṃ mahānīlaṃ, athau jautirasaṃ maṇiṃ|

aikatau san‍nipātaitvā, buddhathūpaṃ achādayuṃ||

64.

 ‘‘sabbaṃ maṇimayaṃ āsi, tāvatā buddhacaitiyaṃ|

tiyaujanasamubbiddhaṃ tīṇi yaujanamubbiddhaṃ (sī॰ ka॰), ālaukakaraṇaṃ tadā||

65.

 ‘‘garuḷā  ca samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

manussā daivā nāgā ca, buddhathūpaṃ akaṃsu tai||

66.

 ‘‘‘mā nau pamattā assumha, appamattā sadaivakā|

mayampi thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau’||

67.

 ‘‘sabbaṃ maṇimayaṃ thūpaṃ, akaruṃ tai ca kañ‍cukaṃ sabbamaṇimayaṃ thūpai, akaruttarakañ‍cukaṃ (sī॰)|

yaujanaṃ taipi vaḍḍhaisuṃ, āyataṃ buddhacaitiyaṃ||

68.

 ‘‘catuyaujanamubbiddhau, buddhathūpau viraucati|

aubhāsaiti disā sabbā, sataraṃsīva uggatau||

69.

 ‘‘kumbhaṇḍā ca samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

manussā caiva daivā ca, nāgā ca garuḷā tathā||

pac‍caikaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhassa, akaṃsu thūpamuttamaṃ||

70.

 ‘‘‘mā nau pamattā assumha, appamattā sadaivakā|

mayampi thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau|

ratanaihi chādaissāma, āyataṃ buddhacaitiyaṃ’||

71.

 ‘‘yaujanaṃ taipi vaḍḍhaisuṃ, āyataṃ buddhacaitiyaṃ|

pañ‍cayaujanamubbiddhau, thūpau aubhāsatai tadā||

72.

 ‘‘yakkhā  tattha samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

manussā daivā nāgā ca, garuḷā kumbhaaṇḍakā||

73.

 ‘‘pac‍caikaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhassa, akaṃsu thūpamuttamaṃ|

‘mā nau pamattā assumha, appamattā sadaivakā||

74.

 ‘‘‘mayampi  thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau|

phalikāhi chādaissāma, āyataṃ buddhacaitiyaṃ’||

75.

 ‘‘yaujanaṃ taipi vaḍḍhaisuṃ, āyataṃ buddhacaitiyaṃ|

cha yaujanāni ubbiddhau, thūpau aubhāsatai tadā||

76.

 ‘‘gandhabbā ca samāgantvā, aikatau mantayuṃ tadā|

‘manujā daivatā nāgā, garuḷā kumbhayakkhakā||

77.

 ‘‘‘sabbaikaṃsu buddhathūpaṃ, mayamaittha akārakā|

mayampi thūpaṃ kassāma, laukanāthassa tādinau’||

78.

 ‘‘vaidiyau  satta katvāna, chattamāraupayiṃsu tai|

sabbasauṇṇamayaṃ thūpaṃ, gandhabbā kārayuṃ tadā||

79.

 ‘‘sattayaujanamubbiddhau, thūpau aubhāsatai tadā|

rattindivā na ñāyanti, ālaukau hauti ālaukā haunti (syā॰ ka॰) sabbadā||

80.

 ‘‘abhibhaunti  na tassābhā, candasūrā satārakā|

samantā yaujanasatai, padīpaupi na paj‍jali||

81.

 ‘‘taina kālaina yai kaici, thūpaṃ pūjainti mānusā|

na tai thūpamāruhanti, ambarai ukkhipanti tai||

82.

 ‘‘daivaihi ṭhapitau yakkhau, abhisammatanāmakau|

dhajaṃ vā pupphadāmaṃ vā, abhiraupaiti uttari||

83.

 ‘‘na tai passanti taṃ yakkhaṃ, dāmaṃ passanti gacchatau|

aivaṃ passitvā gacchantā, sabbai gacchanti suggatiṃ||

84.

 ‘‘viruddhā visaddhā (sī॰) yai pāvacanai, pasan‍nā yai ca sāsanai|

pāṭihairaṃ daṭṭhukāmā, thūpaṃ pūjainti mānusā||

85.

 ‘‘nagarai haṃsavatiyā, ahausiṃ bhatakau varakau (syā॰ ka॰) tadā|

āmauditaṃ janaṃ disvā, aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā||

86.

 ‘‘‘uḷārau bhagavā haisau, yassa dhātudharaidisaṃ|

imā ca janatā tuṭṭhā, kāraṃ kubbaṃ na tapparai kubbantanappakaṃ (sī॰)||

87.

 ‘‘‘ahampi  kāraṃ kassāmi, laukanāthassa tādinau|

tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, bhavissāmi anāgatai’||

88.

 ‘‘sudhautaṃ  rajakaināhaṃ, uttaraiyyapaṭaṃ mama|

vaiḷaggai ālagaitvāna, dhajaṃ ukkhipimambarai||

89.

 ‘‘abhisammatakau gayha, ambaraihāsi mai dhajaṃ|

vātairitaṃ dhajaṃ disvā, bhiyyau hāsaṃ janaisahaṃ||

90.

 ‘‘tattha  cittaṃ pasādaitvā, samaṇaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ|

taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaitvā, vipākaṃ pucchahaṃ dhajai||

91.

 ‘‘sau mai kathaisi ānanda, pītisañ‍jananaṃ mama|

‘tassa dhajassa vipākaṃ, anubhaussasi sabbadā||

92.

 ‘‘‘hatthī  assā rathā pattī, sainā ca caturaṅginī|

parivāraissanti taṃ nic‍caṃ, dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

93.

 ‘‘‘saṭṭhitūriyasahassāni, bhairiyau samalaṅkatā|

parivāraissanti taṃ nic‍caṃ, dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

94.

 ‘‘‘chaḷāsītisahassāni, nāriyau samalaṅkatā|

vicittavatthābharaṇā, āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā||

95.

 ‘‘‘aḷārapamhā hasulā, susañ‍ñā tanumajjhimā|

parivāraissanti taṃ nic‍caṃ, dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

96.

 ‘‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissasi|

asītikkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissasi||

97.

 ‘‘‘sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissasi|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

98.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

99.

 ‘‘‘daivalaukā cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

puñ‍ñakammaina saṃyuttau, brahmabandhu bhavissasi||

100.

 ‘‘‘asītikauṭiṃ  chaḍḍaitvā, dāsai kammakarai bahū|

gautamassa bhagavatau, sāsanai pabbajissasi||

101.

 ‘‘‘ārādhayitvā  sambuddhaṃ, gautamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ|

upavānauti nāmaina, haissasi satthu sāvakau’||

102.

 ‘‘satasahassai kataṃ kammaṃ, phalaṃ dassaisi mai idha|

sumuttau saravaigauva kilaisai jhāpayī mama||

103.

 ‘‘cak‍kavattissa santassa, catudīpissarassa mai|

tiyaujanāni samantā, ussīsanti dhajā sadā||

104.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

105.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā upavānau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

upavānatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. tisaraṇagamaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

106.

 ‘‘nagarai candavatiyā bandhumatiyā (aṭṭha॰), mātuupaṭṭhākau mātupaṭṭhāyakau (sī॰), mātupaṭṭhānakau (syā॰) ahuṃ|

andhā mātā pitā mayhaṃ, tai pausaimi ahaṃ tadā||

107.

 ‘‘rahaugatau  nisīditvā, aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā|

pausaintau mātāpitarau, pabbaj‍jaṃ na labhāmahaṃ||

108.

 ‘‘mahandhakārapihitā tamandhakārapihitā (syā॰), tividhaggīhi ḍayharai|

aitādisai bhavai bhayai (sī॰) jātai, natthi kauci vināyakau||

109.

 ‘‘buddhau laukai samuppan‍nau, dippati dibbati (ka॰) jinasāsanaṃ (sī॰) dāni sāsanaṃ|

sak‍kā uddharituṃ attā, puñ‍ñakāmaina jantunā||

110.

 ‘‘uggayha tīṇi saraṇai, paripuṇṇāni gaupayiṃ|

taina kammaina sukataina, paṭimaukkhāmi duggatiṃ||

111.

 ‘‘nisabhau  nāma samaṇau, buddhassa aggasāvakau|

tamahaṃ upagantvāna, saraṇagamanaṃ gahiṃ||

112.

 ‘‘vassasatasahassāni, āyu vij‍jati tāvadai|

tāvatā saraṇagamanaṃ, paripuṇṇaṃ agaupayiṃ||

113.

 ‘‘carimai vattamānamhi, saraṇaṃ taṃ anussariṃ|

taina kammaina sukataina, tāvatiṃsaṃ agacchahaṃ||

114.

 ‘‘daivalaukagatau santau, puñ‍ñakammasamāhitau|

yaṃ daisaṃ yaṃ yaṃ daisaṃ (syā॰) upapaj‍jāmi upagacchāmi (sī॰), aṭṭha haitū labhāmahaṃ||

115.

 ‘‘disāsu  pūjitau haumi, tikkhapañ‍ñau bhavāmahaṃ|

sabbai daivānuvattanti, amitabhaugaṃ labhāmahaṃ||

116.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇau sabbattha, paṭikantau bhavāmahaṃ|

mittānaṃ acalau haumi, yasau abbhuggatau mamaṃ||

117.

 ‘‘asītikkhattu  daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

dibbasukhaṃ anubhaviṃ, accharāhi purakkhatau||

118.

 ‘‘pañ‍casattatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

119.

 ‘‘pacchimai bhavai sampattai, puñ‍ñakammasamāhitau|

purai sāvatthiyaṃ jātau, mahāsālai suaḍḍhakai||

120.

 ‘‘nagarā nikkhamitvāna, dārakaihi purakkhatau|

hasakhiḍḍasamaṅgīhaṃ sāhaṃ khiḍḍasamaṅgī (syā॰), saṅghārāmaṃ upāgamiṃ||

121.

 ‘‘tatthaddasāsiṃ tatthaddasāhaṃ (ka॰) samaṇaṃ, vippamuttaṃ nirūpadhiṃ|

sau mai dhammamadaisaisi, saraṇañ‍ca adāsi mai||

122.

 ‘‘sauhaṃ sutvāna saraṇaṃ, saraṇaṃ mai anussariṃ|

aikāsanai nisīditvā, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

123.

 ‘‘jātiyā sattamai vassai, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ|

upasampādayi buddhau, guṇamañ‍ñāya cakkhumā||

124.

 ‘‘aparimaiyyai  itau kappai, saraṇāni agacchahaṃ|

tatau mai sukataṃ kammaṃ, phalaṃ dassaisi mai idha||

125.

 ‘‘sugaupitaṃ mai saraṇaṃ, mānasaṃ suppaṇīhitaṃ|

anubhautvā yasaṃ sabbaṃ, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

126.

 ‘‘yaisaṃ sautāvadhānatthi, suṇautha mama bhāsatau|

ahaṃ atthaṃ (syā॰) vau kathayissāmi, sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ padaṃ mama||

127.

 ‘‘‘buddhau  laukai samuppan‍nau, vattatai jinasāsanaṃ|

amatā vāditā bhairī, saukasal‍lavinaudanā||

128.

 ‘‘‘yathāsakaina thāmaina, puñ‍ñakkhaittai anuttarai|

adhikāraṃ karaiyyātha, passayissatha nibbutiṃ||

129.

 ‘‘‘paggayha tīṇi saraṇai, pañ‍casīlāni gaupiya|

buddhai cittaṃ pasādaitvā, dukkhassantaṃ karissatha||

130.

 ‘‘‘sammā dhammaṃ bhāvaitvāna mamaupamaṃ karitvāna (sī॰ syā॰), sīlāni  parigaupiya|

aciraṃ arahattaṃ vau, sabbaipi pāpuṇissatha||

131.

 ‘‘‘taivij‍jau iddhipattaumhi, caitaupariyakauvidau|

sāvakau tai mahāvīra, saraṇau caraṇai (sī॰ syā॰) vandati satthunau’||

132.

 ‘‘aparimaiyyai  itau kappai, saraṇaṃ buddhassa gacchahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, saraṇaṃ gamanai phalaṃ||

133.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tisaraṇagamaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tisaraṇagamaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. pañ‍casīlasamādāniyatthairaapadānaṃ

134.

 ‘‘nagarai candavatiyā, bhatakau āsahaṃ tadā|

parakammāyanai yuttau, pabbaj‍jaṃ na labhāmahaṃ||

135.

 ‘‘mahandhakārapihitā, tividhaggīhi ḍayharai|

kaina nu khau upāyaina, visaṃyuttau bhavai ahaṃ||

136.

 ‘‘daiyyadhammau ca mai natthi, varākau bhatakau ahaṃ|

yaṃnūnāhaṃ pañ‍casīlaṃ, rakkhaiyyaṃ paripūrayaṃ||

137.

 ‘‘anaumadassissa muninau, nisabhau nāma sāvakau|

tamahaṃ upasaṅkamma, pañ‍casikkhāpadaggahiṃ||

138.

 ‘‘vassasatasahassāni, āyu vij‍jati tāvadai|

tāvatā pañ‍casīlāni, paripuṇṇāni gaupayiṃ||

139.

 ‘‘mac‍cukālai ca sampattai, daivā assāsayanti maṃ|

‘rathau sahassayuttau tai, mārisāyaṃ mārisassa (ka॰) upaṭṭhitau’||

140.

 ‘‘vattantai carimai cittai, mama sīlaṃ anussariṃ|

taina kammaina sukataina, tāvatiṃsaṃ agacchahaṃ||

141.

 ‘‘tiṃsakkhattuñ‍ca  daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

dibbasukhaṃ dibbaṃ sukhaṃ (sī॰) anubhaviṃ, accharāhi purakkhatau||

142.

 ‘‘pañ‍casattatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

143.

 ‘‘daivalaukā cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

purai vaisāliyaṃ jātau, mahāsālai suaḍḍhakai||

144.

 ‘‘vassūpanāyikai  kālai, dippantai dibbanti (ka॰) jinasāsanai|

mātā ca mai pitā caiva, pañ‍casikkhāpadaggahuṃ||

145.

 ‘‘saha  sutvānahaṃ sīlaṃ, mama sīlaṃ anussariṃ|

aikāsanai nisīditvā, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

146.

 ‘‘jātiyā pañ‍cavassaina, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ|

upasampādayi buddhau, guṇamañ‍ñāya cakkhumā||

147.

 ‘‘paripuṇṇāni gaupaitvā, pañ‍casikkhāpadānahaṃ|

aparimaiyyai itau kappai, vinipātaṃ na gacchahaṃ||

148.

 ‘‘svāhaṃ  yasamanubhaviṃ, taisaṃ sīlāna vāhasā|

kappakauṭimpi kittaintau, kittayai aikadaisakaṃ||

149.

 ‘‘pañ‍casīlāni gaupaitvā, tayau haitū labhāmahaṃ|

dīghāyukau mahābhaugau, tikkhapañ‍ñau bhavāmahaṃ||

150.

 ‘‘saṃkittaintau ca pakittaintauva (sī॰), pakittaintai ca (syā॰) sabbaisaṃ, abhimattañ‍ca paurisaṃ|

bhavābhavai saṃsaritvā, aitai ṭhānai labhāmahaṃ||

151.

 ‘‘aparimaiyyasīlaisu, vattantā jinasāvakā|

bhavaisu yadi raj‍jaiyyuṃ, vipākau kīdisau bhavai||

152.

 ‘‘suciṇṇaṃ mai pañ‍casīlaṃ, bhatakaina tapassinā vipassinā (sī॰)|

taina sīlainahaṃ aj‍ja, maucayiṃ sabbabandhanā||

153.

 ‘‘aparimaiyyai itau kappai, pañ‍casīlāni gaupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pañ‍casīlānidaṃ phalaṃ||

154.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā pañ‍casīlasamādāniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pañ‍casīlasamādāniyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. an‍nasaṃsāvakatthairaapadānaṃ

155.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇai|

kañ‍canagghiyasaṃkāsaṃ, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ||

156.

 ‘‘siddhatthaṃ  laukapaj‍jautaṃ, appamaiyyaṃ anaupamaṃ|

alatthaṃ paramaṃ pītiṃ, disvā dantaṃ jutindharaṃ||

157.

 ‘‘sambuddhaṃ abhināmaitvā, bhaujayiṃ taṃ mahāmuniṃ|

mahākāruṇikau laukai nāthau (sī॰), anumaudi mamaṃ tadā||

158.

 ‘‘tasmiṃ  mahākāruṇikai, paramassāsakārakai|

buddhai cittaṃ pasādaitvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

159.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhikkhādānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

160.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā an‍nasaṃsāvakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

an‍nasaṃsāvakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dhūpadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

161.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

kuṭidhūpaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

162.

 yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

sabbaisampi piyau haumi, dhūpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

163.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ dhūpamadadiṃ tadā yaṃ dhūpanamadāsahaṃ (ka॰)|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhūpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

164.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhūpadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

dhūpadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. pulinapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

165.

 ‘‘vipassissa  bhagavatau, baudhiyā pādaputtamai|

purāṇapulinaṃ hitvā chaḍḍaitvā (sī॰ syā॰), suddhapulinamākiriṃ||

166.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ pulinamadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pulinadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

167.

 ‘‘tiṃsatimai  tipañ‍ñāsai (sī॰ syā॰) itau kappai, rājā āsiṃ janādhibhū|

mahāpulinanāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

168.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pulinapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pulinapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. uttiyatthairaapadānaṃ

169.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, susumārau ahaṃ tadā|

sagaucarappasutauhaṃ sabhaujanapasutāhaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), nadītitthaṃ agacchahaṃ||

170.

 ‘‘siddhatthau tamhi samayai, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

nadiṃ taritukāmau sau, nadītitthaṃ upāgami||

171.

 ‘‘upāgatai ca upāgatamhi (syā॰ ka॰) sambuddhai, ahampi tatthupāgamiṃ|

upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ, imaṃ vācaṃ udīrayiṃ||

172.

 ‘‘‘abhirūha mahāvīra, tāraissāmi ahaṃ tuvaṃ|

paittikaṃ visayaṃ mayhaṃ, anukampa mahāmuni’||

173.

 ‘‘mama uggaj‍janaṃ sutvā, abhirūhi mahāmuni|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, tāraisiṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

174.

 ‘‘nadiyā  pārimai tīrai, siddhatthau laukanāyakau|

assāsaisi mamaṃ tattha, amataṃ pāpuṇissasi||

175.

 ‘‘tamhā  kāyā cavitvāna, daivalaukaṃ āgacchahaṃ|

dibbasukhaṃ anubhaviṃ, accharāhi purakkhatau||

176.

 ‘‘sattakkhattuñ‍ca  daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakāsahaṃ|

tīṇikkhattuṃ cak‍kavattī, mahiyā issarau ahuṃ||

177.

 ‘‘vivaikamanuyuttauhaṃ, nipakau ca susaṃvutau|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

178.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, tāraisiṃ yaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, taraṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

179.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uttiyau uttiriyau (sī॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

uttiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. aikañ‍jalikatthairaapadānaṃ

180.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇai|

vipassiṃ satthavāhaggaṃ, naravaraṃ vināyakaṃ||

181.

 ‘‘adantadamanaṃ  tādiṃ, mahāvādiṃ mahāmatiṃ|

disvā pasan‍nau sumanau, aikañ‍jalimakāsahaṃ||

182.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yamañ‍jaliṃ kariṃ yaṃ añ‍jalimakariṃ (syā॰), añ‍jalimakariṃ (ka॰) tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, añ‍jalissa idaṃ phalaṃ||

183.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikañ‍jalikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikañ‍jalikatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. khaumadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

184.

 ‘‘nagarai  bandhumatiyā, ahausiṃ vāṇijau tadā|

tainaiva dāraṃ pausaimi, raupaimi bījasampadaṃ||

185.

 ‘‘rathiyaṃ paṭipan‍nassa, vipassissa mahaisinau|

aikaṃ khaumaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, kusalatthāya satthunau||

186.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ khaumamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, khaumadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

187.

 ‘‘sattarasai sattavīsai (sī॰ syā॰) itau kappai, aikau sindhavasandhanau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, catudīpamhi issarau||

188.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā khaumadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

khaumadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

susūti upavānau ca, saraṇau sīlagāhakau|

an‍nasaṃsāvakau khaumadāyī ca, dasaiva tatiyai gaṇai|

añ‍jalī khaumadāyī ca, dasaiva tatiyai gaṇai|

pañ‍cālīsītisataṃ vuttā, gāthāyau sabbapiṇḍitā||

subhūtivaggau tatiyau|


catutthabhāṇavāraṃ|


4. kuṇḍadhānavaggau

1. kuṇḍadhānatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘sattāhaṃ  paṭisal‍līnaṃ, sayambhuṃ aggapuggalaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ||

2.

 ‘‘vuṭṭhitaṃ kālamañ‍ñāya, padumuttaraṃ mahāmuniṃ|

mahantiṃ kadalīkaṇṇiṃ, gahaitvā upagacchahaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘paṭiggahaitvā  paṭiggahaisi (syā॰ ka॰) bhagavā, sabbañ‍ñū taṃ phalaṃ (sī॰) laukanāyakau|

mama cittaṃ pasādaintau, paribhuñ‍ji mahāmuni||

4.

 ‘‘paribhuñ‍jitvā sambuddhau, satthavāhau anuttarau|

sakāsanai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

5.

 ‘‘‘yai  ca santi samitārau yai vasanti samaitārau (sī॰), yakkhā imamhi pabbatai|

arañ‍ñai bhūtabhabyāni bhūtagaṇā sabbai (syā॰), suṇantu vacanaṃ mama’||

6.

 ‘‘yau sau buddhaṃ upaṭṭhāsi, migarājaṃva kaisariṃ migarājāva kaisarī (sī॰)|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

7.

 ‘‘‘aikādasañ‍cakkhattuṃ, sau sauyamaikādasakkhattuṃ (sī॰) daivarājā bhavissati|

catutiṃsatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

8.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

9.

 ‘‘‘ak‍kausitvāna samaṇai, sīlavantai anāsavai|

pāpakammavipākaina, nāmadhaiyyaṃ labhissati bhavissati (ka॰)||

10.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

kuṇḍadhānauti nāmaina, sāvakau sau bhavissati’||

11.

 ‘‘pavivaikamanuyuttau, jhāyī jhānaratau ahaṃ|

tausayitvāna satthāraṃ, viharāmi anāsavau||

12.

 ‘‘sāvakaihi  sāvakaggaihi (sī॰) parivutau, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhatau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, salākaṃ gāhayī jinau||

13.

 ‘‘aikaṃsaṃ  cīvaraṃ katvā, vanditvā laukanāyakaṃ|

vadataṃ varassa puratau, paṭhamaṃ aggahaisahaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘taina kammaina bhagavā, dasasahassīkampakau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

15.

 ‘‘vīriyaṃ mai dhuradhaurayhaṃ, yaugakkhaimādhivāhanaṃ|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

16.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kuṇḍadhānau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kuṇḍadhānatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. sāgatatthairaapadānaṃ

17.

 ‘‘saubhitau  nāma nāmaina, ahausiṃ brāhmaṇau tadā|

purakkhatau sasissaihi, ārāmaṃ agamāsahaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘bhagavā tamhi samayai, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhatau|

ārāmadvārā nikkhamma, aṭṭhāsi purisuttamau||

19.

 ‘‘tamaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, dantaṃ dantapurakkhataṃ|

sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, santhaviṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘yai kaici pādapā sabbai, mahiyā tai virūharai|

buddhimantau  tathā sattā, ruhanti jinasāsanai||

21.

 ‘‘satthavāhausi sappañ‍ñau, mahaisi bahukai janai|

vipathā uddharitvāna, pathaṃ ācikkhasai tuvaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘dantau  dantaparikiṇṇau purakkhatau (syā॰), jhāyī jhānarataihi ca|

ātāpī pahitattaihi, upasantaihi tādibhi||

23.

 ‘‘alaṅkatau parisāhi, puñ‍ñañāṇaihi saubhati|

pabhā niddhāvatai tuyhaṃ, sūriyaudayanai yathā||

24.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittaṃ  disvāna, mahaisī padumuttarau|

bhikkhusaṅghai ṭhitau satthā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

25.

 ‘‘‘yau sau hāsaṃ janaitvāna, mamaṃ kittaisi brāhmaṇau|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, daivalaukai ramissati||

26.

 ‘‘‘tusitā hi cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

gautamassa bhagavatau, sāsanai pabbajissati||

27.

 ‘‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, arahattaṃ tuṭṭhahaṭṭhaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) labhissati|

sāgatau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

28.

 ‘‘pabbajitvāna kāyaina, pāpakammaṃ vivaj‍jayiṃ|

vacīduc‍caritaṃ hitvā, ājīvaṃ parisaudhayiṃ||

29.

 ‘‘aivaṃ  viharamānauhaṃ, taijaudhātūsu kauvidau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sāgatau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sāgatatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. mahākac‍cānatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘padumuttaranāthassa, padumaṃ nāma caitiyaṃ|

silāsanaṃ sīhāsanaṃ (ka॰) kārayitvā, suvaṇṇainābhilaipayiṃ||

32.

 ‘‘ratanāmayachattañ‍ca, paggayha vāḷabījaniṃ vāḷabījanī (sī॰ syā॰)|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, laukabandhussa tādinau||

33.

 ‘‘yāvatā  daivatā bhummā bhūmā (ka॰), sabbai san‍nipatuṃ tadā|

ratanāmayachattānaṃ, vipākaṃ kathayissati||

34.

 ‘‘tañ‍ca sabbaṃ suṇissāma, kathayantassa satthunau|

bhiyyau hāsaṃ janaiyyāma, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

35.

 ‘‘haimāsanai nisīditvā, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

bhikkhusaṅghaparibyūḷhau paribbūḷhau (sī॰), imā gāthā abhāsatha||

36.

 ‘‘‘yainidaṃ  āsanaṃ din‍naṃ, sauvaṇṇaṃ ratanāmayaṃ|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

37.

 ‘‘‘tiṃsakappāni  daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

samantā yaujanasataṃ, ābhāyābhibhavissati||

38.

 ‘‘‘manussalaukamāgantvā, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

pabhassarauti nāmaina, uggataijau bhavissati||

39.

 ‘‘‘divā vā yadi vā rattiṃ, sataraṃsīva uggatau|

samantā aṭṭharatanaṃ, uj‍jautissati khattiyau||

40.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

41.

 ‘‘‘tusitā  hi cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

kac‍cānau nāma nāmaina, brahmabandhu bhavissati||

42.

 ‘‘‘sau pacchā pabbajitvāna, arahā haissatināsavau|

gautamau laukapaj‍jautau, aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaissati||

43.

 ‘‘‘saṃkhittapucchitaṃ saṃkhittaṃ pucchitaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) pañhaṃ, vitthāraina kathaissati|

kathayantau ca taṃ pañhaṃ, ajjhāsayaṃ ajjhāsaṃ (sī॰), abbhāsaṃ (ka॰) pūrayissati’||

44.

 ‘‘aḍḍhai kulai abhijātau, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū|

auhāya dhanadhañ‍ñāni, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘saṃkhittainapi pucchantai, vitthāraina kathaimahaṃ|

ajjhāsayaṃ taisaṃ pūraimi, tausaimi dvipaduttamaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘tausitau  mai mahāvīrau, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

47.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahākac‍cānau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

mahākac‍cānatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. kāḷudāyitthairaapadānaṃ

48.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

addhānaṃ paṭipan‍nassa, caratau cārikaṃ tadā||

49.

 ‘‘suphul‍laṃ padumaṃ gayha, uppalaṃ mal‍likañ‍cahaṃ|

paraman‍naṃ gahaitvāna, adāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

50.

 ‘‘paribhuñ‍ji mahāvīrau, paraman‍naṃ subhaujanaṃ|

tañ‍ca pupphaṃ gahaitvāna, janassa sampadassayi||

51.

 ‘‘iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ kantayidaṃ (syā॰), piyaṃ laukai, jalajaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ|

suduk‍karaṃ kataṃ taina, yau mai pupphaṃ adāsidaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘yau  pupphamabhiraupaisi, paraman‍nañ‍cadāsi mai|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

53.

 ‘‘‘dasa aṭṭha cakkhattuṃ dasa caṭṭhakkhattuṃ (sī॰), dasamaṭṭhakkhattuṃ (syā॰) sau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

uppalaṃ padumañ‍cāpi, mal‍likañ‍ca taduttari||

54.

 ‘‘‘assa  puñ‍ñavipākaina, dibbagandhasamāyutaṃ|

ākāsai chadanaṃ katvā, dhārayissati tāvadai||

55.

 ‘‘‘pañ‍cavīsatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

pathabyā raj‍jaṃ pañ‍casataṃ, vasudhaṃ āvasissati||

56.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina nāmaina (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), satthā laukai bhavissati||

57.

 ‘‘‘sakakammābhiraddhau  sau, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

sakyānaṃ nandijananau, ñātibandhu bhavissati||

58.

 ‘‘‘sau pacchā pabbajitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

59.

 ‘‘‘paṭisambhidamanuppattaṃ, katakic‍camanāsavaṃ|

gautamau laukabandhu taṃ sau (sī॰), aitadaggai ṭhapaissati||

60.

 ‘‘‘padhānapahitattau sau, upasantau nirūpadhi|

udāyī nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

61.

 ‘‘rāgau  dausau ca mauhau ca, mānau makkhau ca dhaṃsitau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

62.

 ‘‘tausayiñ‍cāpi sambuddhaṃ, ātāpī nipakau ahaṃ|

pasāditau pamauditau (sī॰) ca sambuddhau, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

63.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā kāḷudāyī thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kāḷudāyīthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. maugharājatthairaapadānaṃ

64.

 ‘‘atthadassī  tu bhagavā, sayambhū aparājitau|

bhikkhusaṅghaparibyūḷhau, rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jatha||

65.

 ‘‘sissaihi samparivutau, gharamhā abhinikkhamiṃ|

nikkhamitvānahaṃ tattha, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

66.

 ‘‘abhivādiya sambuddhaṃ, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, santhaviṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

67.

 ‘‘yāvatā rūpinau sattā, arūpī vā asañ‍ñinau|

sabbai tai tava ñāṇamhi, antau haunti samaugadhā||

68.

 ‘‘sukhumacchikajālaina, udakaṃ yau parikkhipai|

yai kaici udakai pāṇā, antaujālai bhavanti tai||

69.

 ‘‘yaisañ‍ca caitanā atthi, rūpinau ca arūpinau|

sabbai tai tava ñāṇamhi, antau haunti samaugadhā||

70.

 ‘‘samuddharasimaṃ laukaṃ, andhakārasamākulaṃ|

tava dhammaṃ suṇitvāna, kaṅkhāsautaṃ taranti tai||

71.

 ‘‘avij‍jānivutai laukai, andhakāraina autthaṭai|

tava  ñāṇamhi jautantai, andhakārā padhaṃsitā||

72.

 ‘‘tuvaṃ cakkhūsi sabbaisaṃ, mahātamapanūdanau|

tava dhammaṃ suṇitvāna, nibbāyati bahuj‍janau||

73.

 ‘‘puṭakaṃ  pūrayitvāna pīṭharaṃ (sī॰), putaraṃ (syā॰), madhukhuddamanaiḷakaṃ|

ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, upanaisiṃ mahaisinau||

74.

 ‘‘paṭiggaṇhi mahāvīrau, sahatthaina mahā isī|

bhuñ‍jitvā tañ‍ca sabbañ‍ñū, vaihāsaṃ nabhamuggami||

75.

 ‘‘antalikkhai ṭhitau satthā, atthadassī narāsabhau|

mama cittaṃ pasādaintau, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

76.

 ‘‘‘yainidaṃ thavitaṃ ñāṇaṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhau ca thaumitau|

taina cittappasādaina, duggatiṃ sau na gacchati||

77.

 ‘‘‘catuddasañ‍cakkhattuṃ catusaṭṭhiñ‍ca (syā॰) sau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

pathabyā raj‍jaṃ aṭṭhasataṃ, vasudhaṃ āvasissati||

78.

 ‘‘‘pañ‍caiva  satakkhattuñ‍ca atha pañ‍casatakkhattuṃ (sī॰), cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ asaṅkhaiyyaṃ, mahiyā kārayissati||

79.

 ‘‘‘ajjhāyakau mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidāna pāragū|

gautamassa bhagavatau, sāsanai pabbajissati||

80.

 ‘‘‘gambhīraṃ nipuṇaṃ atthaṃ, ñāṇaina vicinissati|

maugharājāti nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

81.

 ‘‘‘tīhi  vij‍jāhi sampan‍naṃ, katakic‍camanāsavaṃ|

gautamau  satthavāhaggau, aitadaggai ṭhapaissati’||

82.

 ‘‘hitvā mānusakaṃ yaugaṃ, chaitvāna bhavabandhanaṃ|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

83.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā maugharājau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

maugharājatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. adhimuttatthairaapadānaṃ

84.

 ‘‘nibbutai laukanāthamhi, atthadassīnaruttamai|

upaṭṭhahiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

85.

 ‘‘nimantaitvā  bhikkhusaṅghaṃ saṃgharatanaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), ujubhūtaṃ samāhitaṃ|

ucchunā maṇḍapaṃ katvā, bhaujaisiṃ saṅghamuttamaṃ||

86.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

sabbai sattai abhibhaumi atibhaumi (sī॰ ka॰), puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

87.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ucchudānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

88.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā adhimuttau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

adhimuttatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. lasuṇadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

89.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, tāpasau āsahaṃ tadā|

lasuṇaṃ upajīvāmi, lasuṇaṃ mayhabhaujanaṃ||

90.

 ‘‘khāriyau  pūrayitvāna, saṅghārāmamagacchahaṃ|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, saṅghassa lasuṇaṃ adaṃ||

91.

 ‘‘vipassissa naraggassa, sāsanai niratassahaṃ|

saṅghassa lasuṇaṃ datvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

92.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, lasuṇaṃ yamadaṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, lasuṇassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

93.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā lasuṇadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

lasuṇadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. āyāgadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

94.

 ‘‘nibbutai  laukanāthamhi, sikhimhi vadataṃ varai|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, avandiṃ thūpamuttamaṃ||

95.

 ‘‘vaḍḍhakīhi  kathāpaitvā, mūlaṃ datvānahaṃ tadā|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, āyāgaṃ kārapaisahaṃ||

96.

 ‘‘aṭṭha kappāni daivaisu, abbaukiṇṇaṃ abbaucchin‍naṃ (sī॰) vasiṃ ahaṃ|

avasaisaisu kappaisu, vaukiṇṇaṃ saṃsariṃ ahaṃ||

97.

 ‘‘kāyai visaṃ na kamati, satthāni na ca hanti mai|

udakaihaṃ na miyyāmi, āyāgassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

98.

 ‘‘yadicchāmi ahaṃ vassaṃ, mahāmaighau pavassati|

daivāpi mai vasaṃ ainti, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

99.

 ‘‘sattaratanasampan‍nau, tisakkhattuṃ ahausahaṃ|

na maṃ kaicāvajānanti, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

100.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, āyāgaṃ yamakārayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, āyāgassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

101.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā āyāgadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

āyāgadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dhammacak‍kikatthairaapadānaṃ

102.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, sīhāsanassa sammukhā|

dhammacak‍kaṃ mai ṭhapitaṃ, sukataṃ viñ‍ñuvaṇṇitaṃ||

103.

 ‘‘cāruvaṇṇauva saubhāmi, sayauggabalavāhanau|

parivārainti maṃ nic‍caṃ, anuyantā bahuj‍janā||

104.

 ‘‘saṭṭhitūriyasahassaihi, paricāraimahaṃ sadā|

parivāraina saubhāmi, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

105.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ cak‍kaṃ ṭhapayiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhammacak‍kassidaṃ phalaṃ||

106.

 ‘‘itau aikādasai kappai, aṭṭhāsiṃsu janādhipā|

sahassarājanāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

107.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhammacak‍kikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

dhammacak‍kikatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. kapparukkhiyatthairaapadānaṃ

108.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa bhagavatau, thūpasaiṭṭhassa sammukhā|

vicittadussai lagaitvā laggaitvā (sī॰ syā॰), kapparukkhaṃ ṭhapaisahaṃ||

109.

 ‘‘yaṃ  yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

saubhayantau mama dvāraṃ, kapparukkhau patiṭṭhati||

110.

 ‘‘ahañ‍ca  parisā caiva, yai kaici mama vassitā nissitā (sī॰)|

tamhā dussaṃ gahaitvāna, nivāsaima mayaṃ sadā tadā (syā॰)||

111.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ rukkhaṃ ṭhapayiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kapparukkhassidaṃ phalaṃ||

112.

 ‘‘itau ca sattamai kappai, sucaiḷā aṭṭha khattiyā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

113.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kapparukkhiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kapparukkhiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

kuṇḍadhānavaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kuṇḍasāgatakac‍cānā, udāyī maugharājakau|

adhimuttau lasuṇadau, āyāgī dhammacak‍kikau|

kapparukkhī ca dasamau, gāthā dvayadasasataṃ gāthāyau dvādasasataṃ (sī॰)||


5. upālivaggau

1. bhāginaiyyupālitthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘khīṇāsavasahassaihi, parivutau paraitau (ka॰ aṭṭha) laukanāyakau|

vivaikamanuyuttau sau, gacchatai paṭisal‍lituṃ||

2.

 ‘‘ajinaina nivatthauhaṃ, tidaṇḍaparidhārakau|

bhikkhusaṅghaparibyūḷhaṃ, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘aikaṃsaṃ  ajinaṃ katvā, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

sambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā, santhaviṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘yathāṇḍajā ca saṃsaidā, aupapātī jalābujā|

kākādipakkhinau sabbai, antalikkhacarā sadā||

5.

 ‘‘yai kaici pāṇabhūtatthi, sañ‍ñinau vā asañ‍ñinau|

sabbai tai tava ñāṇamhi, antau haunti samaugadhā||

6.

 ‘‘gandhā ca pabbataiyyā yai, himavantanaguttamai|

sabbai tai tava sīlamhi, kalāyapi na yuj‍jarai||

7.

 ‘‘mauhandhakārapakkhandau, ayaṃ laukau sadaivakau|

tava ñāṇamhi jautantai, andhakārā vidhaṃsitā||

8.

 ‘‘yathā atthaṅgatai sūriyai, haunti sattā tamaugatā|

aivaṃ buddhai anuppan‍nai, hauti laukau tamaugatau||

9.

 ‘‘yathaudayantau ādic‍cau, vinaudaiti tamaṃ sadā|

tathaiva tvaṃ buddhasaiṭṭha, viddhaṃsaisi tamaṃ sadā||

10.

 ‘‘padhānapahitattausi, buddhau laukai sadaivakai|

tava kammābhiraddhaina, tausaisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

11.

 ‘‘taṃ sabbaṃ anumauditvā, padumuttarau mahāmuni|

nabhaṃ abbhuggamī dhīrau, haṃsarājāva ambarai||

12.

 ‘‘abbhuggantvāna  sambuddhau, mahaisi padumuttarau|

antalikkhai ṭhitau satthā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

13.

 ‘‘yainidaṃ  thavitaṃ ñāṇaṃ, aupammaihi samāyutaṃ|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

14.

 ‘‘‘aṭṭhārasañ‍ca khattuṃ sau, daivarājā bhavissati|

pathabyā raj‍jaṃ tisataṃ, vasudhaṃ āvasissati||

15.

 ‘‘‘pañ‍cavīsatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

17.

 ‘‘‘tusitā hi cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

hīnauva jātiyā santau, upāli nāma haissati||

18.

 ‘‘‘sau  pacchā pabbajitvāna, virājaitvāna pāpakaṃ|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

19.

 ‘‘‘tuṭṭhau ca gautamau buddhau, sakyaputtau mahāyasau|

vinayādhigataṃ tassa, aitadaggai ṭhapaissati’||

20.

 ‘‘saddhāyāhaṃ  pabbajitau, katakic‍cau anāsavau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

21.

 ‘‘bhagavā cānukampī maṃ, vinayaihaṃ visāradau|

sakakammābhiraddhau ca, viharāmi anāsavau||

22.

 ‘‘saṃvutau pātimaukkhamhi, indriyaisu ca pañ‍casu|

dhāraimi vinayaṃ sabbaṃ, kaivalaṃ ratanākaraṃ ratanaggharaṃ (ka॰)||

23.

 ‘‘mamañ‍ca guṇamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukai anuttarau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

24.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upālithairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

bhāginaiyyupālitthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. sauṇakauḷivisatthairaapadānaṃ

25.

 ‘‘anaumadassissa  muninau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

sudhāya laipanaṃ katvā, caṅkamaṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘nānāvaṇaihi pupphaihi, caṅkamaṃ santhariṃ ahaṃ|

ākāsai vitānaṃ katvā, bhaujayiṃ buddhamuttamaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘añ‍jaliṃ  paggahaitvāna, abhivādaitvāna subbataṃ pupphakaṃ (ka॰)|

dīghasālaṃ bhagavatau, niyyādaisimahaṃ tadā||

28.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukai anuttarau|

paṭiggahaisi bhagavā, anukampāya cakkhumā||

29.

 ‘‘paṭiggahaitvāna sambuddhau, dakkhiṇaiyyau sadaivakai|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

30.

 ‘‘‘yau sau haṭṭhaina cittaina, dīghasālaṃ adāsi akāsi (sī॰) mai|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

31.

 ‘‘‘imassa  mac‍cukālamhi, puñ‍ñakammasamaṅginau|

sahassayuttassarathau, upaṭṭhissati tāvadai||

32.

 ‘‘‘taina yānainayaṃ pausau, daivalaukaṃ gamissati|

anumaudissarai daivā, sampattai kusalabbhavai kusalai bhavai (sī॰ syā॰)||

33.

 ‘‘‘mahārahaṃ byamhaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, ratanamattikalaipanaṃ|

kūṭāgāravarūpaitaṃ, byamhaṃ ajjhāvasissati||

34.

 ‘‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati|

pañ‍cavīsati kappāni, daivarājā bhavissati||

35.

 ‘‘‘sattasattatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

yasaudharasanāmā yasaidharā samānā (sī॰) tai, sabbaipi aikanāmakā||

36.

 ‘‘‘dvai sampattī anubhautvā, vaḍḍhaitvā cinitvā (syā॰) puñ‍ñasañ‍cayaṃ|

aṭṭhavīsatikappamhi, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

37.

 ‘‘‘tatrāpi  byamhaṃ pavaraṃ, vissakammaina māpitaṃ|

dasasaddāvivittaṃ taṃ, puramajjhāvasissati||

38.

 ‘‘‘aparimaiyyai  itau kappai, bhūmipālau mahiddhikau|

auk‍kākau nāma nāmaina, rājā raṭṭhai bhavissati||

39.

 ‘‘‘sauḷasitthisahassānaṃ, sabbāsaṃ pavarā ca sā pavarāva yā (syā॰), pavarā piyā (?)|

abhijātā khattiyānī, nava puttai janaissati||

40.

 ‘‘‘nava puttai janaitvāna, khattiyānī marissati|

taruṇī ca piyā kañ‍ñā, mahaisittaṃ karissati||

41.

 ‘‘‘auk‍kākaṃ tausayitvāna, varaṃ kañ‍ñā labhissati|

varaṃ laddhāna sā kañ‍ñā, puttai pabbājayissati||

42.

 ‘‘‘pabbājitā ca tai sabbai, gamissanti naguttamaṃ|

jātibhaidabhayā sabbai, bhaginīhi vasissarai saṃvasissarai (sī॰)||

43.

 ‘‘‘aikā ca kañ‍ñā byādhīhi, bhavissati parikkhatā purakkhatā (syā॰ ka॰)|

mā nau jāti pabhij‍jīti, nikhaṇissanti khattiyā||

44.

 ‘‘‘khattiyau nīharitvāna, tāya saddhiṃ vasissati|

bhavissati tadā bhaidau, auk‍kākakulasambhavau||

45.

 ‘‘‘taisaṃ  pajā bhavissanti, kauḷiyā nāma jātiyā|

tattha mānusakaṃ bhaugaṃ, anubhaussatinappakaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘‘tamhā kāyā cavitvāna, daivalaukaṃ gamissati|

tatrāpi pavaraṃ byamhaṃ, labhissati manauramaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘‘daivalaukā  cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

āgantvāna manussattaṃ, sauṇau nāma bhavissati||

48.

 ‘‘‘āraddhavīriyau pahitattau, padahaṃ satthu sāsanai|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

49.

 ‘‘‘anantadassī bhagavā, gautamau sakyapuṅgavau|

visaisañ‍ñū mahāvīrau, aggaṭṭhānai ṭhapaissati’||

50.

 ‘‘vuṭṭhamhi  daivai caturaṅgulamhi, tiṇai anilairitaaṅgaṇamhi|

ṭhatvāna yaugassa payuttatādinau, tatauttariṃ pāramatā na vij‍jati||

51.

 ‘‘uttamai damathai dantau, cittaṃ mai supaṇīhitaṃ|

bhārau mai auhitau sabbau, nibbutaumhi anāsavau||

52.

 ‘‘aṅgīrasau  mahānāgau, abhijātauva kaisarī|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ||

53.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sauṇau kauḷivisau kauḷiyavaissau (sī॰ syā॰), kauṭikaṇṇau (ka॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sauṇakauḷivisatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. kāḷigaudhāputtabhaddiyatthairaapadānaṃ

54.

 ‘‘padumuttarasambuddhaṃ, maittacittaṃ mahāmuniṃ|

upaiti janatā sabbā, sabbalaukagganāyakaṃ||

55.

 ‘‘sattukañ‍ca baddhakañ‍ca vatthaṃ saināsanañ‍caiva (sī॰), sattukañ‍ca padakañ‍ca (sī॰ aṭṭha॰), sattukañ‍ca pavākañ‍ca (syā॰), āmisaṃ pānabhaujanaṃ|

dadanti satthunau sabbai, puñ‍ñakkhaittai anuttarai||

56.

 ‘‘ahampi  dānaṃ dassāmi, daivadaivassa tādinau|

buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ nimantaitvā, saṅghampi ca anuttaraṃ||

57.

 ‘‘uyyaujitā mayā caitai, nimantaisuṃ tathāgataṃ|

kaivalaṃ bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca, puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ anuttaraṃ||

58.

 ‘‘satasahassapal‍laṅkaṃ, sauvaṇṇaṃ gaunakatthataṃ|

tūlikāpaṭalikāya, khaumakappāsikaihi ca|

mahārahaṃ pañ‍ñāpayiṃ, āsanaṃ buddhayuttakaṃ||

59.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  laukavidū, daivadaivau narāsabhau|

bhikkhusaṅghaparibyūḷhau, mama dvāramupāgami||

60.

 ‘‘pac‍cuggantvāna sambuddhaṃ, laukanāthaṃ yasassinaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, abhināmayiṃ saṅgharaṃ sakaṃ gharaṃ (sī॰)||

61.

 ‘‘bhikkhūnaṃ satasahassaṃ, buddhañ‍ca laukanāyakaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, paraman‍naina tappayiṃ||

62.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

bhikkhusaṅghai  nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

63.

 ‘‘‘yainidaṃ āsanaṃ din‍naṃ, sauvaṇṇaṃ gaunakatthataṃ|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

64.

 ‘‘‘catusattatikkhattuṃ  sau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

anubhaussati sampattiṃ, accharāhi purakkhatau||

65.

 ‘‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ sahassaṃ, vasudhaṃ āvasissati|

aikapañ‍ñāsakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

66.

 ‘‘‘sabbāsu bhavayaunīsu, uc‍cākulī uc‍cākulai (ka॰) bhavissati|

sau ca pacchā pabbajitvā, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

bhaddiyau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

67.

 ‘‘‘vivaikamanuyuttaumhi, pantasainanivāsahaṃ|

phalañ‍cādhigataṃ sabbaṃ, cattak‍laisaumhi aj‍jahaṃ||

68.

 ‘‘‘mama sabbaṃ kammaṃ (?) abhiñ‍ñāya, sabbañ‍ñū laukanāyakau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaisi maṃ’||

69.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā bhaddiyau kāḷigaudhāya puttau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

bhaddiyassa kāḷigaudhāya puttatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. san‍niṭṭhāpakatthairaapadānaṃ

70.

 ‘‘arañ‍ñai  kuṭikaṃ katvā, vasāmi pabbatantarai|

lābhālābhaina santuṭṭhau, yasaina ayasaina ca||

71.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

vasīsatasahassaihi bhikkhusatasahassaihi (syā॰), āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

72.

 ‘‘upāgataṃ mahānāgaṃ mahāvīraṃ (sī॰), jalajuttamanāmakaṃ|

tiṇasantharaṃ tiṇattharaṃ (ka॰) pañ‍ñāpaitvā, adāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

73.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, āmaṇḍaṃ pānīyañ‍cahaṃ|

adāsiṃ ujubhūtassa, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

74.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai satasahassai itau kappai (sī॰), yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, āmaṇḍassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

75.

 ‘‘aikatālīsakappamhi, aikau āsiṃ arindamau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

76.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā san‍niṭṭhāpakau san‍nidhāpakau (sī॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

san‍niṭṭhāpakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. pañ‍cahatthiyatthairaapadānaṃ

77.

 ‘‘sumaidhau  nāma sambuddhau, gacchatai antarāpaṇai|

aukkhittacakkhu khittacakkhu (ka॰ sī॰ ka॰) mitabhāṇī, satimā saṃvutindriyau||

78.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca uppalahatthāni, āvaiḷatthaṃ ahaṃsu mai|

taina buddhaṃ apūjaisiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

79.

 ‘‘āraupitā  ca tai pupphā, chadanaṃ assu satthunau|

samādhiṃsu saṃsāviṃsu (sī॰) mahānāgaṃ, sissā ācariyaṃ yathā||

80.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

81.

 ‘‘itau vīsakappasatai, ahaisuṃ pañ‍ca khattiyā|

hatthiyā nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

82.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pañ‍cahatthiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pañ‍cahatthiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. padumacchadaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

83.

 ‘‘nibbutai laukanāthamhi, vipassimhaggapuggalai|

suphul‍lapadumaṃ gayha, citamāraupayiṃ ahaṃ||

84.

 ‘‘āraupitai  ca citakai, vaihāsaṃ nabhamuggami|

ākāsai chadanaṃ ākāsacchadanaṃ (sī॰) katvā, citakamhi adhārayi||

85.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

86.

 ‘‘sattatālīsitau  kappai, padumissaranāmakau|

cāturantau vijitāvī, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

87.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumacchadaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

padumacchadaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sayanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

88.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa bhagavatau, maittacittassa tādinau|

sayanaggaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, dussabhaṇḍaihi dussabhaṇḍaina (syā॰) atthataṃ||

89.

 ‘‘paṭiggahaisi  bhagavā, kappiyaṃ sayanāsanaṃ|

uṭṭhāya sayanā āsanā (sī॰) tamhā, vaihāsaṃ uggamī jinau||

90.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ sayanamadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sayanassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

91.

 ‘‘aikapañ‍ñāsitau  kappai, varakau varuṇau (sī॰ syā॰) daivasavhayau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

92.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sayanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sayanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. caṅkamanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

93.

 ‘‘atthadassissa muninau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

iṭṭhakāhi cinitvāna, caṅkamaṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ||

94.

 ‘‘uc‍catau pañ‍caratanaṃ, caṅkamaṃ sādhumāpitaṃ|

āyāmatau hatthasataṃ, bhāvanīyyaṃ manauramaṃ||

95.

 ‘‘paṭiggahaisi bhagavā, atthadassī naruttamau|

hatthaina pulinaṃ gayha, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

96.

 ‘‘‘iminā  pulinadānaina, caṅkamaṃ sukataina ca|

sattaratanasampan‍naṃ, pulinaṃ anubhaussati||

97.

 ‘‘‘tīṇi kappāni daivaisu, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

anubhaussati sampattiṃ, accharāhi purakkhatau||

98.

 ‘‘‘manussalaukamāgantvā, rājā raṭṭhai bhavissati|

tikkhattuṃ cak‍kavattī ca, mahiyā sau bhavissati’||

99.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, caṅkamassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

100.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā caṅkamanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

caṅkamanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. subhaddatthairaapadānaṃ

101.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

janataṃ uddharitvāna, nibbāyati mahāyasau||

102.

 ‘‘nibbāyantai ca sambuddhai, dasasahassi kampatha|

janakāyau mahā āsi, daivā san‍nipatuṃ tadā||

103.

 ‘‘candanaṃ pūrayitvāna, tagarāmal‍likāhi ca|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, āraupayiṃ ālaipaisiṃ (sī॰), āraupaisiṃ (syā॰) naruttamaṃ||

104.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukai anuttarau|

nipan‍nakauva sambuddhau, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

105.

 ‘‘‘yau  mai pacchimakai kālai, gandhamālaina gandhamal‍laina (syā॰ ka॰) napuṃsakaikattaṃ manasikātabbaṃ chādayi|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

106.

 ‘‘‘itau cutau ayaṃ pausau, tusitakāyaṃ gamissati|

tattha raj‍jaṃ karitvāna, nimmānaṃ sau gamissati||

107.

 ‘‘‘aitainaiva upāyaina, datvā mālaṃ malyaṃ (sī॰), mal‍laṃ (syā॰ ka॰) varuttamaṃ|

sakakammābhiraddhau sau, sampattiṃ anubhaussati||

108.

 ‘‘‘punāpi  tusitai kāyai, nibbattissatiyaṃ narau|

tamhā kāyā cavitvāna, manussattaṃ gamissati||

109.

 ‘‘‘sakyaputtau mahānāgau, aggau laukai sadaivakai|

baudhayitvā bahū sattai, nibbāyissati cakkhumā||

110.

 ‘‘‘tadā  saupagatau santau, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ, pañhaṃ pucchissati tadā||

111.

 ‘‘‘hāsayitvāna sambuddhau, sabbañ‍ñū laukanāyakau|

puñ‍ñakammaṃ pariñ‍ñāya, sac‍cāni vivarissati||

112.

 ‘‘‘āraddhau ca ayaṃ pañhau, tuṭṭhau aikaggamānasau|

satthāraṃ abhivādaitvā, pabbaj‍jaṃ yācayissati||

113.

 ‘‘‘pasan‍namānasaṃ disvā, sakakammaina tausitaṃ|

pabbājaissati sau buddhau, aggamaggassa kauvidau||

114.

 ‘‘‘vāyamitvānayaṃ pausau, sammāsambuddhasāsanai|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau’||

pañ‍camabhāṇavāraṃ|

115.

 ‘‘pubbakammaina  saṃyuttau, aikaggau susamāhitau|

buddhassa aurasau puttau, dhammajaumhi sunimmitau||

116.

 ‘‘dhammarājaṃ upagamma, apucchiṃ pañhamuttamaṃ|

kathayantau ca mai pañhaṃ, dhammasautaṃ upānayi||

117.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ dhammamañ‍ñāya, vihāsiṃ sāsanai ratau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

118.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, jalajuttamanāyakau|

nibbāyi anupādānau, dīpauva tailasaṅkhayā||

119.

 ‘‘sattayaujanikaṃ āsi, thūpañ‍ca ratanāmayaṃ|

dhajaṃ tattha apūjaisiṃ, sabbabhaddaṃ manauramaṃ||

120.

 ‘‘kassapassa ca buddhassa, tissau nāmaggasāvakau|

puttau mai aurasau āsi, dāyādau jinasāsanai||

121.

 ‘‘tassa hīnaina manasā, vācaṃ bhāsiṃ abhaddakaṃ|

taina kammavipākaina, pacchā mai āsi bhaddakaṃ pacchimai addasaṃ jinaṃ (sī॰)||

122.

 ‘‘upavattanai  sālavanai, pacchimai sayanai muni|

pabbājaisi mahāvīrau, hitau kāruṇikau jinau||

123.

 ‘‘aj‍jaiva  dāni pabbaj‍jā, aj‍jaiva upasampadā|

aj‍jaiva parinibbānaṃ, sammukhā dvipaduttamai||

124.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā subhaddau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

subhaddatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. cundatthairaapadānaṃ

125.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

agghiyaṃ kārayitvāna, jātipupphaihi chādayiṃ||

126.

 ‘‘niṭṭhāpaitvāna taṃ pupphaṃ, buddhassa upanāmayiṃ|

pupphāvasaisaṃ paggayha, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

127.

 ‘‘kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ, buddhaṃ laukagganāyakaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, pupphagghiyamupānayiṃ||

128.

 ‘‘vitiṇṇakaṅkhau sambuddhau, tiṇṇaughaihi purakkhatau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

129.

 ‘‘‘dibbagandhaṃ pavāyantaṃ, yau mai pupphagghiyaṃ adā|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

130.

 ‘‘‘itau cutau ayaṃ pausau, daivasaṅghapurakkhatau|

jātipupphaihi parikiṇṇau, daivalaukaṃ gamissati||

131.

 ‘‘‘ubbiddhaṃ bhavanaṃ tassa, sauvaṇṇañ‍ca maṇīmayaṃ|

byamhaṃ pātubhavissati, puñ‍ñakammappabhāvitaṃ||

132.

 ‘‘‘catusattatikkhattuṃ  sau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

anubhaussati sampattiṃ, accharāhi purakkhatau||

133.

 ‘‘‘pathabyā raj‍jaṃ tisataṃ, vasudhaṃ āvasissati|

pañ‍casattatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

134.

 ‘‘‘duj‍jayau  nāma nāmaina, haissati manujādhipau|

anubhautvāna taṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, sakakammaṃ apassitau sakakammūpasaṃhitau (syā॰)||

135.

 ‘‘‘vinipātaṃ  agantvāna, manussattaṃ gamissati|

hirañ‍ñaṃ tassa hirañ‍ñassa ca (sī॰ ka॰) nicitaṃ, kauṭisatamanappakaṃ||

136.

 ‘‘‘nibbattissati yaunimhi, brāhmaṇai sau bhavissati|

vaṅgantassa sutau dhīmā, sāriyā aurasau piyau||

137.

 ‘‘‘sau ca pacchā pabbajitvā, aṅgīrasassa sāsanai|

cūḷacundauti cūlacundauti (sī॰) nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau||

138.

 ‘‘‘sāmaṇairauva sau santau, khīṇāsavau bhavissati|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau’||

139.

 ‘‘upaṭṭhahiṃ mahāvīraṃ, añ‍ñai ca paisalai bahū|

bhātaraṃ mai cupaṭṭhāsiṃ, uttamatthassa pattiyā||

140.

 ‘‘bhātaraṃ mai upaṭṭhitvā, dhātuṃ pattamhi auhiya aupiya (sī॰), auciya (syā॰)|

sambuddhaṃ upanāmaisiṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

141.

 ‘‘ubhau  hatthaihi paggayha, buddhau laukai sadaivakai|

sandassayantau taṃ dhātuṃ, kittayi aggasāvakaṃ||

142.

 ‘‘cittañ‍ca  suvimuttaṃ mai, saddhā mayhaṃ patiṭṭhitā|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

143.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidānuppattā, vimaukkhāpi ca phassitā paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai (syā॰)|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā cundau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

cundatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

upālivaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

upāli sauṇau bhaddiyau, san‍niṭṭhāpakahatthiyau|

chadanaṃ saiyyacaṅkamaṃ, subhaddau cundasavhayau|

gāthāsataṃ satālīsaṃ ca tālīsaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), catassau ca taduttari||


6. bījanivaggau

1. vidhūpanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

bījanikā vījanikā (sī॰ syā॰) mayā din‍nā, dvipadindassa tādinau||

2.

 ‘‘sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, paggahaitvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

sambuddhamabhivādaitvā, pak‍kamiṃ uttarāmukhau||

3.

 ‘‘bījaniṃ  paggahaitvāna, satthā laukagganāyakau laukai anuttarau (sī॰)|

bhikkhusaṅghai ṭhitau santau, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

4.

 ‘‘‘iminā bījanidānaina, cittassa paṇidhīhi caitanāpaṇidhīhi (añ‍ñattha) ca|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, vinipātaṃ na gacchati’||

5.

 ‘‘āraddhavīriyau pahitattau, caitauguṇasamāhitau|

jātiyā sattavassauhaṃ, arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ||

6.

 ‘‘saṭṭhikappasahassamhi, bījamānasanāmakā|

sauḷasāsiṃsu rājānau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

7.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vidhūpanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

vidhūpanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. sataraṃsitthairaapadānaṃ

8.

 ‘‘ubbiddhaṃ sailamāruyha, nisīdi purisuttamau|

pabbatassāvidūramhi, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū||

9.

 ‘‘upaviṭṭhaṃ  mahāvīraṃ, daivadaivaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, santhaviṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘‘aisa  buddhau mahāvīrau, varadhammappakāsakau|

jalati aggikhandhauva, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhatau||

11.

 ‘‘‘mahāsamuddauva‘kkhubbhau ’kkhaubhau (sī॰ syā॰), aṇṇavauva duruttarau|

migarājāvasambhītau chambhitau (ka॰), dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā’||

12.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, padumuttaranāyakau|

bhikkhusaṅghai ṭhitau satthā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

13.

 ‘‘‘yaināyaṃ yaināhaṃ (ka॰) añ‍jalī din‍nau, buddhasaiṭṭhau ca thaumitau|

tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati||

14.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, aṅgīrasasanāmakau|

vivaṭṭacchadau vivatthacchaddau (sī॰) sambuddhau, uppaj‍jissati tāvadai||

15.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

sataraṃsīti nāmaina, arahā sau bhavissati’||

16.

 ‘‘jātiyā sattavassauhaṃ, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

sataraṃsimhi nāmaina, pabhā niddhāvatai mama||

17.

 ‘‘maṇḍapai rukkhamūlai vā, jhāyī jhānaratau ahaṃ|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

18.

 ‘‘saṭṭhikappasahassamhi, caturau rāmanāmakā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

19.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā sataraṃsi thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sataraṃsitthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. sayanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

20.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, sabbalaukānukampinau|

sayanaṃ tassa pādāsiṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

21.

 ‘‘taina  sayanadānaina, sukhaittai bījasampadā|

bhaugā nibbattarai tassa, sayanassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘ākāsai saiyyaṃ kappaimi, dhāraimi pathaviṃ imaṃ|

pāṇaisu mai issariyaṃ, sayanassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘pañ‍cakappasahassamhi, aṭṭha āsuṃ mahātaijā mahāvarā (sī॰), mahāvīrā (syā॰)|

catuttiṃsai kappasatai, caturau ca mahabbalā||

24.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sayanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sayanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. gandhaudakiyatthairaapadānaṃ

25.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, mahābaudhimahau ahu|

vicittaṃ ghaṭamādāya, gandhaudakamadāsahaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘nhānakālai ca baudhiyā, mahāmaighau pavassatha|

nin‍nādau ca mahā āsi, asaniyā phalantiyā||

27.

 ‘‘tainaivāsanivaigaina, tattha kālaṅkatau kālakatau (sī॰ syā॰) ahaṃ ahuṃ (sī॰)|

daivalaukai ṭhitau santau, imā gāthā abhāsahaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘‘ahau  buddhau ahau dhammau, ahau nau satthusampadā|

kaḷaivaraṃ kalaibaraṃ (sī॰) mai patitaṃ, daivalaukai ramāmahaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘‘ubbiddhaṃ bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ, satabhūmaṃ samuggataṃ|

kañ‍ñāsatasahassāni, parivārainti maṃ sadā||

30.

 ‘‘‘ābādhā mai na vij‍janti, saukau mayhaṃ na vij‍jati|

pariḷāhaṃ na passāmi, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘‘aṭṭhavīsai kappasatai, rājā saṃvasitau ahuṃ|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau’||

32.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhaudakiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

gandhaudakiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. aupavayhatthairaapadānaṃ

33.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, ājānīyamadāsahaṃ|

niyyādaitvāna sambuddhai sambuddhaṃ (sī॰ ka॰), agamāsiṃ sakaṃ gharaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘daivalau nāma nāmaina, satthunau aggasāvakau|

varadhammassa dāyādau, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘sapattabhārau bhagavā, ājānaiyyau na kappati|

tava saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, adhivāsaisi cakkhumā||

36.

 ‘‘agghāpaitvā vātajavaṃ, sindhavaṃ sīghavāhanaṃ|

padumuttarabuddhassa, khamanīyamadāsahaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ daivai ca mānusai bhavai (sī॰ ka॰)|

khamanīyaṃ vātajavaṃ, cittaṃ nibbattatai ājānīyā vātajavā, vitti nibbattarai (syā॰), khamanīyā vātajavā, cittā nibbattarai (sī॰) mama||

38.

 ‘‘lābhaṃ  taisaṃ suladdhaṃva, yai labhantupasampadaṃ|

punapi payirupāsaiyyaṃ, buddhau laukai sacai bhavai||

39.

 ‘‘aṭṭhavīsatikkhattuṃhaṃ, rājā āsiṃ mahabbalau|

cāturantau vijitāvī, jambusaṇḍassa jambudīpassa (syā॰), jambumaṇḍassa (ka॰) jambuissarau||

40.

 ‘‘idaṃ pacchimakaṃ mayhaṃ, carimau vattatai bhavau|

pattausmi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, hitvā jayaparājayaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘catutiṃsasahassamhi, mahātaijausi khattiyau|

sataratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

42.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aupavayhau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aupavayhatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. saparivārāsanatthairaapadānaṃ

43.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, piṇḍapātaṃ adāsahaṃ|

gantvā kiliṭṭhakaṃ ṭhānaṃ taṃ bhaujanaṭṭhānaṃ (sī॰), mal‍likāhi parikkhitaṃ parikkhipiṃ (sī॰)||

44.

 ‘‘tamhāsanamhi  āsīnau, buddhau laukagganāyakau|

akittayi piṇḍapātaṃ, ujubhūtau samāhitau||

45.

 ‘‘yathāpi bhaddakai khaittai, bījaṃ appampi raupitaṃ|

sammā dhāraṃ pavaicchantai, phalaṃ tausaiti kassakaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘tathaivāyaṃ piṇḍapātau, sukhaittai raupitau tayā|

bhavai nibbattamānamhi, phalaṃ tai nibbattamānaṃ hi, phalatau (sī॰) tausayissati tappayissati (ka॰)||

47.

 ‘‘idaṃ vatvāna sambuddhau, jalajuttamanāmakau|

piṇḍapātaṃ gahaitvāna, pak‍kāmi uttarāmukhau||

48.

 ‘‘saṃvutau pātimaukkhasmiṃ, indriyaisu ca pañ‍casu|

pavivaikamanuyuttau, viharāmi anāsavau||

49.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saparivārāsanau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saparivārāsanatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. pañ‍cadīpakatthairaapadānaṃ

50.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, sabbabhūtānukampinau|

saddahitvāna susaṇṭhahitvā (sī॰) saddhammai, ujudiṭṭhi ahausahaṃ||

51.

 ‘‘padīpadānaṃ  pādāsiṃ, parivāraitvāna baudhiyaṃ|

saddahantau padīpāni, akariṃ tāvadai ahaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

ākāsai uk‍kaṃ dhārainti, dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘tiraukuṭṭaṃ tirausailaṃ, samatiggayha pabbataṃ|

samantā yaujanasataṃ, dassanaṃ anubhaumahaṃ||

54.

 ‘‘taina kammāvasaisaina, pattaumhi āsavakkhayaṃ|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, dvipadindassa sāsanai||

55.

 ‘‘catuttiṃsai kappasatai, satacakkhusanāmakā|

rājāhaisuṃ mahātaijā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

56.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pañ‍cadīpakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pañ‍cadīpakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. dhajadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

57.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, baudhiyā pādaputtamai|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, dhajamāraupayiṃ ahaṃ||

58.

 ‘‘patitapattāni gaṇhitvā, bahiddhā chaḍḍayiṃ ahaṃ|

antausuddhaṃ bahisuddhaṃ, adhimuttamanāsavaṃ||

59.

 ‘‘sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ, avandiṃ baudhimuttamaṃ|

padumuttarau  laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau||

60.

 ‘‘bhikkhusaṅghai ṭhitau satthā, imā gāthā abhāsatha|

‘‘‘iminā dhajadānaina, upaṭṭhānaina cūbhayaṃ||

61.

 ‘‘‘kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ sau na gacchati|

daivaisu daivasaubhagyaṃ, anubhaussatinappakaṃ||

62.

 ‘‘‘anaikasatakkhattuñ‍ca, rājā raṭṭhai bhavissati|

uggatau nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

63.

 ‘‘‘sampattiṃ  anubhautvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

gautamassa bhagavatau, sāsanaibhiramissati’||

64.

 ‘‘padhānapahitattaumhi, upasantau nirūpadhi|
