dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

65.

 ‘‘aikapañ‍ñāsasahassai, kappai uggatasavhayau savhayā (syā॰)|

pañ‍ñāsasatasahassai, khattiyau maighasavhayau khattiyā khaimasavhayā (syā॰)||

66.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhajadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

dhajadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. padumatthairaapadānaṃ

67.

 ‘‘catusac‍caṃ  pakāsaintau, varadhammappavattakau|

vassatai vassaiti (?) amataṃ vuṭṭhiṃ, nibbāpaintau mahājanaṃ||

68.

 ‘‘sadhajaṃ sadaṇḍaṃ (sī॰) padumaṃ gayha, aḍḍhakausai ṭhitau ahaṃ|

padumuttaramunissa, pahaṭṭhau ukkhipimambarai||

69.

 ‘‘āgacchantai ca padumai, abbhutau āsi tāvadai|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, paggaṇhi vadataṃ varau||

70.

 ‘‘karasaiṭṭhaina  paggayha, jalajaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ|

bhikkhusaṅghai ṭhitau satthā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

71.

 ‘‘‘yainidaṃ padumaṃ khittaṃ, sabbañ‍ñumhi vināyakai sabbañ‍ñutamanāyakai (syā॰ ka॰)|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

72.

 ‘‘‘tiṃsakappāni  daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

pathabyā raj‍jaṃ sattasataṃ, vasudhaṃ āvasissati||

73.

 ‘‘‘tattha pattaṃ gaṇaitvāna, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

ākāsatau pupphavuṭṭhi, abhivassissatī tadā||

74.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma nāmaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

75.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau’||

76.

 ‘‘nikkhamitvāna kucchimhā, sampajānau patissatau|

jātiyā pañ‍cavassauhaṃ, arahattaṃ apāpuṇiṃ||

77.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

padumatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. asanabaudhiyatthairaapadānaṃ

78.

 ‘‘jātiyā  sattavassauhaṃ, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, upagacchiṃ naruttamaṃ||

79.

 ‘‘tissassāhaṃ bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, raupayiṃ baudhimuttamaṃ||

80.

 ‘‘asanau nāmadhaiyyaina, dharaṇīruhapādapau|

pañ‍cavassai paricariṃ, asanaṃ baudhimuttamaṃ||

81.

 ‘‘pupphitaṃ pādapaṃ disvā, abbhutaṃ laumahaṃsanaṃ|

sakaṃ kammaṃ pakittaintau, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ upāgamiṃ||

82.

 ‘‘tissau tadā sau sambuddhau, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

83.

 ‘‘‘yaināyaṃ  raupitā baudhi, buddhapūjā ca sak‍katā|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

84.

 ‘‘‘tiṃsakappāni daivaisu, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

catusaṭṭhi cakkhattuṃ sau, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

85.

 ‘‘‘tusitā  hi cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

dvai sampattī anubhautvā, manussattai ramissati||

86.

 ‘‘‘padhānapahitattau sau, upasantau nirūpadhi|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau’||

87.

 ‘‘vivaikamanuyuttauhaṃ, upasantau nirūpadhi|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

88.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, baudhiṃ raupaisahaṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, baudhiraupassidaṃ phalaṃ||

89.

 ‘‘catusattatitau kappai, daṇḍasainauti vissutau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī tadā ahuṃ||

90.

 ‘‘taisattatimhitau kappai, sattāhaisuṃ mahīpatī|

samantanaimināmaina, rājānau cak‍kavattinau||

91.

 ‘‘paṇṇavīsatitau  kappai, puṇṇakau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

92.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā asanabaudhiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

asanabaudhiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

bījanivaggau chaṭṭhau|


tassuddānaṃ –

bījanī sataraṃsī ca, sayanaudakivāhiyau|

parivārau padīpañ‍ca, dhajau padumapūjakau|

baudhi ca dasamau vuttau, gāthā dvainavuti tathā||


7. sakacintaniyavaggau

1. sakacintaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘pavanaṃ  kānanaṃ disvā, appasaddaman‍nāvilaṃ|

isīnaṃ anuciṇṇaṃva, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘thūpaṃ katvāna pulinaṃ vaiḷunā (aṭṭha॰), vaiḷinaṃ (syā॰), nānāpupphaṃ samaukiriṃ|

sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ, nimmitaṃ abhivandahaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘sattaratanasampan‍nau, rājā raṭṭhamhi issarau|

sakakammābhiraddhauhaṃ, pupphapūjāyidaṃ thūpapūjāyidaṃ (sī॰) phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphapūjāyidaṃ thūpapūjāyidaṃ (sī॰) phalaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘asītikappainantayasau, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, catudīpamhi issarau||

6.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sakacintaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sakacintaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. avaupupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

7.

 ‘‘vihārā abhinikkhamma, abbhuṭṭhahiya abbhuṭṭhāsi ca (syā॰ ka॰) caṅkamai|

catusac‍caṃ pakāsantau, daisaiti daisaintau (syā॰ ka॰) amataṃ padaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘sikhissa  giramañ‍ñāya, buddhasaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

nānāpupphaṃ gahaitvāna, ākāsamhi samaukiriṃ||

9.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina dvipadinda, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha|

pattaumhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, hitvā jayaparājayaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘itau vīsatikappamhi, sumaidhau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā avaupupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

avaupupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. pac‍cāgamaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘sindhuyā  nadiyā tīrai, cak‍kavākau ahaṃ tadā|

suddhasaivālabhakkhauhaṃ, pāpaisu ca susañ‍ñatau||

14.

 ‘‘addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai|

tuṇḍaina sālaṃ paggayha, vipassissābhiraupayiṃ||

15.

 ‘‘yassa  saddhā tathāgatai, acalā supatiṭṭhitā|

taina cittappasādaina, duggatiṃ sau na gacchati||

16.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, buddhasaiṭṭhassa santikai|

vihaṅgamaina santaina, subījaṃ raupitaṃ mayā||

17.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ pupphapūjāyidaṃ (sī॰) phalaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘sucārudassanā nāma, aṭṭhaitai aikanāmakā|

kappai sattarasai āsuṃ, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

19.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pac‍cāgamaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pac‍cāgamaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. parappasādakatthairaapadānaṃ

20.

 ‘‘usabhaṃ  pavaraṃ vīraṃ, mahaisiṃ vijitāvinaṃ|

suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, kau disvā nappasīdati||

21.

 ‘‘himavāvāparimaiyyau, sāgarauva duruttarau|

tathaiva jhānaṃ buddhassa, kau disvā nappasīdati||

22.

 ‘‘vasudhā yathāppamaiyyā, cittā vanavaṭaṃsakā|

tathaiva  sīlaṃ buddhassa, kau disvā nappasīdati||

23.

 ‘‘anilañ‍jasāsaṅkhubbhau anilajauva asaṅkhaubhau (sī॰), yathākāsau asaṅkhiyau|

tathaiva ñāṇaṃ buddhassa, kau disvā nappasīdati||

24.

 ‘‘imāhi  catugāthāhi, brāhmaṇau sainasavhayau|

buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ thavitvāna, siddhatthaṃ aparājitaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘catun‍navutikappāni, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jatha|

sugatiṃ sukhasampattiṃ sugatīsu susumpattiṃ (sī॰ syā॰), anubhausimanappakaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, thavitvā laukanāyakaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, thaumanāya thaumanassa (syā॰) idaṃ phalaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘cātuddasamhi kappamhi, caturau āsumuggatā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

28.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā parappasādakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

parappasādakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. bhisadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

29.

 ‘‘vaissabhū nāma nāmaina, isīnaṃ tatiyau ahu|

kānanaṃ vanamaugayha, vihāsi purisuttamau||

30.

 ‘‘bhisamuḷālaṃ  gaṇhitvā, agamaṃ buddhasantikaṃ|

tañ‍ca buddhassa pādāsiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

31.

 ‘‘karaina ca parāmaṭṭhau, vaissabhūvarabuddhinā|

sukhāhaṃ nābhijānāmi, samaṃ taina kutauttariṃ||

32.

 ‘‘carimau vattatai mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

hatthināgaina santaina, kusalaṃ raupitaṃ mayā nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau (syā॰)||

33.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhisadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘samaudhānā ca rājānau, sauḷasa manujādhipā|

kappamhi cuddasai tairasai (sī॰ syā॰) āsuṃ, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

35.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhisadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

bhisadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sucintitatthairaapadānaṃ

36.

 ‘‘giriduggacarau  āsiṃ, abhijātauva kaisarī|

migasaṅghaṃ vadhitvāna, jīvāmi pabbatantarai||

37.

 ‘‘atthadassī  tu bhagavā, sabbañ‍ñū vadataṃ varau|

mamuddharitukāmau sau, āgacchi pabbatuttamaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘pasadañ‍ca migaṃ hantvā, bhakkhituṃ samupāgamiṃ|

bhagavā tamhi samayai, bhikkhamānau sikkhācārau (syā॰) upāgami||

39.

 ‘‘varamaṃsāni paggayha, adāsiṃ tassa satthunau|

anumaudi mahāvīrau, nibbāpaintau mamaṃ tadā||

40.

 ‘‘taina  cittappasādaina, giriduggaṃ pavisiṃ ahaṃ|

pītiṃ uppādayitvāna, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘aitaina  maṃsadānaina, cittassa paṇidhīhi ca|

pan‍narasai kappasatai, daivalaukai ramiṃ ahaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘avasaisaisu kappaisu, kusalaṃ cintitaṃ nicitaṃ (sī॰), karitaṃ (syā॰) mayā|

tainaiva maṃsadānaina, buddhānussaraṇaina ca||

43.

 ‘‘aṭṭhattiṃsamhi kappamhi, aṭṭha dīghāyunāmakā|

saṭṭhimhitau kappasatai, duvai varuṇanāmakā saraṇanāmakā (syā॰)||

44.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sucintitau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sucintitatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vatthadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

45.

 ‘‘pakkhijātau  tadā āsiṃ, supaṇṇau garuḷādhipau|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ gandhamādanaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘jahitvā garuḷavaṇṇaṃ, māṇavakaṃ adhārayiṃ|

aikaṃ vatthaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, dvipadindassa tādinau||

47.

 ‘‘tañ‍ca dussaṃ paṭiggayha, buddhau laukagganāyakau|

antalikkhai ṭhitau satthā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

48.

 ‘‘‘iminā vatthadānaina, cittassa paṇidhīhi ca|

pahāya garuḷaṃ yauniṃ, daivalaukai ramissati’||

49.

 ‘‘atthadassī tu bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

vatthadānaṃ pasaṃsitvā, pak‍kāmi uttarāmukhau||

50.

 ‘‘bhavai nibbattamānamhi, haunti mai vatthasampadā|

ākāsai chadanaṃ hauti, vatthadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

51.

 ‘‘aruṇavā  aruṇakā (sī॰), aruṇasā (syā॰) satta janā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā|

chattiṃsatimhi āsiṃsu, kappamhi manujādhipā||

52.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vatthadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

vatthadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. ambadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

53.

 ‘‘anaumadassī  bhagavā, nisin‍nau pabbatantarai|

maittāya aphari laukai, appamāṇai nirūpadhi||

54.

 ‘‘kapi ahaṃ tadā āsiṃ, himavantai naguttamai|

disvā anaumadassiṃ taṃ anaumaṃ amitaṃ (sī॰), anaumamadhitaṃ (syā॰), buddhai cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

55.

 ‘‘avidūrai himavantassa, ambāsuṃ phalinau tadā|

tatau pak‍kaṃ gahaitvāna, ambaṃ samadhukaṃ adaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘taṃ  mai buddhau viyākāsi, anaumadassī mahāmuni|

iminā madhudānaina, ambadānaina cūbhayaṃ||

57.

 ‘‘sattapañ‍ñāsakappamhi, daivalaukai ramissati|

avasaisaisu kappaisu, vaukiṇṇaṃ saṃsarissati||

58.

 ‘‘khaipaitvā pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, paripak‍kāya buddhiyā|

vinipātamagantvāna, kilaisai jhāpayissati||

59.

 ‘‘damaina uttamaināhaṃ, damitaumhi mahaisinā|

pattaumhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, hitvā jayaparājayaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘sattasattatikappasatai, ambaṭṭhajasanāmakā|

catuddasa tai rājānau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

61.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā ambadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ambadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sumanatthairaapadānaṃ

62.

 ‘‘sumanau  nāma nāmaina, mālākārau ahaṃ tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, laukāhutipaṭiggahaṃ||

63.

 ‘‘ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, sumanaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, sikhinau laukabandhunau||

64.

 ‘‘imāya pupphapūjāya, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ pupphapūjāyidaṃ (sī॰) phalaṃ||

66.

 ‘‘chabbīsatimhi kappamhi, cattārausuṃ mahāyasā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, rājānau cak‍kavattinau||

67.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumanau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sumanatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. pupphacaṅkauṭiyatthairaapadānaṃ

68.

 ‘‘abhītarūpaṃ  sīhaṃva, garuḷaggaṃva pakkhinaṃ|

byagghūsabhaṃva pavaraṃ, abhijātaṃva kaisariṃ||

69.

 ‘‘sikhiṃ tilaukasaraṇaṃ, anaijaṃ aparājitaṃ|

nisin‍naṃ samaṇānaggaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ||

70.

 ‘‘caṅkauṭakai caṅgauṭakai (sī॰) ṭhapaitvāna, anaujaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ|

saha caṅkauṭakainaiva, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ samaukiriṃ||

71.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, dvipadinda narāsabha|

pattaumhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, hitvā jayaparājayaṃ||

72.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

73.

 ‘‘sampuṇṇai  tiṃsakappamhi, daivabhūtisanāmakā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, pañ‍cāsuṃ cak‍kavattinau||

74.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pupphacaṅkauṭiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pupphacaṅkauṭiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

sakacintaniyavaggau sattamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sakacintī  avaupupphī, sapac‍cāgamanaina ca|

parappasādī bhisadau, sucinti vatthadāyakau||

ambadāyī ca sumanau, pupphacaṅkauṭakīpi ca|

gāthaikasattati vuttā, gaṇitā atthadassibhi||


8. nāgasamālavaggau

1. nāgasamālatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘apāṭaliṃ  ahaṃ pupphaṃ, ujjhitaṃ sumahāpathai|

thūpamhi abhiraupaisiṃ, sikhinau laukabandhunau||

2.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, thūpapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘itau pan‍narasai kappai, bhūmiyau pupphiyau (syā॰) nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

4.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nāgasamālau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

nāgasamālatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. padasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

5.

 ‘‘ak‍kantañ‍ca  padaṃ disvā, tissassādic‍cabandhunau|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, padai cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

6.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, padasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘itau sattamakai kappai, sumaidhau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavatī mahabbalau||

8.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti||

padasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. buddhasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

9.

 ‘‘dumaggai  paṃsukūlikaṃ, laggaṃ disvāna satthunau|

tatau tamañ‍jaliṃ katvā, paṃsukūlaṃ avandahaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘itau  catutthakai kappai, dumasārausi khattiyau|

cāturantau vijitāvī, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā buddhasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

buddhasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. bhisāluvadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘kānanaṃ vanamaugayha, vasāmi vipinai ahaṃ|

vipassiṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘bhisāluvañ‍ca pādāsiṃ, udakaṃ hatthadhauvanaṃ|

vanditvā sirasā pādai, pak‍kāmi uttarāmukhau||

15.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, bhisāluvamadaṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘itau tatiyakai kappai, bhisasammatakhattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

17.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā bhisāluvadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

bhisāluvadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

chaṭṭhabhāṇavāraṃ|

5. aikasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

18.

 ‘‘khaṇḍau  nāmāsi nāmaina, vipassissaggasāvakau|

aikā bhikkhā mayā din‍nā, laukāhutipaṭiggahai||

19.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, dvipadinda narāsabha|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, aikabhikkhāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘cattālīsamhitau kappai, varuṇau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

21.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. tiṇasantharadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

22.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, mahājātassarau ahu|

satapattaihi sañchan‍nau, nānāsakuṇamālayau||

23.

 ‘‘tamhi  nhatvā ca pitvā pītvā (sī॰ syā॰) ca, avidūrai vasāmahaṃ|

addasaṃ samaṇānaggaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai||

24.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukai anuttarau|

abbhatau auruhitvāna, bhūmiyaṃṭhāsi tāvadai||

25.

 ‘‘visāṇaina lāyanaina (syā॰) tiṇaṃ gayha, nisīdanamadāsahaṃ|

nisīdi bhagavā tattha, tissau laukagganāyakau||

26.

 ‘‘sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, avandi laukanāyakaṃ|

paṭikuṭikau uk‍kuṭikau (syā॰ ka॰) apasak‍kiṃ, nijjhāyantau mahāmuniṃ||

27.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, nimmānaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, santharassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘itau  dutiyakai kappai, miga mitta (syā॰) sammatakhattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇasantharadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tiṇasantharadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sūcidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, sambuddhau laukanāyakau|

sumaidhau nāma nāmaina, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇau||

31.

 ‘‘tassa  kañ‍canavaṇṇassa, dvipadindassa tādinau|

pañ‍ca sūcī mayā din‍nā, sibbanatthāya cīvaraṃ||

32.

 ‘‘tainaiva sūcidānaina, nipuṇatthavipassakaṃ|

tikkhaṃ lahuñ‍ca phāsuñ‍ca, ñāṇaṃ mai udapaj‍jatha||

33.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

34.

 ‘‘dvipadādhipatī nāma, rājānau caturau ahuṃ|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

35.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sūcidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sūcidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. pāṭalipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

36.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇai|

kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘saiṭṭhiputtau  tadā āsiṃ, sukhumālau sukhaidhitau|

ucchaṅgai pāṭalipupphaṃ, katvāna katvā taṃ (sī॰ syā॰) abhisaṃhariṃ||

38.

 ‘‘haṭṭhau  haṭṭhaina cittaina, pupphaihi abhipūjayiṃ|

tissaṃ laukaviduṃ nāthaṃ, naradaivaṃ namassahaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘dvainavutai  itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘itau taisaṭṭhikappamhi, abhisammatanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

41.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pāṭalipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pāṭalipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ṭhitañ‍jaliyatthairaapadānaṃ

42.

 ‘‘migaluddau purai āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai kānanai ahaṃ|

tattha addasaṃ tatthaddasāsiṃ (sī॰ syā॰) sambuddhaṃ, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘tatthāhaṃ añ‍jaliṃ katvā, pak‍kāmiṃ pācināmukhau|

avidūrai nisin‍nassa, niyakai paṇṇasantharai||

44.

 ‘‘tatau mai asanīpātau, matthakai nipatī tadā|

sauhaṃ maraṇakālamhi, akāsiṃ punarañ‍jaliṃ||

45.

 ‘‘dvainavutai  itau kappai, añ‍jaliṃ akariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, añ‍jalissa idaṃ phalaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘catupaṇṇāsakappamhi, migakaitusanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

47.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ṭhitañ‍jaliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ṭhitañ‍jaliyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. tipadumiyatthairaapadānaṃ

48.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

dantau dantaparivutau, nagarā nikkhamī tadā||

49.

 ‘‘nagarai  haṃsavatiyaṃ, ahausiṃ mālikau tadā|

yaṃ tattha uttamaṃ tauṇi, padmapupphāni uttamaṃ pupphaṃ, tīṇi pupphāni (sī॰) aggahiṃ||

50.

 ‘‘addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, paṭimaggantarāpaṇai|

saha sauhaṃ (sī॰) disvāna sambuddhaṃ, aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā||

51.

 ‘‘kiṃ mai imaihi pupphaihi, rañ‍ñau upanitaihi mai|

gāmaṃ vā gāmakhaittaṃ vā, sahassaṃ vā labhaiyyahaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘adantadamanaṃ  vīraṃ, sabbasattasukhāvahaṃ|

laukanāthaṃ pūjayitvā, lacchāmi amataṃ dhanaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘aivāhaṃ cintayitvāna, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

tīṇi lauhitakai gayha, ākāsai ukkhipiṃ tadā||

54.

 ‘‘mayā ukkhittamattamhi, ākāsai patthariṃsu tai|

dhāriṃsu matthakai tattha, uddhaṃvaṇṭā adhaumukhā||

55.

 ‘‘yai kaici manujā disvā, uk‍kuṭṭhiṃ sampavattayuṃ|

daivatā antalikkhamhi, sādhukāraṃ pavattayuṃ||

56.

 ‘‘acchairaṃ laukai uppan‍naṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhassa vāhasā|

sabbai dhammaṃ suṇissāma, pupphānaṃ vāhasā mayaṃ||

57.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

vīthiyañhi ṭhitau santau, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

58.

 ‘‘‘yau sau buddhaṃ apūjaisi, rattapadmaihi rattapadumaihi (sī॰ syā॰) māṇavau|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

59.

 ‘‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati|

tiṃsakappāni tiṃsakkhattuñ‍ca (syā॰) daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati||

60.

 ‘‘‘mahāvitthārikaṃ  nāma, byamhaṃ haissati tāvadai|

tiyaujanasatubbiddhaṃ, diyaḍḍhasatavitthataṃ||

61.

 ‘‘‘cattārisatasahassāni, niyyūhā ca sumāpitā|

kūṭāgāravarūpaitā, mahāsayanamaṇḍitā||

62.

 ‘‘‘kauṭisatasahassiyau, parivāraissanti accharā|

kusalā nac‍cagītassa, vāditaipi padakkhiṇā||

63.

 ‘‘‘aitādisai byamhavarai, nārīgaṇasamākulai|

vassissati pupphavassau, dibbau pada (ka॰) lauhitakau sadā||

64.

 ‘‘‘bhittikhīlai nāgadantai, dvārabāhāya tauraṇai|

cak‍kamattā lauhitakā, aulambissanti tāvadai||

65.

 ‘‘‘pattaina pattasañchan‍nai, antaubyamhavarai imaṃ|

attharitvā pārupitvā, tuvaṭṭissanti tāvadai||

66.

 ‘‘‘bhavanaṃ parivāraitvā, samantā satayaujanai|

taipi padmā tai visuddhā (sī॰ syā॰) lauhitakā, dibbagandhaṃ pavāyarai||

67.

 ‘‘‘pañ‍casattatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

68.

 ‘‘‘sampattiyau duvai bhutvā, anīti anupaddavau|

sampattai pariyausānai, nibbānaṃ pāpuṇissati’ phassayissati (sī॰), passayissati (ka॰)||

69.

 ‘‘sudiṭṭhau vata mai buddhau, vāṇij‍jaṃ supayaujitaṃ|

padmāni tīṇi pūjaitvā, anubhausiṃ tisampadā anubhūyanti sampadā (ka॰)||

70.

 ‘‘aj‍ja mai dhammappattassa, vippamuttassa sabbasau|

supupphitaṃ lauhitakaṃ, dhārayissati matthakai||

71.

 ‘‘mama kammaṃ kathaintassa, padumuttarasatthunau|

satapāṇasahassānaṃ, dhammābhisamayau ahu||

72.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, tipadumānidaṃ phalaṃ||

73.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

74.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tipadumiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tipadumiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

nāgasamālavaggau aṭṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

nāgasamālau padasañ‍ñī, sañ‍ñakāluvadāyakau|

aikasañ‍ñī tiṇasanthārau, sūcipāṭalipupphiyau|

ṭhitañ‍jalī tipadumī, gāthāyau pañ‍casattati||


9. timiravaggau

1. timirapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, anusautaṃ vajāmahaṃ|

nisin‍naṃ samaṇaṃ disvā, vippasan‍namanāvilaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā pasādaisiṃ (syā॰), aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā|

tārayissati tiṇṇauyaṃ, dantauyaṃ damayissati||

3.

 ‘‘assāsissati assatthau, santau ca samayissati|

maucayissati muttau ca, nibbāpaissati nibbutau||

4.

 ‘‘aivāhaṃ cintayitvāna, siddhatthassa mahaisinau|

gahaitvā timirapupphaṃ, matthakai aukiriṃ ahaṃ tadā (syā॰)||

5.

 ‘‘añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, katvā ca naṃ padakkhiṇaṃ|

vanditvā satthunau pādai, pak‍kāmiṃ aparaṃ disaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘aciraṃ gatamattaṃ maṃ, migarājā vihaiṭhayi|

papātamanugacchantau, tatthaiva papatiṃ ahaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphapūjāyidaṃ buddhapūjāyidaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) phalaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘chappañ‍ñāsamhi  kappamhi, sattaivāsuṃ mahāyasā mahārahā (syā॰ ka॰)|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

9.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā timirapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

timirapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. gatasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

10.

 ‘‘jātiyā  sattavassauhaṃ, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

avandiṃ satthunau pādai, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

11.

 ‘‘sattanaṅgalakīpupphai, ākāsai ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ|

tissaṃ buddhaṃ samuddissa, anantaguṇasāgaraṃ||

12.

 ‘‘sugatānugataṃ maggaṃ, pūjaitvā haṭṭhamānasau|

añ‍jaliñ‍ca añ‍jalissa (ka॰) tadākāsiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

13.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘itau aṭṭhamakai kappai, tayau aggisikhā ahu|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

15.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gatasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

gatasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. nipan‍nañ‍jalikatthairaapadānaṃ

16.

 ‘‘rukkhamūlai  nisin‍nauhaṃ, byādhitau paramaina ca|

paramakāruñ‍ñapattaumhi, arañ‍ñai kānanai ahaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘anukampaṃ upādāya, tissau satthā upaisi maṃ|

sauhaṃ nipan‍nakau santau, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ||

18.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, sabbasattānamuttamaṃ|

sambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ vandiṃ purisuttamaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘itau  pañ‍camakai kappai, pañ‍caivāsuṃ mahāsikhā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

21.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nipan‍nañ‍jalikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

nipan‍nañ‍jalikatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. adhaupupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

22.

 ‘‘abhibhū nāma sau bhikkhu, sikhinau aggasāvakau|

mahānubhāvau taivij‍jau, himavantaṃ upāgami||

23.

 ‘‘ahampi  himavantamhi, ramaṇīyassamai isi|

vasāmi appamañ‍ñāsu, iddhīsu ca tadā vasī||

24.

 ‘‘pakkhijātau viyākāsai, pabbataṃ adhivattayiṃ abhipatthayiṃ (syā॰), abhimatthayiṃ (ka॰), adhivatthayinti pabbatassa upari gacchintiatthau|

adhaupupphaṃ gahaitvāna, āgacchiṃ agacchiṃ (ka॰) pabbataṃ ahaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘satta pupphāni gaṇhitvā, matthakai aukiriṃ ahaṃ|

ālaukitai ālaukitau (syā॰) ca vīraina, pak‍kāmiṃ pācināmukhau||

26.

 ‘‘āvāsaṃ  abhisambhausiṃ, patvāna assamaṃ ahaṃ|

khāribhāraṃ gahaitvāna, pāyāsiṃ pāvisiṃ (sī॰) pabbatantaraṃ||

27.

 ‘‘ajagarau maṃ pīḷaisi, ghaurarūpau mahabbalau|

pubbakammaṃ saritvāna, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā adhaupupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

adhaupupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. raṃsisañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘pabbatai  himavantamhi, vāsaṃ kappaisahaṃ purai|

ajinuttaravāsauhaṃ, vasāmi pabbatantarai||

31.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, sataraṃsiṃva bhāṇumaṃ|

vanantaragataṃ disvā, sālarājaṃva pupphitaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘raṃsyā raṃsai (syā॰ ka॰) cittaṃ pasādaitvā, vipassissa mahaisinau|

paggayha añ‍jaliṃ vandiṃ, sirasā uk‍kuṭī sirasā uk‍kuṭikau (syā॰), sirasuk‍kuṭikau (ka॰) ahaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, raṃsisañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā raṃsisañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

raṃsisañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaraṃsisañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

35.

 ‘‘pabbatai himavantamhi, vākacīradharau ahaṃ|

caṅkamañ‍ca samārūḷhau, nisīdiṃ pācināmukhau||

36.

 ‘‘pabbatai sugataṃ disvā, phussaṃ jhānarataṃ tadā|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, raṃsyā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

37.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, raṃsisañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā raṃsisañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

dutiyaraṃsisañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. phaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

39.

 ‘‘pabbatai  himavantamhi, kharājinadharau ahaṃ|

phussaṃ jinavaraṃ disvā, phalahatthau phalaṃ adaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘yamahaṃ phalamadāsiṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

bhavai nibbattamānamhi, phalaṃ nibbattatai mama||

41.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

phaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. saddasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

43.

 ‘‘pabbatai  himavantamhi, vasāmi paṇṇasantharai|

phussassa dhammaṃ bhaṇatau, saddai cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

44.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saddasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saddasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. baudhisiñ‍cakatthairaapadānaṃ

46.

 ‘‘vipassissa bhagavatau, mahābaudhimahau ahu|

pabbaj‍jupagatau santau, upagacchiṃ ahaṃ tadā||

47.

 ‘‘kusumaudakamādāya, baudhiyā aukiriṃ ahaṃ|

maucayissati nau muttau, nibbāpaissati nibbutau||

48.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ baudhimabhisiñ‍cayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, baudhisiñ‍cāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘taittiṃsai  vattamānamhi, kappai āsuṃ janādhipā|

udakasaicanā nāma, aṭṭhaitai cak‍kavattinau||

50.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā baudhisiñ‍cakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

baudhisiñ‍cakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. padumapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

51.

 ‘‘paukkharavanaṃ  paviṭṭhau, bhañ‍jantau padumānihaṃ|

tatthaddasaṃ phussaṃ buddhaṃ addasaṃ phussasambuddhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘padumapupphaṃ gahaitvāna, ākāsai ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ|

pāpakammaṃ saritvāna, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘pabbajitvāna kāyaina, manasā saṃvutaina ca|

vacīduc‍caritaṃ hitvā, ājīvaṃ parisaudhayiṃ||

54.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

55.

 ‘‘padumābhāsanāmā ca, aṭṭhārasa mahīpatī|

aṭṭhārasaisu kappaisu, aṭṭhatālīsamāsisuṃ||

56.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

padumapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

timiravaggau navamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

timiranaṅgalīpuppha, nippan‍nañ‍jalikau adhau|

dvai raṃsisañ‍ñī phaladau, saddasañ‍ñī ca saicakau|

padmapupphī ca gāthāyau, chappañ‍ñāsa pakittitā||


10. sudhāvaggau

1. sudhāpiṇḍiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘pūjārahai  pūjayatau, buddhai yadi va sāvakai|

papañ‍casamatik‍kantai, tiṇṇasaukapariddavai||

2.

 ‘‘tai tādisai pūjayatau, nibbutai akutaubhayai|

na sak‍kā puñ‍ñaṃ saṅkhātuṃ, imaittamapi idammattanti (sī॰), imaitthamapi (ka॰) kainaci||

3.

 ‘‘catun‍namapi dīpānaṃ, issaraṃ yaudha kārayai|

aikissā pūjanāyaitaṃ, kalaṃ nāgghati sauḷasiṃ||

4.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  naraggassa, caitiyai phalitantarai|

sudhāpiṇḍau mayā din‍nau, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

5.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, paṭisaṅkhārassidaṃ phalaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘itau tiṃsatikappamhi, paṭisaṅkhārasavhayā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, tairasa cak‍kavattinau||

7.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sudhāpiṇḍiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sudhāpiṇḍiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. sucintikatthairaapadānaṃ

8.

 ‘‘tissassa laukanāthassa, suddhapīṭhamadāsahaṃ|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, buddhassādic‍cabandhunau||

9.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai  aṭṭhatiṃsai (sī॰ syā॰) itau kappai, rājā āsiṃ mahāruci|

bhaugau ca vipulau āsi, sayanañ‍ca anappakaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘pīṭhaṃ  buddhassa datvāna, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

anubhaumi sakaṃ kammaṃ, pubbai sukatamattanau||

11.

 ‘‘dvainavutai  itau kappai, yaṃ pīṭhamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pīṭhadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘aṭṭhatiṃsai itau kappai, tayau tai cak‍kavattinau|

ruci uparuci caiva, mahāruci tatiyakau||

13.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sucintikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sucintikatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. aḍḍhacaiḷakatthairaapadānaṃ

14.

 ‘‘tissassāhaṃ bhagavatau, upaḍḍhadussamadāsahaṃ|

paramakāpañ‍ñapattaumhi paramakāruñ‍ñapattaumhi (syā॰ ka॰), duggataina duggandhaina (sī॰) samappitau||

15.

 ‘‘upaḍḍhadussaṃ datvāna, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ|

avasaisaisu kappaisu, kusalaṃ kāritaṃ mayā||

16.

 ‘‘dvainavutai  itau kappai, yaṃ dussamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dussadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘aikūnapañ‍ñāsakappamhi aikapañ‍ñāsakappamhi (syā॰), rājānau cak‍kavattinau|

samantacchadanā nāma, bāttiṃsāsuṃ khattiyāsuṃ (syā॰ ka॰) janādhipā||

18.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aḍḍhacaiḷakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aḍḍhacaiḷakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. sūcidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

19.

 ‘‘kammārauhaṃ  purai āsiṃ, bandhumāyaṃ puruttamai|

sūcidānaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, vipassissa mahaisinau||

20.

 ‘‘vajiraggasamaṃ ñāṇaṃ, hauti kammaina tādisaṃ|

virāgaumhi vimuttaumhi vibhavaumhi vibhattaumhi (ka॰), pattaumhi āsavakkhayaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘atītai  ca bhavai sabbai, vattamānai canāgatai atītā ca bhavā sabbai, vattamānā ca’nāgatā (syā॰ ka॰)|

ñāṇaina viciniṃ sabbaṃ, sūcidānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, sattāsuṃ vajiravhayā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

23.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sūcidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sūcidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. gandhamāliyatthairaapadānaṃ

24.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, gandhathūpaṃ akāsahaṃ|

sumanaihi paṭicchan‍naṃ, buddhānucchavikaṃ kataṃ||

25.

 ‘‘kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ, buddhaṃ laukagganāyakaṃ|

indīvaraṃva jalitaṃ, ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘byagghūsabhaṃva pavaraṃ, abhijātaṃva kaisariṃ|

nisin‍naṃ samaṇānaggaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ||

27.

 ‘‘vanditvā satthunau pādai, pak‍kāmiṃ uttarāmukhau|

catun‍navutitau kappai, gandhamālaṃ yatau adaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘buddhai katassa kārassa, phalaināhaṃ visaisatau|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘cattārīsamhi aikūnai, kappai āsiṃsu sauḷasa|

daivagandhasanāmā tai, rājānau cak‍kavattinau||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhamāliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

gandhamāliyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. tipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘migaluddau  purai āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai kānanai ahaṃ brahā (syā॰)|

pāṭaliṃ haritaṃ disvā, tīṇi pupphāni aukiriṃ||

32.

 ‘‘patitapattāni sattapattāni (sī॰), satapattāni (ka॰), sukkhapaṇṇāni (syā॰) gaṇhitvā, bahi chaḍḍaisahaṃ tadā|

antausuddhaṃ bahisuddhaṃ, suvimuttaṃ anāsavaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ, vipassiṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

pāṭaliṃ abhivādaitvā, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ baudhimabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, baudhipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘samantapāsādikā nāma, tairasāsiṃsu rājinau|

itau taittiṃsakappamhi tiṃsatikappamhi (syā॰), cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

36.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. madhupiṇḍikatthairaapadānaṃ

37.

 ‘‘vipinai  vivanai (syā॰ aṭṭha॰) kānanai disvā, appasaddai nirākulai|

siddhatthaṃ isinaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘nibbutattaṃ  nibbutaggaṃ (ka॰), nibbūtikaṃ (syā॰) mahānāgaṃ, nisabhājāniyaṃ yathā|

ausadhiṃva viraucantaṃ, daivasaṅghanamassitaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘vitti mamāhu tāvadai vitti mai pāhunā tāva (sī॰ syā॰), ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji tāvadai|

vuṭṭhitassa samādhimhā, madhuṃ datvāna satthunau||

40.

 ‘‘vanditvā  satthunau pādai, pak‍kāmiṃ pācināmukhau|

catuttiṃsamhi kappamhi, rājā āsiṃ sudassanau||

41.

 ‘‘madhu bhisaihi savati, bhaujanamhi ca tāvadai|

madhuvassaṃ pavassittha, pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ madhuṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, madhudānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘catuttiṃsai itau kappai, cattārau tai sudassanā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

44.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā madhupiṇḍikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

madhupiṇḍikatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. saināsanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

45.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa bhagavatau, adāsiṃ paṇṇasantharaṃ|

samantā upahārañ‍ca, kusumaṃ aukiriṃ ahaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘pāsādaivaṃ guṇaṃ rammaṃ pāsādai ca guhaṃ rammaṃ (syā॰), anubhaumi mahārahaṃ|

mahagghāni ca pupphāni, sayanaibhisavanti mai||

47.

 ‘‘sayanaihaṃ tuvaṭṭāmi, vicittai pupphasanthatai|

pupphavuṭṭhi ca sayanai, abhivassati tāvadai||

48.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, adāsiṃ paṇṇasantharaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, santharassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘tiṇasantharakā nāma, sattaitai cak‍kavattinau|

itau tai pañ‍camai kappai, uppaj‍jiṃsu janādhipā||

50.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saināsanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saināsanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vaiyyāvac‍cakatthairaapadānaṃ

51.

 ‘‘vipassissa  bhagavatau, mahāpūgagaṇau ahu|

vaiyyāvac‍cakarau āsiṃ, sabbakic‍caisu vāvaṭau byāvaṭau (sī॰ syā॰)||

52.

 ‘‘daiyyadhammau ca mai natthi, sugatassa mahaisinau|

avandiṃ satthunau pādai, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

53.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, vaiyyāvac‍caṃ akāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vaiyyāvac‍cassidaṃ phalaṃ||

54.

 ‘‘itau ca aṭṭhamai kappai, rājā āsiṃ sucintitau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

55.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vaiyyāvac‍cakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

vaiyyāvac‍cakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. buddhupaṭṭhākatthairaapadānaṃ

56.

 ‘‘vipassissa bhagavatau, ahausiṃ saṅkhadhammakau|

nic‍cupaṭṭhānayuttaumhi, sugatassa mahaisinau||

57.

 ‘‘upaṭṭhānaphalaṃ  passa, laukanāthassa tādinau|

saṭṭhitūriyasahassāni, parivārainti maṃ sadā||

58.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, upaṭṭhahiṃ mahāisiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

59.

 ‘‘catuvīsai catunavutai (syā॰) itau kappai, mahānigghausanāmakā|

sauḷasāsiṃsu rājānau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

60.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā buddhupaṭṭhākau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

buddhupaṭṭhākatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

sudhāvaggau dasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

sudhā  sucinti caiḷañ‍ca, sūcī ca gandhamāliyau|

tipupphiyau madhusainā, vaiyyāvac‍cau cupaṭṭhakau|

samasaṭṭhi ca gāthāyau, asmiṃ vaggai pakittitā||

atha vagguddānaṃ –

buddhavaggau hi paṭhamau, sīhāsani subhūti ca|

kuṇḍadhānau upāli ca, bījanisakacinti ca||

nāgasamālau  timirau, sudhāvaggaina tai dasa|

catuddasasatā gāthā, pañ‍capañ‍ñāsamaiva ca||

buddhavaggadasakaṃ|


paṭhamasatakaṃ samattaṃ|


11. bhikkhadāyivaggau

1. bhikkhadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

pavarā pavanā (syā॰) abhinikkhantaṃ, vanā nibbanamāgataṃ vānā nibbānamāgataṃ (syā॰)||

2.

 ‘‘kaṭacchubhikkhaṃ pādāsiṃ, siddhatthassa mahaisinau|

pañ‍ñāya upasantassa, mahāvīrassa tādinau||

3.

 ‘‘padainānupadāyantaṃ padainānupadāyantau (sī॰ syā॰), nibbāpaintai mahājanaṃ|

uḷārā vitti mai jātā, buddhai ādic‍cabandhunai vitti mai pāhunā tāva, buddhassādic‍cabandhunau (syā॰)||

4.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhikkhādānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘sattāsītimhitau kappai, mahāraiṇu sanāmakā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, sattaitai cak‍kavattinau||

6.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā bhikkhadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

bhikkhadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. ñāṇasañ‍ñikatthairaapadānaṃ

7.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, nisabhājāniyaṃ yathā|

tidhāpabhin‍naṃ mātaṅgaṃ, kuñ‍jaraṃva mahaisinaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘aubhāsaintaṃ disā sabbā, uḷurājaṃva pūritaṃ|

rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jantaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ apassahaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘ñāṇai  cittaṃ pasādaitvā, paggahaitvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, siddhatthamabhivādayiṃ||

10.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ñāṇasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘taisattatimhitau kappai, sauḷasāsuṃ naruttamā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ñāṇasañ‍ñikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ñāṇasañ‍ñikatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. uppalahatthiyatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘tivarāyaṃ  nivāsīhaṃ, ahausiṃ mālikau tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, siddhatthaṃ laukapūjitaṃ laukanāyakaṃ (sī॰)||

14.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, pupphahatthamadāsahaṃ|

yattha yatthupapaj‍jāmi, tassa kammassa vāhasā||

15.

 ‘‘anubhaumi phalaṃ iṭṭhaṃ, pubbai sukatamattanau|

parikkhittau sumal‍laihi, pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphapūjāyidaṃ buddhapūjāyidaṃ (sī॰) phalaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘catun‍navutupādāya, ṭhapaitvā vattamānakaṃ|

pañ‍carājasatā tattha, naj‍jasamasanāmakā naj‍jupamasanāmakā (sī॰ syā॰)||

18.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uppalahatthiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

uppalahatthiyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. padapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

19.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, jātipupphamadāsahaṃ|

pādaisu satta pupphāni, hāsainaukiritāni mai||

20.

 ‘‘taina  kammainahaṃ aj‍ja, abhibhaumi narāmarai|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

21.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘samantagandhanāmāsuṃ, tairasa cak‍kavattinau|

itau pañ‍camakai kappai, cāturantā janādhipā||

23.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

padapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. muṭṭhipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

24.

 ‘‘sudassanauti nāmaina, mālākārau ahaṃ tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘jātipupphaṃ gahaitvāna, pūjayiṃ padumuttaraṃ|

visuddhacakkhu sumanau, dibbacakkhuṃ samajjhagaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘aitissā pupphapūjāya, cittassa paṇidhīhi ca|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘sauḷasāsiṃsu  rājānau, daivuttarasanāmakā|

chattiṃsamhi itau kappai, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

28.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā muṭṭhipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

muṭṭhipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. udakapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

29.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai|

ghatāsanaṃva jalitaṃ, ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘pāṇinā udakaṃ gayha, ākāsai ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ|

sampaṭicchi mahāvīrau, buddhau kāruṇikau isi mayi (syā॰)||

31.

 ‘‘antalikkhai  ṭhitau satthā, padumuttaranāmakau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha||

32.

 ‘‘‘iminā dakadānaina, pītiuppādanaina ca|

kappasatasahassampi, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jati’ nupapaj‍jasi (ka॰)||

33.

 ‘‘taina kammaina dvipadinda, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha|

pattaumhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, hitvā jayaparājayaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘sahassarājanāmaina, tayau tai cak‍kavattinau|

pañ‍casaṭṭhikappasatai, cāturantā janādhipā||

35.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udakapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

udakapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. naḷamāliyatthairaapadānaṃ

36.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

tiṇattharai nisin‍nassa, upasantassa tādinau||

37.

 ‘‘naḷamālaṃ gahaitvāna, bandhitvā bījitvā (ka॰) bījaniṃ ahaṃ|

buddhassa upanāmaisiṃ, dvipadindassa tādinau||

38.

 ‘‘paṭiggahaitvā sabbañ‍ñū, bījaniṃ laukanāyakau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha||

39.

 ‘‘‘yathā  mai kāyau nibbāti, pariḷāhau na vij‍jati|

tathaiva tividhaggīhi, cittaṃ tava vimuc‍catu’||

40.

 ‘‘sabbai  daivā samāgacchuṃ, yai kaici vananissitā|

saussāma buddhavacanaṃ, hāsayantañ‍ca dāyakaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘nisin‍nau  bhagavā tattha, daivasaṅghapurakkhatau|

dāyakaṃ sampahaṃsaintau, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

42.

 ‘‘‘iminā bījanidānaina, cittassa paṇidhīhi ca|

subbatau nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

43.

 ‘‘‘taina  kammāvasaisaina, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

mālutau nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī bhavissati’||

44.

 ‘‘‘iminā bījanidānaina, sammānavipulaina ca|

kappasatasahassampi, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jati||

45.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, subbatā aṭṭhatiṃsa tai|

aikūnatiṃsasahassai, aṭṭha mālutanāmakā||

46.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā naḷamāliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

naḷamāliyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

sattamabhāṇavāraṃ|

8. āsanupaṭṭhāhakatthairaapadānaṃ

47.

 ‘‘kānanaṃ vanamaugayha, appasaddaṃ nirākulaṃ|

sīhāsanaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, atthadassissa tādinau||

48.

 ‘‘mālāhatthaṃ  gahaitvāna, katvā ca naṃ padakkhiṇaṃ|

satthāraṃ payirupāsitvā, pak‍kāmiṃ uttarāmukhau||

49.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina dvipadinda, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha|

san‍nibbāpaimi sandiṭṭhāpaimi (ka॰) attānaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā||

50.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasakappasatai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sīhāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

51.

 ‘‘itau  sattakappasatai, san‍nibbāpaka san‍niṭṭhau nāma (ka॰) khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

52.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā āsanupaṭṭhāhakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

āsanupaṭṭhāhakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. biḷālidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

53.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, vasāmi paṇṇasantharai|

ghāsaisu gaidhamāpan‍nau, saiyyasīlau cahaṃ saiyasīlauvahaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) tadā||

54.

 ‘‘khaṇantālu khaṇamālu (syā॰) kalambāni, biḷālitak‍kalāni ca|

kaulaṃ bhal‍lātakaṃ bil‍laṃ, āhatvā paṭiyāditaṃ||

55.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘upāgataṃ mahānāgaṃ, daivadaivaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

biḷāliṃ paggahaitvāna, pattamhi aukiriṃ ahaṃ||

57.

 ‘‘paribhuñ‍ji mahāvīrau, tausayantau mamaṃ tadā|

paribhuñ‍jitvāna sabbañ‍ñū, imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha||

58.

 ‘‘‘sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, biḷāliṃ mai adā tuvaṃ|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jasi’||

59.

 ‘‘carimaṃ  vattatai mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

60.

 ‘‘catupañ‍ñāsitau kappai, sumaikhaliyasavhayau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

61.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā biḷālidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

biḷālidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. raiṇupūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

62.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, sataraṃsiṃva bhāṇumaṃ|

aubhāsaintaṃ disā sabbā, uḷurājaṃva pūritaṃ||

63.

 ‘‘purakkhataṃ sāvakaihi, sāgaraihaiva maidaniṃ|

nāgaṃ paggayha raiṇūhi, vipassissābhiraupayiṃ||

64.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ raiṇumabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘paṇṇatālīsitau kappai, raiṇu nāmāsi khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

66.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā raiṇupūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

raiṇupūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

bhikkhadāyivaggau aikādasamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

bhikkhadāyī ñāṇasañ‍ñī, hatthiyau padapūjakau|

muṭṭhipupphī udakadau, naḷamāli upaṭṭhakau|

biḷālidāyī raiṇu ca, gāthāyau cha ca saṭṭhi ca||


12. mahāparivāravaggau

1. mahāparivārakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘vipassī  nāma bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

aṭṭhasaṭṭhisahassaihi, pāvisi bandhumaṃ tadā||

2.

 ‘‘nagarā abhinikkhamma, agamaṃ dīpacaitiyaṃ|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘cul‍lāsītisahassāni, yakkhā mayhaṃ upantikai|

upaṭṭhahanti sak‍kac‍caṃ maṃ nic‍caṃ (ka॰), indaṃva tidasā gaṇā||

4.

 ‘‘bhavanā  abhinikkhamma, dussaṃ paggayhahaṃ tadā|

sirasā abhivādaisiṃ, tañ‍cādāsiṃ mahaisinau||

5.

 ‘‘ahau buddhau ahau dhammau, ahau nau satthu sampadā|

buddhassa ānubhāvaina, vasudhāyaṃ pakampatha||

6.

 ‘‘tañ‍ca acchariyaṃ disvā, abbhutaṃ laumahaṃsanaṃ|

buddhai cittaṃ pasādaimi, dvipadindamhi tādinai||

7.

 ‘‘sauhaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, dussaṃ datvāna satthunau|

saraṇañ‍ca upāgacchiṃ, sāmac‍cau saparij‍janau||

8.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘itau pan‍narasai kappai, sauḷasāsuṃ suvāhanā sauḷasāsiṃsu vāhanau (syā॰)|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

10.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahāparivārakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

mahāparivārakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. sumaṅgalatthairaapadānaṃ

11.

 ‘‘atthadassī  jinavarau, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

vihārā abhinikkhamma, taḷākaṃ upasaṅkami||

12.

 ‘‘nhatvā pitvā ca sambuddhau, uttaritvaikacīvarau|

aṭṭhāsi bhagavā tattha, vilaukaintau disaudisaṃ||

13.

 ‘‘bhavanai upaviṭṭhauhaṃ, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, apphauṭaisiṃ ahaṃ tadā||

14.

 ‘‘sataraṃsiṃva jautantaṃ, pabhāsantaṃva kañ‍canaṃ iminā pādadvayaina purimapādadvayassa puratau bhavitabbaṃ|

nac‍cagītai payuttauhaṃ, pañ‍caṅgatūriyamhi ca||

15.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

sabbai sattai abhibhaumi, vipulau hauti mai yasau ayañ‍ca gāthā pariggahaitigāthāya anantaramaiva ṭhātuṃ yuttā||

16.

 ‘‘namau  tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

attānaṃ tausayitvāna, parai tausaisi tvaṃ muni||

17.

 ‘‘pariggahai  pariggayha (sī॰), pariggahitvā (syā॰), pariggahaina (ka॰) nisīditvā, hāsaṃ katvāna subbatai|

upaṭṭhahitvā sambuddhaṃ, tusitaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘sauḷasaitau kappasatai, dvinavaaikacintitā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

19.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumaṅgalau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sumaṅgalatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. saraṇagamaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

20.

 ‘‘ubhin‍naṃ daivarājūnaṃ, saṅgāmau samupaṭṭhitau|

ahausi samupabyūḷhau samupabbūḷhau (sī॰), mahāghausau avattatha pavattatha (sī॰)||

21.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

antalikkhai ṭhitau satthā, saṃvaijaisi mahājanaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘sabbai  daivā attamanā, nikkhittakavacāvudhā|

sambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā, aikaggāsiṃsu tāvadai||

23.

 ‘‘mayhaṃ amhaṃ (sī॰) saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, vācāsabhimudīrayi|

anukampakau laukavidū, nibbāpaisi mahājanaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘paduṭṭhacittau  manujau, aikapāṇaṃ vihaiṭhayaṃ|

taina cittappadausaina, apāyaṃ upapaj‍jati||

25.

 ‘‘saṅgāmasīsai nāgauva, bahū pāṇai vihaiṭhayaṃ|

nibbāpaitha sakaṃ cittaṃ, mā hañ‍ñitthau punappunaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘dvin‍nampi yakkharājūnaṃ, sainā sā vimhitā ahu saināyau vimhitā ahū (sī॰), saināpi samitā ahu (syā॰)|

saraṇañ‍ca upāgacchuṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ sutādinaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘sañ‍ñāpaitvāna  janataṃ, padamuddhari uddhari pana (sī॰ syā॰) cakkhumā|

paikkhamānauva daivaihi, pak‍kāmi uttarāmukhau||

28.

 ‘‘paṭhamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchiṃ, dvipadindassa tādinau|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘mahādundubhināmā ca, sauḷasāsuṃ rathaisabhā|

tiṃsakappasahassamhi, rājānau cak‍kavattinau||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saraṇagamaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saraṇagamaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. aikāsaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘varuṇau nāma nāmaina, daivarājā ahaṃ tadā|

upaṭṭhahaisiṃ sambuddhaṃ, sayauggabalavāhanau||

32.

 ‘‘nibbutai  laukanāthamhi, atthadassīnaruttamai|

tūriyaṃ sabbamādāya, agamaṃ baudhimuttamaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘vāditaina ca nac‍caina, sammatāḷasamāhitau|

sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ, upaṭṭhiṃ baudhimuttamaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘upaṭṭhahitvā  taṃ baudhiṃ, dharaṇīruhapādapaṃ|

pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvāna, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘sakakammābhiraddhauhaṃ, pasan‍nau baudhimuttamai|

taina cittappasādaina, nimmānaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘saṭṭhitūriyasahassāni, parivārainti maṃ sadā|

manussaisu ca daivaisu, vattamānaṃ bhavābhavai||

37.

 ‘‘tividhaggī nibbutā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai ayañ‍ca gāthā paṭisambhidā catassaitigāthāya aikasambandhā bhavitaṃ yuttā||

38.

 ‘‘subāhū  nāma nāmaina, catuttiṃsāsu khattiyā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, pañ‍cakappasatai itau||

39.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikāsaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikāsaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. suvaṇṇapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

40.

 ‘‘vipassī  nāma bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

nisin‍nau janakāyassa, daisaisi amataṃ padaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna, dvipadindassa tādinau|

sauṇṇapupphāni cattāri, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

42.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇacchadanaṃ āsi, yāvatā parisā tadā|

buddhābhā ca suvaṇṇābhā, ālaukau vipulau ahu||

43.

 ‘‘udaggacittau  sumanau, vaidajātau katañ‍jalī|

vittisañ‍jananau taisaṃ, diṭṭhadhammasukhāvahau||

44.

 ‘‘āyācitvāna sambuddhaṃ, vanditvāna ca subbataṃ|

pāmauj‍jaṃ janayitvāna, sakaṃ bhavanupāgamiṃ||

45.

 ‘‘bhavanai upaviṭṭhauhaṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ|

taina cittappasādaina, tusitaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘sauḷasāsiṃsu rājānau, naimisammatanāmakā|

taitālīsai itau kappai, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

48.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā suvaṇṇapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

suvaṇṇapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. citakapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

49.

 ‘‘vasāmi  rājāyatanai, sāmac‍cau saparij‍janau|

parinibbutai bhagavati, sikhinau laukabandhunau||

50.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, citakaṃ agamāsahaṃ|

tūriyaṃ tattha vādaitvā, gandhamālaṃ samaukiriṃ||

51.

 ‘‘citamhi pūjaṃ katvāna, vanditvā citakaṃ ahaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, sakaṃ bhavanupāgamiṃ||

52.

 ‘‘bhavanai upaviṭṭhauhaṃ, citapūjaṃ anussariṃ|

taina kammaina dvipadinda, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha||

53.

 ‘‘anubhautvāna sampattiṃ, daivaisu mānusaisu ca|

pattaumhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, hitvā jayaparājayaṃ||

54.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

55.

 ‘‘aikūnatiṃsakappamhi, itau sauḷasa rājānau|

uggatā nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

56.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

citakapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. buddhasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

57.

 ‘‘yadā  vipassī laukaggau, āyusaṅkhāramaussaji|

pathavī sampakampittha, maidanī jalamaikhalā||

58.

 ‘‘autataṃ vitthataṃ autataṃ vitataṃ (syā॰) mayhaṃ, suvicittavaṭaṃsakaṃ sucicittaṃ papañ‍cakaṃ (syā॰)|

bhavanampi pakampittha, buddhassa āyusaṅkhayai||

59.

 ‘‘tāsau  mayhaṃ samuppan‍nau, bhavanai sampakampitai|

uppādau uppātau (?) nu kimatthāya, ālaukau vipulau ahu||

60.

 ‘‘vaissavaṇau idhāgamma, nibbāpaisi mahājanaṃ|

pāṇabhūtai pāṇabhutaṃ (syā॰), pāṇabhūnaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) bhayaṃ natthi, aikaggā hautha saṃvutā sagāravā (syā॰)||

61.

 ‘‘ahau buddhau ahau dhammau, ahau nau satthu sampadā|

yasmiṃ uppaj‍jamānamhi, pathavī paṭhavī (sī॰ syā॰) sampakampati||

62.

 ‘‘buddhānubhāvaṃ kittaitvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ|

avasaisaisu kappaisu, kusalaṃ caritaṃ karitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), kāritaṃ (ka॰) mayā||

63.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

64.

 ‘‘itau  cuddasakappamhi, rājā āsiṃ patāpavā|

samitau nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

65.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā buddhasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

buddhasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. maggasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

66.

 ‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, sāvakā vanacārinau|

vippanaṭṭhā brahārañ‍ñai, andhāva anusuyyarai anusuyarai (sī॰)||

67.

 ‘‘anussaritvā  sambuddhaṃ, padumuttaranāyakaṃ|

tassa tai muninau puttā, vippanaṭṭhā mahāvanai||

68.

 ‘‘bhavanā auruhitvāna, agamiṃ bhikkhusantikaṃ|

taisaṃ maggañ‍ca ācikkhiṃ, bhaujanañ‍ca adāsahaṃ||

69.

 ‘‘taina kammaina dvipadinda, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha|

jātiyā sattavassauhaṃ, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

70.

 ‘‘sacakkhū  nāma nāmaina, dvādasa cak‍kavattinau|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, pañ‍cakappasatai itau||

71.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā maggasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

maggasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pac‍cupaṭṭhānasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

72.

 ‘‘atthadassimhi sugatai, nibbutai samanantarā|

yakkhayauniṃ upapaj‍jiṃ, yasaṃ pattau cahaṃ tadā||

73.

 ‘‘dul‍laddhaṃ  vata mai āsi, duppabhātaṃ duruṭṭhitaṃ|

yaṃ mai bhaugai vij‍jamānai, parinibbāyi cakkhumā||

74.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, sāgarau nāma sāvakau|

mamuddharitukāmau sau, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

75.

 ‘‘kiṃ nu saucasi mā bhāyi, cara dhammaṃ sumaidhasa|

anuppadin‍nā buddhaina, sabbaisaṃ bījasampadā||

76.

 ‘‘sau cai pūjaiyya sambuddhaṃ, tiṭṭhantaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

dhātuṃ sāsapamattampi, nibbutassāpi pūjayai||

77.

 ‘‘samai  cittappasādamhi, samaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ mahaggataṃ|

tasmā thūpaṃ karitvāna, pūjaihi jinadhātuyau||

78.

 ‘‘sāgarassa vacau sutvā, buddhathūpaṃ akāsahaṃ|

pañ‍cavassai paricariṃ, muninau thūpamuttamaṃ||

79.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina dvipadinda, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha|

sampattiṃ anubhautvāna, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

80.

 ‘‘bhūripañ‍ñā ca cattārau, sattakappasatai itau|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

81.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pac‍cupaṭṭhānasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pac‍cupaṭṭhānasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. jātipūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

82.

 ‘‘jāyantassa  vipassissa, ālaukau vipulau ahu|

pathavī ca pakampittha, sasāgarā sapabbatā||

83.

 ‘‘naimittā ca viyākaṃsu, buddhau laukai bhavissati|

aggau ca sabbasattānaṃ, janataṃ uddharissati||

84.

 ‘‘naimittānaṃ  suṇitvāna, jātipūjamakāsahaṃ|

aidisā pūjanā natthi, yādisā jātipūjanā||

85.

 ‘‘saṅkharitvāna saṃharitvāna (sī॰ syā॰), saṅkaritvāna (ka॰)

kusalaṃ, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

jātipūjaṃ karitvāna, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

86.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

sabbai sattai abhibhaumi, jātipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

87.

 ‘‘dhātiyau maṃ upaṭṭhanti, mama cittavasānugā|

na tā sak‍kaunti kaupaituṃ, jātipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

88.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pūjamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, jātipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

89.

 ‘‘supāricariyā nāma, catuttiṃsa janādhipā|

itau tatiyakappamhi, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

90.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā jātipūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

jātipūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

mahāparivāravaggau dvādasamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

parivārasumaṅgalā, saraṇāsanapupphiyā|

citapūjī buddhasañ‍ñī, maggupaṭṭhānajātinā|

gāthāyau  navuti vuttā, gaṇitāyau vibhāvihi||


13. sairaiyyavaggau

1. sairaiyyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘ajjhāyakau  mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidāna pāragū|

abbhaukāsai ṭhitau santau, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘sīhaṃ yathā vanacaraṃ, byaggharājaṃva nittasaṃ|

tidhāpabhin‍namātaṅgaṃ, kuñ‍jaraṃva mahaisinaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘sairaiyyakaṃ gahaitvāna, ākāsai ukkhipiṃ nikkhipiṃ (ka॰) ahaṃ|

buddhassa ānubhāvaina, parivārainti sabbasau sabbatau (sī॰)||

4.

 ‘‘adhiṭṭhahi mahāvīrau, sabbañ‍ñū laukanāyakau|

samantā pupphacchadanā, aukiriṃsu narāsabhaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘tatau sā pupphakañ‍cukā, antauvaṇṭā bahimukhā|

sattāhaṃ chadanaṃ katvā, tatau antaradhāyatha||

6.

 ‘‘tañ‍ca acchariyaṃ disvā, abbhutaṃ laumahaṃsanaṃ|

buddhai cittaṃ pasādaisiṃ, sugatai laukanāyakai||

7.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘pan‍narasasahassamhi, kappānaṃ pañ‍cavīsati|

vītamalā cittamālā (sī॰), vilāmālā (syā॰) samānā ca, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

9.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sairaiyyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sairaiyyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. pupphathūpiyatthairaapadānaṃ

10.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, kuk‍kurau nāma kuk‍kuṭau nāma (sī॰) pabbatau|

vaimajjhai tassa vasati, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū||

11.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca sissasahassāni, parivārainti maṃ sadā|

pubbuṭṭhāyī ca tai āsuṃ, mantaisu ca visāradā||

12.

 ‘‘buddhau  laukai samuppan‍nau, taṃ vijānātha nau bhavaṃ|

asītibyañ‍janānassa, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇā||

13.

 ‘‘byāmappabhau jinavarau, ādic‍cauva viraucati|

sissānaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū||

14.

 ‘‘assamā abhinikkhamma, disaṃ pucchati sissakai brāhmaṇau (syā॰)|

yamhi daisai mahāvīrau, vasati laukanāyakau||

15.

 ‘‘tāhaṃ disaṃ namassissaṃ, jinaṃ appaṭipuggalaṃ|

udaggacittau sumanau, pūjaisiṃ taṃ tathāgataṃ||

16.

 ‘‘aitha  sissā gamissāma, dakkhissāma tathāgataṃ|

vanditvā satthunau pādai, saussāma jinasāsanaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘aikāhaṃ abhinikkhamma, byādhiṃ paṭilabhiṃ ahaṃ|

byādhinā pīḷitau santau, sālaṃ vāsayituṃ gamiṃ||

18.

 ‘‘sabbai sissai samānaitvā, apucchiṃ tai tathāgataṃ|

kīdisaṃ laukanāthassa, guṇaṃ paramabuddhinau||

19.

 ‘‘tai mai puṭṭhā viyākaṃsu, yathā dassāvinau tathā|

sak‍kac‍caṃ buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ taṃ, daisaisuṃ dassaisuṃ (sī॰ syā॰) mama sammukhā||

20.

 ‘‘taisāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

pupphaihi thūpaṃ katvāna, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘tai mai sarīraṃ jhāpaitvā, agamuṃ buddhasantikaṃ|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, satthāramabhivādayuṃ||

22.

 ‘‘pupphaihi thūpaṃ katvāna, sugatassa mahaisinau|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘cattālīsasahassamhi, kappai sauḷasa khattiyā|

nāmainaggisamā nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

24.

 ‘‘vīsakappasahassamhi, rājānau cak‍kavattinau|

ghatāsanasanāmāva, aṭṭhattiṃsa mahīpatī||

25.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā pupphathūpiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pupphathūpiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. pāyasadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

26.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇau  sambuddhau, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇau|

pavanā padhānau (ka॰) abhinikkhantau, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhatau suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ …pai॰… purakkhataṃ-aivaṃ dutiyantavasaina sī॰ syā॰ pautthakaisu dissati||

27.

 ‘‘mahac‍cā sahatthā (syā॰ ka॰) kaṃsapātiyā, vaḍḍhaitvā pāyasaṃ pāyāsaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) ahaṃ|

āhutiṃ yiṭṭhukāmau sau, upanaisiṃ baliṃ ahaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘bhagavā tamhi samayai, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

caṅkamaṃ susamārūḷhau, ambarai anilāyanai||

29.

 ‘‘tañ‍ca acchariyaṃ disvā, abbhutaṃ laumahaṃsanaṃ|

ṭhapayitvā kaṃsapātiṃ, vipassiṃ abhivādayiṃ||

30.

 ‘‘tuvaṃ daivausi buddhausi (syā॰) sabbañ‍ñū, sadaivai sahamānusai|

anukampaṃ upādāya, paṭigaṇha mahāmuni||

31.

 ‘‘paṭiggahaisi bhagavā, sabbañ‍ñū laukanāyakau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukai mahāmuni anuttarau (syā॰)||

32.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pāyasassa pāyāsassa (syā॰ ka॰) idaṃ phalaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘aikatālīsitau  kappai, buddhau nāmāsi khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

34.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pāyasadāyakau pāyāsadāyakau (syā॰ ka॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pāyasadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. gandhaudakiyatthairaapadānaṃ

35.

 ‘‘nisaj‍ja  pāsādavarai, vipassiṃ addasaṃ jinaṃ|

kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva, sabbañ‍ñuṃ tamanāsakaṃ sabbañ‍ñuttamanāyakaṃ (syā॰), sabbañ‍ñutamanāsavaṃ (ka॰)||

36.

 ‘‘pāsādassāvidūrai ca, gacchati laukanāyakau|

pabhā niddhāvatai tassa, yathā ca sataraṃsinau||

37.

 ‘‘gandhaudakañ‍ca  paggayha, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ samaukiriṃ|

taina cittappasādaina, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ gandhaudakamākiriṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, sugandhau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

40.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā gandhaudakiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

gandhaudakiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. sammukhāthavikatthairaapadānaṃ

41.

 ‘‘jāyamānai vipassimhi, nimittaṃ byākariṃ ahaṃ|

‘nibbāpayiñ‍ca nibbāpayaṃ ca (sī॰ syā॰), nibbāpayantau (?) janataṃ, buddhau laukai bhavissati||

42.

 ‘‘‘yasmiñ‍ca  jāyamānasmiṃ, dasasahassi kampati|

sau dāni bhagavā satthā, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā||

43.

 ‘‘‘yasmiñ‍ca jāyamānasmiṃ, ālaukau vipulau ahu|

sau dāni bhagavā satthā, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā||

44.

 ‘‘‘yasmiñ‍ca jāyamānasmiṃ, saritāyau na sandayuṃ sandisuṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

sau dāni bhagavā satthā, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā||

45.

 ‘‘‘yasmiñ‍ca jāyamānasmiṃ, avīcaggi na paj‍jali|

sau dāni bhagavā satthā, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā||

46.

 ‘‘‘yasmiñ‍ca  jāyamānasmiṃ, pakkhisaṅghau na saṃcari|

sau dāni bhagavā satthā, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā||

47.

 ‘‘‘yasmiñ‍ca jāyamānasmiṃ, vātakkhandhau na vāyati|

sau dāni bhagavā satthā, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā||

48.

 ‘‘‘yasmiñ‍ca  jāyamānasmiṃ, sabbaratanāni jautayuṃ jautisuṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

sau dāni bhagavā satthā, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā||

49.

 ‘‘‘yasmiñ‍ca jāyamānasmiṃ, sattāsuṃ padavik‍kamā|

sau dāni bhagavā satthā, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā||

50.

 ‘‘‘jātamattau  ca sambuddhau, disā sabbā vilaukayi|

vācāsabhimudīraisi, aisā buddhāna dhammatā’||

51.

 ‘‘saṃvaijayitvā janataṃ, thavitvā laukanāyakaṃ|

sambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā, pak‍kāmiṃ pācināmukhau||

52.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhithaumayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, thaumanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘itau navutikappamhi, sammukhāthavikavhayau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

54.

 ‘‘pathavīdundubhi nāma duddasi nāma (ka॰), aikūnanavutimhitau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

55.

 ‘‘aṭṭhāsītimhitau  kappai, aubhāsau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

56.

 ‘‘sattāsītimhitau kappai, saritacchaidanavhayau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

57.

 ‘‘agginibbāpanau nāma, kappānaṃ chaḷasītiyā|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

58.

 ‘‘gatipacchaidanau nāma, kappānaṃ pañ‍casītiyā|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

59.

 ‘‘rājā  vātasamau nāma, kappānaṃ cul‍lasītiyā|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

60.

 ‘‘ratanapaj‍jalau  nāma, kappānaṃ taiasītiyā|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

61.

 ‘‘padavik‍kamanau nāma, kappānaṃ dvaiasītiyā|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

62.

 ‘‘rājā vilaukanau nāma, kappānaṃ aikasītiyā|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

63.

 ‘‘girasārauti nāmaina, kappaisītimhi khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

64.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sammukhāthavikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sammukhāthavikatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kusumāsaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

65.

 ‘‘nagarai  dhañ‍ñavatiyā, ahausiṃ brāhmaṇau tadā|

lakkhaṇai itihāsai ca, sanighaṇḍusakaiṭubhai||

66.

 ‘‘padakau  vaiyyākaraṇau, nimittakauvidau ahaṃ|

mantai ca sissai vācaisiṃ, tiṇṇaṃ vaidāna pāragū||

67.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca uppalahatthāni, piṭṭhiyaṃ ṭhapitāni mai|

āhutiṃ yiṭṭhukāmauhaṃ, pitumātusamāgamai||

68.

 ‘‘tadā vipassī bhagavā, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhatau|

aubhāsaintau disā sabbā, āgacchati narāsabhau||

69.

 ‘‘āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvāna, nimantaitvā mahāmuniṃ|

santharitvāna taṃ pupphaṃ, abhinaisiṃ sakaṃ gharaṃ||

70.

 ‘‘yaṃ mai atthi sakai gaihai, āmisaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ|

tāhaṃ buddhassa pādāsiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

71.

 ‘‘bhuttāviṃ kālamañ‍ñāya, pupphahatthamadāsahaṃ|

anumauditvāna sabbañ‍ñū, pak‍kāmi uttarāmukhau||

72.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

73.

 ‘‘anantaraṃ itau kappai, rājāhuṃ varadassanau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

74.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kusumāsaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kusumāsaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. phaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

75.

 ‘‘ajjhāyakau mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidāna pāragū|

himavantassāvidūrai, vasāmi assamai ahaṃ||

76.

 ‘‘aggihuttañ‍ca mai atthi, puṇḍarīkaphalāni ca|

puṭakai nikkhipitvāna, dumaggai laggitaṃ mayā||

77.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mamuddharitukāmau sau, bhikkhantau mamupāgami||

78.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, phalaṃ buddhassadāsahaṃ|

vittisañ‍jananau mayhaṃ, diṭṭhadhammasukhāvahau||

79.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇau sambuddhau, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

antalikkhai ṭhitau satthā, imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha||

80.

 ‘‘‘iminā phaladānaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jasi’||

81.

 ‘‘tainaiva  suk‍kamūlaina, anubhautvāna sampadā|

pattaumhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, hitvā jayaparājayaṃ||

82.

 ‘‘itau sattasatai kappai, rājā āsiṃ sumaṅgalau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

83.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

phaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. ñāṇasañ‍ñikatthairaapadānaṃ

84.

 ‘‘pabbatai himavantamhi, vasāmi pabbatantarai|

pulinaṃ saubhanaṃ disvā, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ||

85.

 ‘‘ñāṇai upanidhā natthi, saṅkhāraṃ saṅgāmaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), saṅkhātaṃ (thairagāthā aṭṭha॰) natthi satthunau|

sabbadhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, ñāṇaina adhimuc‍cati||

86.

 ‘‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

ñāṇaina tai samau natthi, yāvatā ñāṇamuttamaṃ||

87.

 ‘‘ñāṇai cittaṃ pasādaitvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ|

avasaisaisu kappaisu, kusalaṃ caritaṃ karitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), kiriyaṃ (ka॰) mayā||

88.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ñāṇasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

89.

 ‘‘itau sattatikappamhi taisattatikappai (sī॰ syā॰), aikau pulinapupphiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

90.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ñāṇasañ‍ñikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ñāṇasañ‍ñikatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. gaṇṭhipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

91.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇau sambuddhau, vipassī dakkhiṇārahau|

purakkhatau sāvakaihi, ārāmā abhinikkhami||

92.

 ‘‘disvānahaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ, sabbañ‍ñuṃ tamanāsakaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, gaṇṭhipupphaṃ gatamaggaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) apūjayiṃ||

93.

 ‘‘taina  cittappasādaina, dvipadindassa tādinau|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, puna vandiṃ tathāgataṃ||

94.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

95.

 ‘‘aikatālīsitau  kappai, caraṇau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

96.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gaṇṭhipupphiyau gandhapupphiyau (syā॰ ka॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

gaṇṭhipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. padumapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

97.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, gautamau nāma pabbatau|

nānārukkhaihi sañchan‍nau, mahābhūtagaṇālayau||

98.

 ‘‘vaimajjhamhi ca tassāsi, assamau abhinimmitau|

purakkhatau sasissaihi, vasāmi assamai ahaṃ||

99.

 ‘‘āyantu  mai sissagaṇā, padumaṃ āharantu mai|

buddhapūjaṃ karissāmi, dvipadindassa tādinau||

100.

 ‘‘aivanti tai paṭissutvā, padumaṃ āhariṃsu mai|

tathā nimittaṃ katvāhaṃ, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

101.

 ‘‘sissai tadā samānaitvā, sādhukaṃ anusāsahaṃ|

mā khau tumhai pamaj‍jittha, appamādau sukhāvahau||

102.

 ‘‘aivaṃ  samanusāsitvā, tai sissai vacanakkhamai|

appamādaguṇai yuttau, tadā kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

103.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

104.

 ‘‘aikapañ‍ñāsakappamhi, rājā āsiṃ jaluttamau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

105.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

padumapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

sairaiyyavaggau tairasamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sairaiyyakau pupphathūpi, pāyasau gandhathaumakau|

āsani phalasañ‍ñī ca, gaṇṭhipadumapupphiyau|

pañ‍cuttarasatā gāthā, gaṇitā atthadassibhi||


14. saubhitavaggau

1. saubhitatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

mahatau janakāyassa, daisaiti amataṃ padaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, vācāsabhimudīritaṃ vācāsabhimudīrayiṃ (?)|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, aikaggau āsahaṃ tadā||

3.

 ‘‘yathā  samuddau udadhīnamaggau, nairū nagānaṃ pavarau siluc‍cayau|

tathaiva yai cittavasaina vattarai, na buddhañāṇassa kalaṃ upainti tai||

4.

 ‘‘dhammavidhiṃ dhammai vidhiṃ (sī॰) ṭhapaitvāna, buddhau kāruṇikau isi|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

5.

 ‘‘‘yau sau ñāṇaṃ pakittaisi, buddhamhi laukanāyakai|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ na gamissati||

6.

 ‘‘‘kilaisai jhāpayitvāna, aikaggau susamāhitau|

saubhitau nāma nāmaina, haissati satthu sāvakau’||

7.

 ‘‘pañ‍ñāsai  kappasahassai, sattaivāsuṃ yasuggatā samuggatā (syā॰)|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

8.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saubhitau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saubhitatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. sudassanatthairaapadānaṃ

10.

 ‘‘vinatā  nadiyā vitthatāya nadiyā (syā॰) tīrai, pilakkhu pilakkhau (sī॰ thairagāthā aṭṭha॰) phalitau ahu|

tāhaṃ rukkhaṃ gavaisantau, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘kaitakaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, vaṇṭai chaitvānahaṃ tadā|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, sikhinau laukabandhunau||

12.

 ‘‘yaina ñāṇaina pattausi, ac‍cutaṃ amataṃ padaṃ|

taṃ ñāṇaṃ abhipūjaimi, buddhasaiṭṭha mahāmuni||

13.

 ‘‘ñāṇamhi pūjaṃ katvāna, pilakkhumaddasaṃ ahaṃ|

paṭiladdhaumhi taṃ pañ‍ñaṃ taṃ sañ‍ñaṃ (syā॰), taṃ puñ‍ñaṃ (ka॰), ñāṇapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ñāṇapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘itau  tairasakappamhi, dvādasāsuṃ phaluggatā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahapphalā||

16.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sudassanau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sudassanatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. candanapūjanakatthairaapadānaṃ

17.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, ahausiṃ kin‍narau tadā|

pupphabhakkhau cahaṃ āsiṃ, pupphānivasanau tathā pupphānaṃ vasanau ahaṃ (syā॰)||

18.

 ‘‘atthadassī tu bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

vipinaggaina niyyāsi, haṃsarājāva ambarai||

19.

 ‘‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña, cittaṃ tai suvisaudhitaṃ|

pasan‍namukhavaṇṇausi, vippasan‍namukhindriyau||

20.

 ‘‘aurauhitvāna  ākāsā, bhūripañ‍ñau sumaidhasau|

saṅghāṭiṃ pattharitvāna, pal‍laṅkaina upāvisi||

21.

 ‘‘vilīnaṃ  candanādāya, agamāsiṃ jinantikaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

22.

 ‘‘abhivādaitvāna  sambuddhaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

pāmauj‍jaṃ janayitvāna, pak‍kāmiṃ uttarāmukhau||

23.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, candanaṃ yaṃ apūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘catuddasai kappasatai, itau āsiṃsu tai tayau|

rauhaṇī nāma rauhitā nāma (sī॰), rauhiṇī nāma (syā॰) nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

25.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā candanapūjanakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

candanapūjanakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

aṭṭhamabhāṇavāraṃ|

4. pupphacchadaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

26.

 ‘‘sunandau  nāma nāmaina, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū|

ajjhāyakau yācayaugau, vājapaiyyaṃ ayājayi||

27.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, aggau kāruṇikau isi|

janataṃ anukampantau, ambarai caṅkamī tadā||

28.

 ‘‘caṅkamitvāna sambuddhau, sabbañ‍ñū laukanāyakau|

maittāya aphari sattai, appamāṇai appamāṇaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) nirūpadhi||

29.

 ‘‘vaṇṭai  chaitvāna pupphāni, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū|

sabbai sissai samānaitvā, ākāsai ukkhipāpayi||

30.

 ‘‘yāvatā  nagaraṃ āsi, pupphānaṃ chadanaṃ tadā|

buddhassa ānubhāvaina, sattāhaṃ na vigacchatha||

31.

 ‘‘tainaiva suk‍kamūlaina, anubhautvāna sampadā|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, tiṇṇau laukai visattikaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘aikārasai  kappasatai, pañ‍catiṃsāsu khattiyā|

ambaraṃsasanāmā tai, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

33.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pupphacchadaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pupphacchadaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. rahausañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

34.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, vasabhau nāma pabbatau|

tasmiṃ pabbatapādamhi, assamau āsi māpitau||

35.

 ‘‘tīṇi sissasahassāni, vācaisiṃ brāhmaṇau brāhmaṇai (sī॰) tadā|

saṃharitvāna saṃsāvitvāna (syā॰) tai sissai, aikamantaṃ upāvisiṃ||

36.

 ‘‘aikamantaṃ  nisīditvā, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū|

buddhavaidaṃ gavaisantau pavaisantau (ka॰), ñāṇai cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

37.

 ‘‘tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā, nisīdiṃ paṇṇasantharai|

pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvāna, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ñāṇasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘sattavīsatikappamhi, rājā siridharau ahu|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

40.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā rahausañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

rahausañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. campakapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

41.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva jautantaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai|

aubhāsaintaṃ disā sabbā, ausadhiṃ viya tārakaṃ ausadhī viya tārakā (ka॰)||

42.

 ‘‘tayau  māṇavakā āsuṃ, sakai sippai susikkhitā|

khāribhāraṃ gahaitvāna, anvainti mama pacchatau||

43.

 ‘‘puṭakai  satta pupphāni, nikkhittāni tapassinā|

gahaitvā tāni ñāṇamhi, vaissabhussābhiraupayiṃ||

44.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ñāṇapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘aikūnatiṃsakappamhi, vipulābha vihatābhā (syā॰) sanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī rājā hausi (ka॰) mahabbalau||

46.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā campakapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

campakapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. atthasandassakatthairaapadānaṃ

47.

 ‘‘visālamāḷai  āsīnau, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

khīṇāsavaṃ balappattaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ||

48.

 ‘‘satasahassā  taivij‍jā, chaḷabhiñ‍ñā mahiddhikā|

parivārainti sambuddhaṃ, kau disvā nappasīdati||

49.

 ‘‘ñāṇai upanidhā yassa, na vij‍jati sadaivakai|

anantañāṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, kau disvā nappasīdati||

50.

 ‘‘dhammakāyañ‍ca  dīpaintaṃ, kaivalaṃ ratanākaraṃ|

vikappaituṃ vikaupaituṃ (sī॰ syā॰) na sak‍kaunti, kau disvā nappasīdati||

51.

 ‘‘imāhi tīhi gāthāhi, nāradauvhayavacchalau saragacchiyau (sī॰), puragacchiyau (syā॰)|

padumuttaraṃ thavitvāna, sambuddhaṃ aparājitaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, buddhasanthavanaina ca|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ, nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘itau tiṃsakappasatai, sumittau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

54.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā atthasandassakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

atthasandassakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. aikapasādaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

55.

 ‘‘nāradau iti mai nāmaṃ nāradau iti nāmaina (syā॰ ka॰) upari taivīsatimavaggai pana chaṭṭhāpadānai ‘‘mai nāmaṃ‘‘ic‍caiva dissati, kaisavau iti maṃ vidū|

kusalākusalaṃ aisaṃ, agamaṃ buddhasantikaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘maittacittau kāruṇikau, atthadassī mahāmuni|

assāsayantau sattai sau, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā||

57.

 ‘‘sakaṃ  cittaṃ pasādaitvā, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

satthāraṃ abhivādaitvā, pak‍kāmiṃ pācināmukhau||

58.

 ‘‘sattarasai  kappasatai, rājā āsi mahīpati|

amittatāpanau nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

59.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikapasādaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikapasādaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sālapupphadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

60.

 ‘‘migarājā tadā āsiṃ, abhijātau sukaisarī|

giriduggaṃ gavaisantau, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

61.

 ‘‘ayaṃ nu khau mahāvīrau, nibbāpaiti mahājanaṃ|

yaṃnūnāhaṃ upāsaiyyaṃ, daivadaivaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

62.

 ‘‘sākhaṃ sālassa bhañ‍jitvā, sakausaṃ pupphamāhariṃ|

upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ, adāsiṃ pupphamuttamaṃ||

63.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

64.

 ‘‘itau  ca navamai kappai, viraucanasanāmakā|

tayau āsiṃsu rājānau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

65.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sālapupphadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sālapupphadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. piyālaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

66.
