 ‘‘pārāvatau  paraudhakau (syā॰) tadā āsiṃ, paraṃ anuparaudhakau|

pabbhārai saiyyaṃ kappaimi, avidūrai sikhisatthunau||

67.

 ‘‘sāyaṃ pātañ‍ca passāmi, buddhaṃ laukagganāyakaṃ|

daiyyadhammau ca mai natthi, dvipadindassa tādinau||

68.

 ‘‘piyālaphalamādāya, agamaṃ buddhasantikaṃ|

paṭiggahaisi bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau||

69.

 ‘‘tatau paraṃ upādāya, paricāriṃ vināyakaṃ|

taina cittappasādaina, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

70.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

71.

 ‘‘itau  pan‍narasai kappai, tayau āsuṃ piyālinau|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

72.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā piyālaphaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

piyālaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

saubhitavaggau cuddasamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

saubhitasudassanau ca, candanau pupphachadanau|

rahau campakapupphī ca, atthasandassakaina ca||

aikapasādī aikaraṃsi (syā॰) sāladadau, dasamau phaladāyakau|

gāthāyau sattati dvai ca, gaṇitāyau vibhāvibhi||


15. chattavaggau

1. atichattiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘parinibbutai  bhagavati, atthadassīnaruttamai|

chattātichattaṃ chattādhichattaṃ (sī॰) kāraitvā, thūpamhi abhiraupayiṃ||

2.

 ‘‘kālaina kālamāgantvā, namassiṃ laukanāyakaṃ satthu caitiyaṃ (sī॰)|

pupphacchadanaṃ katvāna, chattamhi abhiraupayiṃ||

3.

 ‘‘sattarasai kappasatai, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

manussattaṃ na gacchāmi, thūpapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā atichattiyau adhichattiyau (sī॰ syā॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

atichattiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. thambhāraupakatthairaapadānaṃ

5.

 ‘‘nibbutai  laukanāthamhi, dhammadassīnarāsabhai|

āraupaisiṃ dhajatthambhaṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhassa caitiyai||

6.

 ‘‘nissaiṇiṃ māpayitvāna, thūpasaiṭṭhaṃ samāruhiṃ|

jātipupphaṃ gahaitvāna, thūpamhi abhiraupayiṃ||

7.

 ‘‘ahau  buddhau ahau dhammau, ahau nau satthu sampadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, thūpapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, thūpasīkhasanāmakā|

sauḷasāsiṃsu rājānau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

9.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā thambhāraupakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

thambhāraupakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. vaidikārakatthairaapadānaṃ

10.

 ‘‘nibbutai laukanāthamhi, piyadassīnaruttamai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, muttāvaidimakāsahaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘maṇīhi parivāraitvā, akāsiṃ vaidimuttamaṃ|

vaidikāya mahaṃ katvā, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

maṇī dhārainti ākāsai, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

13.

 ‘‘sauḷasitau kappasatai, maṇippabhāsanāmakā|

chattiṃsāsiṃsu bāttiṃsāsiṃsu (sī॰ syā॰) rājānau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

14.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vaidikārakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

vaidikārakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. saparivāriyatthairaapadānaṃ

15.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

jalitvā aggikkhandhauva, sambuddhau parinibbutau||

16.

 ‘‘nibbutai ca mahāvīrai, thūpau vitthārikau ahu|

dūratauva ahaurattaṃ (sī॰), thūpadattaṃ (syā॰) upaṭṭhainti, dhātugaihavaruttamai||

17.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, akaṃ candanavaidikaṃ|

dissati thūpakhandhau ca dīyati dhūmakkhandhau ca (sī॰), dīyati dhūpagandhau ca (syā॰), thūpānucchavikau tadā||

18.

 ‘‘bhavai  nibbattamānamhi, daivattai atha mānusai|

aumattaṃ mai na passāmi, pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘pañ‍cadasakappasatai, itau aṭṭha janā ahuṃ|

sabbai samattanāmā tai, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

20.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā saparivāriyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saparivāriyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. umāpupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

21.

 ‘‘nibbutai laukamahitai laukanāthamhi (sī॰), āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahai|

siddhatthamhi bhagavati, mahāthūpamahau ahu||

22.

 ‘‘mahai pavattamānamhi, siddhatthassa mahaisinau|

umāpupphaṃ ummāpupphaṃ (sabbattha) gahaitvāna, thūpamhi abhiraupayiṃ||

23.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, thūpapūjāyidaṃ pupphapūjāyidaṃ (syā॰), buddhapūjāyidaṃ (ka॰) phalaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘itau ca navamai kappai, saumadaivasanāmakā|

pañ‍cāsītisu rājānau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

25.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā umāpupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

umāpupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. anulaipadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

26.

 ‘‘anaumadassīmuninau, baudhivaidimakāsahaṃ|

sudhāya piṇḍaṃ datvāna, pāṇikammaṃ akāsahaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘disvā  taṃ sukataṃ kammaṃ, anaumadassī naruttamau|

bhikkhusaṅghai ṭhitau satthā, imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha||

28.

 ‘‘‘iminā sudhakammaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

sampattiṃ anubhautvāna, dukkhassantaṃ karissati’||

29.

 ‘‘pasan‍namukhavaṇṇaumhi, aikaggau susamāhitau|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

30.

 ‘‘itau kappasatai āsiṃ, paripuṇṇai anūnakai paripuṇṇau anūnakau (syā॰)|

rājā sabbaghanau nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

31.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā anulaipadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

anulaipadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. maggadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

32.

 ‘‘uttaritvāna  nadikaṃ, vanaṃ gacchati cakkhumā|

tamaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, siddhatthaṃ varalakkhaṇaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘kudāla kuddāla (sī॰ syā॰) piṭakamādāya, samaṃ katvāna taṃ pathaṃ|

satthāraṃ abhivādaitvā, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

34.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, maggadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘sattapañ‍ñāsakappamhi, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

nāmaina suppabuddhauti, nāyakau sau narissarau||

36.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā maggadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

maggadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. phalakadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

37.

 ‘‘yānakārau  purai āsiṃ, dārukammai susikkhitau|

candanaṃ phalakaṃ katvā, adāsiṃ laukabandhunau||

38.

 ‘‘pabhāsati  idaṃ byamhaṃ, suvaṇṇassa sunimmitaṃ|

hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ, dibbayānaṃ upaṭṭhitaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘pāsādā  sivikā caiva, nibbattanti yadicchakaṃ|

akkhubbhaṃ akkhaubhaṃ (sī॰) ratanaṃ mayhaṃ, phalakassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, phalakaṃ yamahaṃ dadiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phalakassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘sattapañ‍ñāsakappamhi, caturau nimmitāvhayā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

42.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phalakadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

phalakadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vaṭaṃsakiyatthairaapadānaṃ

43.

 ‘‘sumaidhau nāma nāmaina, sayambhū aparājitau|

vivaikamanubrūhantau, ajjhaugahi mahāvanaṃ||

44.

 ‘‘saḷalaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, ganthitvāna bandhitvāna (sī॰) vaṭaṃsakaṃ|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, sammukhā laukanāyakaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘ūnavīsai kappasatai, sauḷasāsuṃ sunimmitā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

47.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vaṭaṃsakiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

vaṭaṃsakiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. pal‍laṅkadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

48.

 ‘‘sumaidhassa  bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

pal‍laṅkau hi mayā din‍nau, sauttarasapacchadau||

49.

 ‘‘sattaratanasampan‍nau, pal‍laṅkau āsi sau tadā|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, nibbattati sadā mama||

50.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, pal‍laṅkamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pal‍laṅkassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

51.

 ‘‘vīsakappasahassamhi, suvaṇṇābhā tayau janā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

52.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pal‍laṅkadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pal‍laṅkadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

chattavaggau pan‍narasamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

chattaṃ thambhau ca vaidi ca, parivārumapupphiyau|

anulaipau ca maggau ca, phalakau ca vaṭaṃsakau|

pal‍laṅkadāyī ca gāthāyau, chappañ‍ñāsa pakittitāti||


16. bandhujīvakavaggau

1. bandhujīvakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘candaṃva  vimalaṃ suddhaṃ, vippasan‍namanāvilaṃ|

nandībhavaparikkhīṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ laukai visattikaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘nibbāpayantaṃ janataṃ, tiṇṇaṃ disvā (?) tārayataṃ varaṃ tārayataṃ muniṃ (syā॰)|

muniṃ vanamhi jhāyantaṃ vanasmiṃ jhāyamānaṃ taṃ (sī॰ syā॰), aikaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘bandhujīvakapupphāni, lagaitvā suttakainahaṃ|

buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ, sikhinau laukabandhunau||

4.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘itau sattamakai kappai, manujindau mahāyasau|

samantacakkhu nāmāsi, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

6.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bandhujīvakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

bandhujīvakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. tambapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

7.

 ‘‘parakammāyanai  yuttau, aparādhaṃ akāsahaṃ|

vanantaṃ abhidhāvissaṃ, bhayavairasamappitau||

8.

 ‘‘pupphitaṃ pādapaṃ disvā, piṇḍibandhaṃ sunimmitaṃ|

tambapupphaṃ gahaitvāna, baudhiyaṃ aukiriṃ ahaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘sammaj‍jitvāna  taṃ baudhiṃ, pāṭaliṃ pādaputtamaṃ|

pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvāna, baudhimūlai upāvisiṃ||

10.

 ‘‘gatamaggaṃ  gavaisantā, āgacchuṃ mama santikaṃ|

tai ca disvānahaṃ tattha, āvaj‍jiṃ baudhimuttamaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘vanditvāna ahaṃ baudhiṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

anaikatālai papatiṃ, giriduggai bhayānakai||

12.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, baudhipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

13.

 ‘‘itau ca tatiyai kappai, rājā susañ‍ñatau ahaṃ saṃthusitau ahuṃ (sī॰)|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

14.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tambapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tambapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. vīthisammaj‍jakatthairaapadānaṃ

15.

 ‘‘udaintaṃ  sataraṃsiṃva, pītaraṃsiṃva sitaraṃsiṃva (sī॰ syā॰) bhāṇumaṃ|

pan‍narasai yathā candaṃ, niyyantaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘aṭṭhasaṭṭhisahassāni, sabbai khīṇāsavā ahuṃ|

parivāriṃsu sambuddhaṃ, dvipadindaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘sammaj‍jitvāna taṃ vīthiṃ, niyyantai laukanāyakai|

ussāpaisiṃ dhajaṃ tattha, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

18.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ dhajaṃ abhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘itau catutthakai kappai, rājāhausiṃ mahabbalau|

sabbākāraina sampan‍nau, sudhajau iti vissutau||

20.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vīthisammaj‍jakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

vīthisammaj‍jakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. kak‍kārupupphapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

21.

 ‘‘daivaputtau  ahaṃ santau, pūjayiṃ sikhināyakaṃ|

kak‍kārupupphaṃ paggayha, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

22.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘itau ca navamai kappai, rājā sattuttamau ahuṃ|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

24.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kak‍kārupupphapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kak‍kārupupphapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. mandāravapupphapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

25.

 ‘‘daivaputtau  ahaṃ santau, pūjayiṃ sikhināyakaṃ|

mandāravaina pupphaina, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

26.

 ‘‘sattāhaṃ chadanaṃ āsi, dibbaṃ mālaṃ tathāgatai|

sabbai janā samāgantvā, namassiṃsu tathāgataṃ||

27.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘itau ca dasamai kappai, rājāhausiṃ jutindharau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mandāravapupphapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

mandāravapupphapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kadambapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, kuk‍kuṭau nāma pabbatau|

tamhi pabbatapādamhi, satta buddhā vasanti tai||

31.

 ‘‘kadambaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, dīparājaṃva uggataṃ|

ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, satta buddhai samaukiriṃ||

32.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, sattāsuṃ pupphanāmakā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

34.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kadambapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kadambapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tiṇasūlakatthairaapadānaṃ

35.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, bhūtagaṇau nāma pabbatau|

vasataikau jinau tattha, sayambhū laukanissaṭau||

36.

 ‘‘tiṇasūlaṃ gahaitvāna, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ|

aikūnasatasahassaṃ, kappaṃ na vinipātikau||

37.

 ‘‘itau aikādasai kappai, aikausiṃ dharaṇīruhau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

38.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇasūlakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tiṇasūlakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. nāgapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

39.

 ‘‘suvacchau  nāma nāmaina, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū|

purakkhatau sasissaihi, vasatai pabbatantarai||

40.

 ‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mamuddharitukāmau sau, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘vaihāsamhi  caṅkamati, dhūpāyati jalatai tathā|

hāsaṃ mamaṃ viditvāna, pak‍kāmi pācināmukhau||

42.

 ‘‘tañ‍ca acchariyaṃ disvā, abbhutaṃ laumahaṃsanaṃ|

nāgapupphaṃ gahaitvāna, gatamaggamhi aukiriṃ||

43.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ pupphaṃ aukiriṃ ahaṃ|

taina cittappasādaina, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

44.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai kappasatai aikatiṃsai itau kammai (syā॰), rājā āsi mahārahau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

45.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nāgapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

nāgapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pun‍nāgapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

46.

 ‘‘kānanaṃ  vanamaugayha, vasāmi luddakau ahaṃ|

pun‍nāgaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ||

47.

 ‘‘taṃ  pupphaṃ aucinitvāna, sugandhaṃ gandhitaṃ subhaṃ|

thūpaṃ karitvā pulinai, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

48.

 ‘‘dvainavutai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘aikamhi navutai kappai, aikau āsiṃ tamaunudau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

50.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pun‍nāgapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pun‍nāgapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. kumudadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

51.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, mahājātassarau ahu|

padumuppalasañchan‍nau, puṇḍarīkasamautthaṭau||

52.

 ‘‘kukutthau nāma nāmaina, tatthāsiṃ sakuṇau tadā|

sīlavā buddhisampan‍nau, puñ‍ñāpuñ‍ñaisu kauvidau||

53.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

jātassarassāvidūrai, sañ‍carittha mahāmuni||

54.

 ‘‘jalajaṃ kumudaṃ chaitvā, upanaisiṃ mahaisinau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, paṭiggahi mahāmuni||

55.

 ‘‘tañ‍ca  dānaṃ daditvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘sauḷasaitau kappasatai, āsuṃ varuṇanāmakā|

aṭṭha aitai janādhipā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

57.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kumudadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kumudadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

bandhujīvakavaggau sauḷasamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

bandhujīvau tambapupphī, vīthikak‍kārupupphiyau|

mandāravau kadambī ca, sūlakau nāgapupphiyau|

pun‍nāgau kaumudī gāthā, chappañ‍ñāsa pakittitāti||


17. supāricariyavaggau

1. supāricariyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘padumau  nāma nāmaina, dvipadindau narāsabhau|

pavanā abhinikkhamma, dhammaṃ daisaiti cakkhumā||

2.

 ‘‘yakkhānaṃ samayau āsi, avidūrai mahaisinau|

yaina kic‍caina sampattā, ajjhāpaikkhiṃsu tāvadai||

3.

 ‘‘buddhassa giramañ‍ñāya, amatassa ca daisanaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, apphauṭaitvā upaṭṭhahiṃ||

4.

 ‘‘suciṇṇassa phalaṃ passa, upaṭṭhānassa satthunau|

tiṃsakappasahassaisu, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘ūnatiṃsai kappasatai, samalaṅkatanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

6.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā supāricariyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

supāricariyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. kaṇavairapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

7.

 ‘‘siddhatthau  nāma bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

purakkhatau sāvakaihi, nagaraṃ paṭipaj‍jatha||

8.

 ‘‘rañ‍ñau antaipurai āsiṃ, gaupakau abhisammatau|

pāsādai upaviṭṭhauhaṃ, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘kaṇavairaṃ  karavīraṃ (sak‍katānulaumaṃ), kaṇavīraṃ (pākata) gahaitvāna, bhikkhusaṅghai samaukiriṃ|

buddhassa visuṃ katvāna, tatau bhiyyau samaukiriṃ||

10.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ raupayiṃ (syā॰)|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘sattāsītimhitau kappai, caturāsuṃ mahiddhikā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kaṇavairapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kaṇavairapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. khaj‍jakadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘tissassa khau bhagavatau, pubbai phalamadāsahaṃ|

nāḷikairañ‍ca pādāsiṃ, khaj‍jakaṃ abhisammataṃ||

14.

 ‘‘buddhassa  tamahaṃ datvā, tissassa tu mahaisinau|

maudāmahaṃ kāmakāmī, upapaj‍jiṃ kāmakārī, upapaj‍jaṃ (sī॰) yamicchakaṃ sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati||

15.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘itau tairasakappamhi, rājā indasamau ahu|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

17.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā khaj‍jakadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

khaj‍jakadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. daisapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

18.

 ‘‘atthadassī  tu bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

abbhuggantvāna vaihāsaṃ, gacchatai anilañ‍jasai||

19.

 ‘‘yamhi  daisai ṭhitau satthā, abbhuggacchi mahāmuni|

tāhaṃ daisaṃ apūjaisiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

20.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, addasaṃ yaṃ mahāmuniṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, daisapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘aikādasai  kappasatai, gausujātasanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

22.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā daisapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

daisapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. kaṇikārachattiyatthairaapadānaṃ

23.

 ‘‘vaissabhū nāma sambuddhau, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

divāvihārāya muni, augāhayi mahāvanaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃ aucinitvā, chattaṃ katvānahaṃ tadā|

pupphacchadanaṃ katvāna, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

25.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘itau vīsatikappamhi, sauṇṇābhā aṭṭha khattiyā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

27.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā kaṇikārachattiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kaṇikārachattiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sappidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

28.

 ‘‘phussau  nāmāsi nāmātha (sī॰) bhagavā, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

gacchatai vīthiyaṃ vīrau, nibbāpaintau mahājanaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘anupubbaina  bhagavā, āgacchi mama santikaṃ|

tatau taṃ tatauhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) pattaṃ paggayha, sappitailamadāsahaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ sappimadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sappidānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘chappañ‍ñāsai itau kappai, aikau āsi samaudakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

32.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sappidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sappidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. yūthikapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

33.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, anusautaṃ vajāmahaṃ|

sayambhuṃ addasaṃ tattha, sālarājaṃva phul‍litaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘pupphaṃ yūthikamādāya, upagacchiṃ mahāmuniṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

35.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘sattasaṭṭhimhitau kappai, aikau sāmuddharau ahu|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

37.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā yūthikapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

yūthikapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. dussadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

38.

 ‘‘tivarāyaṃ  purai rammai, rājaputtausahaṃ rājaputtau ahaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) tadā|

paṇṇākāraṃ labhitvāna, upasantassadāsahaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘adhivāsaisi  bhagavā, vatthaṃ navaṃ (ka॰) hatthaina āmasi|

siddhatthau adhivāsaitvā, vaihāsaṃ nabhamuggami||

40.

 ‘‘buddhassa gacchamānassa, dussā dhāvanti pacchatau|

tattha cittaṃ pasādaisiṃ, buddhau nau aggapuggalau||

41.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ dussamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dussadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘sattasaṭṭhimhitau kappai, cak‍kavattī tadā ahu|

parisuddhauti nāmaina, manujindau mahabbalau||

43.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dussadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

dussadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. samādapakatthairaapadānaṃ

44.

 ‘‘nagarai bandhumatiyā, mahāpūgagaṇau ahu|

taisāhaṃ pavarau āsiṃ, mama baddhacarā paṭṭhacarā (syā॰) ca tai||

45.

 ‘‘sabbai tai san‍nipātaitvā, puñ‍ñakammai samādayiṃ|

māḷaṃ kassāma saṅghassa, puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ anuttaraṃ||

46.

 ‘‘sādhūti  tai paṭissutvā, mama chandavasānugā|

niṭṭhāpaitvā ca taṃ māḷaṃ, vipassissa adamhasai||

47.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ māḷamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, māḷadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘aikūnasattatikappai  aikūnasaṭṭhikappamhi (sī॰ syā॰),

aikau āsi janādhipau|

ādaiyyau nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

49.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā samādapakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

samādapakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. pañ‍caṅguliyatthairaapadānaṃ

50.

 ‘‘tissau  nāmāsi bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

pavisati gandhakuṭiṃ, vihārakusalau muni||

51.

 ‘‘sugandhamālamādāya, agamāsiṃ jinantikaṃ|

apasaddau ca sambuddhai, pañ‍caṅgulimadāsahaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ gandhamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pañ‍caṅgulissidaṃ pañ‍caṅguliyidaṃ (sī॰) phalaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘dvaisattatimhitau  kappai, rājā āsiṃ sayampabhau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

54.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pañ‍caṅguliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pañ‍caṅguliyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

supāricariyavaggau sattarasamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

supāricari kaṇavairī, khaj‍jakau daisapūjakau|

kaṇikārau sappidadau, yūthikau dussadāyakau|

māḷau ca pañ‍caṅgulikau, catupañ‍ñāsa gāthakāti||


18. kumudavaggau

1. kumudamāliyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘pabbatai  himavantamhi, mahājātassarau ahu|

tatthajau rakkhasau āsiṃ, ghaurarūpau mahabbalau||

2.

 ‘‘kumudaṃ pupphatai tattha, cak‍kamattāni jāyarai|

aucināmi ca taṃ pupphaṃ, balinau samitiṃ tadā||

3.

 ‘‘atthadassī  tu bhagavā, dvipadindau narāsabhau|

pupphasaṅkaucitaṃ pupphaṃ saṅkaucitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), pupphaṃ samaucitaṃ (?) disvā, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘upāgatañ‍ca sambuddhaṃ, daivadaivaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

sabbañ‍ca pupphaṃ paggayha, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

5.

 ‘‘yāvatā himavantantā, parisā sā himavantasmiṃ, yāva mālā (syā॰) tadā ahu|

tāvacchadanasampan‍nau, agamāsi tathāgatau||

6.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘itau pan‍narasai kappai, sattāhaisuṃ janādhipā|

sahassarathanāmā tai, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

8.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā kumudamāliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kumudamāliyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. nissaiṇidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

9.

 ‘‘kauṇḍañ‍ñassa  bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

ārauhatthāya pāsādaṃ, nissaiṇī kāritā mayā||

10.

 ‘‘taina  cittappasādaina, anubhautvāna sampadā|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

11.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsamhi kappānaṃ, sahassamhi tayau ahuṃ mahā (sī॰ syā॰)|

sambahulā nāma rājānau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nissaiṇidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

nissaiṇidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. rattipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘migaluddau  purai āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai kānanai ahaṃ|

vipassiṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ, daivadaivaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘rattikaṃ  pupphitaṃ disvā, kuṭajaṃ dharaṇīruhaṃ|

samūlaṃ paggahaitvāna, upanaisiṃ mahaisinau||

15.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘itau ca aṭṭhamai kappai, suppasan‍nasanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, rājāhausiṃ mahabbalau||

17.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā rattipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

rattipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. udapānadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

18.

 ‘‘vipassinau  bhagavatau, udapānau katau mayā|

piṇḍapātañ‍ca datvāna gahaitvāna (syā॰), niyyādaisimahaṃ tadā||

19.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, udapānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā udapānadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

udapānadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. sīhāsanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

21.

 ‘‘nibbutai laukanāthamhi, padumuttaranāyakai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, sīhāsanamadāsahaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘bahūhi gandhamālaihi, diṭṭhadhammasukhāvahai|

tattha pūjañ‍ca katvāna, nibbāyati bahuj‍janau||

23.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau  sumanau, vanditvā baudhimuttamaṃ|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘pan‍narasasahassamhi, kappānaṃ aṭṭha āsu tai aṭṭha āsayuṃ (ka॰)|

siluc‍cayasanāmā ca, rājānau cak‍kavattinau||

25.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sīhāsanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sīhāsanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. maggadattikatthairaapadānaṃ

26.

 ‘‘anaumadassī  bhagavā, dvipadindau narāsabhau|

diṭṭhadhammasukhatthāya, abbhaukāsamhi caṅkami||

27.

 ‘‘uddhatai  pādai pupphāni, saubhaṃ muddhani tiṭṭharai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, vanditvā pupphamaukiriṃ||

28.

 ‘‘vīsakappasahassamhi, itau pañ‍ca janā ahuṃ|

pupphacchadaniyā nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā maggadattikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

maggadattikatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. aikadīpiyatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘padumuttarassa muninau, saḷalai baudhimuttamai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, aikadīpaṃ adāsahaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘bhavai nibbattamānamhi, nibbattai puñ‍ñasañ‍cayai|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘sauḷasai  kappasahassai, itau tai caturau janā|

candābhā nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

33.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikadīpiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikadīpiyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

navamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

8. maṇipūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

34.

 ‘‘auraina  himavantassa, nadikā sampavattatha|

tassā cānupakhaittamhi, sayambhū vasatai tadā||

35.

 ‘‘maṇiṃ  paggayha pal‍laṅkaṃ, sādhucittaṃ manauramaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

36.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ maṇiṃ abhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘itau ca dvādasai kappai, sataraṃsīsanāmakā|

aṭṭha tai āsuṃ rājānau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

38.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā maṇipūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

maṇipūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tikicchakatthairaapadānaṃ

39.

 ‘‘nagarai bandhumatiyā, vaij‍jau āsiṃ susikkhitau|

āturānaṃ sadukkhānaṃ, mahājanasukhāvahau||

40.

 ‘‘byādhitaṃ samaṇaṃ disvā, sīlavantaṃ mahājutiṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, bhaisaj‍jamadadiṃ tadā||

41.

 ‘‘araugau āsi tainaiva, samaṇau saṃvutindriyau|

asaukau nāma nāmaina, upaṭṭhākau vipassinau||

42.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ ausadhamadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhaisaj‍jassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘itau ca aṭṭhamai kappai, sabbausadhasanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

44.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tikicchakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tikicchakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. saṅghupaṭṭhākatthairaapadānaṃ

45.

 ‘‘vaissabhumhi  bhagavati, ahausārāmikau ahaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, upaṭṭhiṃ saṅghamuttamaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘itau tai sattamai kappai, sattaivāsuṃ samaudakā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

48.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saṅghupaṭṭhākau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saṅghupaṭṭhākatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

kumudavaggau aṭṭhārasamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kumudau atha nissaiṇī, rattikau udapānadau|

sīhāsanī maggadadau, aikadīpī maṇippadau|

tikicchakau upaṭṭhākau, aikapañ‍ñāsa gāthakāti||


19. kuṭajapupphiyavaggau

1. kuṭajapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, sataraṃsiṃva uggataṃ|

disaṃ anuvilaukaintaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai||

2.

 ‘‘kuṭajaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, saṃvitthatasamautthataṃ|

rukkhatau aucinitvāna, phussassa abhiraupayiṃ||

3.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘itau sattarasai kappai, tayau āsuṃ supupphitā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

5.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kuṭajapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kuṭajapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. bandhujīvakatthairaapadānaṃ

6.

 ‘‘siddhatthau  nāma sambuddhau, sayambhū sabbhi vaṇṇitau|

samādhiṃ sau samāpan‍nau, nisīdi pabbatantarai||

7.

 ‘‘jātassarai  gavaisantau, dakajaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ|

bandhujīvakapupphāni, addasaṃ samanantaraṃ||

8.

 ‘‘ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, upāgacchiṃ mahāmuniṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, siddhatthassābhiraupayiṃ||

9.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘itau  cātuddasai kappai, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

samuddakappau nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

11.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bandhujīvakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

bandhujīvakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. kauṭumbariyatthairaapadānaṃ

12.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva jautantaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai|

appamaiyyaṃva udadhiṃ, vitthataṃ dharaṇiṃ yathā||

13.

 ‘‘pūjitaṃ paraitaṃ (sī॰) daivasaṅghaina, nisabhājāniyaṃ yathā|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, upāgacchiṃ naruttamaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘sattapupphāni  paggayha, kauṭumbarasamākulaṃ|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, sikhinau laukabandhunau||

15.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘itau vīsatikappamhi, mahānailasanāmakau|

aikau āsi mahātaijau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

17.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kauṭumbariyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kauṭumbariyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. pañ‍cahatthiyatthairaapadānaṃ

18.

 ‘‘tissau  nāmāsi bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

purakkhatau sāvakaihi, rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jatha||

19.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca  uppalahatthā ca, cāturā ṭhapitā mayā|

āhutiṃ dātukāmauhaṃ, paggaṇhiṃ vatasiddhiyā puttaumhi hitasiddhiyā (syā॰)||

20.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇai|

buddharaṃsīhi phuṭṭhausmi buddharaṃsyābhiphuṭṭhaumhi (sī॰), buddharaṃsābhighuṭṭhausmi (ka॰), pūjaisiṃ dvipaduttamaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘dvainavutai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘itau tairasakappamhi, pañ‍ca susabhasammatā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

23.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pañ‍cahatthiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pañ‍cahatthiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. isimuggadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

24.

 ‘‘udaintaṃ sataraṃsiṃva, pītaraṃsiṃva sitaraṃsiṃva (sī॰) bhāṇumaṃ|

kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva, padumuttaranāyakaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘isimuggāni pisitvā isisuggāni piṃsaitvā (sī॰), isimuggaṃ nimantaitvā (syā॰), madhukhuddai anīḷakai|

pāsādaiva ṭhitau santau, adāsiṃ laukabandhunau||

26.

 ‘‘aṭṭhasatasahassāni, ahaisuṃ buddhasāvakā|

sabbaisaṃ pattapūraintaṃ pattapūraṃ taṃ (sī॰), tatau cāpi bahuttaraṃ||

27.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘cattālīsamhi  sahassai, kappānaṃ aṭṭhatiṃsa tai|

isimuggasanāmā mahisamantanāmā (syā॰) tai, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā isimuggadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

isimuggadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. baudhiupaṭṭhākatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘nagarai  rammavatiyā, āsiṃ murajavādakau|

nic‍cupaṭṭhānayuttaumhi, gatauhaṃ baudhimuttamaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘sāyaṃ pātaṃ upaṭṭhitvā, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

aṭṭhārasakappasatai, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘pan‍narasai kappasatai, itau āsiṃ janādhipau|

murajau damathau (syā॰) nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

33.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā baudhiupaṭṭhākau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

baudhiupaṭṭhākatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. aikacintikatthairaapadānaṃ

34.

 ‘‘yadā  daivau daivā (ka॰) daivakāyā, cavatai cavanti (ka॰) āyusaṅkhayā|

tayau saddā niccharanti, daivānaṃ anumaudataṃ||

35.

 ‘itau bhau sugatiṃ gaccha, manussānaṃ sahabyataṃ|

manussabhūtau saddhammai, labha saddhaṃ anuttaraṃ||

36.

 ‘‘‘sā tai saddhā niviṭṭhāssa, mūlajātā patiṭṭhitā|

yāvajīvaṃ asaṃhīrā, saddhammai suppavaiditai||

37.

 ‘‘‘kāyaina  kusalaṃ katvā, vācāya kusalaṃ bahuṃ|

manasā kusalaṃ katvā, abyāpaj‍jaṃ abyāpajjhaṃ (syā॰), appamāṇaṃ (itivuttakai 83) nirūpadhiṃ||

38.

 ‘‘‘tatau aupadhikaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, katvā dānaina taṃ bahuṃ|

añ‍ñaipi mac‍cai saddhammai, brahmacariyai nivaisaya’||

39.

 ‘‘imāya anukampāya, daivā daivaṃ yadā vidū|

cavantaṃ anumaudanti, aihi daiva punappunaṃ daivapuraṃ puna (syā॰)||

40.

 ‘‘saṃvaigau mai saṃviggauhaṃ (syā॰) tadā āsi, daivasaṅghai samāgatai|

kaṃsu nāma ahaṃ yauniṃ, gamissāmi itau cutau||

41.

 ‘‘mama  saṃvaigamañ‍ñāya, samaṇau bhāvitindriyau|

mamuddharitukāmau sau, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘sumanau nāma nāmaina, padumuttarasāvakau|

atthadhammānusāsitvā, saṃvaijaisi mamaṃ tadā||

43.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ  vacanaṃ sutvā, buddhai cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

taṃ dhīraṃ abhivādaitvā, tattha kālaṃkatau ahaṃ||

44.

 ‘‘upapaj‍jiṃ sa upapaj‍jissaṃ (sī॰) tatthaiva, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikacintikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikacintikatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. tikaṇṇipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

46.

 ‘‘daivabhūtau ahaṃ santau, accharāhi purakkhatau|

pubbakammaṃ saritvāna, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ||

47.

 ‘‘tikaṇṇipupphaṃ kiṃkaṇipupphaṃ (ka॰) paggayha, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

buddhamhi abhiraupaisiṃ, vipassimhi narāsabhai||

48.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘taisattatimhitau kappai, caturāsuṃ ramuttamā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

50.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tikaṇṇipupphiyau kiṃkaṇikapupphiyau (ka॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tikaṇṇipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. aikacāriyatthairaapadānaṃ

51.

 ‘‘tāvatiṃsaisu  daivaisu, mahāghausau tadā ahu|

buddhau ca laukai nibbāti, mayañ‍camha sarāginau||

52.

 ‘‘taisaṃ saṃvaigajātānaṃ, saukasal‍lasamaṅginaṃ|

sabalaina upatthaddhau, agamaṃ buddhasantikaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘mandāravaṃ gahaitvāna, saṅgīti saṇhitaṃ (sī॰), saṅgitaṃ (syā॰) abhinimmitaṃ|

parinibbutakālamhi, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

54.

 ‘‘sabbai daivānumaudiṃsu, accharāyau ca mai tadā|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

55.

 ‘‘saṭṭhikappasahassamhi, itau sauḷasa tai janā|

mahāmal‍lajanā nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

56.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā aikacāriyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikacāriyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. tivaṇṭipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

57.

 ‘‘abhibhūtaṃ  panijjhanti abhibhūtaupanijjhanti (sī॰), sabbai saṅgamma tai mamaṃ abhibhuṃ thairaṃ panijjhāma, sabbai saṅgamma tai mayaṃ (syā॰)|

taisaṃ nijjhāyamānānaṃ, pariḷāhau ajāyatha||

58.

 ‘‘sunandau nāma nāmaina, buddhassa sāvakau tadā|

dhammadassissa muninau, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

59.

 ‘‘yai mai baddhacarā āsuṃ, tai mai pupphaṃ aduṃ tadā|

tāhaṃ pupphaṃ gahaitvāna, sāvakai abhiraupayiṃ||

60.

 ‘‘sauhaṃ kālaṃkatau tattha, punāpi upapaj‍jahaṃ|

aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, vinipātaṃ na gacchahaṃ||

61.

 ‘‘tairasaitau  kappasatai, aṭṭhāsuṃ dhūmakaitunau|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

62.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tivaṇṭipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tivaṇṭipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

kuṭajapupphiyavaggau aikūnavīsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kuṭajau  bandhujīvī ca, kauṭumbarikahatthiyau|

isimuggau ca baudhi ca, aikacintī tikaṇṇikau|

aikacārī tivaṇṭi ca, gāthā dvāsaṭṭhi kittitāti||


20. tamālapupphiyavaggau

1. tamālapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘cul‍lāsītisahassāni, thambhā sauvaṇṇayā ahū|

daivalaṭṭhipaṭibhāgaṃ, vimānaṃ mai sunimmitaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘tamālapupphaṃ paggayha, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ, sikhinau laukabandhunau||

3.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘itau vīsatimai kappai, candatittauti aikakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

5.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā tamālapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tamālapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. tiṇasanthārakatthairaapadānaṃ

6.

 ‘‘yadā  vanavāsī yaṃ dāyavāsikau (sī॰) isi, tiṇaṃ lāyati satthunau|

sabbai padakkhiṇāvaṭṭā padakkhiṇāvattā (sī॰ syā॰), pathabyā puthavyā (sī॰) nipatiṃsu tai||

7.

 ‘‘tamahaṃ tiṇamādāya, santhariṃ dharaṇuttamai|

tīṇaiva tālapattāni, āharitvānahaṃ tadā||

8.

 ‘‘tiṇaina  chadanaṃ katvā, siddhatthassa adāsahaṃ|

sattāhaṃ dhārayuṃ tassa tattha (syā॰), daivamānusasatthunau||

9.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ tiṇaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, tiṇadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘pañ‍casaṭṭhimhitau  kappai, cattārausuṃ mahaddhanā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

11.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇasanthārakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tiṇasanthārakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. khaṇḍapul‍liyatthairaapadānaṃ

12.

 ‘‘phussassa  khau bhagavatau, thūpau āsi mahāvanai|

kuñ‍jaraihi tadā bhin‍nau, parūḷhau pādapau parūḷhapādapau (sī॰), saṃrūḷhau pādapau (syā॰) tahiṃ||

13.

 ‘‘visamañ‍ca samaṃ katvā, sudhāpiṇḍaṃ adāsahaṃ|

tilaukagarunau tassa, guṇaihi paritausitau paritau sutau (ka॰)||

14.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sudhāpiṇḍassidaṃ phalaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘sattasattatikappamhi, jitasaināsuṃ sauḷasa|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

16.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā khaṇḍaphul‍liyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

khaṇḍapul‍liyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. asaukapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

17.

 ‘‘tivarāyaṃ  tipurāyaṃ (syā॰) purai rammai, rājuyyānaṃ ahu tadā|

uyyānapālau tatthāsiṃ, rañ‍ñau baddhacarau ahaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘padumau  nāma nāmaina, sayambhū sappabhau ahu|

nisin‍naṃ nisin‍nau (ka॰) puṇḍarīkamhi, chāyā na jahi taṃ muniṃ||

19.

 ‘‘asaukaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, piṇḍibhāraṃ sudassanaṃ|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, jalajuttamanāminau||

20.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘sattatiṃsamhitau kappai, sauḷasa araṇañ‍jahā aruṇañ‍jahā (sī॰)|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

22.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā asaukapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

asaukapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. aṅkaulakatthairaapadānaṃ

23.

 ‘‘aṅkaulaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, mālāvaraṃ sakausakaṃ samaugadhaṃ (syā॰)|

aucinitvāna taṃ pupphaṃ, agamaṃ buddhasantikaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘siddhatthau tamhi samayai, patilīnau mahāmuni|

muhuttaṃ paṭimānaitvā, guhāyaṃ pupphamaukiriṃ||

25.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphadānassidaṃ buddhapūjāyidaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) phalaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘chattiṃsamhi itau kappai, āsaikau daivagaj‍jitau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

27.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aṅkaulakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aṅkaulakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kisalayapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

28.

 ‘‘nagarai  dvāravatiyā, mālāvacchau mamaṃ ahu|

udapānau ca tatthaiva, pādapānaṃ virauhanau||

29.

 ‘‘sabalaina upatthaddhau, siddhatthau aparājitau|

mamānukampamānau sau, gacchatai anilañ‍jasai||

30.

 ‘‘añ‍ñaṃ kiñ‍ci na passāmi, pūjāyauggaṃ mahaisinau|

asaukaṃ pal‍lavaṃ disvā, ākāsai ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘buddhassa tai kisalayā, gacchatau yanti pacchatau|

tāhaṃ disvāna saṃvijiṃ sauhaṃ disvāna taṃ iddhiṃ (sī॰ syā॰), ahau buddhassuḷāratā||

32.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, pal‍lavaṃ abhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘sattatiṃsai sattavīsai (sī॰ syā॰) itau kappai, aikau aikissarau ahu|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

34.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kisalayapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kisalayapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tindukadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

35.

 ‘‘giriduggacarau āsiṃ, mak‍kaṭau thāmavaigikau|

phalinaṃ tindukaṃ disvā, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ||

36.

 ‘‘nikkhamitvā katipāhaṃ, viciniṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, siddhatthaṃ tibhavantaguṃ||

37.

 ‘‘mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukai anuttarau|

khīṇāsavasahassaihi, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘pāmauj‍jaṃ  janayitvāna, phalahatthau upāgamiṃ|

paṭiggahaisi bhagavā, sabbañ‍ñū vadataṃ varau||

39.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘sattapañ‍ñāsakappamhi, upanandasanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

41.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tindukadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tindukadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. muṭṭhipūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

42.

 ‘‘sumaidhau nāma bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

pacchimai anukampāya, padhānaṃ padahī jinau||

43.

 ‘‘tassa caṅkamamānassa, dvipadindassa tādinau|

girinailassa pupphānaṃ, muṭṭhiṃ buddhassa raupayiṃ||

44.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

tiṃsakappasahassāni, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘taivīsatikappasatai, sunailau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, aikau āsiṃ mahabbalau||

46.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā muṭṭhipūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

muṭṭhipūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kiṃkaṇikapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

47.

 ‘‘sumaṅgalauti nāmaina, sayambhū aparājitau|

pavanā nikkhamitvāna, nagaraṃ pāvisī jinau||

48.

 ‘‘piṇḍacāraṃ  caritvāna, nikkhamma nagarā muni|

katakic‍cauva sambuddhau, sau vasī vanamantarai||

49.

 ‘‘kiṃkaṇipupphaṃ  paggayha, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, sayambhussa mahaisinau||

50.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

51.

 ‘‘chaḷāsītimhitau kappai, apilāsisanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

52.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā kiṃkaṇikapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kiṃkaṇikapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. yūthikapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

53.

 ‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

pavanā nikkhamitvāna, vihāraṃ yāti cakkhumā||

54.

 ‘‘ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, yūthikaṃ pupphamuttamaṃ|

buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ, maittacittassa tādinau||

55.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, anubhautvāna sampadā|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘itau pañ‍ñāsakappaisu, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

samittanandanau nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

57.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā yūthikapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

yūthikapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

tamālapupphiyavaggau vīsatimau|

tassuddānaṃ  –

tamālatiṇasanthārau, khaṇḍaphul‍li asaukiyau|

aṅkaulakī kisalayau, tindukau nailapupphiyau|

kiṃkaṇikau yūthikau ca, gāthā pañ‍ñāsa aṭṭha cāti||

atha  vagguddānaṃ –

bhikkhādāyī  parivārau, sairaiyyau saubhitau tathā|

chattañ‍ca bandhujīvī ca, supāricariyaupi ca||

kumudau kuṭajau caiva, tamāli dasamau katau|

chasatāni ca gāthāni, chasaṭṭhi ca tatuttari||

bhikkhāvaggadasakaṃ|


dutiyasatakaṃ samattaṃ|


21. kaṇikārapupphiyavaggau

1. kaṇikārapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃ  pupphitaṃ disvā, aucinitvānahaṃ tadā|

tissassa abhiraupaisiṃ, aughatiṇṇassa tādinau||

2.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘pañ‍cattiṃsai itau kappai, aruṇapāṇīti vissutau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

4.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kaṇikārapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kaṇikārapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. minailapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

5.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇau bhagavā, sataraṃsī patāpavā|

caṅkamanaṃ samārūḷhau, maittacittau sikhīsabhau||

6.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, vanditvā thaumaitvā (syā॰) ñāṇamuttamaṃ|

minailapupphaṃ paggayha, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

7.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘aikūnatiṃsakappamhi, sumaighaghananāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

9.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā minailapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

minailapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. kiṅkaṇipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

10.

 ‘‘kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsau, sabbañ‍ñū laukanāyakau|

audakaṃ dahamauggayha, sināyi laukanāyakau||

11.

 ‘‘paggayha kiṅkaṇiṃ kiṅkiṇiṃ (sī॰) pupphaṃ, vipassissābhiraupayiṃ|

udaggacittau sumanau, dvipadindassa tādinau||

12.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

13.

 ‘‘sattavīsatikappamhi, rājā bhīmarathau ahu|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

14.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kiṅkaṇipupphiyau kiṅkiṇikapupphiyau (sī॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kiṅkaṇipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. taraṇiyatthairaapadānaṃ

15.

 ‘‘atthadassī tu bhagavā, dvipadindau narāsabhau|

purakkhatau sāvakaihi, gaṅgātīramupāgami||

16.

 ‘‘samatitti kākapaiyyā, gaṅgā āsi duruttarā|

uttārayiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ, buddhañ‍ca dvipaduttamaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai  kappasatai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, taraṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘tairasaitau  kappasatai, pañ‍ca sabbaubhavā sabbhaugavā (sī॰) ahuṃ|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

19.

 ‘‘pacchimai  ca bhavai asmiṃ, jātauhaṃ brāhmaṇai kulai|

saddhiṃ tīhi sahāyaihi, pabbajiṃ satthu sāsanai||

20.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā taraṇiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

taraṇiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. nigguṇḍipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

21.

 ‘‘vipassissa  bhagavatau, āsimārāmikau ahaṃ|

nigguṇḍipupphaṃ paggayha, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

22.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘pañ‍cavīsai pañ‍catiṃsai (sī॰ syā॰) itau kappai, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

mahāpatāpanāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

24.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nigguṇḍipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

nigguṇḍipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. udakadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

25.

 ‘‘bhuñ‍jantaṃ samaṇaṃ disvā, vippasan‍namanāvilaṃ|

ghaṭainaudakamādāya, siddhatthassa adāsahaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘nimmalau  haumahaṃ aj‍ja, vimalau khīṇasaṃsayau|

bhavai nibbattamānamhi, phalaṃ nibbattatai mama subhaṃ (sī॰)||

27.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, udakaṃ yamadāsahaṃ yaṃ tadā adaṃ (sī॰), adadiṃ tadā (syā॰)|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dakadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘aikasaṭṭhimhitau kappai, aikauva vimalau ahu|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udakadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

udakadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. salalamāliyatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva  jautantaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai|

aubhāsaintaṃ disā sabbā, siddhatthaṃ narasārathiṃ||

31.

 ‘‘dhanuṃ advaijjhaṃ katvāna, usuṃ san‍nayhahaṃ tadā|

pupphaṃ savaṇṭaṃ chaitvāna, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

32.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘aikapañ‍ñāsitau kappai, aikau āsiṃ jutindharau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

34.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā salalamāliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

salalamāliyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. kauraṇḍapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

35.

 ‘‘ak‍kantañ‍ca  padaṃ disvā, cak‍kālaṅkārabhūsitaṃ|

padainānupadaṃ yantau, vipassissa mahaisinau||

36.

 ‘‘kauraṇḍaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, samūlaṃ pūjitaṃ mayā|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, avandiṃ padamuttamaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘sattapañ‍ñāsakappamhi, aikau vītamalau ahuṃ|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

39.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā kauraṇḍapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kauraṇḍapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ādhāradāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

40.

 ‘‘ādhārakaṃ  mayā din‍naṃ, sikhinau laukabandhunau|

dhāraimi pathaviṃ sabbaṃ, kaivalaṃ vasudhaṃ imaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

42.

 ‘‘sattavīsai itau kappai, ahaisuṃ caturau janā|

samantavaraṇā nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

43.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ādhāradāyakau pariyādāniyau (ka॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ādhāradāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. pāpanivāriyatthairaapadānaṃ

44.

 ‘‘tissassa  tu bhagavatau, daivadaivassa tādinau|

aikacchattaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

45.

 ‘‘nivutaṃ hauti mai pāpaṃ, kusalassupasampadā|

ākāsai chattaṃ dhārainti, pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘carimaṃ  vattatai mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

47.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ chattamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, chattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘dvaisattatimhitau kappai, aṭṭhāsiṃsu janādhipā|

mahānidānanāmaina, rājānau cak‍kavattinau||

49.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pāpanivāriyau vātātapanivāriyau (sī॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pāpanivāriyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

kaṇikārapupphiyavaggau aikavīsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

kaṇikārau minailañ‍ca, kiṅkaṇi taraṇaina ca|

nigguṇḍipupphī dakadau, salalau ca kuraṇḍakau|

ādhārakau pāpavārī, aṭṭhatālīsa gāthakāti||


22. hatthivaggau

1. hatthidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, dvipadindassa tādinau|

nāgasaiṭṭhau mayā din‍nau, īsādantau urūḷhavā||

2.

 ‘‘uttamatthaṃ anubhaumi, santipadamanuttaraṃ|

nāgadānaṃ aggadānaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) mayā din‍naṃ, sabbalaukahitaisinau||

3.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ nāga dāna (sī॰ ka॰) madadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, nāgadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘aṭṭhasattatikappamhi, sauḷasāsiṃsu khattiyā|

samantapāsādikā nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

5.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā hatthidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

hatthidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. pānadhidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

6.

 ‘‘ārañ‍ñikassa isinau, cirarattatapassinau jhāyinau, maittacittatapassinau (syā॰)|

buddhassa dhammassa (syā॰ ka॰) bhāvitattassa, adāsiṃ pānadhiṃ ahaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina dvipadinda, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha|

dibbayānaṃ sabbaṃ yānaṃ (sī॰) anubhaumi, pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘catun‍navutai  itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pānadhissa idaṃ phalaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘sattasattatitau  kappai, aṭṭha āsiṃsu khattiyā|

suyānā nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

10.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pānadhidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pānadhidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. sac‍casañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

11.

 ‘‘vaissabhū tamhi samayai, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhatau|

daisaiti ariyasac‍cāni, nibbāpaintau mahājanaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘paramakāruñ‍ñapattaumhi, samitiṃ agamāsahaṃ|

sauhaṃ nisin‍nakau santau, dhammamassausi satthunau||

13.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna, daivalaukaṃ agacchahaṃ|

tiṃsakappāni daivaisu, avasiṃ tatthahaṃ purai||

14.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sac‍casañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘chabbīsamhi itau kappai, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

aikaphusitanāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

16.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sac‍casañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sac‍casañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. aikasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

17.

 ‘‘dumaggai paṃsukūlikaṃ paṃsukūlakaṃ (?), laggaṃ disvāna satthunau|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, paṃsukūlaṃ avandahaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘pañ‍cavīsai  itau kappai, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

amitābhauti nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

20.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā aikasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. raṃsisañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

21.

 ‘‘udaintaṃ sataraṃsiṃva, pītaraṃsiṃva sitaraṃsiṃ va (sī॰ syā॰) bhāṇumaṃ|

byagghūsabhaṃva pavaraṃ, sujātaṃ pabbatantarai||

22.

 ‘‘buddhassa ānubhāvau sau, jalatai pabbatantarai|

raṃsai raṃsyā (?) cittaṃ pasādaitvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘avasaisaisu kappaisu, kusalaṃ caritaṃ mayā|

taina cittappasādaina, buddhānussatiyāpi ca||

24.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassaitau, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘sattapañ‍ñāsakappamhi, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

sujātau nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

26.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā raṃsisañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

raṃsisañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sandhitatthairaapadānaṃ

27.

 ‘‘assatthai  haritaubhāsai, saṃvirūḷhamhi pādapai|

aikaṃ buddhagataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, alabhiṃhaṃ alabhissaṃ (sī॰) patissatau||

28.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

tassā sañ‍ñāya vāhasā, pattau mai āsavakkhayau||

29.

 ‘‘itau  tairasakappamhi, dhaniṭṭhau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sandhitau saṇṭhitau (sī॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sandhitatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tālavaṇṭadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘tālavaṇṭaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, tissassādic‍cabandhunau|

gimhanibbāpanatthāya, pariḷāhaupasantiyā||

32.

 ‘‘san‍nibbāpaimi rāgaggiṃ, dausaggiñ‍ca taduttariṃ|

nibbāpaimi ca mauhaggiṃ, tālavaṇṭassidaṃ phalaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

34.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, tālavaṇṭassidaṃ phalaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘taisaṭṭhimhi itau kappai, mahānāmasanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

36.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tālavaṇṭadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tālavaṇṭadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. ak‍kantasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

37.

 ‘‘kusāṭakaṃ  gahaitvāna, upajjhāyassahaṃ purai|

mantañ‍ca anusikkhāmi, ganthādausassa kaṇḍabhaidassa (sī॰), gaṇḍabhaidassa (syā॰) pattiyā||

38.

 ‘‘addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

usabhaṃ pavaraṃ aggaṃ, tissaṃ buddhaṃ gaṇuttamaṃ gajuttamaṃ (syā॰)||

39.

 ‘‘kusāṭakaṃ  pattharitaṃ, ak‍kamantaṃ naruttamaṃ|

samuggataṃ mahāvīraṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘disvā  taṃ laukapaj‍jautaṃ, vimalaṃ candasan‍nibhaṃ|

avandiṃ satthunau pādai, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

41.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ adāsiṃ kusāṭakaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kusāṭakassidaṃ phalaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘sattatiṃsai itau kappai, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

sunandau nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

43.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ak‍kantasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ak‍kantasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sappidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

44.

 ‘‘nisin‍nau pāsādavarai, nārīgaṇapurakkhatau|

byādhitaṃ samaṇaṃ disvā, abhināmaisahaṃ gharaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘upaviṭṭhaṃ mahāvīraṃ, daivadaivaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

sappitailaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, siddhatthassa mahaisinau||

46.

 ‘‘passaddhadarathaṃ disvā, vippasan‍namukhindriyaṃ|

vanditvā satthunau pādai, anusaṃsāvayiṃ purai||

47.

 ‘‘disvā  maṃ suppasan‍nattaṃ suppasan‍nantaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) suppasan‍nacittanti atthau, iddhiyā pāramiṅgatau|

nabhaṃ abbhuggamī dhīrau, haṃsarājāva ambarai||

48.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sappitailassidaṃ phalaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘itau sattarasai kappai, jutidaiva dutidaiva (syā॰), tutidaiva (ka॰) sanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

50.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sappidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sappidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. pāpanivāriyatthairaapadānaṃ

51.

 ‘‘piyadassissa  bhagavatau, caṅkamaṃ saudhitaṃ mayā|

naḷakaihi paṭicchan‍naṃ, vātātapanivāraṇaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘pāpaṃ vivaj‍janatthāya, kusalassupasampadā|

kilaisānaṃ pahānāya, padahiṃ satthu sāsanai||

53.

 ‘‘itau  aikādasai kappai, aggidaivauti vissutau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

54.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pāpanivāriyau vātātapanivāriyau (?) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pāpanivāriyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

hatthivaggau bāvīsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

hatthi pānadhi sac‍cañ‍ca, aikasañ‍ñi ca raṃsikau|

sandhitau tālavaṇṭañ‍ca, tathā ak‍kantasañ‍ñakau|

sappi pāpanivārī ca, catuppañ‍ñāsa gāthakāti||


23. ālambaṇadāyakavaggau

1. ālambaṇadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘atthadassissa  bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

ālambaṇaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, dvipadindassa tādinau||

2.

 ‘‘dharaṇiṃ paṭipaj‍jāmi, vipulaṃ sāgarapparaṃ|

pāṇaisu ca issariyaṃ, vattaimi vasudhāya ca||

3.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘itau dvaisaṭṭhikappamhi, tayau āsiṃsu khattiyā|

aikāpassitanāmā tai, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

5.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ālambaṇadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ālambaṇadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. ajinadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

6.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, gaṇasatthārakau ahaṃ|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘cammakhaṇḍaṃ  mayā din‍naṃ, sikhinau laukabandhunau|

taina kammaina dvipadinda, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha||

8.

 ‘‘sampattiṃ  anubhautvāna, kilaisai jhāpayiṃ ahaṃ|

dhāraimi antimaṃ daihaṃ, sammāsambuddhasāsanai||

9.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, ajinaṃ yaṃ adāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ajinassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘itau pañ‍camakai kappai, rājā āsiṃ sudāyakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

11.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ajinadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ajinadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. dvairataniyatthairaapadānaṃ

12.

 ‘‘migaluddau purai āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai kānanai ahaṃ|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ||

13.

 ‘‘maṃsapaisi mayā din‍nā, vipassissa mahaisinau|

sadaivakasmiṃ laukasmiṃ, issaraṃ kārayāmahaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘iminā  maṃsadānaina, ratanaṃ nibbattatai mama|

duvaimai ratanā laukai, diṭṭhadhammassa pattiyā||

15.

 ‘‘taihaṃ sabbai anubhaumi, maṃsadānassa sattiyā|

gattañ‍ca mudukaṃ mayhaṃ, pañ‍ñā nipuṇavaidanī||

16.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ maṃsamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, maṃsadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘itau catutthakai kappai, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

mahārauhitanāmau sau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

18.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dvairataniyau dvirataniyau (sī॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

dvairataniyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

dasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

4. ārakkhadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

19.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, vaidi kārāpitā vaidikā kāritā (syā॰) mayā|

ārakkhau ca mayā din‍nau, sugatassa mahaisinau||

20.

 ‘‘taina  kammavisaisaina, na passiṃ bhayabhairavaṃ|

kuhiñ‍ci upapan‍nassa, tāsau mayhaṃ na vij‍jati||

21.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ vaidiṃ kārayiṃ purai|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vaidikāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘itau chaṭṭhamhi kappamhi, apassainasanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

23.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ārakkhadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ārakkhadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. abyādhikatthairaapadānaṃ

24.

 ‘‘vipassissa bhagavatau, aggisālaṃ adāsahaṃ|

byādhikānañ‍ca āvāsaṃ, uṇhaudakapaṭiggahaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘taina kammainayaṃ mayhaṃ, attabhāvau sunimmitau|

byādhāhaṃ nābhijānāmi, puñ‍ñakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ sālamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, aggisālāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘itau ca sattamai kappai, aikausiṃ aparājitau aikau āsiṃ narādhipau (syā॰)|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

28.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā abyādhikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

abyādhikatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. aṅkaulapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

29.

 ‘‘nāradau  varadau (ka॰) iti mai nāmaṃ, kassapau iti maṃ vidū|

addasaṃ samaṇānaggaṃ, vipassiṃ daivasak‍kataṃ||

30.

 ‘‘anubyañ‍janadharaṃ buddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

aṅkaulapupphaṃ vakulapupphaṃ (syā॰), bakaulapupphaṃ (ka॰) paggayha, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

31.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘catusattatitau kappai, raumasau nāma khattiyau|

āmuk‍kamālābharaṇau, sayauggabalavāhanau||

33.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aṅkaulapupphiyau vakulapupphiyau (syā॰), bakaulapupphiyau (ka॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aṅkaulapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sauvaṇṇavaṭaṃsakiyatthairaapadānaṃ

34.

 ‘‘uyyānabhūmiṃ  niyyantau, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

vaṭaṃsakaṃ gahaitvāna, sauvaṇṇaṃ sādhunimmitaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘sīghaṃ tatau samāruyha, hatthikkhandhagatau ahaṃ|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, sikhinau laukabandhunau||

36.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘sattavīsai itau kappai, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

mahāpatāpanāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

38.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sauvaṇṇavaṭaṃsakiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sauvaṇṇavaṭaṃsakiyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. miñ‍javaṭaṃsakiyatthairaapadānaṃ

39.

 ‘‘nibbutai  laukanāthamhi, sikhimhi vadataṃ varai|

vaṭaṃsakaihi ākiṇṇaṃ, baudhipūjaṃ akāsahaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pūjamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, baudhipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘itau chabbīsatikappai, ahuṃ maighabbhanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

42.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā miñ‍javaṭaṃsakiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

miñ‍javaṭaṃsakiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sukatāvaiḷiyatthairaapadānaṃ

43.

 ‘‘asitau  nāma nāmaina, mālākārau ahaṃ ahuṃ (?) tadā|

āvaiḷaṃ paggahaitvāna, rañ‍ñau dātuṃ vajāmahaṃ||

44.

 ‘‘asampattaumhi asampattamhi (sī॰), asampattauva (?) rājānaṃ, addasaṃ sikhināyakaṃ|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

45.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘pañ‍cavīsai  itau kappai, rājāhausiṃ mahabbalau|

vaibhārau nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

47.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sukatāvaiḷiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sukatāvaiḷiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. aikavandaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

48.

 ‘‘usabhaṃ  pavaraṃ vīraṃ, vaissabhuṃ vijitāvinaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, buddhasaiṭṭhamavandahaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

50.

 ‘‘catuvīsatikappamhi, vikatānandanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

51.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikavandaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikavandaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

ālambaṇadāyakavaggau taivīsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

ālambaṇañ‍ca  ajinaṃ, maṃsadārakkhadāyakau|

abyādhi aṅkaulaṃ vakulaṃ (syā॰), bakuḷaṃ (ka॰) sauṇṇaṃ, miñ‍jaāvaiḷavandanaṃ|

pañ‍capañ‍ñāsa gāthāyau, gaṇitā atthadassibhi||


24. udakāsanavaggau

1. udakāsanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘ārāmadvārā  nikkhamma, phalakaṃ santhariṃ ahaṃ|

udakañ‍ca upaṭṭhāsiṃ, uttamatthassa pattiyā||

2.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, āsanai caudakai phalaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘itau pan‍narasai kappai, abhisāmasamavhayau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

4.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udakāsanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

udakāsanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. bhājanapālakatthairaapadānaṃ

5.

 ‘‘nagarai  bandhumatiyā, kumbhakārau ahaṃ tadā|

bhājanaṃ anupālaisiṃ, bhikkhusaṅghassa tāvadai||

6.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, bhājanaṃ anupālayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhājanassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘taipañ‍ñāsai itau kappai, anantajālināmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

8.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhājanapālakau bhājanadāyakau (sī॰ syā॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

bhājanapālakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. sālapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

9.

 ‘‘aruṇavatiyā  nagarai, ahausiṃ pūpikau tadā|

mama dvāraina gacchantaṃ, sikhinaṃ addasaṃ jinaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘buddhassa pattaṃ paggayha, sālapupphaṃ adāsahaṃ|

sammaggatassa buddhassa, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

11.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhidāsahaṃ yaṃ khaj‍jakamadāsahaṃ (sī॰), yaṃ khaj‍jamabhidāsahaṃ (ka॰) sālapupphanāmakaṃ khaj‍jakaṃ vā bhavaiyya|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sālapupphassidaṃ phalaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘itau cuddasakappamhi, ahausiṃ amitañ‍jalau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

13.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sālapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sālapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. kilañ‍jadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

14.

 ‘‘tivarāyaṃ purai rammai, naḷakārau ahaṃ tadā|

siddhatthai laukapaj‍jautai, pasan‍nā janatā tahiṃ||

15.

 ‘‘pūjatthaṃ laukanāthassa, kilañ‍jaṃ pariyaisati|

buddhapūjaṃ karauntānaṃ, kilañ‍jaṃ adadiṃ ahaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kilañ‍jassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘sattasattatikappamhi, rājā āsiṃ jaladdharau jutindharau (sī॰)|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

18.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kilañ‍jadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kilañ‍jadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. vaidikārakatthairaapadānaṃ

19.

 ‘‘vipassinau  bhagavatau, baudhiyā pādaputtamai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, kāraisiṃ vaidikaṃ ahaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, kāraisiṃ vaidikaṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vaidikāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘itau  aikādasai kappai, ahausiṃ sūriyassamau|

sattarattanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

22.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vaidikārakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

vaidikārakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. vaṇṇakāratthairaapadānaṃ

23.

 ‘‘nagarai  aruṇavatiyā, vaṇṇakārau ahaṃ tadā|

caitiyai dussabhaṇḍāni, nānāvaṇṇaṃ rajaisahaṃ rajiṃ ahaṃ (ka॰), rajaimahaṃ (syā॰)||

24.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ vaṇṇaṃ rajayiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vaṇṇadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘itau taivīsatikappai, vaṇṇasama candupama (sī॰), candasama (syā॰) sanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

26.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā vaṇṇakārau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

vaṇṇakāratthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. piyālapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

27.

 ‘‘migaluddau  purai āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai kānanai ahaṃ|

piyālaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, gatamaggai khipiṃ ahaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā piyālapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

piyālapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. ambayāgadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘sakai  sippai apatthaddhau, agamaṃ kānanaṃ ahaṃ|

sambuddhaṃ yantaṃ disvāna, ambayāgaṃ adāsahaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ambayāgassidaṃ phalaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambayāgadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ambayāgadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. jagatikārakatthairaapadānaṃ

33.

 ‘‘nibbutai  laukanāthamhi, atthadassi naruttamai|

jagatī kāritā mayhaṃ, buddhassa thūpamuttamai||

34.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, jagatiyā idaṃ phalaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā jagatikārakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

jagatikārakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. vāsidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

36.

 ‘‘kammārauhaṃ  purai āsiṃ, tivarāyaṃ puruttamai|

aikā vāsi mayā din‍nā, sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ sayambhumhiparājitai (?)||

37.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ vāsimadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vāsidānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā vāsidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

vāsidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

udakāsanavaggau catuvīsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

udakāsanabhājanaṃ, sālapupphī kilañ‍jakau|

vaidikā vaṇṇakārau ca, piyālaambayāgadau|

jagatī vāsidātā ca, gāthā tiṃsa ca aṭṭha ca||


25. tuvaradāyakavaggau

1. tuvaradāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘migaluddau  purai āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai kānanai ahaṃ|

bharitvā tuvaramādāya bharitvā turavamādāya (ka॰), bhaj‍jitaṃ tuvaramādāya (?) aittha tuvaranti muggakalāyasadisaṃ tuvaraṭṭhinti tadaṭṭhakathā| tuvarau dhañ‍ñabhaidaiti sak‍katābhidhānai, saṅghassa adadiṃ ahaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, tuvarassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tuvaradāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tuvaradāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. nāgakaisariyatthairaapadānaṃ

4.

 ‘‘dhanuṃ advaijjhaṃ katvāna, vanamajjhaugahiṃ ahaṃ|

kaisaraṃ augataṃ ausaraṃ (syā॰), ausaṭaṃ (sī॰) disvā, patapattaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, tissassa laukabandhunau||

6.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘taisattatimhi  kappamhi sattasattatimai kappai (syā॰), satta kaisaranāmakā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

8.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nāgakaisariyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

nāgakaisariyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. naḷinakaisariyatthairaapadānaṃ

9.

 ‘‘jātassarassa  vaimajjhai, vasāmi jalakuk‍kuṭau|

addasāhaṃ athaddasaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) daivadaivaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai||

10.

 ‘‘tuṇḍaina kaisariṃ kaisaraṃ (syā॰) gayha, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, tissassa laukabandhunau||

11.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘taisattatimhi kappamhi, satta kaisaranāmakā satapattasanāmakau (sī॰ syā॰)|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

13.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā naḷinakaisariyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

naḷinakaisariyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. viravapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

14.

 ‘‘khīṇāsavasahassaihi, niyyāti laukanāyakau|

viravapupphamādāya viravipupphaṃ paggayha (sī॰), buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

15.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā viravapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

viravapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. kuṭidhūpakatthairaapadānaṃ

17.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, ahausiṃ kuṭigaupakau|

kālaina kālaṃ dhūpaisiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

18.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhūpadānassidaṃ buddhapūjāyidaṃ (sī॰) phalaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kuṭidhūpakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kuṭidhūpakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pattadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

20.

 ‘‘paramaina damathaina, siddhatthassa mahaisinau|

pattadānaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, ujubhūtassa tādinau||

21.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pattadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pattadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dhātupūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

23.

 ‘‘nibbutai laukanāthamhi, siddhatthamhi naruttamai|

aikā dhātu mayā laddhā, dvipadindassa tādinau||

24.

 ‘‘tāhaṃ  dhātuṃ gahaitvāna, buddhassādic‍cabandhunau|

pañ‍cavassai paricariṃ, tiṭṭhantaṃva naruttamaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ dhātuṃ pūjayiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhātupaṭṭhahanai phalaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhātupūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

dhātupūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. sattalipupphapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

27.

 ‘‘satta  sattalipupphāni, sīsai katvānahaṃ tadā|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, vaissabhumhi naruttamai vibhattivipal‍lāsau cintaitabbau||

28.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattalipupphapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sattalipupphapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. bimbijāliyatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

catusac‍caṃ pakāsaiti, dīpaiti amataṃ padaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘bimbijālakapupphāni bimbajālakapupphāni (ka॰), puthu katvānahaṃ tadā|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, dvipadindassa tādinau||

32.

 ‘‘aṭṭhasaṭṭhimhitau kappai, caturau kiñ‍jakaisarā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

33.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bimbijāliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

bimbijāliyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. uddālakadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

34.

 ‘‘kakudhau nāma nāmaina, sayambhū aparājitau|

pavanā nikkhamitvāna, anuppattau mahānadiṃ||

35.

 ‘‘uddālakaṃ  gahaitvāna, sayambhussa adāsahaṃ|

saṃyatassujubhūtassa, pasan‍namānasau ahaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uddālakadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

uddālakadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

tuvaradāyakavaggau pañ‍cavīsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

tuvaranāganaḷinā, viravī kuṭidhūpakau|

pattau dhātusattaliyau, bimbi uddālakaina ca|

sattatiṃsati gāthāyau, gaṇitāyau vibhāvibhi||


26. thaumakavaggau

1. thaumakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘daivalaukai  ṭhitau santau, vipassissa mahaisinau|

dhammaṃ suṇitvā muditau, imaṃ vācaṃ abhāsahaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

bahuj‍janaṃ bahuṃ janaṃ (sī॰) tārayasi, daisaintau amataṃ padaṃ’||

3.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ vācamabhaṇiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, thaumanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā thaumakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

thaumakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. aikāsanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

5.

 ‘‘vijahitvā daivavaṇṇaṃ, sabhariyau idhāgamiṃ|

adhikāraṃ kattukāmau, buddhasaiṭṭhassa sāsanai||

6.

 ‘‘daivalau nāma nāmaina, padumuttarasāvakau|

tassa bhikkhā mayā din‍nā, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

7.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, piṇḍapātassidaṃ phalaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikāsanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikāsanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. citakapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

9.

 ‘‘ānandau  nāma sambuddhau, sayambhū aparājitau|

arañ‍ñai parinibbāyi, amanussamhi kānanai||

10.

 ‘‘daivalaukā idhāgantvā, citaṃ katvānahaṃ tadā|

sarīraṃ tattha jhāpaisiṃ, sak‍kārañ‍ca akāsahaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

citakapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. ticampakapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, vikatau vikanau (sī॰ syā॰) nāma pabbatau|

tassa vaimajjhai vasati, samaṇau bhāvitindriyau||

14.

 ‘‘disvāna tassaupasamaṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

tīṇi campakapupphāni, gahaitvāna samaukiriṃ||

15.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

16.
