 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ticampakapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ticampakapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. sattapāṭaliyatthairaapadānaṃ

17.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva jautantaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai|

satta pāṭalipupphāni, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

18.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattapāṭaliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sattapāṭaliyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. upāhanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

20.

 ‘‘ahausiṃ  candanau nāma, sambuddhassatrajau tadā|

aikaupāhanau mayā din‍nau, baudhiṃ sampaj‍ja mai tuvaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pānadhiṃ yamupāhanaṃ (sī॰), yaṃ pāduṃ (syā॰) dadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, upāhanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upāhanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

upāhanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. mañ‍jaripūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

23.

 ‘‘mañ‍jarikaṃ karitvāna, rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jahaṃ|

addasaṃ samaṇānaggaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ||

24.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau  sumanau, paramāya ca pītiyā|

ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

25.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘itau  taisattatikappai, aikau āsiṃ mahīpati|

jautiyau nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

27.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mañ‍jaripūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

mañ‍jaripūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. paṇṇadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

28.

 ‘‘pabbatai  himavantamhi, vākacīradharau ahaṃ|

alauṇapaṇṇabhakkhaumhi, niyamaisu ca saṃvutau||

29.

 ‘‘pātarāsai anuppattai, siddhatthau upagacchi maṃ|

tāhaṃ buddhassa pādāsiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

30.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ paṇṇamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, paṇṇadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘sattavīsatikappamhi, rājā āsiṃ sadatthiyau yadatthiyau (sī॰ syā॰)|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

32.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paṇṇadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

paṇṇadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kuṭidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

33.

 ‘‘vipinacārī sambuddhau, rukkhamūlai vasī tadā|

paṇṇasālaṃ karitvāna, adāsiṃ aparājitai||

34.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ paṇṇakuṭikaṃ adaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kuṭidānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘aṭṭhavīsai  aṭṭhatiṃsai (syā॰) itau kappai, sauḷasāsiṃsu rājānau|

sabbattha abhivassīti, vuc‍carai cak‍kavattinau||

36.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kuṭidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kuṭidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. aggapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

37.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai|

aubhāsayantaṃ raṃsaina raṃsiyā (syā॰), sikhinaṃ sikhinaṃ yathā||

38.

 ‘‘aggajaṃ pupphamādāya, upāgacchiṃ naruttamaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

39.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘pañ‍cavīsatikappamhi, ahausi amitaugatau amitavhayau (sī॰)|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

41.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aggapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aggapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

thaumakavaggau chabbīsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

thaumakaikāsanacitakaṃ, campakau sattapāṭali|

pānadhi  pāhanau (sī॰), pādu (syā॰) mañ‍jarī paṇṇaṃ, kuṭidau aggapupphiyau|

gāthāyau gaṇitā caittha, aikatālīsamaiva cāti||


27. padumukkhipavaggau

1. ākāsukkhipiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  siddhatthaṃ, gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇai|

jalajaggai duvai gayha, upāgacchiṃ narāsabhaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘aikañ‍ca pupphaṃ pādaisu, buddhasaiṭṭhassa nikkhipiṃ|

aikañ‍ca pupphaṃ paggayha, ākāsai ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘itau chattiṃsakappamhi, aikau āsiṃ mahīpati|

antalikkhakarau nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

5.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ākāsukkhipiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ākāsukkhipiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. tailamakkhiyatthairaapadānaṃ

6.

 ‘‘siddhatthamhi  bhagavati, nibbutamhi narāsabhai|

baudhiyā vaidikāyāhaṃ, tailaṃ makkhaisi tāvadai||

7.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ tailaṃ makkhayiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, makkhanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘catuvīsai itau kappai, succhavi nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

9.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tailamakkhiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tailamakkhiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. aḍḍhacandiyatthairaapadānaṃ

10.

 ‘‘tissassa  khau bhagavatau, baudhiyā pādaputtamai|

aḍḍhacandaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, dharaṇīruhapādapai||

11.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ canda yaṃ puppha (ka॰) mabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, baudhipūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘pañ‍cavīsai  itau kappai, daivalau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

13.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aḍḍhacandiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aḍḍhacandiyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. padīpadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

14.

 ‘‘daivabhūtau ahaṃ santau, auruyha pathaviṃ tadā|

padīpai pañ‍ca pādāsiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

15.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ padīpamadaṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dīpadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘pañ‍capañ‍ñāsakai kappai, aikau āsiṃ mahīpati|

samantacakkhunāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

17.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padīpadāyakau apaṇṇadīpiyau (sī॰ ka॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

padīpadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. biḷālidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

18.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, raumasau nāma pabbatau|

tamhi pabbatapādamhi, samaṇau bhāvitindriyau||

19.

 ‘‘biḷāliyau  gahaitvāna, samaṇassa adāsahaṃ|

anumaudi mahāvīrau, sayambhū aparājitau||

20.

 ‘‘biḷālī tai mama din‍nā, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

bhavai nibbattamānamhi, phalaṃ nibbattataṃ tava||

21.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ biḷālimadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, biḷāliyā idaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā biḷālidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

biḷālidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. macchadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

23.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, uk‍kusau āsahaṃ tadā|

mahantaṃ macchaṃ paggayha, siddhatthamuninau adaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ macchamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, macchadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā macchadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

macchadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. javahaṃsakatthairaapadānaṃ

26.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, āsiṃ vanacarau tadā|

siddhatthaṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai||

27.

 ‘‘añ‍jaliṃ  paggahaitvāna, ul‍laukaintau mahāmuniṃ|

sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, avandiṃ nāyakaṃ ahaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yamavandiṃ narāsabhaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā javahaṃsakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

javahaṃsakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. saḷalapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, ahausiṃ kin‍narau tadā|

vipassiṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ, raṃsijālasamākulaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, paramāya ca pītiyā|

paggayha saḷalaṃ pupphaṃ, vipassiṃ aukiriṃ ahaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saḷalapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saḷalapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. upāgatāsayatthairaapadānaṃ

34.

 ‘‘himavantassa vaimajjhai, sarau āsi sunimmitau|

tatthāhaṃ rakkhasau āsiṃ, haiṭhasīlau bhayānakau||

35.

 ‘‘anukampakau kāruṇikau, vipassī laukanāyakau|

mamuddharitukāmau sau, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘upāgataṃ  mahāvīraṃ, daivadaivaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

āsayā abhinikkhamma, avandiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ vandiṃ purisuttamaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upāgatāsayau upāgatahāsaniyau (syā॰), upāgatāhāsaniyau (ka॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

upāgatāsayatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. taraṇiyatthairaapadānaṃ

39.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇau  sambuddhau, vipassī dakkhiṇārahau|

nadītīrai ṭhitau satthā, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhatau||

40.

 ‘‘nāvā na vij‍jatai tattha, santāraṇī mahaṇṇavai|

nadiyā abhinikkhamma, tāraisiṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ tāraisiṃ naruttamaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, taraṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā taraṇiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

taraṇiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

padumukkhipavaggau sattavīsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

ukkhipī tailacandī ca, dīpadau ca biḷālidau|

macchau javau saḷaladau, rakkhasau taraṇau dasa|

gāthāyau caittha saṅkhātā, tālīsaṃ caikamaiva cāti||


28. suvaṇṇabibbauhanavaggau

1. suvaṇṇabibbauhaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘aikāsanaṃ  ahamadaṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi|

bibbauhanañ‍ca bimbauhanañ‍ca (sī॰ pī॰) pādāsiṃ, uttamatthassa pattiyā||

2.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, bibbauhanamadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bibbauhanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘itau taisaṭṭhimai kappai, asamau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

4.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā suvaṇṇabibbauhaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

suvaṇṇabibbauhaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. tilamuṭṭhidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

5.

 ‘‘mama  saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, satthā laukagganāyakau|

manaumayaina kāyaina, iddhiyā upasaṅkami||

6.

 ‘‘satthāraṃ upasaṅkantaṃ, vanditvā purisuttamaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, tilamuṭṭhimadāsahaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, tilamuṭṭhiyidaṃ phalaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘itau sauḷasakappamhi, tantisau khantiyau (syā॰) nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

9.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tilamuṭṭhidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tilamuṭṭhidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. caṅkauṭakiyatthairaapadānaṃ

10.

 ‘‘mahāsamuddaṃ  nissāya, vasatī pabbatantarai|

pac‍cuggantvāna katvāna pac‍cuggantvāna’kāsahaṃ (aṭṭha॰), pac‍cuggamanaṃ katvāna (?), caṅkauṭaka caṅgauṭaka (sī॰) madāsahaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa mahaisinau, sabbasattānukampinau sayambhussānukampinau (syā॰)|

pupphacaṅkauṭakaṃ datvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, caṅkauṭakamadaṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, caṅkauṭakassidaṃ phalaṃ||

13.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā caṅkauṭakiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

caṅkauṭakiyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. abbhañ‍janadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

14.

 ‘‘kauṇḍañ‍ñassa  bhagavatau, vītarāgassa tādinau|

ākāsasamacittassa akak‍kasacittassātha (aṭṭha॰), nippapañ‍cassa jhāyinau||

15.

 ‘‘sabbamauhātivattassa, sabbalaukahitaisinau|

abbhañ‍janaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, dvipadindassa tādinau||

16.

 ‘‘aparimaiyyai itau kappai, abbhañ‍janamadaṃ tadā ambhañ‍janamadāsahaṃ (syā॰)|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, abbhañ‍janassidaṃ phalaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘itau pan‍narasai kappai, cirappau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

18.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā abbhañ‍janadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

abbhañ‍janadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. aikañ‍jalikatthairaapadānaṃ

19.

 ‘‘udumbarai  vasantassa, niyatai paṇṇasantharai|

vutthaukāsau mayā din‍nau, samaṇassa mahaisinau||

20.

 ‘‘tissassa dvipadindassa, laukanāthassa tādinau|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, santhariṃ pupphasantharaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ kariṃ pupphasantharaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, santharassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘itau cuddasakappamhi, ahausiṃ manujādhipau|

aikaañ‍jalikau nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

23.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikañ‍jalikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikañ‍jalikatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pautthakadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

24.

 ‘‘satthāraṃ  dhammamārabbha, saṅghañ‍cāpi mahaisinaṃ|

pautthadānaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, dakkhiṇaiyyai anuttarai||

25.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pautthadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pautthakadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pautthakadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. citakapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

27.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, anusautaṃ vajāmahaṃ|

satta māluvapupphāni, citamāraupayiṃ ahaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, citakaṃ yamapūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘sattasaṭṭhimhitau kappai, paṭijaggasanāmakā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, sattāsuṃ cak‍kavattinau paṭijaggasanāmakau| sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau (syā॰)||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

citakapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. āluvadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘pabbatai  himavantamhi, mahāsindhu sudassanā|

tatthaddasaṃ vītarāgaṃ, suppabhāsaṃ sudassanaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘paramaupasamai yuttaṃ, disvā vimhitamānasau|

āluvaṃ tassa pādāsiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

33.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, āluvassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā āluvadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

āluvadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. aikapuṇḍarīkatthairaapadānaṃ

35.

 ‘‘raumasau  nāma nāmaina, sayambhū subbatau sappabhau (syā॰) tadā|

puṇḍarīkaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

36.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, puṇḍarīkamadaṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, puṇḍarīkassidaṃ phalaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikapuṇḍarīkau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikapuṇḍarīkatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. taraṇīyatthairaapadānaṃ

38.

 ‘‘mahāpathamhi visamai, saitu kārāpitau mayā|

taraṇatthāya laukassa, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

39.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yau saitu kāritau mayā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, saitudānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘pañ‍capañ‍ñāsitau kappai, aikau āsiṃ samaugadhau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

41.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā taraṇīyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

taraṇīyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

suvaṇṇabibbauhanavaggau aṭṭhavīsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

suvaṇṇaṃ  tilamuṭṭhi ca, caṅkauṭabbhañ‍janañ‍jalī|

pautthakau citamāluvā, aikapuṇḍarī saitunā|

dvaicattālīsa gāthāyau, gaṇitāyau vibhāvibhīti||

aikādasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|


29. paṇṇadāyakavaggau

1. paṇṇadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘paṇṇasālai  nisin‍naumhi, paṇṇabhaujanabhaujanau|

upaviṭṭhañ‍ca maṃ santaṃ, upāgacchi mahāisi mahāmuni (sī॰)||

2.

 ‘‘siddhatthau laukapaj‍jautau, sabbalaukatikicchakau|

tassa paṇṇaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, nisin‍naṃ nisin‍nassa (syā॰ aṭṭha॰) paṇṇasantharai||

3.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ paṇṇamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, paṇṇadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paṇṇadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

paṇṇadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. phaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

5.

 ‘‘sinairusamasantausau, dharaṇīsama dharaṇīdhara (sī॰ syā॰) sādisau|

vuṭṭhahitvā samādhimhā, bhikkhāya mamupaṭṭhitau||

6.

 ‘‘harītakaṃ harītakiṃ (syā॰) āmalakaṃ, ambajambuvibhītakaṃ|

kaulaṃ bhal‍lātakaṃ bil‍laṃ, phārusakaphalāni ca||

7.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa mahaisissa, sabbalaukānukampinau|

tañ‍ca sabbaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

8.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘sattapañ‍ñāsitau  kappai, aikajjhau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

10.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

phaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. pac‍cuggamaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

11.

 ‘‘sīhaṃ  yathā vanacaraṃ, nisabhājāniyaṃ yathā|

kakudhaṃ vilasantaṃva, āgacchantaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘siddhatthaṃ  laukapaj‍jautaṃ, sabbalaukatikicchakaṃ|

akāsiṃ pac‍cuggamanaṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

13.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, pac‍cuggacchiṃ narāsabhaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pac‍cuggamanai idaṃ phalaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘sattatiṃsai sattavīsai (sī॰ syā॰) itau kappai, aikau āsiṃ janādhipau|

saparivārauti nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

15.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pac‍cuggamaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pac‍cuggamaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. aikapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

16.

 ‘‘dakkhiṇamhi duvāramhi, pisācau āsahaṃ tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, pītaraṃsiṃva bhāṇumaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘vipassissa naraggassa, sabbalaukahitaisinau|

aikapupphaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, dvipadindassa tādinau||

18.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. maghavapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

20.

 ‘‘nammadānadiyā tīrai, sayambhū aparājitau|

samādhiṃ sau samāpan‍nau, vippasan‍nau anāvilau||

21.

 ‘‘disvā pasan‍nasumanau, sambuddhaṃ aparājitaṃ|

tāhaṃ maghavapupphaina, sayambhuṃ pūjayiṃ tadā||

22.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā maghavapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

maghavapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. upaṭṭhākadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

24.

 ‘‘rathiyaṃ  paṭipaj‍jantaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

dvipadindaṃ mahānāgaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘pak‍kausāpiya tassāhaṃ, sabbalaukahitaisinau|

upaṭṭhākau mayā din‍nau, siddhatthassa mahaisinau||

26.

 ‘‘paṭiggahaitvā paṭiggahaisi (ka॰) sambuddhau, niyyādaisi mahāmuni mahāisi (ka॰)|

uṭṭhāya āsanā tamhā, pak‍kāmi pācināmukhau||

27.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, upaṭṭhākamadaṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘sattapañ‍ñāsitau kappai, balasainasanāmakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā upaṭṭhākadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

upaṭṭhākadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. apadāniyatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘apadānaṃ sugatānaṃ, kittayiṃhaṃ mahaisinaṃ|

pādai ca sirasā vandiṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

31.

 ‘‘dvainavutai  itau kappai, apadānaṃ pakittayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā apadāniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

apadāniyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. sattāhapabbajitatthairaapadānaṃ

33.

 ‘‘vipassissa  bhagavatau, saṅghau sak‍katamānitau|

byasanaṃ mai anuppattaṃ, ñātibhaidau purai ahu||

34.

 ‘‘pabbaj‍jaṃ upagantvāna, byasanupasamāyahaṃ|

sattāhābhiratau tattha, satthusāsanakamyatā||

35.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yamahaṃ pabbajiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pabbaj‍jāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘sattasaṭṭhimhitau  kappai, satta āsuṃ mahīpatī|

sunikkhamāti ñāyanti, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

37.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā sattāhapabbajitau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sattāhapabbajitatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. buddhupaṭṭhāyikatthairaapadānaṃ

38.

 ‘‘vaiṭambhinīti  vaiṭambarīti (sī॰), vaidhambhinīti (syā॰) mai nāmaṃ, pitusantaṃ pitā’santaṃ (?) mamaṃ tadā|

mama hatthaṃ gahaitvāna, upānayi mahāmuniṃ||

39.

 ‘‘imaimaṃ uddisissanti, buddhā laukagganāyakā|

taihaṃ upaṭṭhiṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

40.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, buddhai upaṭṭhahiṃ paricariṃ (sī॰ syā॰) tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, upaṭṭhānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘taivīsamhi itau kappai, caturau āsu khattiyā|

samaṇupaṭṭhākā nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

42.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā buddhupaṭṭhāyikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

buddhupaṭṭhāyikatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. pubbaṅgamiyatthairaapadānaṃ

43.

 ‘‘cul‍lāsītisahassāni, pabbajimha akiñ‍canā|

taisaṃ pubbaṅgamau āsiṃ, uttamatthassa pattiyā||

44.

 ‘‘sarāgā  sabhavā samauhā (syā॰) caitai, vippasan‍namanāvilā|

upaṭṭhahiṃsu sak‍kac‍caṃ, pasan‍nā saihi pāṇibhi||

45.

 ‘‘khīṇāsavā vantadausā, katakic‍cā anāsavā|

phariṃsu maittacittaina, sayambhū aparājitā||

46.

 ‘‘taisaṃ upaṭṭhahitvāna, sambuddhānaṃ patissatau|

maraṇañ‍ca anuppattau, daivattañ‍ca agamhasai||

47.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ sīlamanupālayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sañ‍ñamassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pubbaṅgamiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pubbaṅgamiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

paṇṇadāyakavaggau aikūnatiṃsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

paṇṇaṃ phalaṃ pac‍cuggamaṃ, aikapupphi ca maghavā|

upaṭṭhākāpadānañ‍ca, pabbaj‍jā buddhupaṭṭhākau|

pubbaṅgamau ca gāthāyau, aṭṭhatālīsa kittitā||


30. citakapūjakavaggau

1. citakapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘ajitau  nāma nāmaina, ahausiṃ brāhmaṇau tadā|

āhutiṃ yiṭṭhukāmauhaṃ, nānāpupphaṃ samānayiṃ||

2.

 ‘‘jalantaṃ citakaṃ disvā, sikhinau laukabandhunau|

tañ‍ca pupphaṃ samānaitvā, citakai aukiriṃ ahaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘sattavīsai sattatiṃsai (ka॰) itau kappai, sattāsuṃ manujādhipā|

supaj‍jalitanāmā tai, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

5.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

citakapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. pupphadhārakatthairaapadānaṃ

6.

 ‘‘vākacīradharau  āsiṃ, ajinuttaravāsanau|

abhiñ‍ñā pañ‍ca nibbattā, candassa parimaj‍jakau||

7.

 ‘‘vipassiṃ laukapaj‍jautaṃ, disvā abhigataṃ mamaṃ|

pāricchattakapupphāni, dhāraisiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhāraṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘sattāsītimhitau  kappai, aikau āsiṃ mahīpati|

samantadhāraṇau nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

10.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pupphadhārakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pupphadhārakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. chattadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

11.

 ‘‘puttau mama pabbajitau, kāsāyavasanau tadā|

sau ca buddhattaṃ sampattau, nibbutau laukapūjitau||

12.

 ‘‘vicinantau  sakaṃ puttaṃ, agamaṃ pacchatau ahaṃ|

nibbutassa mahantassa, citakaṃ agamāsahaṃ||

13.

 ‘‘paggayha añ‍jaliṃ tattha, vanditvā citakaṃ ahaṃ|

saitacchattañ‍ca paggayha, āraupaisiṃ ahaṃ tadā||

14.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ chattamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, chattadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘pañ‍cavīsai  itau kappai, satta āsuṃ janādhipā|

mahārahasanāmā tai, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

16.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā chattadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

chattadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. saddasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

17.

 ‘‘anuggatamhi ādic‍cai, panādau pasādau (syā॰ aṭṭha॰) vipulau ahu|

buddhasaiṭṭhassa laukamhi, pātubhāvau mahaisinau||

18.

 ‘‘ghausa sadda (sī॰ syā॰) massausahaṃ  tattha, na ca passāmi taṃ jinaṃ|

maraṇañ‍ca anuppattau, buddhasañ‍ñamanussariṃ||

19.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saddasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saddasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. gausīsanikkhaipakatthairaapadānaṃ

21.

 ‘‘ārāmadvārā nikkhamma, gausīsaṃ santhataṃ mayā|

anubhaumi sakaṃ kammaṃ, pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘ājāniyā vātajavā, sindhavā sīghavāhanā|

anubhaumi sabbamaitaṃ, gausīsassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘ahau kāraṃ paramakāraṃ, sukhattai sukataṃ mayā|

saṅghai katassa kārassa, na añ‍ñaṃ kalamagghati||

24.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ sīsaṃ santhariṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, santharassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘pañ‍casattatikappamhi, suppatiṭṭhitanāmakau|

aikau āsiṃ mahātaijau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

26.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gausīsanikkhaipakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

gausīsanikkhaipakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pādapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

27.

 ‘‘pabbatai  himavantamhi, ahausiṃ kin‍narau tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, pītaraṃsiṃva bhāṇumaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘upaitaṃ tamahaṃ upaitaupi tadā (syā॰), upaisiṃ tamahaṃ (?) buddhaṃ, vipassiṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

candanaṃ tagarañ‍cāpi, pādai ausiñ‍cahaṃ tadā||

29.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ pādaṃ abhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pādapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pādapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pādapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. daisakittakatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘upasālakanāmauhaṃ, ahausiṃ brāhmaṇau tadā|

kānanaṃ vanamaugāḷhaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘disvāna vandiṃ pādaisu, laukāhutipaṭiggahaṃ|

pasan‍nacittaṃ maṃ ñatvā, buddhau antaradhāyatha||

33.

 ‘‘kānanā abhinikkhamma, buddhasaiṭṭhamanussariṃ|

taṃ daisaṃ kittayitvāna, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ daisamabhikittayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā daisakittakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

daisakittakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. saraṇagamaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

36.

 ‘‘pabbatai  himavantamhi, ahausiṃ luddakau tadā|

vipassiṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘upāsitvāna  sambuddhaṃ, vaiyyāvac‍camakāsahaṃ|

saraṇañ‍ca upāgacchiṃ, dvipadindassa tādinau||

38.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, saraṇaṃ upagacchahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, saraṇāgamanapphalaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saraṇagamaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saraṇagamaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ambapiṇḍiyatthairaapadānaṃ

40.

 ‘‘raumasau nāma nāmaina, dānavau iti vissutau|

ambapiṇḍī mayā din‍nā ambapiṇḍau mayā din‍nau (syā॰), vipassissa mahaisinau||

41.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yamambamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ambadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ambapiṇḍiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ambapiṇḍiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. anusaṃsāvakatthairaapadānaṃ

43.

 ‘‘piṇḍāya  caramānāhaṃ, vipassimaddasaṃ jinaṃ|

uḷuṅgabhikkhaṃ pādāsiṃ, dvipadindassa tādinau||

44.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau  sumanau, abhivādaisahaṃ tadā|

anusaṃsāvayiṃ buddhaṃ, uttamatthassa pattiyā||

45.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, anusaṃsāvayiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, anusaṃsāvanā phalaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā anusaṃsāvakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

anusaṃsāvakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

citakapūjakavaggau tiṃsatimau|

tassuddānaṃ –

citakaṃ  pārichattau ca, saddagausīsasantharaṃ|

pādau padaisaṃ saraṇaṃ, ambau saṃsāvakaupi ca|

aṭṭhatālīsa gāthāyau, gaṇitāyau vibhāvibhi||

atha  vagguddānaṃ –

kaṇikārau  hatthidadau, ālambaṇudakāsanaṃ|

tuvaraṃ thaumakau caiva, ukkhaipaṃ sīsupadhānaṃ||

paṇṇadau citapūjī ca, gāthāyau caiva sabbasau|

cattāri ca satānīha, aikapañ‍ñāsamaiva ca||

pañ‍cavīsasatā sabbā, dvāsattati taduttari|

tisataṃ apadānānaṃ, gaṇitā atthadassibhi||

kaṇikāravaggadasakaṃ|


tatiyasatakaṃ samattaṃ|


31. padumakaisaravaggau

1. padumakaisariyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘isisaṅghai  ahaṃ pubbai, āsiṃ mātaṅgavāraṇau|

mahaisīnaṃ pasādaina, padmakaisaramaukiriṃ||

2.

 ‘‘pac‍caikajinasaiṭṭhaisu, dhutarāgaisu tādisu|

taisu cittaṃ pasādaitvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, kaisaraṃ aukiriṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā padumakaisariyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

padumakaisariyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. sabbagandhiyatthairaapadānaṃ

5.

 ‘‘gandhamālaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, vipassissa mahaisinau|

adāsiṃ ujubhūtassa, kausaiyyavatthamuttamaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ vatthamadadiṃ gandhamadadiṃ (syā॰) purai|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, gandhadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘itau  pan‍narasai kappai, sucaiḷau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

8.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sabbagandhiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sabbagandhiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. paraman‍nadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

9.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva  jautantaṃ, udayantaṃva bhāṇumaṃ|

vipassiṃ addasaṃ buddhaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘añ‍jaliṃ  paggahaitvāna, abhinaisiṃ sakaṃ gharaṃ|

abhinaitvāna sambuddhaṃ, paraman‍namadāsahaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, paraman‍namadiṃ paraman‍naṃ dadiṃ (sī॰), paraman‍namadaṃ (syā॰) tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, paraman‍nassidaṃ phalaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paraman‍nadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

paraman‍nadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. dhammasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘vipassinau bhagavatau, mahābaudhimahau ahu|

rukkhaṭṭhassaiva sambuddhau, rukkhaṭṭhaiyaiva sambuddhai (sī॰), rukkhaṭṭhau iva sambuddhau (aṭṭha॰) aittha rukkhaṭṭhassaiva baudhimahakārajanassa sambuddhau catusac‍caṃ pakāsaitīti atthaupi sak‍kā ñātuṃ laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau laukajaiṭṭhai narāsabhai (sī॰)||

14.

 ‘‘bhagavā tamhi samayai, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhatau|

catusac‍caṃ pakāsaiti, vācāsabhimudīrayaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘saṅkhittaina ca daisaintau, vitthāraina ca daisayaṃ daisayi (syā॰), bhāsati (ka॰)|

vivaṭṭacchadau sambuddhau, nibbāpaisi mahājanaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

vanditvā satthunau pādai, pak‍kāmiṃ uttarāmukhau||

17.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ dhammamasuṇiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhammasavassidaṃ phalaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘taittiṃsamhi  itau kappai, aikau āsiṃ mahīpati|

sutavā nāma nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

19.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhammasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

dhammasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. phaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

20.

 ‘‘bhāgīrathīnadītīrai, ahausi assamau tadā|

tamahaṃ assamaṃ gacchiṃ, phalahatthau apaikkhavā||

21.

 ‘‘vipassiṃ tattha addakkhiṃ, pītaraṃsiṃva bhāṇumaṃ|

yaṃ mai atthi phalaṃ sabbaṃ, adāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ phalaṃ adadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā phaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

phaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sampasādakatthairaapadānaṃ

24.

 ‘‘‘namau tai buddha vīratthu, vippamuttausi sabbadhi|

byasanamhi byasanaṃ hi (sī॰) anuppattau, tassa mai saraṇaṃ bhava’||

25.

 ‘‘siddhatthau tassa byākāsi, laukai appaṭipuggalau|

‘mahaudadhisamau saṅghau, appamaiyyau anuttarau||

26.

 ‘‘‘tattha tvaṃ virajai khaittai, anantaphaladāyakai|

saṅghai cittaṃ pasādaitvā, subījaṃ vāpa cāpi (sī॰), vāpi (syā॰) raupaya||

27.

 ‘‘idaṃ  vatvāna sabbañ‍ñū, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

mamaiva anusāsitvā, vaihāsaṃ nabhamuggami||

28.

 ‘‘aciraṃ gatamattamhi, sabbañ‍ñumhi narāsabhai|

maraṇaṃ samanuppattau, tusitaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘tadāhaṃ virajai khaittai, anantaphaladāyakai|

saṅghai cittaṃ pasādaitvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, pasādamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pasādassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sampasādakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sampasādakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ārāmadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

32.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, ārāmau raupitau mayā|

sandacchāyaisu sītachāyaisu (syā॰), santacchāyaisu (ka॰) rukkhaisu, upāsantaisu pakkhisu||

33.

 ‘‘addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

ārāmaṃ abhināmaisiṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, phalaṃ pupphamadāsahaṃ|

tatau jātappasādauva, taṃ vanaṃ pariṇāmayiṃ||

35.

 ‘‘buddhassa yamidaṃ dāsiṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

bhavai nibbattamānamhi, nibbattati phalaṃ mama||

36.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ ārāmamadaṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ārāmassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘sattatiṃsai itau kappai, sattāsuṃ mudusītalā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

38.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ārāmadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ārāmadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. anulaipadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

39.

 ‘‘atthadassissa muninau, addasaṃ sāvakaṃ ahaṃ|

navakammaṃ karauntassa, sīmāya upagacchahaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘niṭṭhitai navakammai ca, anulaipamadāsahaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, puñ‍ñakkhaittai anuttarai||

41.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai  kappasatai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, anulaipassidaṃ phalaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā anulaipadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

anulaipadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. buddhasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

43.

 ‘‘udaintaṃ  sataraṃsiṃva, pītaraṃsiṃva bhāṇumaṃ|

vanantaragataṃ santaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

44.

 ‘‘addasaṃ supinantaina, siddhatthaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

tattha cittaṃ pasādaitvā, sugatiṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā buddhasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

buddhasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. pabbhāradāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

47.

 ‘‘piyadassinau bhagavatau, pabbhārau saudhitau mayā|

ghaṭakañ‍ca upaṭṭhāsiṃ, paribhaugāya tādinau||

48.

 ‘‘taṃ mai buddhau viyākāsi, piyadassī mahāmuni|

sahassakaṇḍau satabhaiṇḍu satagaiṇḍu (syā॰ ka॰), dhajālu haritāmayau||

49.

 ‘‘nibbattissati  sau yūpau, ratanañ‍ca anappakaṃ|

pabbhāradānaṃ datvāna, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

50.

 ‘‘itau bāttiṃsakappamhi, susuddhau nāma khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

51.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pabbhāradāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pabbhāradāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

padumakaisaravaggau aikatiṃsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kaisaraṃ gandhaman‍nañ‍ca, dhammasañ‍ñī phalaina ca|

pasādārāmadāyī ca, laipakau buddhasañ‍ñakau|

pabbhāradau ca gāthāyau, aikapañ‍ñāsa kittitā||


32. ārakkhadāyakavaggau

1. ārakkhadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘dhammadassissa  muninau, vati kārāpitā mayā|

ārakkhau ca mayā din‍nau, dvipadindassa tādinau||

2.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

taina kammavisaisaina, pattau mai āsavakkhayau||

3.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ārakkhadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ārakkhadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. bhaujanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

4.

 ‘‘sujātau sālalaṭṭhīva, saubhañ‍janamivuggatau|

indalaṭṭhirivākāsai, viraucati sadā jinau||

5.

 ‘‘tassa daivātidaivassa, vaissabhussa mahaisinau|

adāsi bhaujanamahaṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

6.

 ‘‘taṃ mai buddhau anumaudi, sayambhū aparājitau|

bhavai nibbattamānamhi, phalaṃ nibbattatū tava||

7.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhaujanassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘pañ‍cavīsai itau kappai, aikau āsiṃ amittakau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

9.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhaujanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

bhaujanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. gatasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

10.

 ‘‘ākāsaiva  padaṃ natthi, ambarai anilañ‍jasai|

siddhatthaṃ jinamaddakkhiṃ, gacchantaṃ tidivaṅgaṇai tidivaṅgaṇaṃ (syā॰ ka॰)||

11.

 ‘‘anilainairitaṃ disvā, sammāsambuddhacīvaraṃ|

vitti mamāhu tāvadai vitti mai pāhuṇā tāva (syā॰), vitti mai tāvadai jātā (sī॰), disvāna gamanaṃ muniṃ munai (sī॰)||

12.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

13.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā gatasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

gatasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. sattapadumiyatthairaapadānaṃ

14.

 ‘‘nadīkūlai vasāmahaṃ, naisādau nāma brāhmaṇau|

satapattaihi pupphaihi, sammaj‍jitvāna assamaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, siddhatthaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

disvā nabhaina vanaina (syā॰ ka॰) gacchantaṃ, hāsau mai udapaj‍jatha||

16.

 ‘‘pac‍cuggantvāna sambuddhaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

assamaṃ atināmaitvā, jalajaggaihi aukiriṃ||

17.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘itau tai sattamai kappai, caturau pādapāvarā|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

19.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattapadumiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sattapadumiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. pupphāsanadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

20.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, pītaraṃsiṃva sataraṃsiṃva (sī॰ syā॰) bhāṇumaṃ|

avidūraina gacchantaṃ, siddhatthaṃ aparājitaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘tassa pac‍cuggamitvāna, pavaisaitvāna assamaṃ|

pupphāsanaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

22.

 ‘‘añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, vaidajātau tadā ahaṃ|

buddhai cittaṃ pasādaitvā, taṃ kammaṃ pariṇāmayiṃ||

23.

 ‘‘yaṃ  mai atthi kataṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, sayambhumhaparājitai|

sabbaina taina kusalaina, vimalau haumi sāsanai||

24.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, pupphāsanamadaṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphāsanassidaṃ phalaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pupphāsanadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pupphāsanadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. āsanasanthavikatthairaapadānaṃ

26.

 ‘‘caitiyaṃ uttamaṃ nāma, sikhinau laukabandhunau|

arañ‍ñai irīṇai vanai, andhāhiṇḍāmahaṃ tadā||

27.

 ‘‘pavanā  nikkhamantaina, diṭṭhaṃ sīhāsanaṃ mayā|

aikaṃsaṃ añ‍jaliṃ katvā, santhaviṃ thavissaṃ (sī॰) laukanāyakaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘divasabhāgaṃ  thavitvāna, buddhaṃ laukagganāyakaṃ|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, imaṃ vācaṃ udīrayiṃ||

29.

 ‘‘‘namau  tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

sabbañ‍ñūsi mahāvīra, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha’||

30.

 ‘‘abhitthavitvā sikhinaṃ, nimittakaraṇainahaṃ|

āsanaṃ abhivādaitvā, pak‍kāmiṃ uttarāmukhau||

31.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ thaviṃ vadataṃ varaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, thaumanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘sattavīsai itau kappai, atulā satta āsu tai|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

33.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā āsanasanthavikau āsanasanthavakau (?), āsanathavikau (ka॰), āsanatthavikau (sī॰ syā॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

āsanasanthavikatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. saddasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

34.

 ‘‘sudassanau  mahāvīrau, daisaiti amataṃ padaṃ|

parivutau sāvakaihi, vasati gharamuttamai||

35.

 ‘‘tāya  vācāya madhurāya, saṅgaṇhāti saṅgaṇhantai (sī॰) mahājanaṃ|

ghausau ca vipulau āsi, āsīsau āsaṃsau (sī॰) daivamānusai||

36.

 ‘‘nigghausasaddaṃ sutvāna, siddhatthassa mahaisinau|

saddai cittaṃ pasādaitvā, avandiṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saddasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saddasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. tiraṃsiyatthairaapadānaṃ

39.

 ‘‘kaisariṃ  abhijātaṃva, aggikkhandhaṃva pabbatai|

aubhāsaintaṃ disā sabbā nivāsaintaṃ (ka॰), disāsin‍naṃ (syā॰), siddhatthaṃ pabbatantarai||

40.

 ‘‘sūriyassa ca ālaukaṃ, candālaukaṃ tathaiva ca|

buddhālaukañ‍ca disvāna, vitti mai udapaj‍jatha||

41.

 ‘‘tayau ālaukai disvāna, sambuddhaṃ buddhañ‍ca (sī॰) sāvakuttamaṃ|

aikaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā, santhaviṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘tayau  hi ālaukakarā, laukai laukatamaunudā|

candau ca sūriyau cāpi, buddhau ca laukanāyakau||

43.

 ‘‘aupammaṃ upadassaitvā, kittitau mai mahāmuni|

buddhassa vaṇṇaṃ kittaitvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

44.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhikittayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘aikasaṭṭhimhitau  kappai, aikau ñāṇadharau ahu|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

46.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tiraṃsiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tiraṃsiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kandalipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

47.

 ‘‘sindhuyā  nadiyā tīrai, ahausiṃ kassakau tadā|

parakammāyanai yuttau, parabhattaṃ apassitau||

48.

 ‘‘sindhuṃ anucarantauhaṃ, siddhatthaṃ jinamaddasaṃ|

samādhinā nisin‍naṃva, satapattaṃva pupphitaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘satta  kandalipupphāni, vaṇṭai chaitvānahaṃ tadā|

matthakai abhiraupaisiṃ, buddhassādic‍cabandhunau||

50.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, anukūlai samāhitaṃ|

tidhāpabhin‍namātaṅgaṃ, kuñ‍jaraṃva durāsadaṃ||

51.

 ‘‘tamahaṃ upagantvāna, nipakaṃ bhāvitindriyaṃ|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, avandiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kandalipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kandalipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. kumudamāliyatthairaapadānaṃ

54.

 ‘‘usabhaṃ pavaraṃ vīraṃ, mahaisiṃ vijitāvinaṃ|

vipassinaṃ mahāvīraṃ, abhijātaṃva kaisariṃ||

55.

 ‘‘rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jantaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

gahaitvā kumudaṃ mālaṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ samaukiriṃ||

56.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

57.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kumudamāliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kumudamāliyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

ārakkhadāyakavaggau bāttiṃsatimau battiṃsatimau (sī॰ syā॰)|


tassuddānaṃ –

ārakkhadau bhaujanadau, gatasañ‍ñī padumiyau|

pupphāsanī santhavikau, saddasañ‍ñī tiraṃsiyau|

kandalikau kumudī ca, sattapañ‍ñāsa gāthakāti||


33. umāpupphiyavaggau

1. umāpupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘samāhitaṃ  samāpan‍naṃ, siddhatthamaparājitaṃ|

samādhinā upaviṭṭhaṃ, addasāhaṃ naruttamaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘umāpupphaṃ gahaitvāna, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ|

sabbapupphā aikasīsā, uddhaṃvaṇṭā adhaumukhā||

3.

 ‘‘sucittā viya tiṭṭhantai, ākāsai pupphasantharā|

taina cittappasādaina, tusitaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘pañ‍capañ‍ñāsitau kappai, aikau āsiṃ mahīpati|

samantachadanau nāma, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

6.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā umāpupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

umāpupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. pulinapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

7.

 ‘‘kakudhaṃ  vilasantaṃva, nisabhājāniyaṃ yathā|

ausadhiṃva viraucantaṃ, aubhāsantaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, avandiṃ satthunau ahaṃ|

satthāraṃ parivaṇṇaisiṃ, sakakammaina tausayiṃ tausitau (sī॰)||

9.

 ‘‘susuddhaṃ  pulinaṃ gayha, gatamaggai samaukiriṃ|

ucchaṅgaina gahaitvāna, vipassissa mahaisinau||

10.

 ‘‘tatau  upaḍḍhapulinaṃ, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

divāvihārai ausiñ‍ciṃ, dvipadindassa tādinau||

11.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, pulinaṃ yamasiñ‍cahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pulinassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pulinapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pulinapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. hāsajanakatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘dumaggai paṃsukūlakaṃ paṃsukūlikaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), laggaṃ disvāna satthunau|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, bhiyyau uc‍cāritaṃ mayā||

14.

 ‘‘dūratau  pana disvāna patidisvāna (sī॰ syā॰), hāsau mai udapaj‍jatha|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, bhiyyau cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

15.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā hāsajanakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

hāsajanakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. yañ‍ñasāmikatthairaapadānaṃ

17.

 ‘‘jātiyā  sattavassauhaṃ, ahausiṃ mantapāragū|

kulavattaṃ kulavaṃsaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) adhāraisiṃ, yañ‍ñau ussāhitau mayā||

18.

 ‘‘cul‍lāsītisahassāni, pasū hañ‍ñanti mai tadā|

sārathambhupanītāni tārasmīhi upanītāni (ka॰), sārasmiṃhi upanītāni (syā॰), yañ‍ñatthāya upaṭṭhitā||

19.

 ‘‘uk‍kāmukhapahaṭṭhauva, khadiraṅgārasan‍nibhau|

udayantauva sūriyau, puṇṇamāyaiva puṇṇamāsaiva (sī॰) candimā||

20.

 ‘‘siddhatthau sabbasiddhatthau, tilaukamahitau hitau|

upagantvāna sambuddhau, idaṃ vacanamabravi||

21.

 ‘‘‘ahiṃsā  sabbapāṇīnaṃ, kumāra mama ruc‍cati|

thaiyyā ca aticārā ca, maj‍japānā ca ārati||

22.

 ‘‘‘rati ca samacariyāya, bāhusac‍caṃ katañ‍ñutā|

diṭṭhai dhammai parattha ca, dhammā aitai pasaṃsiyā||

23.

 ‘‘‘aitai dhammai bhāvayitvā, sabbasattahitai ratau hitaisitau (ka॰)|

buddhai cittaṃ pasādaitvā, bhāvaihi maggamuttamaṃ’||

24.

 ‘‘idaṃ vatvāna sabbañ‍ñū, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

mamaivaṃ anusāsitvā, vaihāsaṃ uggatau gatau||

25.

 ‘‘pubbai cittaṃ visaudhaitvā, pacchā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

taina cittappasādaina, tusitaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yadā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā yañ‍ñasāmikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

yañ‍ñasāmikatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. nimittasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

28.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, vasāmi assamai ahaṃ|

suvaṇṇamigamaddakkhiṃ, carantaṃ vipinai ahaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘migai  cittaṃ pasādaitvā, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ|

taina cittappasādaina, añ‍ñai buddhai anussariṃ||

30.

 ‘‘abbhatītā ca yai buddhā, vattamānā anāgatā|

aivamaivaṃ viraucanti, migarājāva tai tayau||

31.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau  kappai, yaṃ sañ‍ñamalabhiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhasañ‍ñāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘sattavīsai itau kappai, aikau āsiṃ mahīpati|

arañ‍ñasatthau nāmaina, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

33.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nimittasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

nimittasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. an‍nasaṃsāvakatthairaapadānaṃ

34.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇai|

kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘siddhatthaṃ sabbasiddhatthaṃ, anaijaṃ aparājitaṃ|

sambuddhaṃ atināmaitvā, bhaujayiṃ taṃ mahāmuniṃ||

36.

 ‘‘muni  kāruṇikau laukai, aubhāsayi mamaṃ tadā|

buddhai cittaṃ pasādaitvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhikkhādānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā an‍nasaṃsāvakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

an‍nasaṃsāvakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. nigguṇḍipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

39.

 ‘‘yadā  daivau daivakāyā, cavatai āyusaṅkhayā|

tayau saddā niccharanti, daivānaṃ anumaudataṃ||

40.

 ‘‘‘itau bhau sugatiṃ gaccha, manussānaṃ sahabyataṃ|

manussabhūtau saddhammai, labha saddhaṃ anuttaraṃ||

41.

 ‘‘‘sā  tai saddhā niviṭṭhāssa, mūlajātā patiṭṭhitā|

yāvajīvaṃ asaṃhīrā, saddhammai suppavaiditai||

42.

 ‘‘‘kāyaina kusalaṃ katvā, vācāya kusalaṃ bahuṃ|

manasā kusalaṃ katvā, abyāpaj‍jaṃ nirūpadhiṃ||

43.

 ‘‘‘tatau  aupadhikaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, katvā dānaina taṃ bahuṃ|

añ‍ñaipi mac‍cai saddhammai, brahmacariyai nivaisaya’||

44.

 ‘‘imāya anukampāya, daivādaivaṃ yadā vidū|

cavantaṃ anumaudanti, aihi daiva punappunaṃ daivapuraṃ puna (sī॰)||

45.

 ‘‘saṃvaigau mai saṃviggauhaṃ (syā॰) tadā āsi, daivasaṅghai samāgatai|

kaṃsu nāma ahaṃ yauniṃ, gamissāmi itau cutau||

46.

 ‘‘mama saṃvaigamañ‍ñāya, samaṇau bhāvitindriyau|

mamuddharitukāmau sau, āgacchi mama santikaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘sumanau nāma nāmaina, padumuttarasāvakau|

atthadhammānusāsitvā, saṃvaijaisi mamaṃ tadā||

dvādasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

48.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, buddhai cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

taṃ dhīraṃ abhivādaitvā, tattha kālaṃkatau ahaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘upapaj‍jiṃ sa upapaj‍jissaṃ (sī॰) tatthaiva, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

vasantau mātukucchimhi, puna dhāraiti mātuyā||

50.

 ‘‘tamhā kāyā cavitvāna, tidasai upapaj‍jahaṃ|

aitthantarai na passāmi, daumanassamahaṃ tadā||

51.

 ‘‘tāvatiṃsā  cavitvāna, mātukucchiṃ samauk‍kamiṃ|

nikkhamitvāna kucchimhā, kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ ajānahaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘jātiyā  sattavassauva jātiyā sattavassaina (syā॰), ārāmaṃ pāvisiṃ ahaṃ|

gautamassa bhagavatau, sakyaputtassa tādinau||

53.

 ‘‘vitthārikai vitthāritai (sī॰ ka॰) pāvacanai, bāhujañ‍ñamhi sāsanai|

addasaṃ sāsanakarai, bhikkhavau tattha satthunau||

54.

 ‘‘sāvatthi  nāma nagaraṃ, rājā tatthāsi kausalau|

rathaina nāgayuttaina, upaisi baudhimuttamaṃ||

55.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ nāgaṃ disvāna, pubbakammaṃ anussariṃ|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, samayaṃ agamāsahaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘jātiyā sattavassauva, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

yau sau buddhaṃ upaṭṭhāsi, ānandau nāma sāvakau||

57.

 ‘‘gatimā dhitimā caiva, satimā ca bahussutau|

rañ‍ñau cittaṃ pasādaintau, niyyādaisi mahājuti||

58.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna, pubbakammaṃ anussariṃ|

tatthaiva ṭhitakau santau, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

59.

 ‘‘aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

sambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā, imaṃ vācaṃ udīrayiṃ||

60.

 ‘‘‘padumuttarabuddhassa, dvipadindassa satthunau|

nigguṇḍipupphaṃ paggayha, sīhāsanai ṭhapaisahaṃ||

61.

 ‘‘‘taina kammaina dvipadinda, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha|

pattaumhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, hitvā jayaparājayaṃ’||

62.

 ‘‘pañ‍cavīsasahassamhi, kappānaṃ manujādhipā|

abbudanirabbudāni, aṭṭhaṭṭhāsiṃsu khattiyā||

63.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā nigguṇḍipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

nigguṇḍipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. sumanāvaiḷiyatthairaapadānaṃ

64.

 ‘‘vaissabhussa  bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

sabbai janā samāgamma, mahāpūjaṃ karaunti tai||

65.

 ‘‘sudhāya piṇḍaṃ katvāna, āvaiḷaṃ sumanāyahaṃ|

sīhāsanassa puratau, abhiraupaisahaṃ tadā||

66.

 ‘‘sabbai  janā samāgamma, paikkhanti pupphamuttamaṃ|

kainidaṃ pūjitaṃ pupphaṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhassa tādinau||

67.

 ‘‘taina cittappasādaina, nimmānaṃ upapaj‍jahaṃ|

anubhaumi anubhausiṃ (sī॰) sakaṃ kammaṃ, pubbai sukatamattanau||

68.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

sabbaisānaṃ piyau haumi, pupphapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

69.

 ‘‘nābhijānāmi  kāyaina, vācāya uda caitasā|

saṃyatānaṃ tapassīnaṃ, kataṃ ak‍kausitaṃ mayā||

70.

 ‘‘taina sucaritaināhaṃ, cittassa paṇidhīhi ca|

sabbaisaṃ pūjitau haumi, anak‍kausassidaṃ phalaṃ||

71.

 ‘‘itau aikādasai kappai, sahassārausi khattiyau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

72.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumanāvaiḷiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sumanāvaiḷiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pupphacchattiyatthairaapadānaṃ

73.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

sac‍caṃ pakāsayantassa, nibbāpaintassa pāṇinau||

74.

 ‘‘jalajaṃ  āharitvāna, satapattaṃ manauramaṃ|

pupphassa chattaṃ katvāna, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

75.

 ‘‘siddhatthau  ca laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

bhikkhusaṅghai ṭhitau satthā, imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsatha||

76.

 ‘‘‘yau mai cittaṃ pasādaitvā, pupphacchattaṃ adhārayiṃ|

taina cittappasādaina, duggatiṃ sau na gacchati’||

77.

 ‘‘idaṃ vatvāna sambuddhau, siddhatthau laukanāyakau|

uyyaujaitvāna parisaṃ, vaihāsaṃ nabhamuggami||

78.

 ‘‘vuṭṭhitai  naradaivamhi, saitacchattampi vuṭṭhahi|

puratau buddhasaiṭṭhassa, gacchati chattamuttamaṃ||

79.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ chattaṃ abhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pupphacchattassidaṃ phalaṃ||

80.

 ‘‘catusattatikappamhi, aṭṭha jalasikhā ahū|

sattaratanasampan‍nā, cak‍kavattī mahabbalā||

81.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pupphacchattiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pupphacchattiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. saparivārachattadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

82.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

ākāsai jalavuṭṭhīva vassatai vassati (sī॰ syā॰), vassaiti (?) dhammavuṭṭhiyā||

83.

 ‘‘tamaddasāsiṃ  sambuddhaṃ, daisaintaṃ amataṃ padaṃ|

sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, agamāsiṃ sakaṃ gharaṃ||

84.

 ‘‘chattaṃ alaṅkataṃ gayha, upagacchiṃ naruttamaṃ|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, ākāsai ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ||

85.

 ‘‘susaṅgahitayānaṃva, dantauva sāvakuttamau|

upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ, matthakai sampatiṭṭhahi||

86.

 ‘‘anukampakau kāruṇikau, buddhau laukagganāyakau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

87.

 ‘‘‘yaina chattamidaṃ din‍naṃ, alaṅkataṃ manauramaṃ|

taina cittappasādaina, duggatiṃ sau na gacchati||

88.

 ‘‘‘sattakkhattuñ‍ca  daivaisu, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

bāttiṃsakkhattuñ‍ca rājā, cak‍kavattī bhavissati||

89.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

90.

 ‘‘‘tassa  dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau’||

91.

 ‘‘buddhassa giramañ‍ñāya, vācāsabhimudīritaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, bhiyyau hāsaṃ janaisahaṃ||

92.

 ‘‘jahitvā mānusaṃ yauniṃ, dibbaṃ yauniṃ daivayauniṃ (sī॰), dibbayauniṃ (syā॰) majjhagaṃ|

vimānamuttamaṃ mayhaṃ, abbhuggataṃ manauramaṃ||

93.

 ‘‘vimānā  nikkhamantassa, saitacchattaṃ dharīyati|

tadā sañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhiṃ, pubbakammassidaṃ phalaṃ||

94.

 ‘‘daivalaukā cavitvāna, manussattañ‍ca āgamiṃ|

chattiṃsakkhattuṃ cak‍kavattī, sattakappasatamhitau||

95.

 ‘‘tamhā kāyā cavitvāna, āgacchiṃ agañchiṃ (?) tidasaṃ puraṃ|

saṃsaritvānupubbaina, mānusaṃ punarāgamiṃ||

96.

 ‘‘auk‍kantaṃ mātukucchiṃ maṃ, saittacchattaṃ adhārayuṃ|

jātiyā sattavassauhaṃ, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

97.

 ‘‘sunandau nāma nāmaina, brāhmaṇau mantapāragū|

phalikaṃ chattamādāya, sāvakaggassa sau tadā||

98.

 ‘‘anumaudi  mahāvīrau, sāriputtau mahākathī|

sutvānumaudanaṃ tassa, pubbakammamanussariṃ||

99.

 ‘‘añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

saritvā purimaṃ kammaṃ, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

100.

 ‘‘uṭṭhāya āsanā tamhā, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

sambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā, imaṃ vācaṃ udīriyiṃ||

101.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, buddhau laukai anuttarau|

padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau||

102.

 ‘‘tassa chattaṃ mayā din‍naṃ, vicittaṃ samalaṅkataṃ|

ubhau hatthaihi paggaṇhi, sayambhū aggapuggalau||

103.

 ‘‘ahau  buddhau ahau dhammau, ahau nau satthusampadā|

aikacchattassa dānaina, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jahaṃ||

104.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

105.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saparivārachattadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saparivārachattadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

umāpupphiyavaggau taittiṃsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

umāpupphañ‍ca pulinaṃ, hāsau yañ‍ñau nimittakau|

saṃsāvakau nigguṇḍī ca, sumanaṃ pupphachattakau|

saparivārachattau ca, gāthā sattasatuttarāti||


34. gandhaudakavaggau

1. gandhadhūpiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, gandhadhūpaṃ adāsahaṃ|

sumanaihi paṭicchan‍naṃ, buddhānucchavikañ‍ca taṃ||

2.

 ‘‘kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ, buddhaṃ laukagganāyakaṃ|

indīvaraṃva jalitaṃ, ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘byagghusabhaṃva pavaraṃ, abhijātaṃva kaisariṃ|

nisin‍naṃ samaṇānaggaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ||

4.

 ‘‘disvā cittaṃ pasādaitvā, paggahaitvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

vanditvā satthunau pādai, pak‍kāmiṃ uttarāmukhau||

5.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ gandhamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, gandhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhadhūpiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

gandhadhūpiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. udakapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

7.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai|

ghatāsanaṃva jalitaṃ, ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘pāṇinā udakaṃ gayha, ākāsai ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ|

sampaṭicchi mahāvīrau, buddhau kāruṇikau isi||

9.

 ‘‘antalikkhai ṭhitau satthā, padumuttaranāmakau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, imaṃ gāthamabhāsatha||

10.

 ‘‘‘iminā  dakadānaina, pītiuppādanaina ca|

kappasatasahassampi, duggatiṃ nupapaj‍jasi’||

11.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina dvipadinda, laukajaiṭṭha narāsabha|

pattaumhi acalaṃ ṭhānaṃ, hitvā jayaparājayaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘sahassarājanāmaina, tayau ca cak‍kavattinau|

pañ‍casaṭṭhikappasatai, cāturantā janādhipā||

13.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā udakapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

udakapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. pun‍nāgapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

14.

 ‘‘kānanaṃ  vanamaugayha, vasāmi luddakau ahaṃ|

pun‍nāgaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ anussariṃ||

15.

 ‘‘taṃ pupphaṃ aucinitvāna, sugandhaṃ gandhitaṃ subhaṃ|

thūpaṃ katvāna pulinai, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

16.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘aikamhi navutai kappai, aikau āsiṃ tamaunudau|

sattaratanasampan‍nau, cak‍kavattī mahabbalau||

18.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pun‍nāgapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pun‍nāgapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. aikadussadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

19.

 ‘‘nagarai haṃsavatiyā, ahausiṃ tiṇahārakau|

tiṇahāraina jīvāmi, taina pausaimi dārakai||

20.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

tamandhakāraṃ nāsaitvā, uppaj‍ji laukanāyakau||

21.

 ‘‘sakai  gharai nisīditvā, aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā|

‘buddhau laukai samuppan‍nau, daiyyadhammau ca natthi mai||

22.

 ‘‘‘idaṃ mai sāṭakaṃ aikaṃ, natthi mai kauci dāyakau|

dukkhau nirayasamphassau, raupayissāmi dakkhiṇaṃ’||

23.

 ‘‘aivāhaṃ cintayitvāna, sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

aikaṃ dussaṃ gahaitvāna, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ||

24.

 ‘‘aikaṃ dussaṃ daditvāna, uk‍kuṭṭhiṃ sampavattayiṃ|

yadi buddhau tuvaṃ vīra, tāraihi maṃ mahāmuni||

25.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mama dānaṃ pakittaintau, akā mai anumaudanaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘‘iminā aikadussaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

kappasatasahassāni, vinipātaṃ na gacchati||

27.

 ‘‘‘chattiṃsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

taittiṃsakkhattuṃ rājā ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘‘daivalaukai manussai vā, saṃsarantau tuvaṃ bhavai|

rūpavā guṇasampan‍nau, anavak‍kantadaihavā|

akkhaubhaṃ amitaṃ dussaṃ, labhissasi yadicchakaṃ’||

29.

 ‘‘idaṃ  vatvāna sambuddhau, jalajuttamanāmakau|

nabhaṃ abbhuggamī dhīrau, haṃsarājāva ambarai||

30.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

bhaugai mai ūnatā natthi, aikadussassidaṃ phalaṃ||

31.

 ‘‘paduddhārai paduddhārai, dussaṃ nibbattatai mamaṃ|

haiṭṭhā dussamhi tiṭṭhāmi, upari chadanaṃ mama||

32.

 ‘‘cak‍kavāḷamupādāya, sakānanaṃ sapabbataṃ|

icchamānau cahaṃ aj‍ja, dussaihi chādayaiyyahaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘tainaiva  aikadussaina, saṃsarantau bhavābhavai|

suvaṇṇavaṇṇau hutvāna, saṃsarāmi bhavābhavai||

34.

 ‘‘vipākaṃ aikadussassa, najjhagaṃ katthacikkhayaṃ|

ayaṃ mai antimā jāti, vipac‍cati idhāpi mai||

35.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ dussamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, aikadussassidaṃ phalaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

37.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikadussadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikadussadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. phusitakampiyatthairaapadānaṃ

38.

 ‘‘vipassī  nāma sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati

sambuddhau, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

khīṇāsavaihi sahitau, saṅghārāmai vasī tadā||

39.

 ‘‘ārāmadvārā nikkhamma, vipassī sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati laukanāyakau|

saha satasahassaihi, aṭṭha sahassasatasissaihi, aṭṭha (ka॰), aṭṭha satasahassaihi, saha (?) khīṇāsavaihi sau||

40.

 ‘‘ajinaina nivatthauhaṃ, vākacīradharaupi ca|

kusumaudakamādāya kusumbhaudaka… (sī॰ syā॰), sambuddhaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ||

41.

 ‘‘sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, vaidajātau katañ‍jalī|

kusumaudakamādāya, buddhamabbhuk‍kiriṃ ahaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sambuddhau, jalajuttamanāmakau sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati|

mama kammaṃ pakittaitvā, agamā yaina patthitaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘phusitā  pañ‍casahassā, yaihi pūjaisahaṃ jinaṃ|

aḍḍhataiyyasahassaihi, daivaraj‍jaṃ akārayiṃ||

44.

 ‘‘aḍḍhataiyyasahassaihi, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

avasaisaina kammaina, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

45.

 ‘‘daivarājā yadā haumi ahausiṃ (syā॰ ka॰), manujādhipatī yadā tadā (syā॰ ka॰)|

tamaiva nāmadhaiyyaṃ mai, phusitau nāma haumahaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘daivabhūtassa santassa, athāpi mānusassa vā|

samantā byāmatau mayhaṃ, phusitaṃva pavassati||

47.

 ‘‘bhavā  ugghāṭitā mayhaṃ, kilaisā jhāpitā mama|

sabbāsavaparikkhīṇau, phusitassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘candanassaiva mai kāyā, tathā gandhau pavāyati|

sarīratau mama gandhau, aḍḍhakausai pavāyati||

49.

 ‘‘dibbagandhaṃ sampavantaṃ, puñ‍ñakammasamaṅginaṃ|

gandhaṃ ghatvāna jānanti, phusitau āgatau idha||

50.

 ‘‘sākhāpalāsakaṭṭhāni, tiṇānipi ca sabbasau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, gandhau sampaj‍jatai khaṇai||

51.

 ‘‘satasahassitau sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati kappai, candanaṃ abhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phusitassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phusitakampiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

phusitakampiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pabhaṅkaratthairaapadānaṃ

53.

 ‘‘padumuttarabhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

vipinai caitiyaṃ āsi, vāḷamigasamākulai||

54.

 ‘‘na  kauci visahi gantuṃ, caitiyaṃ abhivandituṃ|

tiṇakaṭṭhalataunaddhaṃ, paluggaṃ āsi caitiyaṃ||

55.

 ‘‘vanakammikau tadā āsiṃ, pitumātumatainahaṃ pitupaitāmahainahaṃ (sī॰), pitāpaitāmahainahaṃ (syā॰)|

addasaṃ vipinai thūpaṃ, luggaṃ tiṇalatākulaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘disvānāhaṃ buddhathūpaṃ, garucittaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃ|

buddhasaiṭṭhassa thūpauyaṃ, paluggau acchatī vanai||

57.

 ‘‘nacchan‍naṃ nappatirūpaṃ, jānantassa guṇāguṇaṃ|

buddhathūpaṃ asaudhaitvā, añ‍ñaṃ kammaṃ payaujayai||

58.

 ‘‘tiṇakaṭṭhañ‍ca val‍liñ‍ca, saudhayitvāna caitiyai|

vanditvā aṭṭha vārāni aṭṭha ṭhānāni (ka॰), paṭikuṭikau agacchahaṃ||

59.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘tattha mai sukataṃ byamhaṃ, sauvaṇṇaṃ sapabhassaraṃ|

saṭṭhiyaujanamubbiddhaṃ, tiṃsayaujanavitthataṃ||

61.

 ‘‘tisatāni ca vārāni, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

pañ‍cavīsatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

62.

 ‘‘bhavābhavai saṃsarantau, mahābhaugaṃ labhāmahaṃ|

bhaugai mai ūnatā natthi, saudhanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

63.

 ‘‘sivikā  hatthikhandhaina, vipinai gacchatau mama|

yaṃ yaṃ disāhaṃ gacchāmi, saraṇaṃ sampatai sijjhatai (ka॰) vanaṃ||

64.

 ‘‘khāṇuṃ  vā kaṇṭakaṃ vāpi, nāhaṃ passāmi cakkhunā|

puñ‍ñakammaina saṃyuttau, sayamaivāpanīyarai||

65.

 ‘‘kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍau kilāsau ca, apamārau vitacchikā|

daddu kacchu kaṇḍu (syā॰) ca mai natthi, saudhanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

66.

 ‘‘añ‍ñampi  mai acchariyaṃ, buddhathūpassa saudhanai buddhathūpamhi saudhitai (syā॰)|

nābhijānāmi mai kāyai, jātaṃ piḷakabindukaṃ||

67.

 ‘‘añ‍ñampi mai acchariyaṃ, buddhathūpamhi saudhitai sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati, tathā uparipi|

duvai bhavai saṃsarāmi, daivattai atha mānusai||

68.

 ‘‘añ‍ñampi mai acchariyaṃ, buddhathūpamhi saudhitai|

suvaṇṇavaṇṇau sabbattha, sappabhāsau bhavāmahaṃ||

69.

 ‘‘añ‍ñampi mai acchariyaṃ, buddhathūpamhi saudhitai|

amanāpaṃ vivaj‍jati, manāpaṃ upatiṭṭhati||

70.

 ‘‘añ‍ñampi mai acchariyaṃ, buddhathūpamhi saudhitai|

visuddhaṃ hauti mai cittaṃ, aikaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ||

71.

 ‘‘añ‍ñampi mai acchariyaṃ, buddhathūpamhi saudhitai|

aikāsanai nisīditvā, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

72.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, saudhanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

73.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā pabhaṅkarau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pabhaṅkaratthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tiṇakuṭidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

74.

 ‘‘nagarai bandhumatiyā, ahausiṃ parakammikau|

parakammāyanai yuttau, parabhattaṃ apassitau||

75.

 ‘‘rahaugatau nisīditvā, aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā|

buddhau laukai samuppan‍nau, adhikārau ca natthi mai||

76.

 ‘‘kālau  mai gatiṃ kālau gatiṃ mai (sī॰ syā॰) saudhaituṃ, khaṇau mai paṭipāditau|

dukkhau nirayasamphassau, apuñ‍ñānañhi pāṇinaṃ||

77.

 ‘‘aivāhaṃ cintayitvāna, kammasāmiṃ upāgamiṃ|

aikāhaṃ kammaṃ yācitvā, vipinaṃ pāvisiṃ ahaṃ||

78.

 ‘‘tiṇakaṭṭhañ‍ca  val‍liñ‍ca, āharitvānahaṃ tadā|

tidaṇḍakai ṭhapaitvāna, akaṃ tiṇakuṭiṃ ahaṃ||

79.

 ‘‘saṅghassatthāya kuṭikaṃ, niyyādaitvāna niyyātaitvāna (sī॰) taṃ ahaṃ|

tadahaiyaiva āgantvā, kammasāmiṃ upāgamiṃ||

80.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ|

tattha mai sukataṃ byamhaṃ, kuṭikāya sunimmitaṃ tiṇakuṭikāya nimmitaṃ (sī॰)||

81.

 ‘‘sahassakaṇḍaṃ  satabhaiṇḍu, dhajālu haritāmayaṃ|

satasahassaniyyūhā, byamhai pātubhaviṃsu mai||

82.

 ‘‘yaṃ yaṃ yaunupapaj‍jāmi, daivattaṃ atha mānusaṃ|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, pāsādau upatiṭṭhati||

83.

 ‘‘bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā, laumahaṃsau na vij‍jati|

tāsaṃ mama na jānāmi, tiṇakuṭikāyidaṃ tiṇakuṭiyidaṃ (ka॰) phalaṃ||

84.

 ‘‘sīhabyagghā ca dīpī ca, acchakaukataracchakā taracchayau (syā॰ ka॰)|

sabbai maṃ parivaj‍jainti, tiṇakuṭikāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

85.

 ‘‘sarīsapā  siriṃsapā (sī॰ syā॰), sariṃsapā (ka॰) ca bhūtā ca, ahī kumbhaṇḍarakkhasā|

taipi maṃ parivaj‍jainti, tiṇakuṭikāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

86.

 ‘‘na pāpasupinassāpi, sarāmi dassanaṃ mama|

upaṭṭhitā sati mayhaṃ, tiṇakuṭikāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

87.

 ‘‘tāyaiva tiṇakuṭikāya, anubhautvāna sampadā|

gautamassa bhagavatau, dhammaṃ sacchikariṃ ahaṃ||

88.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, tiṇakuṭikāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

89.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇakuṭidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tiṇakuṭidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. uttaraiyyadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

90.

 ‘‘nagarai  haṃsavatiyā, ahausiṃ brāhmaṇau tadā|

ajjhāyakau mantadharau, tiṇṇaṃ vaidāna pāragū||

91.

 ‘‘purakkhatau sasissaihi, jātimā ca susikkhitau|

tauyābhisaicanatthāya, nagarā nikkhamiṃ tadā||

92.

 ‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

khīṇāsavasahassaihi, pāvisī nagaraṃ jinau||

93.

 ‘‘sucārurūpaṃ disvāna, ānaiñ‍jakāritaṃ viya|

parivutaṃ arahantaihi, disvā cittaṃ pasādayiṃ||

94.

 ‘‘sirasmiṃ añ‍jaliṃ katvā, namassitvāna subbataṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, uttarīyamadāsahaṃ||

95.

 ‘‘ubhau hatthaihi paggayha, sāṭakaṃ ukkhipiṃ ahaṃ|

yāvatā buddhaparisā, tāva chādaisi sāṭakau||

96.

 ‘‘piṇḍacāraṃ carantassa, mahābhikkhugaṇādinau|

chadaṃ karauntau aṭṭhāsi, hāsayantau mamaṃ tadā||

97.

 ‘‘gharatau  nikkhamantassa, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

vīthiyaṃva ṭhitau satthā, akā mai akāsi (syā॰) anumaudanaṃ||

98.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, yau mai adāsi sāṭakaṃ|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇautha mama bhāsatau||

99.

 ‘‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati|

pañ‍ñāsakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati||

100.

 ‘‘‘daivalaukai vasantassa, puñ‍ñakammasamaṅginau|

samantā yaujanasataṃ, dussacchan‍naṃ bhavissati||

101.

 ‘‘‘chattiṃsakkhattuṃ rājā ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

102.

 ‘‘‘bhavai saṃsaramānassa, puñ‍ñakammasamaṅginau|

manasā patthitaṃ sabbaṃ, nibbattissati tāvadai||

103.

 ‘‘‘kausaiyyakambaliyāni  kausaiyyakambalīyāni (sī॰), khaumakappāsikāni ca|

mahagghāni ca dussāni, paṭilacchatiyaṃ narau||

104.

 ‘‘‘manasā  patthitaṃ sabbaṃ, paṭilacchatiyaṃ narau|

aikadussassa vipākaṃ, anubhaussati sabbadā||

105.

 ‘‘‘sau pacchā pabbajitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

gautamassa bhagavatau, dhammaṃ sacchikarissati’||

106.

 ‘‘ahau mai sukataṃ kammaṃ, sabbañ‍ñussa mahaisinau|

aikāhaṃ sāṭakaṃ datvā, pattaumhi amataṃ padaṃ||

107.

 ‘‘maṇḍapai  rukkhamūlai vā, vasatau suñ‍ñakai gharai|

dhāraiti dussachadanaṃ, samantā byāmatau mama||

108.

 ‘‘aviñ‍ñattaṃ nivāsaimi aviñ‍ñattāni saivāmi (?), cīvaraṃ pac‍cayañ‍cahaṃ|

lābhī lābhimhi (syā॰) an‍nassa pānassa, uttaraiyyassidaṃ phalaṃ||

109.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vatthadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

110.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uttaraiyyadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

uttaraiyyadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dhammasavaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

111.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

catusac‍caṃ pakāsaintau, santāraisi bahuṃ janaṃ||

112.

 ‘‘ahaṃ taina samayaina, jaṭilau uggatāpanau|

dhunantau vākacīrāni, gacchāmi ambarai tadā||

113.

 ‘‘buddhasaiṭṭhassa upari, gantuṃ na visahāmahaṃ|

pakkhīva sailamāsaj‍ja sailamāpaj‍ja (syā॰), gamanaṃ na labhāmahaṃ||

114.

 ‘‘na  mai idaṃ bhūtapubbaṃ, iriyassa vikaupanaṃ|

dakai yathā ummuj‍jitvā, aivaṃ gacchāmi ambarai||

115.

 ‘‘uḷārabhūtau  manujau, haiṭṭhāsīnau haiṭṭhāpi nau (ka॰) bhavissati|

handa mainaṃ gavaisissaṃ, api atthaṃ labhaiyyahaṃ||

116.

 ‘‘aurauhantau antalikkhā, saddamassausi satthunau|

anic‍cataṃ kathaintassa, tamahaṃ uggahiṃ tadā||

117.

 ‘‘anic‍casañ‍ñamuggayha, agamāsiṃ mamassamaṃ|

yāvatāyuṃ vasitvāna, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

118.

 ‘‘carimai vattamānamhi, taṃ dhammasavanaṃ dhammasavaṇaṃ (sī॰) sariṃ|

taina kammaina sukataina, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

119.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramiṃ ahaṃ|

aikapañ‍ñāsakkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ||

120.

 ‘‘aikasattatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

121.

 ‘‘pitugaihai nisīditvā, samaṇau bhāvitindriyau|

gāthāya paridīpaintau, anic‍catamudāhari anic‍cavatthudāhari (syā॰ ka॰)||

122.

 ‘‘anussarāmi taṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, saṃsarantau bhavābhavai|

na kauṭiṃ paṭivijjhāmi na kauci paṭivaj‍jāmi (ka॰), nibbānaṃ ac‍cutaṃ padaṃ ayaṃ gāthā upari 43 vaggai sattamāpadānai purimagāthāya puraitaraṃ dissati||

123.

 ‘‘anic‍cā vata saṅkhārā, uppādavayadhamminau|

uppaj‍jitvā nirujjhanti, taisaṃ vūpasamau sukhau||

124.

 ‘‘saha  gāthaṃ suṇitvāna, pubbakammaṃ anussariṃ|

aikāsanai nisīditvā, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ||

125.

 ‘‘jātiyā sattavassauhaṃ, arahattamapāpuṇiṃ|

upasampādayi buddhau, guṇamañ‍ñāya cakkhumā||

126.

 ‘‘dārakauva  ahaṃ santau, karaṇīyaṃ samāpayiṃ|

kiṃ mai karaṇīyaṃ aj‍ja, sakyaputtassa sāsanai||

127.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, saddhammasavanai phalaṃ||

128.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhammasavaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

dhammasavaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. ukkhittapadumiyatthairaapadānaṃ

129.

 ‘‘nagarai  haṃsavatiyā, ahausiṃ mālikau tadā|

augāhaitvā padumasaraṃ, satapattaṃ aucināmahaṃ||

130.

 ‘‘padumuttarau nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

saha satasahassaihi satasahassasissaihi (ka॰), santacittaihi tādibhi||

131.

 ‘‘khīṇāsavaihi  suddhaihi, chaḷabhiñ‍ñaihi jhāyibhi sau tadā (sī॰), sau saha (ka॰)|

mama vuddhiṃ samanvaisaṃ, āgacchi mama santikaṃ mama santikai (sī॰), purisuttamau (syā॰ ka॰)||

132.

 ‘‘disvānahaṃ daivadaivaṃ, sayambhuṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

vaṇṭai chaitvā satapattaṃ, ukkhipimambarai tadā||

133.

 ‘‘yadi buddhau tuvaṃ vīra, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

sayaṃ gantvā satapattā, matthakai dhārayantu tai||

134.

 ‘‘adhiṭṭhahi mahāvīrau, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

buddhassa ānubhāvaina, matthakai dhārayiṃsu tai||

135.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

136.

 ‘‘tattha  mai sukataṃ byamhaṃ, satapattanti vuc‍cati|

saṭṭhiyaujanamubbiddhaṃ, tiṃsayaujanavitthataṃ||

137.

 ‘‘sahassakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

pañ‍casattatikkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

138.

 ‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ|

anubhaumi sakaṃ kammaṃ, pubbai sukatamattanau||

139.

 ‘‘tainaivaikapadumaina, anubhautvāna sampadā|

gautamassa bhagavatau, dhammaṃ sacchikariṃ ahaṃ||

140.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

141.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, aikapadumassidaṃ phalaṃ||

142.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā ukkhittapadumiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ukkhittapadumiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

gandhaudakavaggau catutiṃsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

gandhadhūpau udakañ‍ca, pun‍nāga aikadussakā|

phusitau ca pabhaṅkarau, kuṭidau uttarīyakau||

savanī aikapadumī, gāthāyau sabbapiṇḍitā|

aikaṃ gāthāsatañ‍caiva, catutālīsamaiva ca||


35. aikapadumiyavaggau

1. aikapadumiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘padumuttarau  nāma jinau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

bhavābhavai vibhāvaintau, tāraisi janataṃ bahuṃ||

2.

 ‘‘haṃsarājā tadā haumi, dijānaṃ pavarau ahaṃ|

jātassaraṃ samaugayha, kīḷāmi haṃsakīḷitaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

jātassarassa upari, āgacchi tāvadai jinau||

4.

 ‘‘disvānahaṃ daivadaivaṃ, sayambhuṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

vaṇṭai chaitvāna padumaṃ, satapattaṃ manauramaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘mukhatuṇḍaina paggayha, pasan‍nau laukanāyakai vippasan‍naina caitasā (syā॰)|

ukkhipitvāna gagaṇai ukkhipitvā nalāṭaina (ka॰), buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ apūjayiṃ||

6.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

antalikkhai ṭhitau satthā, akā mai anumaudanaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘‘iminā aikapadumaina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

kappānaṃ satasahassaṃ, vinipātaṃ na gacchasi’||

8.

 ‘‘idaṃ vatvāna sambuddhau, jalajuttamanāmakau|

mama kammaṃ pakittaitvā, agamā yaina patthitaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikapadumiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikapadumiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. tīṇuppalamāliyatthairaapadānaṃ

11.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, ahausiṃ vānarau tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai||

12.

 ‘‘aubhāsaintaṃ disā sabbā, sālarājaṃva phul‍litaṃ|

lakkhaṇabyañ‍janūpaitaṃ, disvā attamanau ahuṃ||

13.

 ‘‘udaggacittau sumanau, pītiyā haṭṭhamānasau|

tīṇi uppalapupphāni, matthakai abhiraupayiṃ||

14.

 ‘‘pupphāni abhiraupaitvā, vipassissa mahaisinau|

sagāravau bhavitvāna gamitvāna (sī॰), namitvāna (ka॰), pak‍kāmiṃ uttarāmukhau||

15.

 ‘‘gacchantau paṭikuṭikau, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

sailantarai patitvāna papatitvā (syā॰ ka॰), pāpuṇiṃ jīvitakkhayaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ purimaṃ jātiṃ (?) upari 38 vaggai tatiyāpadānai aivamaiva dissati, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘satānaṃ  tīṇikkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jaṃ akārayiṃ|

satānaṃ pañ‍cakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tīṇuppalamāliyau tiuppalamāliyau (sī॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tīṇuppalamāliyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. dhajadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

20.

 ‘‘tissau nāma ahu satthā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

tayaupadhikkhayai tassaupadhikkhayai (sī॰) disvā, dhajaṃ āraupitaṃ mayā||

21.

 ‘‘taina  kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

22.

 ‘‘satānaṃ  tīṇikkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jaṃ akārayiṃ|

satānaṃ pañ‍cakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘padaisaraj‍jaṃ  vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ|

anubhaumi sakaṃ kammaṃ, pubbai sukatamattanau||

24.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, dhajadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘icchamānau cahaṃ aj‍ja, sakānanaṃ sapabbataṃ|

khaumadussaina chādaiyyaṃ, tadā mayhaṃ katai phalaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dhajadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

dhajadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. tikiṅkaṇipūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

27.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, bhūtagaṇau nāma pabbatau|

tatthaddasaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ, dumaggamhi vilaggitaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘tīṇi kiṅkaṇipupphāni, aucinitvānahaṃ tadā|

haṭṭhau haṭṭhaina cittaina, paṃsukūlaṃ apūjayiṃ||

29.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, tiṇṇaṃ pupphānidaṃ phalaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tikiṅkaṇipūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tikiṅkaṇipūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. naḷāgārikatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, hāritau nāma pabbatau|

sayambhū nāradau nāma, rukkhamūlai vasī tadā||

32.

 ‘‘naḷāgāraṃ karitvāna, tiṇaina chādayiṃ ahaṃ|

caṅkamaṃ saudhayitvāna, sayambhussa adāsahaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘catuddasasu  kappaisu, daivalaukai ramiṃ ahaṃ|

catusattatikkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jaṃ akārayiṃ||

34.

 ‘‘catusattati sattasattati (sī॰) kkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘ubbiddhaṃ bhavanaṃ mayhaṃ, indalaṭṭhīva uggataṃ|

sahassathambhaṃ atulaṃ, vimānaṃ sapabhassaraṃ||

36.

 ‘‘dvai sampattī anubhautvā, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

gautamassa bhagavatau, sāsanai pabbajiṃ ahaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘padhānapahitattaumhi, upasantau nirūpadhi|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

38.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā naḷāgārikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

naḷāgārikatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. campakapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

39.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, jāpalau cāpalau (sī॰), chāpalau (syā॰) nāma pabbatau|

buddhau sudassanau nāma, vihāsi pabbatantarai||

40.

 ‘‘pupphaṃ haimavantaṃ haimavataṃ (sī॰), haimavaṇṇaṃ (syā॰) gayha, gacchaṃ vaihāyasainahaṃ|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, aughatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ||

41.

 ‘‘satta  campakapupphāni, sīsai katvānahaṃ tadā|

buddhassa abhiraupaisiṃ, sayambhussa mahaisinau||

42.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

43.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā campakapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

campakapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. padumapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

44.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, raumasau nāma pabbatau|

buddhaupi sambhavau nāma, abbhaukāsai vasī tadā||

45.

 ‘‘bhavanā nikkhamitvāna, padumaṃ dhārayiṃ ahaṃ|

aikāhaṃ dhārayitvāna, puna bhavanupāgamiṃ||

46.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhiraupayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

padumapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|
