tairasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

8. tiṇamuṭṭhidāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

48.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, lambakau nāma pabbatau|

upatissau nāma sambuddhau, abbhaukāsamhi caṅkami||

49.

 ‘‘migaluddau  tadā āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai kānanai ahaṃ|

disvāna taṃ daivadaivaṃ, sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ||

50.

 ‘‘vippasan‍naina cittaina, tadā tassa mahaisinau|

nisīdanatthaṃ buddhassa, tiṇamuṭṭhimadāsahaṃ||

51.

 ‘‘datvāna daivadaivassa, bhiyyau cittaṃ pasādayiṃ|

sambuddhaṃ abhivādaitvā, pak‍kāmiṃ uttarāmukhau||

52.

 ‘‘aciraṃ gatamattaṃ maṃ, migarājā apauthayi ahaiṭhayi (sī॰ syā॰)|

sīhaina pauthitau pātitau (sī॰ syā॰) santau, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘āsan‍nai mai kataṃ kammaṃ, buddhasaiṭṭhai anāsavai|

sumuttau saravaigauva, daivalaukaṃ agañchahaṃ||

54.

 ‘‘yūpau  tattha subhau āsi, puñ‍ñakammābhinimmitau|

sahassakaṇḍau satabhaiṇḍu, dhajālu haritāmayau||

55.

 ‘‘pabhā niddhāvatai tassa, sataraṃsīva uggatau|

ākiṇṇau daivakañ‍ñāhi, āmaudiṃ kāmakāmahaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘daivalaukā cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

āgantvāna manussattaṃ, pattaumhi āsavakkhayaṃ||

57.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, nisīdanamadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, tiṇamuṭṭhiyidaṃ phalaṃ||

58.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā tiṇamuṭṭhidāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tiṇamuṭṭhidāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tindukaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

59.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva  jautantaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, aughatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘tindukaṃ  saphalaṃ disvā, bhinditvāna sakausakaṃ sakauṭakaṃ (sī॰), sakauṭikaṃ (syā॰)|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, sayambhussa madāsahaṃ vaissabhussa adāsahaṃ (sī॰)||

61.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

62.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā tindukaphaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tindukaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. aikañ‍jaliyatthairaapadānaṃ

63.

 ‘‘raumasau raivatau (sī॰) nāma sambuddhau, nadīkūlai vasī tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, pītaraṃsiṃva bhāṇumaṃ||

64.

 ‘‘uk‍kāmukhapahaṭṭhaṃva, khadiraṅgārasan‍nibhaṃ|

ausadhiṃva viraucantaṃ, aikañ‍jalimakāsahaṃ||

65.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ añ‍jalimakāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, añ‍jaliyā idaṃ phalaṃ||

66.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikañ‍jaliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikañ‍jaliyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

aikapadumiyavaggau pañ‍catiṃsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

padumī uppalamālī, dhajau kiṅkaṇikaṃ naḷaṃ kiṅkaṇikau naḷau (sī॰)|

campakau padumau muṭṭhi, tindukaikañ‍jalī tathā|

cha ca saṭṭhi ca gāthāyau, gaṇitāyau vibhāvibhi||


36. saddasañ‍ñakavaggau

1. saddasañ‍ñakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘migaluddau  purai āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai kānanai ahaṃ|

tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, daivasaṅghapurakkhataṃ||

2.

 ‘‘catusac‍caṃ pakāsaintaṃ, uddharantaṃ mahājanaṃ|

assausiṃ madhuraṃ vācaṃ, karavīkarudaupamaṃ rutaupamaṃ (?)||

3.

 ‘‘brahmasarassa muninau, sikhinau laukabandhunau|

ghausai cittaṃ pasādaitvā, pattaumhi āsavakkhayaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, pasādassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saddasañ‍ñakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saddasañ‍ñakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. yavakalāpiyatthairaapadānaṃ

6.

 ‘‘nagarai aruṇavatiyā, āsiṃ yavasikau tadā|

panthai disvāna sambuddhaṃ, yavakalāpaṃ santhariṃ yavakalāpamavatthariṃ (sī॰)||

7.

 ‘‘anukampakau  kāruṇikau, sikhī laukagganāyakau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, nisīdi yavasantharai||

8.

 ‘‘disvā nisin‍naṃ vimalaṃ, mahājhāyiṃ vināyakaṃ|

pāmauj‍jaṃ janayitvāna, tattha kālaṅkatau ahaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, yavattharai idaṃ phalaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā yavakalāpiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

yavakalāpiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. kiṃsukapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

11.

 ‘‘kiṃsukaṃ  pupphitaṃ disvā, paggahaitvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

buddhaṃ saritvā siddhatthaṃ, ākāsai abhipūjayiṃ||

12.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

13.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā kiṃsukapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kiṃsukapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. sakausakakauraṇḍadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

14.

 ‘‘ak‍kantañ‍ca padaṃ disvā, sikhinau laukabandhunau|

aikaṃsaṃ ajinaṃ katvā, padasaiṭṭhaṃ avandahaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘kauraṇḍaṃ pupphitaṃ disvā, pādapaṃ dharaṇīruhaṃ|

sakausakaṃ sakauṭakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) gahaitvāna, padacak‍kaṃ apūjayiṃ||

16.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, padapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sakausaka sakauṭaka (sī॰ syā॰) kauraṇḍadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sakausakakauraṇḍadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. daṇḍadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

18.

 ‘‘kānanaṃ  vanamaugayha, vaiḷuṃ chaitvānahaṃ tadā|

ālambanaṃ karitvāna, saṅghassa adadaṃ ahaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘taina  cittappasādaina, subbatai abhivādiya|

ālambanampi datvāna, pak‍kāmiṃ uttarāmukhau||

20.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ daṇḍamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, daṇḍadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā daṇḍadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

daṇḍadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ambayāgudāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

22.

 ‘‘sataraṃsī  nāma sambuddhau, sayambhū aparājitau|

vuṭṭhahitvā samādhimhā, bhikkhāya mamupāgami||

23.

 ‘‘pac‍caikabuddhaṃ disvāna, ambayāguṃ adāsahaṃ|

vippasan‍namanaṃ tassa, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

24.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, ambayāguyidaṃ phalaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā ambayāgudāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

ambayāgudāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. supuṭakapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

26.

 ‘‘divāvihārā nikkhantau, vipassī laukanāyakau|

bhikkhāya vicarantau sau, mama santikupāgami||

27.

 ‘‘tatau  patītau sumanau, buddhasaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

lauṇasupuṭakaṃ datvā, kappaṃ saggamhi maudahaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ puṭakamadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, puṭakassa idaṃ phalaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā supuṭakapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

supuṭakapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. mañ‍cadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

30.

 ‘‘vipassinau bhagavatau, laukajaiṭṭhassa tādinau|

aikaṃ mañ‍caṃ mayā din‍naṃ, pasan‍naina sapāṇinā||

31.

 ‘‘hatthiyānaṃ  assayānaṃ, dibbayānaṃ samajjhagaṃ|

taina mañ‍cakadānaina, pattaumhi āsavakkhayaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ mañ‍camadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, mañ‍cadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mañ‍cadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

mañ‍cadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. saraṇagamaniyatthairaapadānaṃ

34.

 ‘‘āruhimha tadā nāvaṃ, bhikkhu cājīvakau cahaṃ|

nāvāya bhij‍jamānāya, bhikkhu mai saraṇaṃ adā||

35.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yañ‍ca mai saraṇaṃ adā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, saraṇāgamanai phalaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saraṇagamaniyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saraṇagamaniyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. piṇḍapātikatthairaapadānaṃ

37.

 ‘‘tissau  nāmāsi sambuddhau, vihāsi vipinai tadā|

tusitā hi idhāgantvā, piṇḍapātaṃ adāsahaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘sambuddhamabhivādaitvā, tissaṃ nāma mahāyasaṃ|

sakaṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, tusitaṃ agamāsahaṃ||

39.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, piṇḍapātassidaṃ phalaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā piṇḍapātikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

piṇḍapātikatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

saddasañ‍ñakavaggau chattiṃsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

saddasañ‍ñī yavasikau, kiṃsukauraṇḍapupphiyau|

ālambanau ambayāgu, supuṭī mañ‍cadāyakau|

saraṇaṃ piṇḍapātau ca, gāthā tālīsamaiva ca||


37. mandāravapupphiyavaggau

1. mandāravapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘tāvatiṃsā  idhāgantvā, maṅgalau nāma māṇavau|

mandāravaṃ gahaitvāna, vipassissa mahaisinau||

2.

 ‘‘samādhinā nisin‍nassa, matthakai dhārayiṃ ahaṃ|

sattāhaṃ dhārayitvāna, daivalaukaṃ punāgamiṃ||

3.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mandāravapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

mandāravapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. kak‍kārupupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

5.

 ‘‘yāmā daivā idhāgantvā, gautamaṃ sirivacchasaṃ|

kak‍kārumālaṃ gaukkhanumālaṃ (sī॰) paggayha, buddhassa abhiraupayiṃ||

6.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

7.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kak‍kārupupphiyau gaukkhanupupphiyau (ka॰) thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kak‍kārupupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. bhisamuḷāladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

8.

 ‘‘phussau  nāmāsi sambuddhau, sabbadhammāna pāragū|

vivaikakāmau sabbañ‍ñū sappañ‍ñau (sī॰ syā॰), āgañchi mama santikai||

9.

 ‘‘tasmiṃ cittaṃ pasādaitvā, mahākāruṇikai jinai|

bhisamuḷālaṃ paggayha, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ||

10.

 ‘‘dvainavutai  itau kappai, yaṃ bhisamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, bhisadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

11.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhisamuḷāladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

bhisamuḷāladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. kaisarapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

12.

 ‘‘vij‍jādharau  tadā āsiṃ, himavantamhi pabbatai|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, caṅkamantaṃ mahāyasaṃ||

13.

 ‘‘tīṇi kaisarapupphāni kaisaripupphāni (sī॰), sīsai katvānahaṃ tadā|

upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ, vaissabhuṃ abhipūjayiṃ||

14.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ kammamakariṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kaisarapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kaisarapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. aṅkaulapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

16.

 ‘‘padumau nāma sambuddhau, cittakūṭai vasī tadā|

disvāna taṃ ahaṃ buddhaṃ, sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ upagacchihaṃ (sī॰ syā॰)||

17.

 ‘‘aṅkaulaṃ  pupphitaṃ disvā, aucinitvānahaṃ tadā|

upagantvāna sambuddhaṃ, pūjayiṃ padumaṃ jinaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aṅkaulapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aṅkaulapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. kadambapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

20.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sambuddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ antarāpaṇai|

kañ‍canagghiyasaṅkāsaṃ, bāttiṃsavaralakkhaṇaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘nisaj‍ja pāsādavarai, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

kadambapupphaṃ paggayha, vipassiṃ abhipūjayiṃ||

22.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau  kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kadambapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kadambapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. uddālakapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

24.

 ‘‘anaumau  sujātau (syā॰), anumau (ka॰) nāma sambuddhau, gaṅgākūlai vasī tadā|

uddālakaṃ gahaitvāna, pūjayiṃ aparājitaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uddālakapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

uddālakapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. aikacampakapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

27.

 ‘‘upasantau  ca sambuddhau, vasatī pabbatantarai|

aikacampakamādāya, upagacchiṃ naruttamaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, pac‍caikamunimuttamaṃ|

ubhauhatthaihi paggayha, pūjayiṃ aparājitaṃ||

29.

 ‘‘pañ‍casaṭṭhimhitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā aikacampakapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

aikacampakapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. timirapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, anusautaṃ vajāmahaṃ|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, sālarājaṃva phul‍litaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau  sumanau, pac‍caikamunimuttamaṃ|

gahaitvā timiraṃ pupphaṃ, matthakai aukiriṃ ahaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā timirapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

timirapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. saḷalapupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

35.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, ahausiṃ kin‍narau tadā|

tatthaddasaṃ daivadaivaṃ, caṅkamantaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘aucinitvāna  saḷalaṃ, pupphaṃ buddhassadāsahaṃ|

upasiṅghi mahāvīrau, saḷalaṃ daivagandhikaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘paṭiggahaitvā sambuddhau, vipassī laukanāyakau|

upasiṅghi mahāvīrau, paikkhamānassa mai satau||

38.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau sumanau, vanditvā dvipaduttamaṃ|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, puna pabbatamāruhiṃ||

39.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā saḷalapupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

saḷalapupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

mandāravapupphiyavaggau sattatiṃsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

mandāravañ‍ca kak‍kāru, bhisakaisarapupphiyau|

aṅkaulakau  kadambī ca, uddālī aikacampakau|

timiraṃ saḷalañ‍caiva, gāthā tālīsamaiva ca||


38. baudhivandanavaggau

1. baudhivandakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘pāṭaliṃ  haritaṃ disvā, pādapaṃ dharaṇīruhaṃ|

aikaṃsaṃ añ‍jaliṃ katvā, avandiṃ pāṭaliṃ ahaṃ||

2.

 ‘‘añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, garuṃ katvāna mānasaṃ|

antausuddhaṃ bahisuddhaṃ, suvimuttamanāsavaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘vipassiṃ laukamahitaṃ, karuṇāñāṇasāgaraṃ|

sammukhā viya sambuddhaṃ, avandiṃ pāṭaliṃ ahaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ baudhimabhivandahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, vandanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

5.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā baudhivandakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

baudhivandakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. pāṭalipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

6.

 ‘‘vipassī  nāma bhagavā, sayambhū aggapuggalau|

purakkhatau sasissaihi, pāvisi bandhumaṃ jinau||

7.

 ‘‘tīṇi pāṭalipupphāni, ucchaṅgai ṭhapitāni mai|

sīsaṃ nhāyitukāmauva, nadītitthaṃ agacchahaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘nikkhamma bandhumatiyā, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

indīvaraṃva jalitaṃ, ādittaṃva hutāsanaṃ||

9.

 ‘‘byagghūsabhaṃva  pavaraṃ, abhijātaṃva kaisariṃ|

gacchantaṃ samaṇānaggaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ||

10.

 ‘‘tasmiṃ  pasan‍nau samaṇai (ka॰) sugatai, kilaisamaladhauvanai|

gahaitvā tīṇi pupphāni, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ apūjayiṃ||

11.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pāṭalipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pāṭalipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. tīṇuppalamāliyatthairaapadānaṃ

13.

 ‘‘candabhāgānadītīrai, ahausiṃ vānarau tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai||

14.

 ‘‘aubhāsaintaṃ disā sabbā, sālarājaṃva phul‍litaṃ|

lakkhaṇabyañ‍janūpaitaṃ, disvānattamanau ahaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘udaggacittau sumanau, pītiyā haṭṭhamānasau|

tīṇi uppalapupphāni, matthakai abhiraupayiṃ||

16.

 ‘‘pūjayitvāna pupphāni, phussassāhaṃ mahaisinau|

sagāravau bhavitvāna, pak‍kāmiṃ uttarāmukhau||

17.

 ‘‘gacchantau paṭikuṭikau, vippasan‍naina caitasā|

sailantarai patitvāna, pāpuṇiṃ jīvitakkhayaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘taina kammaina sukataina, caitanāpaṇidhīhi ca|

jahitvā purimaṃ jātiṃ, tāvatiṃsamagacchahaṃ||

19.

 ‘‘satānaṃ tīṇikkhattuñ‍ca, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ|

satānaṃ pañ‍cakkhattuñ‍ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘dvainavutai itau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā tīṇuppalamāliyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

tīṇuppalamāliyatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. paṭṭipupphiyatthairaapadānaṃ

22.

 ‘‘yadā nibbāyi sambuddhau, mahaisī padumuttarau|

samāgamma janā sabbai, sarīraṃ nīharanti tai||

23.

 ‘‘nīharantai sarīramhi, vaj‍jamānāsu bhairisu|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, paṭṭipupphaṃ apūjayiṃ||

24.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sarīrapūjitai phalaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

26.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa buddhasaiṭṭhassa (sī॰) santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā paṭṭipupphiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

paṭṭipupphiyatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. sattapaṇṇiyatthairaapadānaṃ

28.

 ‘‘sumanau  nāma sambuddhau, uppaj‍ji laukanāyakau|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, sattapaṇṇimapūjayiṃ||

29.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, sattapaṇṇimapūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sattapaṇṇipūjāyidaṃ sattapaṇṇissidaṃ (sī॰) phalaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

31.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sattapaṇṇiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sattapaṇṇiyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. gandhamuṭṭhiyatthairaapadānaṃ

33.

 idha gāthāddhaṃ ūnaṃ viya dissati ‘‘citakai karīyamānai, nānāgandhai samāhatai|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, gandhamuṭṭhimapūjayiṃ||

34.

 ‘‘satasahassitau  kappai, citakaṃ yaṃ apūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

36.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā gandhamuṭṭhiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

gandhamuṭṭhiyatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. citakapūjakatthairaapadānaṃ

38.

 ‘‘parinibbutai bhagavati, jalajuttamanāmakai|

āraupitamhi citakai, sālapupphamapūjayiṃ||

39.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ pupphamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, citapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

41.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā citakapūjakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

citakapūjakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. sumanatālavaṇṭiyatthairaapadānaṃ

43.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa  bhagavatau, tālavaṇṭamadāsahaṃ|

sumanaihi paṭicchan‍naṃ, dhārayāmi mahārahaṃ||

44.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, tālavaṇṭamadāsahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, tālavaṇṭassidaṃ phalaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

46.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā sumanatālavaṇṭiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sumanatālavaṇṭiyatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sumanadāmiyatthairaapadānaṃ

48.

 ‘‘siddhatthassa bhagavatau, nhātakassa tapassinau|

katvāna sumanadāmaṃ, dhārayiṃ puratau ṭhitau||

49.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ dāmaṃ dhārayiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, sumanadhāraṇai phalaṃ||

50.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

51.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sumanadāmiyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sumanadāmiyatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. kāsumāriphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

53.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva  jautantaṃ, nisin‍naṃ pabbatantarai|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, laukajaiṭṭhaṃ narāsabhaṃ||

54.

 ‘‘pasan‍nacittau  sumanau, sirai katvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

kāsumāriphalaṃ gayha, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ||

55.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

56.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

57.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

58.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kāsumāriphaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kāsumāriphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

baudhivandanavaggau aṭṭhatiṃsatimau|


tassuddānaṃ –

baudhi pāṭali uppalī, paṭṭi ca sattapaṇṇiyau|

gandhamuṭṭhi  ca citakau, tālaṃ sumanadāmakau|

kāsumāriphalī caiva, gāthā aikūnasaṭṭhikā||


39. avaṭaphalavaggau

1. avaṭaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘sataraṃsi  nāma bhagavā, sayambhū aparājitau|

vivaikakāmau sambuddhau, gaucarāyābhinikkhami||

2.

 ‘‘phalahatthau ahaṃ disvā, upagacchiṃ narāsabhaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, avaṭaṃ avaṇṭaṃ (sī॰), ambaṭaṃ (syā॰) adadiṃ phalaṃ||

3.

 ‘‘catun‍navutitau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

4.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

5.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā avaṭaphaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

avaṭaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. labujadāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

7.

 ‘‘nagarai bandhumatiyā, āsiṃ ārāmikau tadā|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, gacchantaṃ anilañ‍jasai||

8.

 ‘‘labujassa phalaṃ gayha, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

ākāsai ṭhitakau santau, paṭigaṇhi mahāyasau||

9.

 ‘‘vittisañ‍jananaṃ  mayhaṃ, diṭṭhadhammasukhāvahaṃ|

phalaṃ buddhassa datvāna, vippasan‍naina caitasā||

10.

 ‘‘adhigacchiṃ  tadā pītiṃ, vipulañ‍ca sukhuttamaṃ|

uppaj‍jataiva ratanaṃ, nibbattassa tahiṃ tahiṃ||

11.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

12.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

13.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

14.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā labujadāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

labujadāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. udumbaraphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

15.

 ‘‘vinatānadiyā tīrai, vihāsi purisuttamau|

addasaṃ virajaṃ buddhaṃ, aikaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ||

16.

 ‘‘tasmiṃ pasan‍namānasau, kilaisamaladhauvanai|

udumbaraphalaṃ gayha, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ||

17.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

18.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

19.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

20.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ āyasmā udumbaraphaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

udumbaraphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. pilakkhaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

21.

 ‘‘vanantarai  buddhaṃ disvā, atthadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, pilakkhassādadiṃ phalaṃ pilakkhassa phalaṃ adaṃ (sī॰), pilakkhussa phalaṃ adaṃ (syā॰)||

22.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

23.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

24.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

25.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pilakkhaphaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pilakkhaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. phārusaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

26.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ  sambuddhaṃ, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahaṃ|

rathiyaṃ paṭipaj‍jantaṃ, phārusaphalamadāsahaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ ahaṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

28.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

29.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

30.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā phārusaphaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

phārusaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. val‍liphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

31.

 ‘‘sabbai  janā samāgamma, agamiṃsu vanaṃ tadā|

phalamanvaisamānā tai, alabhiṃsu phalaṃ tadā||

32.

 ‘‘tatthaddasāsiṃ  sambuddhaṃ, sayambhuṃ aparājitaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, val‍liphalamadāsahaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

34.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

35.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

36.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā  catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā val‍liphaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

val‍liphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kadaliphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

37.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva jalitaṃ, puṇṇamāyaiva puṇṇamāsaiva (sī॰ ka॰) candimaṃ|

jalantaṃ dīparukkhaṃva, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

38.

 ‘‘kadaliphalaṃ paggayha, adāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ|

pasan‍nacittau sumanau, vanditvāna apak‍kamiṃ||

39.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai  itau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

41.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

42.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kadaliphaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

kadaliphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. panasaphaladāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

43.

 ‘‘aj‍junau  nāma sambuddhau, himavantai vasī tadā|

caraṇaina ca sampan‍nau, samādhikusalau muni||

44.

 ‘‘kumbhamattaṃ  gahaitvāna, panasaṃ jīvajīvakaṃ daivagandhikaṃ (41 vaggai, 5 apadānai)|

chattapaṇṇai ṭhapaitvāna, adāsiṃ satthunau ahaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘aikattiṃsai itau kappai, yaṃ phalamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, phaladānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

46.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

47.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

48.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā panasaphaladāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

panasaphaladāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sauṇakauṭivīsatthairaapadānaṃ

49.

 ‘‘vipassinau  pāvacanai, aikaṃ laiṇaṃ mayā kataṃ|

cātuddisassa saṅghassa, bandhumārājadhāniyā||

50.

 ‘‘dussaihi bhūmiṃ laiṇassa, santharitvā paric‍cajiṃ|

udaggacittau sumanau, akāsiṃ paṇidhiṃ tadā||

51.

 ‘‘ārādhayaiyyaṃ sambuddhaṃ, pabbaj‍jañ‍ca labhaiyyahaṃ|

anuttarañ‍ca nibbānaṃ, phusaiyyaṃ santimuttamaṃ||

52.

 ‘‘tainaiva suk‍kamūlaina, kappai kappaṃ (sī॰), kappa (ka॰) navuti saṃsariṃ|

daivabhūtau manussau ca, katapuñ‍ñau viraucahaṃ||

53.

 ‘‘tatau  kammāvasaisaina, idha pacchimakai bhavai|

campāyaṃ aggasaiṭṭhissa, jātaumhi aikaputtakau||

54.

 ‘‘jātamattassa  mai sutvā, pitu chandau ayaṃ ahu|

dadāmahaṃ kumārassa, vīsakauṭī anūnakā||

55.

 ‘‘caturaṅgulā ca mai laumā, jātā pādatalai ubhau|

sukhumā mudusamphassā, tūlāpicusamā subhā||

56.

 ‘‘atītā navuti kappā, ayaṃ aikau ca uttari|

nābhijānāmi nikkhittai, pādai bhūmyā asanthatai||

57.

 ‘‘ārādhitau  mai sambuddhau, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

arahattañ‍ca mai pattaṃ, sītibhūtaumhi nibbutau||

58.

 ‘‘aggau āraddhavīriyānaṃ, niddiṭṭhau sabbadassinā|

khīṇāsavaumhi arahā, chaḷabhiñ‍ñau mahiddhikau||

59.

 ‘‘aikanavutitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, laiṇadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

61.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

62.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

63.

 ‘‘thairau  kauṭivīsau kauṭivisau (syā॰ ka॰), kauḷivisau (añ‍ñaṭṭhānaisu) sauṇau, bhikkhusaṅghassa aggatau|

pañhaṃ puṭṭhau viyākāsi, anautattai mahāsarai’’ti||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sauṇau kauṭivīsau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sauṇakauṭivīsatthairassāpadānaṃ navamaṃ|

10. pubbakammapilautikabuddhaapadānaṃ

64.

 ‘‘anautattasarāsan‍nai, ramaṇīyai silātalai|

nānāratanapaj‍jautai, nānāgandhavanantarai||

65.

 ‘‘mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina, paraitau upaitau (udānaṭṭhakathāyaṃ 4 vaggai, 8 suttai) laukanāyakau|

āsīnau byākarī tattha, pubbakammāni attanau||

66.

 suṇātha bhikkhavai mayhaṃ, yaṃ kammaṃ pakataṃ mayā| aikaṃ arañ‍ñikaṃ bhikkhuṃ, disvā din‍naṃ pilautikaṃ| patthitaṃ paṭhamaṃ buddhaṃ, buddhattāya mayā tadā| pilautiyassa kammassa, buddhattaipi vipac‍cati| gaupālakau purai āsiṃ, gāviṃ pājaiti gaucaraṃ| pivantiṃ udakaṃ āvilaṃ, gāviṃ disvā nivārayiṃ| taina kammavipākaina, idha pacchimakai bhavai| vipāsitau yadicchakaṃ, na hi pātuṃ labhāmahaṃ (syā॰) ‘‘suṇātha  bhikkhavau mayhaṃ, yaṃ kammaṃ pakataṃ mayā|

pilautikassa kammassa, buddhattaipi vipac‍cati suṇātha bhikkhavai mayhaṃ, yaṃ kammaṃ pakataṃ mayā| aikaṃ arañ‍ñikaṃ bhikkhuṃ, disvā din‍naṃ pilautikaṃ| patthitaṃ paṭhamaṃ buddhaṃ, buddhattāya mayā tadā| pilautiyassa kammassa, buddhattaipi vipac‍cati| gaupālakau purai āsiṃ, gāviṃ pājaiti gaucaraṃ| pivantiṃ udakaṃ āvilaṃ, gāviṃ disvā nivārayiṃ| taina kammavipākaina, idha pacchimakai bhavai| vipāsitau yadicchakaṃ, na hi pātuṃ labhāmahaṃ (syā॰)||

[1]

67.

 ‘‘munāḷi  nāmahaṃ dhuttau, pubbai añ‍ñāsu jātisu añ‍ñāya jātiyā (udāna aṭṭha॰)|

pac‍caikabuddhaṃ surabhiṃ sarabhuṃ (sī॰), abbhācikkhiṃ adūsakaṃ||

68.

 ‘‘taina kammavipākaina, nirayai saṃsariṃ ciraṃ|

bahūvassasahassāni, dukkhaṃ vaidaisi vaidanaṃ||

69.

 ‘‘taina kammāvasaisaina, idha pacchimakai bhavai|

abbhakkhānaṃ mayā laddhaṃ, sundarikāya kāraṇā||

[2]

70.

 ‘‘sabbābhibhussa  buddhassa, nandau nāmāsi sāvakau|

taṃ abbhakkhāya nirayai, ciraṃ saṃsaritaṃ mayā||

71.

 ‘‘dasavassasahassāni, nirayai saṃsariṃ ciraṃ|

manussabhāvaṃ laddhāhaṃ, abbhakkhānaṃ bahuṃ labhiṃ||

72.

 ‘‘taina kammāvasaisaina, ciñ‍camānavikā mamaṃ|

abbhācikkhi abhūtaina, janakāyassa aggatau||

[3]

73.

 ‘‘brāhmaṇau sutavā āsiṃ, ahaṃ sak‍katapūjitau|

mahāvanai pañ‍casatai, mantai vācaimi māṇavai||

74.

 ‘‘tatthāgatau  tamāgatau (ka॰) isi bhīmau, pañ‍cābhiñ‍ñau mahiddhikau|

taṃ cāhaṃ āgataṃ disvā, abbhācikkhiṃ adūsakaṃ||

75.

 ‘‘tatauhaṃ avacaṃ sissai, kāmabhaugī ayaṃ isi|

mayhampi bhāsamānassa, anumaudiṃsu māṇavā||

76.

 ‘‘tatau māṇavakā sabbai, bhikkhamānaṃ kulai kulai|

mahājanassa āhaṃsu, kāmabhaugī ayaṃ isi||

77.

 ‘‘taina  kammavipākaina, pañ‍ca bhikkhusatā imai|

abbhakkhānaṃ labhuṃ sabbai, sundarikāya kāraṇā||

[4]

78.

 ‘‘vaimātubhātaraṃ pubbai, dhanahaitu haniṃ ahaṃ|

pakkhipiṃ giriduggasmiṃ, silāya ca apiṃsayiṃ||

79.

 ‘‘taina kammavipākaina, daivadattau silaṃ khipi|

aṅguṭṭhaṃ piṃsayī pādai, mama pāsāṇasakkharā||

[5]

80.

 ‘‘puraihaṃ dārakau hutvā, kīḷamānau mahāpathai|

pac‍caikabuddhaṃ disvāna, maggai sakalikaṃ sakkhalikaṃ (ka॰) khipiṃ dahiṃ (syā॰)||

81.

 ‘‘taina kammavipākaina, idha pacchimakai bhavai|

vadhatthaṃ maṃ daivadattau, abhimārai payaujayi||

[6]

82.

 ‘‘hatthārauhau  purai āsiṃ, pac‍caikamunimuttamaṃ|

piṇḍāya vicarantaṃ taṃ, āsādaisiṃ gajainahaṃ||

83.

 ‘‘taina kammavipākaina, bhantau dantau (ka॰) nāḷāgirī gajau|

giribbajai puravarai, dāruṇau samupāgami maṃ upāgami (sī॰)||

[7]

84.

 ‘‘rājāhaṃ  patthivau pattikau (syā॰ ka॰), khattiyau (udāna aṭṭha॰) āsiṃ, sattiyā purisaṃ haniṃ|

taina kammavipākaina, nirayai pac‍cisaṃ bhusaṃ||

85.

 ‘‘kammunau tassa saisaina, idāni sakalaṃ mama|

pādai chaviṃ pakappaisi pakaupaisi (sī॰), na hi kammaṃ vinassati||

[8]

86.

 ‘‘ahaṃ kaivaṭṭagāmasmiṃ, ahuṃ kaivaṭṭadārakau|

macchakai ghātitai disvā, janayiṃ saumanassakaṃ saumanassahaṃ (udāna aṭṭha॰)||

87.

 ‘‘taina  kammavipākaina, sīsadukkhaṃ ahū mama|

sabbai sak‍kā ca hañ‍ñiṃsu, yadā hani viṭaṭūbhau viṭaṭubhau (syā॰ ka॰)||

[9]

88.

 ‘‘phussassāhaṃ pāvacanai, sāvakai paribhāsayiṃ|

yavaṃ khādatha bhuñ‍jatha, mā ca bhuñ‍jatha sālayau||

89.

 ‘‘taina kammavipākaina, taimāsaṃ khāditaṃ yavaṃ|

nimantitau brāhmaṇaina, vairañ‍jāyaṃ vasiṃ tadā||

[10]

90.

 ‘‘nibbuddhai vattamānamhi, mal‍laputtaṃ nihaiṭhayiṃ nisaidhayiṃ (syā॰ ka॰)|

taina kammavipākaina, piṭṭhidukkhaṃ ahū mama||

[11]

91.

 ‘‘tikicchakau ahaṃ āsiṃ, saiṭṭhiputtaṃ viraicayiṃ|

taina kammavipākaina, hauti pakkhandikā mama||

[12]

92.

 ‘‘avacāhaṃ jautipālau, sugataṃ kassapaṃ tadā|

kutau nu baudhi muṇḍassa, baudhi paramadul‍labhā||

93.

 ‘‘taina  kammavipākaina, acariṃ duk‍karaṃ bahuṃ|

chabbassānuruvaiḷāyaṃ, tatau baudhimapāpuṇiṃ||

94.

 ‘‘nāhaṃ aitaina maggaina, pāpuṇiṃ baudhimuttamaṃ|

kummaggaina gavaisissaṃ, pubbakammaina vāritau||

95.

 ‘‘puñ‍ñapāpaparikkhīṇau, sabbasantāpavaj‍jitau|

asaukau anupāyāsau, nibbāyissamanāsavau||

96.

 ‘‘aivaṃ jinau viyākāsi, bhikkhusaṅghassa aggatau|

sabbābhiñ‍ñābalappattau, anautattai mahāsarai’’ti||

itthaṃ  sudaṃ bhagavā attanau pubbacaritaṃ kammapilautikaṃ nāma buddhāpadānadhammapariyāyaṃ abhāsitthāti|

pubbakammapilautikaṃ nāma buddhāpadānaṃ dasamaṃ|

avaṭaphalavaggau aikūnacattālīsamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

avaṭaṃ labujañ‍caiva, udumbarapilakkhu ca|

phāru val‍lī ca kadalī, panasau kauṭivīsakau||

pubbakammapilauti ca, apadānaṃ mahaisinau|

gāthāyau aikanavuti, gaṇitāyau vibhāvibhi||

cuddasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|


40. pilindavacchavaggau

1. pilindavacchatthairaapadānaṃ

1.

 ‘‘nagarai  haṃsavatiyā, āsiṃ dauvārikau ahaṃ|

akkhaubhaṃ amitaṃ bhaugaṃ, gharai san‍nicitaṃ mama||

2.

 ‘‘rahaugatau nisīditvā, pahaṃsitvāna mānasaṃ sampahaṃsitva mānasaṃ (sī॰)|

nisaj‍ja pāsādavarai, aivaṃ cintaisahaṃ tadā||

(cintanākārau)

3.

 ‘‘‘bahū maidhigatā bhaugā, phītaṃ antaipuraṃ mama|

rājāpi rājisi (ka॰) san‍nimantaisi, ānandau pathavissarau||

4.

 ‘‘‘ayañ‍ca buddhau uppan‍nau, adhic‍cuppattikau muni|

saṃvij‍janti ca mai bhaugā, dānaṃ dassāmi satthunau||

5.

 ‘‘‘padumaina rājaputtaina, din‍naṃ dānavaraṃ jinai|

hatthināgai ca pal‍laṅkai, apassainañ‍canappakaṃ||

6.

 ‘‘‘ahampi dānaṃ dassāmi, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

adin‍napubbamañ‍ñaisaṃ, bhavissaṃ ādikammikau||

7.

 ‘‘‘cintaitvāhaṃ bahuvidhaṃ, yāgai yassa sukhaṃphalaṃ|

parikkhāradānamaddakkhiṃ, mama saṅkappapūraṇaṃ||

8.

 ‘‘‘parikkhārāni dassāmi, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

adin‍napubbamañ‍ñaisaṃ, bhavissaṃ ādikammikau’||

(dānavatthusampādanaṃ)

9.

 ‘‘naḷakārai  upāgamma, chattaṃ kāraisi tāvadai|

chattasatasahassāni, aikatau san‍nipātayiṃ||

10.

 ‘‘dussasatasahassāni, aikatau san‍nipātayiṃ|

pattasatasahassāni, aikatau san‍nipātayiṃ||

11.

 ‘‘vāsiyau  satthakai cāpi, sūciyau nakhachaidanai|

haiṭṭhāchattai ṭhapāpaisiṃ, kāraitvā tadanucchavai||

12.

 ‘‘vidhūpanai tālavaṇṭai, maurahatthai ca cāmarai|

parissāvanai tailadhārai tailadharai (sī॰), kārayiṃ tadanucchavai||

13.

 ‘‘sūcigharai aṃsabaddhai, athaupi kāyabandhanai|

ādhārakai ca sukatai, kārayiṃ tadanucchavai||

14.

 ‘‘paribhaugabhājanai ca, athaupi lauhathālakai|

bhaisaj‍jai  pūrayitvāna, haiṭṭhāchattai ṭhapaisahaṃ||

15.

 ‘‘vacaṃ usīraṃ laṭṭhimadhuṃ, pipphalī maricāni ca|

harītakiṃ siṅgīvairaṃ, sabbaṃ pūraisi bhājanai||

16.

 ‘‘upāhanā pādukāyau, athau udakapuñchanai|

kattaradaṇḍai sukatai, kārayiṃ tadanucchavai||

17.

 ‘‘ausadhañ‍jananāḷī ca ausadhaṃ añ‍janāpica (syā॰), salākā dhammakuttarā|

kuñ‍cikā pañ‍cavaṇṇaihi, sibbitai kuñ‍cikāgharai||

18.

 ‘‘āyaugai dhūmanaittai ca, athaupi dīpadhārakai|

tumbakai ca karaṇḍai ca, kārayiṃ tadanucchavai||

19.

 ‘‘saṇḍāsai  pipphalai caiva, athaupi malahārakai|

bhaisaj‍jathavikai caiva, kārayiṃ tadanucchavai||

20.

 ‘‘āsandiyau pīṭhakai ca, pal‍laṅkai caturaumayai|

tadanucchavai kārayitvā, haiṭṭhāchattai ṭhapaisahaṃ||

21.

 ‘‘uṇṇābhisī tūlabhisī, athaupi pīṭhikābhisī pīṭhakābhisī (syā॰ ka॰)|

bimbauhanai bibbauhanai (syā॰ ka॰) ca sukatai, kārayiṃ tadanucchavai||

22.

 ‘‘kuruvindai madhusitthai, tailaṃ hatthappatāpakaṃ|

sipāṭiphalakai sucī, mañ‍caṃ attharaṇaina ca||

23.

 ‘‘saināsanai pādapuñchai, sayanāsanadaṇḍakai|

dantapauṇai ca āṭalī kathaliṃ (syā॰), sīsālaipanagandhakai||

24.

 ‘‘araṇī  phalapīṭhai palālapīṭhai (sī॰) ca, pattapidhānathālakai|

udakassa kaṭacchū ca, cuṇṇakaṃ rajanambaṇaṃ rajanammaṇaṃ (sī॰)||

25.

 ‘‘sammaj‍janaṃ  sammuñ‍janaṃ (syā॰), sammaj‍janiṃ, sammuñ‍janiṃ (?) udapattaṃ, tathā vassikasāṭikaṃ|

nisīdanaṃ kaṇḍucchādi, atha antaravāsakaṃ||

26.

 ‘‘uttarāsaṅgasaṅghāṭī, natthukaṃ mukhasaudhanaṃ|

biḷaṅgalauṇaṃ pahūtañ‍ca lauṇabhūtañ‍ca (ka॰), madhuñ‍ca dadhipānakaṃ||

27.

 ‘‘dhūpaṃ dhūmaṃ (ka॰) sitthaṃ pilautiñ‍ca, mukhapuñchanasuttakaṃ|

dātabbaṃ nāma yaṃ atthi, yañ‍ca kappati satthunau||

28.

 ‘‘sabbamaitaṃ samānaitvā, ānandaṃ upasaṅkamiṃ|

upasaṅkamma  rājānaṃ, janaitāraṃ mahaisinau mahaisinaṃ (sī॰), mahāyasaṃ (syā॰), mahissaraṃ (ka॰)|

sirasā  abhivādaitvā, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

(dānaukāsayācanā)

29.

 ‘‘‘aikatau jātasaṃvaddhā, ubhin‍naṃ aikatau manaṃ yasau (syā॰), manau (?)|

sādhāraṇā sukhadukkhai, ubhau ca anuvattakā||

30.

 ‘‘‘atthi caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ, tavādhaiyyaṃ arindama|

yadi sak‍kausi taṃ dukkhaṃ, vinaudaiyyāsi khattiya||

31.

 ‘‘‘tava dukkhaṃ mama dukkhaṃ, ubhin‍naṃ aikatau manau manaṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

niṭṭhitanti vijānāhi, mamādhaiyyaṃ sacai tuvaṃ||

32.

 ‘‘‘jānāhi khau mahārāja, dukkhaṃ mai dubbinaudayaṃ|

pahu samānau gaj‍jasu, aikaṃ tai duc‍cajaṃ varaṃ||

33.

 ‘‘‘yāvatā vijitai atthi, yāvatā mama jīvitaṃ|

aitaihi yadi tai atthau, dassāmi avikampitau||

34.

 ‘‘‘gaj‍jitaṃ khau tayā daiva, micchā taṃ bahu gaj‍jitaṃ|

jānissāmi tuvaṃ aj‍ja, sabbadhammai sac‍cadhammai (?) patiṭṭhitaṃ||

35.

 ‘‘‘atibāḷhaṃ  nipīḷaisi, dadamānassa mai satau|

kiṃ tai mai pīḷitainatthau, patthitaṃ tai kathaihi mai||

36.

 ‘‘‘icchāmahaṃ mahārāja, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ|

bhaujayissāmi sambuddhaṃ, vaj‍jaṃ vañ‍cuṃ (?) mai māhu jīvitaṃ||

37.

 ‘‘‘añ‍ñaṃ taihaṃ varaṃ dammi, mā yācitthau tathāgataṃ ayācitau tathāgatau (syā॰ ka॰)|

adaiyyau kassaci buddhau, maṇi jautirasau yathā||

38.

 ‘‘‘nanu  tai gaj‍jitaṃ daiva, yāva jīvitamattanau vijitamatthitaṃ (ka॰), jīvitamatthikaṃ (syā॰)|

jīvitaṃ  vijitaṃ (ka॰) dadamānaina, yuttaṃ dātuṃ tathāgataṃ||

39.

 ‘‘‘ṭhapanīyau mahāvīrau, adaiyyau kassaci jinau|

na mai paṭissutau buddhau, varassu amitaṃ dhanaṃ||

40.

 ‘‘‘vinicchayaṃ pāpuṇāma, pucchissāma vinicchayai|

yathāsaṇṭhaṃ yathāsantaṃ (sī॰) kathaissanti, paṭipucchāma taṃ tathā||

41.

 ‘‘‘rañ‍ñau hatthai gahaitvāna, agamāsiṃ vinicchayaṃ|

puratau akkhadassānaṃ, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

42.

 ‘‘‘suṇantu  mai akkhadassā, rājā varamadāsi mai|

na kiñ‍ci ṭhapayitvāna, jīvitampi vijitaṃpi (ka॰) pavārayi||

43.

 ‘‘‘tassa mai varadin‍nassa, buddhasaiṭṭhaṃ variṃ ahaṃ|

sudin‍nau hauti mai buddhau, chindatha saṃsayaṃ mama||

44.

 ‘‘‘saussāma tava vacanaṃ, bhūmipālassa rājinau|

ubhin‍naṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, chindissāmaittha saṃsayaṃ||

45.

 ‘‘‘sabbaṃ daiva tayā din‍naṃ, imassa sabbagāhikaṃ sabbagāhitaṃ (syā॰ ka॰)|

na kiñ‍ci ṭhapayitvāna, jīvitampi pavārayi||

46.

 ‘‘‘kicchappattauva hutvāna, yācī varamanuttaraṃ yāvajīvamanuttaraṃ (syā॰ ka॰)|

imaṃ sudukkhitaṃ ñatvā, adāsiṃ sabbagāhikaṃ||

47.

 ‘‘‘parājayau  tuvaṃ tavaṃ (sī॰) daiva, assa daiyyau tathāgatau|

ubhin‍naṃ saṃsayau chin‍nau, yathāsaṇṭhamhi yathāsantamhi (sī॰) tiṭṭhatha||

48.

 ‘‘‘rājā tatthaiva ṭhatvāna, akkhadassaitadabravi|

sammā  mayhampi daiyyātha, puna buddhaṃ labhāmahaṃ||

49.

 ‘‘‘pūraitvā tava saṅkappaṃ, bhaujayitvā tathāgataṃ|

puna daiyyāsi daiyyātha (ka॰) sambuddhaṃ, ānandassa yasassinau’||

(nimantanakathā)

50.

 ‘‘akkhadassaibhivādaitvā, ānandañ‍cāpi khattiyaṃ|

tuṭṭhau pamuditau hutvā, sambuddhamupasaṅkamiṃ||

51.

 ‘‘upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ, aughatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ|

sirasā abhivādaitvā, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

52.

 ‘vasīsatasahassaihi, adhivāsaihi cakkhuma|

hāsayantau mama cittaṃ, nivaisanamupaihi mai’||

53.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mama saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, adhivāsaisi cakkhumā||

54.

 ‘‘adhivāsanamañ‍ñāya, abhivādiya satthunau|

haṭṭhau udaggacittauhaṃ, nivaisanamupāgamiṃ||

(dānapaṭiyādanaṃ)

55.

 ‘‘mittāmac‍cai samānaitvā, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ|

‘sudul‍labhau mayā laddhau, maṇi jautirasau yathā||

56.

 ‘‘‘kaina taṃ pūjayissāma, appamaiyyau anūpamau|

atulau asamau dhīrau, jinau appaṭipuggalau||

57.

 ‘‘‘tathāsamasamau caiva, adutiyau narāsabhau|

duk‍karaṃ adhikārañhi, buddhānucchavikaṃ mayā||

58.

 ‘‘‘nānāpupphai  samānaitvā, karauma pupphamaṇḍapaṃ|

buddhānucchavikaṃ  aitaṃ, sabbapūjā bhavissati’||

59.

 ‘‘uppalaṃ  padumaṃ vāpi, vassikaṃ adhimuttakaṃ atimuttakaṃ (?)|

campakaṃ candanaṃ (ka॰) nāgapupphañ‍ca, maṇḍapaṃ kārayiṃ ahaṃ||

60.

 ‘‘satāsanasahassāni, chattacchāyāya pañ‍ñapiṃ|

pacchimaṃ āsanaṃ mayhaṃ, adhikaṃ satamagghati||

61.

 ‘‘satāsanasahassāni, chattacchāyāya pañ‍ñapiṃ|

paṭiyādaitvā an‍napānaṃ, kālaṃ āraucayiṃ ahaṃ||

62.

 ‘‘āraucitamhi kālamhi, padumuttarau mahāmuni|

vasīsatasahassaihi, nivaisanamupaisi mai||

63.

 ‘‘dhāraintaṃ uparicchattaṃ dhāraintamuparicchattai (sī॰), suphul‍lapupphamaṇḍapai|

vasīsatasahassaihi, nisīdi purisuttamau||

64.

 ‘‘‘chattasatasahassāni, satasahassamāsanaṃ|

kappiyaṃ anavaj‍jañ‍ca, paṭigaṇhāhi cakkhuma’||

65.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

mamaṃ tāraitukāmau sau, sampaṭicchi mahāmuni||

(dānakathā)

66.

 ‘‘bhikkhunau  aikamaikassa, pac‍caikaṃ pattamadāsahaṃ|

jahiṃsu sumbhakaṃ pubbakaṃ (sī॰), sambhataṃ (syā॰), mattikaṃ (?) pattaṃ, lauhapattaṃ adhārayuṃ||

67.

 ‘‘sattarattindivaṃ buddhau, nisīdi pupphamaṇḍapai|

baudhayantau bahū sattai, dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattayi||

68.

 ‘‘dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaintau, haiṭṭhatau pupphamaṇḍapai|

cul‍lāsītisahassānaṃ, dhammābhisamayau ahu||

69.

 ‘‘sattamai divasai pattai, padumuttarau mahāmuni|

chattacchāyāyamāsīnau, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

(byākaraṇaṃ)

70.

 ‘‘‘anūnakaṃ dānavaraṃ, yau mai pādāsi māṇavau|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

71.

 ‘‘‘hatthī  assā rathā pattī, sainā ca caturaṅginī|

parivāraissantimaṃ taṃ (syā॰) nic‍caṃ, sabbadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

72.

 ‘‘‘hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ, sivikā sandamānikā|

upaṭṭhissantimaṃ nic‍caṃ, sabbadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

73.

 ‘‘‘saṭṭhi rathasahassāni, sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā|

parivāraissantimaṃ nic‍caṃ, sabbadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

74.

 ‘‘‘saṭṭhi  tūriyasahassāni, bhairiyau samalaṅkatā|

vaj‍jayissantimaṃ nic‍caṃ, sabbadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

75.

 ‘‘‘chaḷāsītisahassāni, nāriyau samalaṅkatā|

vicittavatthābharaṇā, āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalā āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā (sī॰ syā॰)||

76.

 ‘‘‘aḷārapamhā hasulā, susañ‍ñā tanumajjhimā|

parivāraissantimaṃ nic‍caṃ, sabbadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

77.

 ‘‘‘tiṃsakappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati|

sahassakkhattuṃ daivindau, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati||

78.

 ‘‘‘sahassakkhattuṃ rājā ca, cak‍kavattī bhavissati|

padaisaraj‍jaṃ  vipulaṃ, gaṇanātau asaṅkhiyaṃ||

79.

 ‘‘‘daivalaukai  vasantassa, puñ‍ñakammasamaṅginau|

daivalaukapariyantaṃ, ratanachattaṃ dharissati||

80.

 ‘‘‘icchissati yadā chāyaṃ yadā vāyaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), chadanaṃ dussapupphajaṃ|

imassa cittamañ‍ñāya, nibaddhaṃ chādayissati||

81.

 ‘‘‘daivalaukā cavitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

puñ‍ñakammaina saṃyuttau, brahmabandhu bhavissati||

82.

 ‘‘‘kappasatasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina nāmaina (sabbattha) aivamuparipi| aṭṭhathāyaṃ pana pubbai gauttainātipadaṃ vaṇṇitaṃ, satthā laukai bhavissati||

83.

 ‘‘‘sabbamaitaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, gautamau sakyapuṅgavau|

bhikkhusaṅghai nisīditvā, aitadaggai ṭhapaissati||

84.

 ‘‘‘pilindavacchanāmaina  pilindivacchanāmaina (sī॰), haissati satthusāvakau|

daivānaṃ asurānañ‍ca, gandhabbānañ‍ca sak‍katau||

85.

 ‘‘‘bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnañ‍ca, gihīnañ‍ca tathaiva sau|

piyau hutvāna sabbaisaṃ, viharissatināsavau’||

(dānānisaṃsakathā)

86.

 ‘‘satasahassai kataṃ kammaṃ, phalaṃ dassaisi mai idha|

sumuttau saravaigauva, kilaisai jhāpayī mama jhāpayissati (sī॰ ka॰), jhāpayiṃ ahaṃ (syā॰)||

87.

 ‘‘ahau mai sukataṃ kammaṃ, puñ‍ñakkhaittai anuttarai|

yattha kāraṃ karitvāna, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

88.

 ‘‘anūnakaṃ dānavaraṃ, adāsi yau sau (ka॰) hi māṇavau|

ādipubbaṅgamau  āsi, tassa dānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

(1. chattānisaṃsau)

89.

 ‘‘chattai ca sugatai datvā chattai sugatai datvāna (sī॰ syā॰), saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

aṭṭhānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

90.

 ‘‘sītaṃ  uṇhaṃ na jānāmi, rajaujal‍laṃ na limpati|

anupaddavau anīti ca, haumi apacitau sadā||

91.

 ‘‘sukhumacchavikau haumi, visadaṃ hauti mānasaṃ|

chattasatasahassāni, bhavai saṃsaratau mama||

92.

 ‘‘sabbālaṅkārayuttāni, tassa kammassa vāhasā|

imaṃ jātiṃ ṭhapaitvāna, matthakai dhārayanti mai||

93.

 ‘‘kasmā tasmā (syā॰ ka॰) imāya jātiyā, natthi mai chattadhāraṇā|

mama sabbaṃ kataṃ kammaṃ, vimuttichattapattiyā||

(2. dussānisaṃsau)

94.

 ‘‘dussāni sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

aṭṭhānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

95.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇau  virajau, sappabhāsau patāpavā|

siniddhaṃ hauti mai gattaṃ, bhavai saṃsaratau mama||

96.

 ‘‘dussasatasahassāni, saitā pītā ca lauhitā|

dhārainti matthakai mayhaṃ, dussadānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

97.

 ‘‘kausaiyyakambaliyāni, khaumakappāsikāni ca|

sabbattha paṭilabhāmi, taisaṃ nissandatau ahaṃ||

(3. pattānisaṃsau)

98.

 ‘‘pattai sugatai datvāna, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

dasānisaṃsai  anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

99.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇathālai maṇithālai, rajataipi ca thālakai|

lauhitaṅgamayai thālai, paribhuñ‍jāmi sabbadā||

100.

 ‘‘anupaddavau anīti ca, haumi apacitau sadā|

lābhī an‍nassa pānassa, vatthassa sayanassa ca||

101.

 ‘‘na vinassanti mai bhaugā, ṭhitacittau bhavāmahaṃ|

dhammakāmau sadā haumi, appak‍laisau anāsavau||

102.

 ‘‘daivalaukai manussai vā, anubandhā imai guṇā|

chāyā yathāpi rukkhassa, sabbattha na jahanti maṃ||

(4. vāsiānisaṃsau)

103.

 ‘‘cittabandhanasambaddhā cittabandhanasampan‍nā (ka॰), sukatā vāsiyau bahū|

datvāna buddhasaiṭṭhassa, saṅghassa ca tathaivahaṃ||

104.

 ‘‘aṭṭhānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama|

sūrau  haumavisārī ca, vaisāraj‍jaisu pāramī||

105.

 ‘‘dhitivīriyavā  haumi, paggahītamanau sadā|

kilaisacchaidanaṃ ñāṇaṃ, sukhumaṃ atulaṃ suciṃ|

sabbattha paṭilabhāmi, tassa nissandatau ahaṃ||

(5. satthakānisaṃsau)

106.

 ‘‘akak‍kasai  apharusai, sudhautai satthakai bahū|

pasan‍nacittau datvāna, buddhai saṅghai tathaiva ca||

107.

 ‘‘pañ‍cānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama|

kalyāṇamittaṃ kalyāṇacittaṃ (sī॰) vīriyaṃ, khantiñ‍ca maittasatthakaṃ||

108.

 ‘‘taṇhāsal‍lassa  chin‍nattā, pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ anuttaraṃ|

vajiraina samaṃ ñāṇaṃ, taisaṃ nissandatau labhai||

(6. sūciānisaṃsau)

109.

 ‘‘sūciyau sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

pañ‍cānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

110.

 ‘‘na saṃsayau kaṅkhacchaidau, abhirūpau ca bhaugavā|

tikkhapañ‍ñau sadā haumi, saṃsarantau bhavābhavai||

111.

 ‘‘gambhīraṃ nipuṇaṃ ṭhānaṃ, atthaṃ ñāṇaina passayiṃ|

vajiraggasamaṃ ñāṇaṃ, hauti mai tamaghātanaṃ||

(7. nakhacchaidanānisaṃsau)

112.

 ‘‘nakhacchaidanai sugatai, datvā saṅghai gaṇuttamai|

pañ‍cānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

113.

 ‘‘dāsidāsai dāsidāsa (ka॰) gavassai ca, bhatakai nāṭakai ārakkhakai (sī॰) bahū|

nhāpitai bhattakai sūdai, sabbatthaiva labhāmahaṃ||

(8. vidhūpanatālavaṇṭānisaṃsau)

114.

 ‘‘vidhūpanai sugatai datvā, tālavaṇṭai ca saubhaṇai|

aṭṭhānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

115.

 ‘‘sītaṃ uṇhaṃ na jānāmi, pariḷāhau na vij‍jati|

darathaṃ nābhijānāmi, cittasantāpanaṃ mama||

116.

 ‘‘rāgaggi  dausamauhaggi, mānaggi diṭṭhiaggi ca|

sabbaggī nibbutā mayhaṃ, tassa nissandatau mama||

(9. maurahattha-cāmaraṃ)

117.

 ‘‘maurahatthai  cāmariyau, datvā saṅghai gaṇuttamai|

upasantakilaisauhaṃ, viharāmi anaṅgaṇau||

(10. parissāvana-dhammakaraṃ)

118.

 ‘‘parissāvanai  sugatai, datvā dhammakaruttamai datvā sukatai dhammakuttarai (syā॰ ka॰)|

pañ‍cānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

119.

 ‘‘sabbaisaṃ samatik‍kamma, dibbaṃ āyuṃ labhāmahaṃ|

appasayhau sadā haumi, caurapac‍catthikaihi vā||

120.

 ‘‘satthaina vā visaina vā, vihaisampi na kubbatai|

antarāmaraṇaṃ natthi, taisaṃ nissandatau mama||

(11. tailadhārānisaṃsau)

121.

 ‘‘tailadhārai sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

pañ‍cānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

122.

 ‘‘sucārurūpau subhaddau sugadau (sī॰), suvācau (?), susamuggatamānasau|

avikkhittamanau haumi, sabbārakkhaihi rakkhitau||

(12. sūcigharānisaṃsau)

123.

 ‘‘sūcigharai sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

tīṇānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

124.

 ‘‘caitausukhaṃ kāyasukhaṃ, iriyāpathajaṃ sukhaṃ|

imai guṇai paṭilabhai, tassa nissandatau ahaṃ||

(13. aṃsabaddhānisaṃsau)

125.

 ‘‘aṃsabaddhai jinai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

tīṇānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

126.

 ‘‘saddhammai  gādhaṃ caitauñāṇaṃ ca (sī॰) vindāmi, sarāmi dutiyaṃ bhavaṃ|

sabbattha succhavī haumi, tassa nissandatau ahaṃ||

(14. kāyabandhanānisaṃsau)

127.

 ‘‘kāyabandhai jinai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

chānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

128.

 ‘‘samādhīsu  na kampāmi, vasī haumi samādhisu|

abhaij‍japarisau haumi, ādaiyyavacanau sadā||

129.

 ‘‘upaṭṭhitasati haumi, tāsau mayhaṃ na vij‍jati|

daivalaukai manussai vā, anubandhā imai guṇā||

(15. ādhārakānisaṃsau)

130.

 ‘‘ādhārakai jinai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

pañ‍cavaṇṇaihi dāyādau pañ‍cavaṇṇai bhayābhāvau (syā॰), acalau haumi kainaci||

131.

 ‘‘yai  kaici mai sutā dhammā, satiñāṇappabaudhanā|

dhatā ṭhitā (ka॰) mai na vinassanti, bhavanti suvinicchitā||

(16. bhājanānisaṃsau)

132.

 ‘‘bhājanai paribhaugai ca, datvā buddhai gaṇuttamai|

tīṇānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

133.

 ‘‘sauṇṇamayai maṇimayai, athaupi phalikāmayai|

lauhitaṅgamayai caiva, labhāmi bhājanai ahaṃ||

134.

 ‘‘bhariyā dāsadāsī sabbatthapi aivamaiva dissati ca, hatthissarathapattikai|

itthī patibbatā caiva, paribhaugāni sabbadā||

135.

 ‘‘vij‍jā mantapadai caiva, vividhai āgamai bahū|

sabbaṃ sippaṃ nisāmaimi, paribhaugāni sabbadā||

(17. thālakānisaṃsau)

136.

 ‘‘thālakai sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

tīṇānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

137.

 ‘‘sauṇṇamayai  maṇimayai, athaupi phalikāmayai|

lauhitaṅgamayai caiva, labhāmi thālakai ahaṃ||

138.

 ‘‘asatthakai  asatthakai (sī॰), assaṭṭhakai (syā॰) phalamayai, athau paukkharapattakai|

madhupānakasaṅkhai ca, labhāmi thālakai ahaṃ||

139.

 ‘‘vattai guṇai paṭipatti, ācārakiriyāsu ca|

imai guṇai paṭilabhai, tassa nissandatau ahaṃ||

(18. bhaisaj‍jānisaṃsau)

140.

 ‘‘bhaisaj‍jaṃ  sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

dasānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

141.

 ‘‘āyuvā balavā dhīrau, vaṇṇavā yasavā sukhī|

anupaddavau anīti ca, haumi apacitau sadā|

na mai piyaviyaugatthi, tassa nissandatau mama||

(19. upāhanānisaṃsau)

142.

 ‘‘upāhanai jinai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

tīṇānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

143.

 ‘‘hatthiyānaṃ assayānaṃ, sivikā sandamānikā|

saṭṭhisatasahassāni, parivārainti maṃ sadā||

144.

 ‘‘maṇimayā tambamayā kambalikā (sī॰ ka॰), sauṇṇarajatapādukā|

nibbattanti paduddhārai, bhavai saṃsaratau mama||

145.

 ‘‘niyāmaṃ  sati dhāvanti niyamaṃ paṭidhāvantī (sī॰), niyāmaṃ paṭidhāvanti (syā॰), āguācārasaudhanaṃ ācāraguṇasaudhanaṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

imai guṇai paṭilabhai, tassa nissandatau ahaṃ||

(20. pādukānisaṃsau)

146.

 ‘‘pādukai sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

iddhipādukamāruyha, viharāmi yadicchakaṃ||

(21. udakapuñchanānisaṃsau)

147.

 ‘‘udakapucchanacauḷai, datvā buddhai gaṇuttamai|

pañ‍cānisaṃsai  anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

148.

 ‘‘suvaṇṇavaṇṇau virajau, sappabhāsau patāpavā|

siniddhaṃ hauti mai gattaṃ, rajaujal‍laṃ na limpati|

imai guṇai paṭilabhai, tassa nissandatau ahaṃ||

(22. kattaradaṇḍānisaṃsau)

149.

 ‘‘kattaradaṇḍai sugatai, datvā saṅghai gaṇuttamai|

chānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

150.

 ‘‘puttā  mayhaṃ bahū haunti, tāsau mayhaṃ na vij‍jati|

appasayhau sadā haumi, sabbārakkhaihi rakkhitau|

khalitampi khalitaṃ maṃ (sī॰ ka॰) na jānāmi, abhantaṃ mānasaṃ mama||

(23. ausadhañ‍janānisaṃsau)

151.

 ‘‘ausadhaṃ añ‍janaṃ datvā, buddhai saṅghai gaṇuttamai|

aṭṭhānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

152.

 ‘‘visālanayanau haumi, saitapītau ca lauhitau|

anāvilapasan‍nakkhau, sabbaraugavivaj‍jitau||

153.

 ‘‘labhāmi dibbanayanaṃ, pañ‍ñācakkhuṃ anuttaraṃ|

imai guṇai paṭilabhai, tassa nissandatau ahaṃ||

(24. kuñ‍cikānisaṃsau)

154.

 ‘‘kuñ‍cikai sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

dhammadvāravivaraṇaṃ, labhāmi ñāṇakuñ‍cikaṃ||

(25. kuñ‍cikāgharānisaṃsau)

155.

 ‘‘kuñ‍cikānaṃ gharai datvā, buddhai saṅghai gaṇuttamai|

dvānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama|

appakaudhau  anāyāsau, saṃsarantau bhavai ahaṃ||

(26. āyaugānisaṃsau)

156.

 ‘‘āyaugai  sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

pañ‍cānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

157.

 ‘‘samādhīsu na kampāmi, vasī haumi samādhisu|

abhaij‍japarisau haumi, ādaiyyavacanau sadā|

jāyati bhaugasampatti, bhavai saṃsaratau mama||

(27. dhūmanaittānisaṃsau)

158.

 ‘‘dhūmanaittai jinai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

tīṇānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

159.

 ‘‘sati  mai ujukā hauti, susambandhā ca nhāravau|

labhāmi dibbanayanaṃ dibbasayanaṃ (syā॰), tassa nissandatau ahaṃ||

(28. dīpadhārānisaṃsau)

160.

 ‘‘dīpadhārai  dīpaṭṭhānai (sī॰), dīpadānai (syā॰), dīpaṭṭhāpai (ka॰) jinai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

tīṇānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

161.

 ‘‘jātimā aṅgasampan‍nau, pañ‍ñavā buddhasammatau buddhisammatau (sī॰ ka॰)|

imai guṇai paṭilabhai, tassa nissandatau ahaṃ||

(29. tumbaka-karaṇḍau)

162.

 ‘‘tumbakai ca karaṇḍai ca, datvā buddhai gaṇuttamai|

dasānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

163.

 ‘‘suguttau sadāguttau (sī॰ syā॰) saṃguttau (ka॰) sukhasamaṅgī, mahāyasau tathāgati|

vipattivigatau vibhattigattau (syā॰) sukhumālau, sabbītiparivaj‍jitau||

164.

 ‘‘vipulai ca guṇai lābhī, samāva calanā mama|

suvivaj‍jitaubbaigau, tumbakai ca karaṇḍakai||

165.

 ‘‘labhāmi  caturau vaṇṇai, hatthissaratanāni ca|

tāni mai na vinassanti, tumbadānai idaṃ phalaṃ||

(30. malaharaṇānisaṃsau)

166.

 ‘‘malaharaṇiyau  añ‍jananāḷiyau (sī॰), hatthalilaṅgakai (syā॰ pī॰), hatthalilaṅgatai (ka॰) datvā, buddhai saṅghai gaṇuttamai|

pañ‍cānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

167.

 ‘‘sabbalakkhaṇasampan‍nau, āyupañ‍ñāsamāhitau|

sabbāyāsavinimuttau, kāyau mai hauti sabbadā||

(31. pipphalānisaṃsau)

168.

 ‘‘taṇudhārai sunisitai, saṅghai datvāna pipphalai|

kilaisakantanaṃ ñāṇaṃ, labhāmi atulaṃ suciṃ||

(32. bhaṇḍāsānisaṃsau)

169.

 ‘‘saṇḍāsai sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

kilaisabhañ‍janaṃ kilaisaluñ‍canaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) ñāṇaṃ, labhāmi atulaṃ suciṃ||

(33. natthukānisaṃsau)

170.

 ‘‘natthukai thavikai (?) bhaisaj‍jathavikaiti hi pubbai vuttaṃ sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

aṭṭhānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

171.

 ‘‘saddhaṃ  sīlaṃ hiriñ‍cāpi, atha auttappiyaṃ guṇaṃ|

sutaṃ cāgañ‍ca khantiñ‍ca, pañ‍ñaṃ mai aṭṭhamaṃ guṇaṃ||

(34. pīṭhakānisaṃsau)

172.

 ‘‘pīṭhakai  sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

pañ‍cānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

173.

 ‘‘uc‍cai kulai pajāyāmi, mahābhaugau bhavāmahaṃ|

sabbai maṃ apacāyanti, kitti abbhuggatā mama||

174.

 ‘‘kappasatasahassāni, pal‍laṅkā caturassakā|

parivārainti maṃ nic‍caṃ, saṃvibhāgaratau ahaṃ||

(35. bhisiānisaṃsau)

175.

 ‘‘bhisiyau  sugatai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

chānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

176.

 ‘‘samasugattaupacitau, mudukau cārudassanau|

labhāmi ñāṇaparivāraṃ, bhisidānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

177.

 ‘‘tūlikā vikatikāyau, kaṭṭissā kaṭṭhissā (sī॰), kuṭṭakā (ka॰) cittakā bahū|

varapautthakai kambalai ca, labhāmi vividhai ahaṃ||

178.

 ‘‘pāvārikai ca mudukai, mudukājinavaiṇiyau|

labhāmi vividhatthārai vividhaṭṭhānai (syā॰ ka॰), bhisidānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

179.

 ‘‘yatau sarāmi attānaṃ, yatau pattausmi viñ‍ñutaṃ|

atucchau jhānamañ‍caumhi, bhisidānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

(36. bibbauhanānisaṃsau)

180.

 ‘‘bibbauhanai jinai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

chānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

181.

 ‘‘uṇṇikai padumakai ca, athau lauhitacandanai|

bibbauhanai upādhaimi, uttamaṅgaṃ sadā mama||

182.

 ‘‘aṭṭhaṅgikai maggavarai, sāmañ‍ñai caturau phalai|

taisu ñāṇaṃ uppādaitvā upanaitvā (sī॰), viharai nic‍cakālikaṃ||

183.

 ‘‘dānai  damai saṃyamai ca, appamañ‍ñāsu rūpisu|

taisu ñāṇaṃ uppādaitvā upanaitvā (sī॰), viharai sabbakālikaṃ||

184.

 ‘‘vattai guṇai paṭipatti, ācārakiriyāsu ca|

taisu ñāṇaṃ uppādaitvā ñāṇaṃ upadahitvāna (sī॰), viharai sabbadā ahaṃ||

185.

 ‘‘caṅkamai  vā padhānai vā, vīriyai baudhipakkhiyai|

taisu ñāṇaṃ uppādaitvā, viharāmi yadicchakaṃ||

186.

 ‘‘sīlaṃ  samādhi pañ‍ñā ca, vimutti ca anuttarā|

taisu ñāṇaṃ uppādaitvā ñāṇaṃ upadahitvāna (sī॰), viharāmi sukhaṃ ahaṃ||

(37. phalapīṭhānisaṃsau)

187.

 ‘‘phalapīṭhai palālapīṭṭhai (sī॰) jinai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

dvānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

188.

 ‘‘sauṇṇamayai maṇimayai, dantasāramayai bahū|

pal‍laṅkasaiṭṭhai vindāmi, phalapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ||

(38. pādapīṭhānisaṃsau)

189.

 ‘‘pādapīṭhai jinai datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

labhāmi bahukai yānai, pādapīṭhassidaṃ phalaṃ||

190.

 ‘‘dāsī dāsā ca bhariyā, yai cañ‍ñai anujīvinau|

(39. tailabbhañ‍janānisaṃsau)

191.

 ‘‘tailaabbhañ‍janai tailānabbhañ‍janai (sī॰) datvā, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

pañ‍cānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

192.

 ‘‘abyādhitā rūpavatā, khippaṃ dhammanisantitā|

(40. sappitailānisaṃsau)

193.

 ‘‘sappitailañ‍ca datvāna, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

pañ‍cānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

194.

 ‘‘thāmavā rūpasampan‍nau, pahaṭṭhatanujau sadā|

(41. mukhasaudhanakānisaṃsau)

195.

 ‘‘mukhasaudhanakaṃ  datvā, buddhai saṅghai gaṇuttamai|

pañ‍cānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

196.

 ‘‘visuddhakaṇṭhau madhurassarau, kāsasāsavivaj‍jitau|

uppalagandhau mukhatau, upavāyati mai sadā||

(42. dadhiānisaṃsau)

197.

 ‘‘dadhiṃ datvāna sampan‍naṃ, buddhai saṅghai gaṇuttamai|

bhuñ‍jāmi amataṃ bhattaṃ vittaṃ (sī॰ ka॰), varaṃ kāyagatāsatiṃ||

(43. madhuānisaṃsau)

198.

 ‘‘vaṇṇagandharasaupaitaṃ, madhuṃ datvā jinai gaṇai|

anūpamaṃ atuliyaṃ, pivai muttirasaṃ ahaṃ||

(44.rasānisaṃsau)

199.

 ‘‘yathābhūtaṃ  rasaṃ datvā, buddhai saṅghai gaṇuttamai|

(45. an‍napānānisaṃsau)

200.

 ‘‘an‍naṃ pānañ‍ca datvāna, buddhai saṅghai gaṇuttamai|

dasānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

201.

 ‘‘āyuvā balavā dhīrau, vaṇṇavā yasavā sukhī|

lābhī an‍nassa pānassa, sūrau pañ‍ñāṇavā sadā|

imai guṇai paṭilabhai, saṃsarantau bhavai ahaṃ||

(46. dhūpānisaṃsau)

202.

 ‘‘dhūpaṃ dhūmaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) datvāna sugatai, saṅghai gaṇavaruttamai|

dasānisaṃsai anubhaumi, kammānucchavikai mama||

203.

 ‘‘sugandhadaihau yasavā, sīghapañ‍ñau ca kittimā|

tikkhapañ‍ñau bhūripañ‍ñau, hāsagambhīrapañ‍ñavā||

204.

 ‘‘vaipul‍lajavanapañ‍ñau, saṃsarantau bhavābhavai|

tassaiva vāhasā dāni, pattau santisukhaṃ sivaṃ||

(sādhāraṇānisaṃsau)

205.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

nāgauva bandhanaṃ chaitvā, viharāmi anāsavau||

206.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi, mama buddhassa santikai buddhasaiṭṭhassa santikai (?) aivamuparipi| aitadaiva hi upālitthairāpadānaṭṭhakathāyaṃ vaṇṇitaṃ|

tissau vij‍jā anuppattā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

207.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau, vimaukkhāpi ca aṭṭhimai|

chaḷabhiñ‍ñā sacchikatā, kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā pilindavacchau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

pilindavacchatthairassāpadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. sailatthairaapadānaṃ

208.

 ‘‘nagarai haṃsavatiyā, vīthisāmī ahausahaṃ|

mama ñātī samānaitvā, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

209.

 ‘‘‘buddhau laukai samuppan‍nau, puñ‍ñakkhaittau anuttarau puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ anuttaraṃ (sī॰)|

āsi sau āsīsau (sī॰), ādhārau (pī॰) sabbalaukassa, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau||

210.

 ‘‘‘khattiyā naigamā caiva, mahāsālā ca brāhmaṇā|

pasan‍nacittā sumanā, pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu tai||

211.

 ‘‘‘hatthārauhā  anīkaṭṭhā, rathikā pattikārakā|

pasan‍nacittā sumanā, pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu tai||

212.

 ‘‘‘uggā  ca rājaputtā ca, vaisiyānā ca brāhmaṇā|

pasan‍nacittā sumanā, pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu tai||

213.

 ‘‘‘āḷārikā  kappakā āḷārikā ca sūdā (syā॰) ca, nhāpakā mālakārakā|

pasan‍nacittā sumanā, pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu tai||

214.

 ‘‘‘rajakā paisakārā ca, cammakārā ca nhāpitā|

pasan‍nacittā sumanā, pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu tai||

215.

 ‘‘‘usukārā bhamakārā, cammakārā ca tacchakā|

pasan‍nacittā sumanā, pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu tai||

216.

 ‘‘‘kammārā  sauṇṇakārā ca, tipulauhakarā tathā|

pasan‍nacittā sumanā, pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu tai||

217.

 ‘‘‘bhatakā caiṭakā caiva, dāsakammakarā bahū|

yathāsakaina thāmaina, pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu tai||

218.

 ‘‘‘udahārā kaṭṭhahārā, kassakā tiṇahārakā|

yathāsakaina thāmaina, pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu tai||

219.

 ‘‘‘pupphikā mālikā caiva, paṇṇikā phalahārakā|

yathāsakaina thāmaina, pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu tai||

220.

 ‘‘‘gaṇikā kumbhadāsī ca, pūvikā sūpikā (ka॰) macchikāpi ca|

yathāsakaina thāmaina, pūgadhammaṃ akaṃsu tai||

221.

 ‘‘‘aitha  sabbai samāgantvā, gaṇaṃ bandhāma aikatau|

adhikāraṃ karissāma, puñ‍ñakkhaittai anuttarai’||

222.

 ‘‘tai mai sutvāna vacanaṃ, gaṇaṃ bandhiṃsu tāvadai|

upaṭṭhānasālaṃ sukataṃ, bhikkhusaṅghassa kārayuṃ||

223.

 ‘‘niṭṭhāpaitvāna taṃ sālaṃ, udaggau tuṭṭhamānasau|

paraitau taihi sabbaihi, sambuddhamupasaṅkamiṃ||

224.

 ‘‘upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ, laukanāthaṃ narāsabhaṃ|

vanditvā satthunau pādai, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

225.

 ‘‘‘imai tīṇi satā vīra, purisā aikatau gaṇā|

upaṭṭhānasālaṃ sukataṃ, niyyādainti niyyātainti (sī॰) tuvaṃ tavaṃ (sī॰), tava (syā॰) muni’||

226.

 ‘‘bhikkhusaṅghassa  puratau, sampaṭicchatva cakkhumā|

tiṇṇaṃ satānaṃ puratau, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

227.

 ‘‘‘tisatāpi ca jaiṭṭhau ca, anuvattiṃsu aikatau|

sampattiñhi sampattīhi (syā॰ ka॰) karitvāna, sabbai anubhavissatha||

228.

 ‘‘‘pacchimai  bhavai sampattai, sītibhāvamanuttaraṃ|

ajaraṃ amataṃ santaṃ, nibbānaṃ phassayissatha’||

229.

 ‘‘aivaṃ buddhau viyākāsi, sabbañ‍ñū samaṇuttarau|

buddhassa vacanaṃ sutvā, saumanassaṃ pavaidayiṃ||

230.

 ‘‘tiṃsa  kappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramiṃ ahaṃ|

daivādhipau pañ‍casataṃ, daivaraj‍jamakārayiṃ||

231.

 ‘‘sahassakkhattuṃ  rājā ca, cak‍kavattī ahausahaṃ|

daivaraj‍jaṃ karauntassa, mahādaivā avandisuṃ||

232.

 ‘‘idha mānusakai raj‍jaṃ raj‍jai (sī॰), parisā haunti bandhavā|

pacchimai bhavai sampattai, vāsaiṭṭhau nāma brāhmaṇau||

233.

 ‘‘asītikauṭi nicayau, tassa puttau ahausahaṃ|

sailau iti mama nāmaṃ, chaḷaṅgai pāramiṃ gatau||

234.

 ‘‘jaṅghāvihāraṃ vicaraṃ, sasissaihi purakkhatau|

jaṭābhārikabharitaṃ, kaiṇiyaṃ nāma tāpasaṃ||

235.

 ‘‘paṭiyattāhutiṃ disvā, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ|

‘āvāhau vā vivāhau vā, rājā vā tai nimantitau’||

236.

 ‘‘āhutiṃ nāhutiṃ (?) yiṭṭhukāmauhaṃ, brāhmaṇai daivasammatai|

na nimantaimi rājānaṃ, āhutī mai na vij‍jati||

237.

 ‘‘na catthi mayhamāvāhau, vivāhau mai na vij‍jati|

sakyānaṃ nandijananau, saiṭṭhau laukai sadaivakai||

238.

 ‘‘sabbalaukahitatthāya, sabbasattasukhāvahau|

sau mai nimantitau aj‍ja, tassaitaṃ paṭiyādanaṃ||

239.

 ‘‘timbarūsakavaṇṇābhau, appamaiyyau anūpamau|

rūpaināsadisau buddhau, svātanāya nimantitau||

240.

 ‘‘uk‍kāmukhapahaṭṭhauva, khadiraṅgārasan‍nibhau|

vij‍jūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

241.

 ‘‘pabbataggai  yathā ac‍ci, puṇṇamāyaiva candimā|

naḷaggivaṇṇasaṅkāsau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

242.

 ‘‘asambhītau bhayātītau, bhavantakaraṇau muni|

sīhūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

243.

 ‘‘kusalau  buddhadhammaihi, apasayhau paraihi sau|

nāgūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

244.

 ‘‘saddhammācārakusalau, buddhanāgau asādisau|

usabhūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

245.

 ‘‘anantavaṇṇau amitayasau, vicittasabbalakkhaṇau|

sak‍kūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

246.

 ‘‘vasī gaṇī patāpī ca, taijassī ca durāsadau|

brahmūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

247.

 ‘‘pattadhammau dasabalau, balātibalapāragau|

dharaṇūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

248.

 ‘‘sīlavīcisamākiṇṇau, dhammaviñ‍ñāṇakhaubhitau|

udadhūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

249.

 ‘‘durāsadau duppasahau, acalau uggatau brahā|

nairūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

250.

 ‘‘anantañāṇau asamasamau, atulau aggataṃ gatau|

gaganūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

pan‍narasamaṃ bhāṇavāraṃ|

251.

 ‘‘patiṭṭhā  bhayabhītānaṃ, tāṇau saraṇagāminaṃ|

assāsakau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

252.

 ‘‘āsayau  buddhimantānaṃ, puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ sukhaisinaṃ|

ratanākarau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

253.

 ‘‘assāsakau vaidakarau, sāmañ‍ñaphaladāyakau|

maighūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

254.

 ‘‘laukacakkhu mahātaijau, sabbatamavinaudanau|

sūriyūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

255.

 ‘‘ārammaṇavimuttīsu, sabhāvadassanau muni|

candūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

256.

 ‘‘buddhau samussitau laukai, lakkhaṇaihi alaṅkatau|

appamaiyyau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

257.

 ‘‘yassa ñāṇaṃ appamaiyyaṃ, sīlaṃ yassa anūpamaṃ|

vimutti asadisā yassa, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

258.

 ‘‘yassa dhīti asadisā, thāmau yassa acintiyau|

yassa parak‍kamau jaiṭṭhau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

259.

 ‘‘rāgau  dausau ca mauhau ca, visā sabbai samūhatā|

agadūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

260.

 ‘‘k‍laisabyādhibahudukkha  sabbatamavinaudanau vinaudakau (sī॰ syā॰)|

vaij‍jūpamau mahāvīrau, sau mai buddhau nimantitau||

261.

 ‘‘buddhauti  bhau yaṃ vadaisi, ghausaupaisau sudul‍labhau|

buddhau buddhauti sutvāna, pīti mai udapaj‍jatha||

262.

 ‘‘abbhantaraṃ agaṇhantaṃ, pīti mai bahi niccharai|

sauhaṃ pītimanau santau, idaṃ vacanamabraviṃ||

263.

 ‘‘‘kahaṃ nu khau sau bhagavā, laukajaiṭṭhau narāsabhau|

tattha gantvā namassissaṃ, sāmañ‍ñaphaladāyakaṃ’||

264.

 ‘‘‘paggayha dakkhiṇaṃ bāhuṃ, vaidajātau katañ‍jalī|

ācikkhi mai dhammarājaṃ, saukasal‍lavinaudanaṃ||

265.

 ‘‘‘udaintaṃva  mahāmaighaṃ, nīlaṃ añ‍janasan‍nibhaṃ|

sāgaraṃ viya dissantaṃ, passasaitaṃ mahāvanaṃ||

266.

 ‘‘‘aittha sau vasatai buddhau, adantadamakau muni|

vinayantau ca vainaiyyai, baudhaintau baudhipakkhiyai||

267.

 ‘‘‘pipāsitauva udakaṃ, bhaujanaṃva jighacchitau|

gāvī yathā vacchagiddhā, aivāhaṃ viciniṃ jinaṃ||

268.

 ‘‘‘ācāraupacārañ‍ñū, dhammānucchavisaṃvaraṃ|

sikkhāpaimi sakai sissai, gacchantai jinasantikaṃ||

269.

 ‘‘‘durāsadā bhagavantau, sīhāva aikacārinau|

padai padaṃ nikkhipantā, āgacchaiyyātha māṇavā||

270.

 ‘‘‘āsīvisau yathā ghaurau, migarājāva kaisarī|

mattauva kuñ‍jarau dantī, aivaṃ buddhā durāsadā||

271.

 ‘‘‘uk‍kāsitañ‍ca  khipitaṃ, ajjhupaikkhiya māṇavā|

padai padaṃ nikkhipantā, upaitha buddhasantikaṃ||

272.

 ‘‘‘paṭisal‍lānagarukā, appasaddā durāsadā|

durūpasaṅkamā buddhā, garū haunti sadaivakai||

273.

 ‘‘‘yadāhaṃ  pañhaṃ pucchāmi, paṭisammaudayāmi vā|

appasaddā tadā hautha, munibhūtāva tiṭṭhatha||

274.

 ‘‘‘yaṃ  sau daisaiti sambuddhau saddhammaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), khaimaṃ nibbānapattiyā|

tamaivatthaṃ nisāmaitha, saddhammasavanaṃ sukhaṃ’||

275.

 ‘‘upasaṅkamma sambuddhaṃ, sammaudiṃ muninā ahaṃ|

taṃ kathaṃ vītisāraitvā, lakkhaṇai upadhārayiṃ||

276.

 ‘‘lakkhaṇai dvai ca kaṅkhāmi, passāmi tiṃsalakkhaṇai|

kausauhitavatthaguyhaṃ, iddhiyā dassayī muni||

277.

 ‘‘jivhaṃ nin‍nāmayitvāna, kaṇṇasautai ca nāsikai|

paṭimasi nalāṭantaṃ, kaivalaṃ chādayī jinau||

278.

 ‘‘tassāhaṃ  lakkhaṇai disvā, paripuṇṇai sabyañ‍janai|

buddhauti niṭṭhaṃ gantvāna, saha sissaihi pabbajiṃ||

279.

 ‘‘sataihi tīhi sahitau, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

addhamāsai asampattai, sabbai pattāmha nibbutiṃ||

280.

 ‘‘aikatau kammaṃ katvāna, puñ‍ñakkhaittai anuttarai|

aikatau saṃsaritvāna, aikatau vinivattayuṃ||

281.

 ‘‘gaupānasiyau  datvāna, pūgadhammai vasiṃ ahaṃ|

taina kammaina sukataina, aṭṭha haitū labhāmahaṃ||

282.

 ‘‘disāsu pūjitau haumi, bhaugā ca amitā mama|

patiṭṭhā haumi sabbaisaṃ, tāsau mama na vij‍jati||

283.

 ‘‘byādhayau mai na vij‍janti, dīghāyuṃ pālayāmi ca|

sukhumacchavikau haumi, āvāsai patthitai vasai āvāsai pattai vassai (syā॰), āvāsaiva ṭhitai vasai (ka॰)||

284.

 ‘‘aṭṭha gaupānasī datvā, pūgadhammai vasiṃ ahaṃ|

paṭisambhidārahattañ‍ca, aitaṃ mai aparaṭṭhamaṃ||

285.

 ‘‘sabbavausitavausānau, katakic‍cau anāsavau|

aṭṭhagaupānasī nāma, tava puttau mahāmuni||

286.

 ‘‘pañ‍ca thambhāni datvāna, pūgadhammai vasiṃ ahaṃ|

taina kammaina sukataina, pañ‍ca haitū labhāmahaṃ||

287.

 ‘‘acalau  haumi maittāya, anūnaṅgau bhavāmahaṃ|

ādaiyyavacanau haumi, na dhaṃsaimi yathā ahaṃ||

288.

 ‘‘abhantaṃ hauti mai cittaṃ, akhilau haumi kassaci|

taina kammaina sukataina, vimalau haumi sāsanai||

289.

 ‘‘sagāravau  sappatissau, katakic‍cau anāsavau|

sāvakau tai mahāvīra, bhikkhu taṃ vandatai muni||

290.

 ‘‘katvā sukatapal‍laṅkaṃ, sālāyaṃ pañ‍ñapaisahaṃ|

taina kammaina sukataina, pañ‍ca haitū labhāmahaṃ||

291.

 ‘‘uc‍cai  kulai pajāyitvā, mahābhaugau bhavāmahaṃ|

sabbasampattikau haumi, macchairaṃ mai na vij‍jati||

292.

 ‘‘gamanai patthitai mayhaṃ, pal‍laṅkau upatiṭṭhati|

saha pal‍laṅkasaiṭṭhaina, gacchāmi mama patthitaṃ||

293.

 ‘‘taina pal‍laṅkadānaina, tamaṃ sabbaṃ vinaudayiṃ|

sabbābhiñ‍ñābalappattau, thairau sailau (?) vandati taṃ muni||

294.

 ‘‘parakic‍cattakic‍cāni, sabbakic‍cāni sādhayiṃ|

taina kammaina sukataina, pāvisiṃ abhayaṃ puraṃ||

295.

 ‘‘pariniṭṭhitasālamhi, paribhaugamadāsahaṃ|

taina kammaina sukataina, saiṭṭhattaṃ ajjhupāgatau||

296.

 ‘‘yai kaici damakā laukai, hatthiassai damainti yai|

karitvā kāraṇā nānā, dāruṇaina damainti tai||

297.

 ‘‘na haivaṃ tvaṃ mahāvīra, damaisi naranāriyau|

adaṇḍaina asatthaina, damaisi uttamai damai||

298.

 ‘‘dānassa vaṇṇai kittaintau, daisanākusalau muni|

aikapañhaṃ kathaintauva, baudhaisi tisatai muni||

299.

 ‘‘dantā mayaṃ sārathinā, suvimuttā anāsavā|

sabbābhiñ‍ñābalapattā, nibbutā upadhikkhayai||

300.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

atik‍kantā bhayā sabbai, sālādānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

301.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ…pai॰… viharāmi anāsavau||

302.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ  vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

303.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sailau saparisau bhagavatau santikai imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sailatthairassāpadānaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. sabbakittikatthairaapadānaṃ

304.

 ‘‘kaṇikāraṃva  jalitaṃ jautantaṃ (sī॰), dīparukkhaṃva uj‍jalaṃ jautitaṃ (syā॰)|

ausadhiṃva viraucantaṃ, vij‍jutaṃ gaganai yathā||

305.

 ‘‘asambhītaṃ anuttāsiṃ, migarājaṃva kaisariṃ|

ñāṇālaukaṃ pakāsaintaṃ, maddantaṃ titthiyai gaṇai||

306.

 ‘‘uddharantaṃ imaṃ laukaṃ, chiddantaṃ sabbasaṃsayaṃ|

gaj‍jantaṃ asambhītaṃ (syā॰), gacchantaṃ (ka॰) migarājaṃva, addasaṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

307.

 ‘‘jaṭājinadharau āsiṃ, brahā uju patāpavā|

vākacīraṃ gahaitvāna, pādamūlai apatthariṃ||

308.

 ‘‘kāḷānusāriyaṃ gayha, anulimpiṃ tathāgataṃ|

sambuddhamanulimpaitvā, santhaviṃ laukanāyakaṃ||

309.

 ‘‘samuddharasimaṃ  laukaṃ, aughatiṇṇa aughatiṇṇau (syā॰ ka॰) mahāmuni|

ñāṇālaukaina jautaisi, nāvaṭaṃ pavaraṃ (syā॰), vajira (pī॰) ñāṇamuttamaṃ||

310.

 ‘‘dhammacak‍kaṃ tuvaṃ cak‍kaṃ (ka॰) pavattaisi, maddasai paratitthiyai|

usabhau jitasaṅgāmau, sampakampaisi maidaniṃ||

311.

 ‘‘mahāsamuddai ūmiyau, vailantamhi pabhij‍jarai|

tathaiva tava ñāṇamhi, sabbadiṭṭhī pabhij‍jarai||

312.

 ‘‘sukhumacchikajālaina, saramhi sampatānitai|

antaujālikatā jālagatā (sī॰) pāṇā, pīḷitā haunti tāvadai||

313.

 ‘‘tathaiva titthiyā laukai, puthupāsaṇḍanissitā mūḷhā sac‍cavinissaṭā (syā॰), muṭṭhasac‍cavinissaṭā (ka॰)|

antauñāṇavarai tuyhaṃ, parivattanti mārisa||

314.

 ‘‘patiṭṭhā  vuyhataṃ aughai, tvañhi nāthau abandhunaṃ|

bhayaṭṭitānaṃ saraṇaṃ, muttitthīnaṃ parāyaṇaṃ||

315.

 ‘‘aikavīrau  asadisau, maittākaruṇasañ‍cayau sañ‍ñutau (syā॰)|

asamau susamau santau susīlau asamau santau (sī॰), pañ‍ñavā yuttacāgau ca (syā॰), vasī tādī jitañ‍jayau||

316.

 ‘‘dhīrau  vigatasammauhau, anaijau akathaṃkathī|

tusitau vusitau (sī॰) vantadausausi, nimmalau saṃyatau suci||

317.

 ‘‘saṅgātigau hatamadau gatamadau (syā॰), tamanudau (ka॰), taivij‍jau tibhavantagau|

sīmātigau dhammagaru, gatatthau hitavabbhutau hitavappathau (sī॰ syā॰)||

318.

 ‘‘tārakau  tvaṃ yathā nāvā, nidhīvassāsakārakau|

asambhītau yathā sīhau, gajarājāva dappitau||

319.

 ‘‘thaumaitvā dasagāthāhi, padumuttaraṃ mahāyasaṃ|

vanditvā satthunau pādai, tuṇhī aṭṭhāsahaṃ tadā||

320.

 ‘‘padumuttarau laukavidū, āhutīnaṃ paṭiggahau|

bhikkhusaṅghai ṭhitau satthā, imā gāthā abhāsatha||

321.

 ‘‘‘yau mai sīlañ‍ca ñāṇañ‍ca, saddhammañ‍cāpi vaṇṇayi dhammañ‍cāpi pakittayi (sī॰ syā॰)|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

322.

 ‘‘‘saṭṭhi kappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati|

añ‍ñai daivaibhibhavitvā, issaraṃ kārayissati||

323.

 ‘‘‘sau pacchā pabbajitvāna, suk‍kamūlaina cauditau|

gautamassa bhagavatau, sāsanai pabbajissati||

324.

 ‘‘‘pabbajitvāna kāyaina, pāpakammaṃ vivaj‍jiya|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau’||

325.

 ‘‘yathāpi maighau thanayaṃ, tappaiti maidiniṃ imaṃ|

tathaiva tvaṃ mahāvīra, dhammaina tappayī mamaṃ||

326.

 ‘‘sīlaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, thavitvā laukanāyakaṃ|

pattaumhi paramaṃ santiṃ, nibbānaṃ padamac‍cutaṃ||

327.

 ‘‘ahau  nūna sa bhagavā, ciraṃ tiṭṭhaiyya cakkhumā|

añ‍ñātañ‍ca vijānaiyyuṃ, phusaiyyuṃ añ‍ñātañ‍cāpi jānaiyya, passaiyya (ka॰) amataṃ padaṃ||

328.

 ‘‘ayaṃ  mai pacchimā jāti, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

329.

 ‘‘satasahassitau kappai, yaṃ buddhamabhithaumayiṃ

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, kittanāya idaṃ phalaṃ||

330.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

331.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

332.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sabbakittikau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

sabbakittikatthairassāpadānaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. madhudāyakatthairaapadānaṃ

333.

 ‘‘sindhuyā nadiyā tīrai, sukatau assamau mama|

tattha vācaimahaṃ sissai, itihāsaṃ salakkhaṇaṃ||

334.

 ‘‘dhammakāmā vinītā tai, sautukāmā susāsanaṃ|

chaḷaṅgai pāramippattā, sindhukūlai vasanti tai||

335.

 ‘‘uppātagamanai  caiva, lakkhaṇaisu ca kauvidā|

uttamatthaṃ gavaisantā, vasanti vipinai tadā||

336.

 ‘‘sumaidhau nāma sambuddhau, laukai uppaj‍ji tāvadai|

amhākaṃ anukampantau, upāgacchi vināyakau||

337.

 ‘‘upāgataṃ mahāvīraṃ, sumaidhaṃ laukanāyakaṃ|

tiṇasanthārakaṃ katvā, laukajaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ||

338.

 ‘‘vipinātau  madhuṃ gayha, buddhasaiṭṭhassadāsahaṃ|

sambuddhau paribhuñ‍jitvā, idaṃ vacanamabravi||

339.

 ‘‘‘yau taṃ adāsi madhuṃ mai, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

340.

 ‘‘‘iminā madhudānaina, tiṇasanthārakaina ca|

tiṃsa kappasahassāni, daivalaukai ramissati||

341.

 ‘‘‘tiṃsa  kappasahassamhi, auk‍kākakulasambhavau|

gautamau nāma gauttaina, satthā laukai bhavissati||

342.

 ‘‘‘tassa dhammaisu dāyādau, aurasau dhammanimmitau|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, nibbāyissatināsavau||

343.

 ‘‘‘daivalaukā idhāgantvā, mātukucchiṃ upāgatai|

madhuvassaṃ pavassittha, chādayaṃ madhunā mahiṃ’||

344.

 ‘‘mayi nikkhantamattamhi, kucchiyā ca suduttarā|

tatrāpi madhuvassaṃ mai, vassatai nic‍cakālikaṃ||

345.

 ‘‘agārā  abhinikkhamma, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ|

lābhī an‍nassa pānassa, madhudānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

346.

 ‘‘sabbakāmasamiddhauhaṃ, bhavitvā daivamānusai|

tainaiva madhudānaina, pattaumhi āsavakkhayaṃ||

347.

 ‘‘vuṭṭhamhi daivai caturaṅgulai tiṇai, sampupphitai supupphitai (syā॰) dharaṇīruhai sañchan‍nai vappadaisai (syā॰)|

suñ‍ñai gharai maṇḍaparukkhamūlakai, vasāmi nic‍caṃ sukhitau anāsavau||

348.

 ‘‘majjhai mahantai hīnai ca majjhai mayhaṃ bhavā assu (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰), bhavai sabbai atik‍kamiṃ yai bhavai samatik‍kamiṃ (syā॰ ka॰), yau bhavaisu pakittayi (ka॰)|

aj‍ja mai āsavā khīṇā, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

349.

 ‘‘tiṃsakappasahassamhi, yaṃ dānamadadiṃ tadā|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, madhudānassidaṃ phalaṃ||

350.

 ‘‘kilaisā  jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

351.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

352.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā madhudāyakau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

madhudāyakatthairassāpadānaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. padumakūṭāgāriyatthairaapadānaṃ

353.

 ‘‘piyadassī  nāma bhagavā, sayambhū laukanāyakau|

vivaikakāmau sambuddhau, samādhikusalau muni||

354.

 ‘‘vanasaṇḍaṃ  samaugayha, piyadassī mahāmuni|

paṃsukūlaṃ pattharitvā, nisīdi purisuttamau||

355.

 ‘‘migaluddau purai āsiṃ, arañ‍ñai vipinai (sī॰), irinai (syā॰ ka॰) kānanai ahaṃ|

pasadaṃ migamaisantau, āhiṇḍāmi ahaṃ tadā||

356.

 ‘‘tatthaddasāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ, aughatiṇṇamanāsavaṃ|

pupphitaṃ sālarājaṃva, sataraṃsiṃva uggataṃ||

357.

 ‘‘disvānahaṃ daivadaivaṃ, piyadassiṃ mahāyasaṃ|

jātassaraṃ samaugayha, padumaṃ āhariṃ tadā||

358.

 ‘‘āharitvāna padumaṃ, satapattaṃ manauramaṃ|

kūṭāgāraṃ karitvāna, chādayiṃ padumainahaṃ||

359.

 ‘‘anukampakau  kāruṇikau, piyadassī mahāmuni|

sattarattindivaṃ buddhau, kūṭāgārai vasī jinau||

360.

 ‘‘purāṇaṃ chaḍḍayitvāna, navaina chādayiṃ ahaṃ|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, aṭṭhāsiṃ tāvadai ahaṃ||

361.

 ‘‘vuṭṭhahitvā  samādhimhā, piyadassī mahāmuni|

disaṃ anuvilaukaintau, nisīdi laukanāyakau||

362.

 ‘‘tadā  sudassanau nāma, upaṭṭhākau mahiddhikau|

cittamañ‍ñāya buddhassa, piyadassissa satthunau||

363.

 ‘‘asītiyā sahassaihi, bhikkhūhi parivāritau|

vanantai sukhamāsīnaṃ, upaisi laukanāyakaṃ||

364.

 ‘‘yāvatā vanasaṇḍamhi, adhivatthā ca daivatā|

buddhassa cittamañ‍ñāya, sabbai san‍nipatuṃ tadā||

365.

 ‘‘samāgataisu yakkhaisu, kumbhaṇḍai saharakkhasai|

bhikkhusaṅghai ca sampattai, gāthā pabyāharī sabyāharī (syā॰), mābyāharī (sī॰) jinau||

366.

 ‘‘‘yau maṃ sattāhaṃ pūjaisi, āvāsañ‍ca akāsi mai|

tamahaṃ kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

367.

 ‘‘‘sududdasaṃ sunipuṇaṃ, gambhīraṃ suppakāsitaṃ|

ñāṇaina kittayissāmi, suṇātha mama bhāsatau||

368.

 ‘‘‘catuddasāni  kappāni, daivaraj‍jaṃ karissati|

kūṭāgāraṃ mahantassa brahaṃ tassa (sī॰ syā॰), padmapupphaihi chāditaṃ||

369.

 ‘‘‘ākāsai dhārayissati, pupphakammassidaṃ pubbakammassidaṃ (syā॰) phalaṃ|

catubbīsai catuddasai (syā॰) kappasatai, vaukiṇṇaṃ saṃsarissati||

370.

 ‘‘‘tattha pupphamayaṃ byamhaṃ, ākāsai dhārayissati|

yathā padumapattamhi, tauyaṃ na upalimpati||

371.

 ‘‘‘tathaivīmassa ñāṇamhi, kilaisā naupalimparai|

manasā vinivaṭṭaitvā, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai ayaṃ||

372.

 ‘‘‘cittaṃ  janaitvā naikkhammai, agārā pabbajissati|

tatau pupphamayai byamhai, dhāraintai pupphamayaṃ byamhaṃ, dhāraintaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) nikkhamissati||

373.

 ‘‘‘rukkhamūlai vasantassa, nipakassa satīmatau|

tattha pupphamayaṃ byamhaṃ, matthakai dhārayissati||

374.

 ‘‘‘cīvaraṃ  piṇḍapātañ‍ca, pac‍cayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ|

datvāna bhikkhusaṅghassa, nibbāyissatināsavau’||

375.

 ‘‘kūṭāgāraina caratā caraṇā (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), caritai (syā॰), pabbaj‍jaṃ abhinikkhamiṃ|

rukkhamūlai vasantampi vasantamhi (sī॰), vasataupi (?), kūṭāgāraṃ dharīyati||

376.

 ‘‘cīvarai piṇḍapātai ca, caitanā mai na vij‍jati|

puñ‍ñakammaina saṃyuttau, labhāmi pariniṭṭhitaṃ||

377.

 ‘‘gaṇanātau asaṅkhaiyyā, kappakauṭī bahū mama|

rittakā tai atik‍kantā, pamuttā laukanāyakā||

378.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai kappasatai, piyadassī vināyakau|

tamahaṃ payirupāsitvā, imaṃ yauniṃ upāgatau||

379.

 ‘‘idha passāmi idhaddasāsiṃ (sī॰) sambuddhaṃ, anaumaṃ nāma cakkhumaṃ|

tamahaṃ upagantvāna, pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ||

380.

 ‘‘dukkhassantakarau buddhau, maggaṃ mai daisayī jinau|

tassa dhammaṃ suṇitvāna, pattaumhi acalaṃ padaṃ||

381.

 ‘‘tausayitvāna sambuddhaṃ, gautamaṃ sakyapuṅgavaṃ|

sabbāsavai pariñ‍ñāya, viharāmi anāsavau||

382.

 ‘‘aṭṭhārasai  kappasatai, yaṃ buddhamabhipūjayiṃ|

duggatiṃ nābhijānāmi, buddhapūjāyidaṃ phalaṃ||

383.

 ‘‘kilaisā jhāpitā mayhaṃ, bhavā sabbai samūhatā|

sabbāsavā parikkhīṇā, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

384.

 ‘‘svāgataṃ vata mai āsi…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ||

385.

 ‘‘paṭisambhidā catassau…pai॰… kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ’’||

itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā padumakūṭāgāriyau thairau imā gāthāyau abhāsitthāti|

padumakūṭāgāriyatthairassāpadānaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. bākulatthairaapadānaṃ

386.

 ‘‘himavantassāvidūrai, saubhitau nāma pabbatau|

assamau sukatau mayhaṃ, sakasissaihi māpitau||

387.

 ‘‘maṇḍapā ca bahū tattha, pupphitā sinduvārakā|

kapitthā ca bahū tattha, pupphitā jīvajīvakā campakā nāgakaitakā (syā॰)||

388.

 ‘‘nigguṇḍiyau bahū tattha, badarāmalakāni ca|
